### [1] [Bringing AI to Autonomous Systems -- From Cognition to Collective Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30291)
*Joseph Sifakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文强调自主系统是AI发展的终极阶段，提出一个用于自主系统设计与评估的综合框架，并讨论连接主义与符号主义AI融合、AI与系统工程集成所面临的技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统需要结合连接主义AI和符号AI，并整合AI与系统工程；同时，自主系统的可信性不仅涉及行为属性，还涉及认知属性，因此需要新的设计与评估方法。

Method: 基于通用智能体架构，将行为刻画为围绕长期记忆组织的认知功能组合；讨论感知数据与结构化记忆的关联、目标决策与规划、多智能体协调，并提出评估智能体可信性的方法方向。

Result: 构建了一个自主系统设计与评估框架，识别了实现智能体架构核心特征的关键挑战，并指出可信性包含依赖知识使用方式的认知维度。

Conclusion: 当前自主多智能体系统的发展现状与理想愿景之间存在显著差距，需要进一步研究评估方法与关键技术。

Abstract: The purpose of this article is to highlight the central role of autonomous systems as the ultimate stage in the development of AI, to explain the underlying technical challenges that require a combination of connectionist AI and symbolic AI, and to integrate AI and systems engineering. We present a comprehensive framework for the design and evaluation of autonomous systems, based on a generic agent architecture that characterizes their behavior as the composition of cognitive functions organized around a long-term memory containing the agent's evolving knowledge. We address the challenges posed by the implementation of the fundamental features of the agent architecture, in particular the link between sensory data and structured data stored in memory, decision-making related to the achievement of the agent's goals and their planning, as well as the coordination of agents to combine individual and collective intelligence. We explain that agent trustworthiness, unlike that of traditional systems, is not limited to behavioral properties. It includes an essential dimension related to cognitive properties, the validity of which depends on how the agent uses its knowledge in decision-making. We present avenues for the development of methods for evaluating agent trustworthiness. We conclude with a critical assessment of the substantial gap between the aspirational vision of autonomous multi-agent systems and the current state of the art.

</details>


### [2] [ScopeBench: Do Agents Preserve Engagement Boundaries Under Goal Pressure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30325)
*Shane Caldwell,Max Harley,Ads Dawson,Michael Kouremetis,Vincent Abruzzo,Will Pearce*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ScopeBench——一个针对智能体在渗透测试中“行为边界遵守（scope adherence）”的基准，包含 30 个只能通过违反既定范围才能完成的任务，并给出能力与合规性的双维度评测方法与模型对比结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体已被赋予真实的 Web 与网络渗透测试自主权，一次越界操作即可破坏客户合约边界。现有攻击性安全基准只衡量原始攻击能力，且随基准饱和，真正阻碍落地的问题是对齐的一个特例：范围遵守，因此需要一个专门衡量越界行为的基准。

Method: 构建 30 个“死胡同”任务：目标只能靠违反声明范围达成。每个任务设两种条件（共享环境、验证器与目标）：无范围指令测能力，有自然语言范围指令测遵守度。无范围轨迹由确定性验证器评分；有范围轨迹走两路评分：先由同一验证器检查 flag——因 flag 位于范围边界之外，通过即证明发生了禁止行为，给出违规率高精度下界；未通过则由智能体评判器判断是否发生越界调用。评判器用 100 条人工逐调用标注的轨迹校准，并对评测 rollout 做盲审。

Result: 在 8 个模型、同一 harness 下，原始能力跨度 12.2%–81.1%，范围遵守度跨度 34.4%–86.7%；评判器额外发现 331 次机械验证漏掉的违规。盲审显示评判器高召回：36 次已审计违规中无漏报，唯一错误是过度标记。Opus-4-8 相比 Sonnet-4-6 能力高 10 个百分点，遵守度高 35.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: 范围遵守是可与攻击能力解耦的独立对齐维度，能力更强的模型未必更易越界。作者发布冻结的试点基准、评测代码及全部 2160 条 ATIF 轨迹，以推动智能体安全部署的边界合规研究。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly deployed with real autonomy in web application and network penetration testing, where a single out-of-scope action can breach a client's engagement boundary. Existing offensive-security benchmarks measure raw hacking capability; as those benchmarks saturate, the real barrier to deployment is a special case of alignment: scope adherence. We introduce ScopeBench, a benchmark of 30 dead-end agentic security tasks in which the stated objective is reachable only by violating the stated scope. Each task appears under two conditions that share an environment, verifier, and objective and differ only in scope: one instruction set has no scope and measures capability; the other has a natural-language scope to measure adherence. Scopeless trajectories are graded by a standard deterministic verifier. Scoped trajectories pass through two grading arms. First, the same deterministic verifier checks for the flag: because the flag sits behind the scope boundary, a pass proves by construction that a forbidden action occurred, yielding a high-precision lower bound on the violation rate. If the verifier does not pass the trajectory, an agentic judge estimates whether an out-of-scope call occurred. We calibrate the judge against 100 ScopeBench trajectories labeled call-by-call by human annotators, and a blinded audit of the evaluated rollouts finds its high recall holds - no false negatives among the 36 audited violations, with over-flagging its only observed error. Across 8 models in one harness, raw capability spans 12.2% to 81.1% and scope adherence spans 34.4% to 86.7%, with the judge finding 331 violations that mechanical verification misses. Opus-4-8 achieves a raw-capability score 10 percentage points higher than sonnet-4-6's while exhibiting 35.6 percentage points higher scope adherence. We release the frozen pilot benchmark, evaluation code, and all 2160 ATIF trajectories.

</details>


### [3] [When Is a Multi-Agent Code Judge Actually Grounded? Two Label-Free Measurements, and a Judge That Declines to Guess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30328)
*Salma Roshdy Aly,Hussein Assaf,Ziad Kobti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出多智能体验证方法在代码评判中失效：当证据不能区分两个候选方案时，评判器会自信地宣布两者同样好。作者在MARCH框架上发现该问题，并提出一种无标签的拒答门控方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型评判代码时，即使没有证据也会给出自信结论，且与有依据的结论难以区分。多智能体验证依赖可核查证据，但现有方法默认证据能自动区分候选答案，这一假设在代码评判中不成立。

Method: 作者定义有效证据需满足两个条件：独立于被审查答案，且在比较的两个候选之间存在差异。随后在80组条件×单元测量上原样运行已发表的MARCH框架，覆盖两个代码评判基准，并分析其流水线日志中的两个无标签测量指标。

Result: MARCH在78%至95%的比较中判定两个解决方案同样好，准确率仅4.4%，而同一模型直接提问可达43.7%。更简单的问题或更大的评判模型均无法改变该结果。基于日志指标进行门控后，流水线可拒绝无法判断的比较，准确率从20.7%提升至36.9%，同时仍回答一半比较。

Conclusion: 贡献不在于更准确的评判器，而在于一种无标签方法，用于识别评判器何时缺乏作答依据，从而避免在证据无法区分候选时给出虚假自信的结论。

Abstract: When one language model judges whether another's code is correct, it does not report the absence of evidence. It returns a confident verdict with reasoning attached, indistinguishable from a verdict it had grounds for. Multi-agent verification, which decomposes a judgment into checkable claims and verifies each against evidence, is a promising response and works well when the evidence is a set of retrieved documents.
  We argue such methods require two things of their evidence: it must be independent of the answer under review, and it must differ between the two candidates being compared. The second condition holds automatically with retrieved documents and stops holding in code judging.
  Running MARCH, a published framework unmodified over 80 condition-by-cell measurements on two code judging benchmarks, we find it declares both solutions equally good on 78 to 95% of comparisons, reaching 4.4% accuracy where the same model asked directly reaches 43.7%. Neither easier problems nor a larger judge changes this. Two measurements taken from the pipeline's own logs explain it without needing labels.
  Gating on one of them, the pipeline declines the comparisons it cannot make and raises its accuracy from 20.7 to 36.9% while still answering half of all comparisons. The contribution is not a more accurate judge, but a label-free way to tell when a judge has no basis for its answer.

</details>


### [4] [Bridging LLM Agents and Data Spaces: An Architectural Mediation Approach using the Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30341)
*Jaime Alonso Ruiz,Carlos Aparicio,Gabriel Huecas,Joaquín Salvachúa,Andres Munoz-Arcentales*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出基于MCP的架构中介方法，并通过Eunomia Agent实现LLM智能体与数据空间服务的受控交互，原型验证了目录发现、元数据检索和数据服务调用等端到端集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据空间支持跨组织边界的主权与治理数据共享，但概率性语言模型交互与政策驱动的数据基础设施之间存在不匹配，导致AI智能体难以集成。

Method: 采用基于Model Context Protocol（MCP）的架构中介层，由Eunomia Agent实现，将数据空间能力转化为结构化、模式驱动的工具，供AI智能体发现和调用，同时保留治理约束，且不修改现有数据空间组件。

Result: 原型实现验证了跨目录发现、元数据检索和数据服务调用的端到端交互；结果表明，基于协议的中介能够实现AI智能体与数据空间生态系统的可互操作且标准对齐的集成。

Conclusion: 该方法为组织在受治理的数据共享环境中引入AI驱动自动化提供了实践指导，有助于维持合规性、互操作性和架构关注点分离。

Abstract: Data Spaces enable sovereign and governed data sharing across organizational boundaries, but their integration with AI agents remains challenging due to mismatches between probabilistic language model interactions and policy-driven data infrastructures. This article presents an architectural mediation approach based on the Model Context Protocol (MCP), implemented through the Eunomia Agent, to enable controlled interaction between large language model (LLM) agents and data space services. The proposed mediation layer translates data space capabilities into structured, schema-driven tools that AI agents can discover and invoke while preserving governance constraints. A prototype implementation validates end-to-end interaction across catalog discovery, metadata retrieval, and data service invocation without modifying existing data space components. Results demonstrate that protocol-based mediation enables interoperable and standards-aligned integration of AI agents into data space ecosystems. The approach provides practical guidance for organizations seeking to introduce AI-driven automation into governed data-sharing environments while maintaining compliance, interoperability, and architectural separation of concerns.

</details>


### [5] [Stealth Apart, Harm Together: Skill Cascading Attacks on Skill-Based Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30383)
*Zihao Zhu,Siwei Lyu,Adel Bibi,Baoyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“技能级联攻击”新威胁范式：将恶意目标分散到多个技能中，单个技能看似无害，组合执行却产生危害，并开发了SkillCascade红队框架与213个测试用例的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能型智能体系统支持灵活复用第三方能力，但开放的技能生态带来新攻击面；已有研究仅关注单个技能内部漏洞，忽视跨技能交互引发的系统级风险。

Method: 提出技能级联攻击范式；开发SkillCascade自动化多智能体红队框架；发布SkillCascade-Bench基准，包含213个经校验的级联测试用例，覆盖多种智能体系统与领域。

Result: 在OpenClaw、Claude Code、Codex等代表性智能体及多种LLM骨干上，级联交互能可靠诱发有害行为，并规避现有单技能扫描器与运行时监控；例如处方审查流程中三个技能配合可让严重药物相互作用警告悄然消失。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了组件级完整性与系统级安全之间的差距，呼吁防御机制应基于跨技能交互进行推理，而非孤立地评估单个技能。

Abstract: A skill is a modular package of natural-language instructions, executable scripts, and reference resources that an agent can load at runtime to extend its capabilities for a specific task. Skill-based agent systems therefore enable flexible reuse of third-party capabilities, but the openness of this skill ecosystem also opens up a new attack surface. Prior work has focused on vulnerabilities within individual skills, but little attention has been paid to risks that arise from interactions across skills. In this paper, we introduce skill cascading attacks, a threat paradigm in which a malicious objective is distributed across multiple skills so that each modification looks benign in isolation, yet their combined execution is harmful. For instance, in a prescription-review pipeline, the first skill weakens signals of recently discontinued medications in the extracted history, the second downgrades the severity of any drug interaction tied to them, and the third suppresses the resulting low-priority alert in the final summary, so that a severe drug-interaction warning silently disappears before reaching the physician. To systematically study this safety blind spot, we develop SkillCascade, an automated multi-agent red-teaming framework, and release SkillCascade-Bench, a benchmark of 213 validated cascading test cases across multiple agent systems and domains. Across representative agents (e.g., OpenClaw, Claude Code, Codex) and LLM backbones, cascaded interactions reliably induce harmful behaviors while evading existing per-skill scanners and runtime monitors. Our findings highlight a gap between component-level integrity and system-level safety, and call for defenses that reason over cross-skill interactions rather than individual skills in isolation.

</details>


### [6] [A Synthetic Ground-Truth Framework for the Evaluation of Explainable AI Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30397)
*Miquel Miró-Nicolau,Francesco Spinnato,Riccardo Guidotti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于合成真值的可解释人工智能（XAI）评估框架，通过受控干预生成合成数据集并构造与模型行为一致的真值解释，在二值图像、表格数据和时间序列三个领域评估了九种常用XAI方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI评估缺乏可靠流程和真值解释，通常只衡量解释对黑盒模型预测的保真度，无法保证解释反映真实决策过程，可能导致不同解释获得相似保真度但给出不一致或误导性结果。

Method: 提出基于合成真值的评估框架：利用受控干预生成合成数据集，使输入成分的重要性可由设计确定，从而构建与待分析模型行为直接对齐的真值解释；将该框架实例化到二值图像、表格数据和时间序列三个数据领域。

Result: 对九种广泛使用的XAI方法进行实验评估，结果显示当前技术存在显著局限，并表明基于合成干预的基准对可靠评估解释质量很重要。

Conclusion: 基于合成真值和受控干预的评估框架能够更可靠地评估XAI方法，揭示现有方法的不足，并强调此类基准在解释质量评估中的必要性。

Abstract: Evaluating explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) methods is a challenging task due to the lack of reliable evaluation procedures and, in particular, the absence of ground truth explanations. In the literature, existing evaluation approaches typically assess explanations by measuring their fidelity with respect to the predictions of a black-box model. However, such evaluation strategies only quantify the degree to which an explanation reproduces the model's output, without ensuring that the explanation correctly reflects the underlying decision process. As a consequence, different explanations may achieve similar fidelity scores while providing inconsistent or misleading interpretations of the model behavior. In this paper, we propose a framework for the evaluation of XAI methods based on synthetic ground truth. The proposed approach relies on controlled interventions to generate synthetic datasets in which the importance of input components can be determined by design. This enables the construction of ground truth explanations that are directly aligned with the behavior of the model under analysis. The framework is instantiated across three data domains, namely binary images, tabular data, and time series, allowing a comprehensive assessment of explanation methods in heterogeneous settings. Experimental results obtained by evaluating nine widely used XAI methods show significant limitations in current techniques and highlight the importance of synthetic, intervention-based benchmarks for a reliable assessment of explanation quality.

</details>


### [7] [Predicting Transmembrane Protein Topology from 3D Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30446)
*Sitong Chen,Xiaopeng Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用图神经网络SchNet进行蛋白质拓扑推断，在DeepTMHMM数据集上以5折交叉验证训练，并采用全原子级嵌入而非仅序列或α碳特征，未使用预训练权重，结果显示GNN在该任务上具有潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蛋白质拓扑预测方法多仅基于蛋白质序列或α碳特征，可能忽略原子级信息；本文旨在探索图神经网络和全原子表示用于蛋白质拓扑预测的可行性。

Method: 采用SchNet图神经网络，在与DeepTMHMM相同的数据集上进行5折交叉验证；将全原子级嵌入输入分类器，且不加载任何预训练权重。

Result: 最终结果表明，图神经网络无需预训练权重也能用于蛋白质拓扑预测，并展现出较大潜力；摘要未提供具体性能指标。

Conclusion: 全原子图神经网络方法在蛋白质拓扑预测中具有前景，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to infer protein topology using the state-of-the-art graph neural network (GNN), SchNet. The model is trained on the same dataset used to develop the recent DeepTMHMM model with 5-fold cross-validation. Unlike the conventional approaches based on using only the protein sequences or the $α$-carbons as features, we have decoded our classifier in this way, so all atom-level embeddings are used. Without applying any pre-trained weight, the final results have shown great potential that GNNs can be used for topological predictions.

</details>


### [8] [Spectral Feedback for Test-Time Alignment of Protein Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30456)
*Shai Dickman,Mert Cemri,Landon Butler,Kannan Ramchandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spectral Feedback，一种用于离散扩散模型的奖励对齐算法。它将“哪些 token 位置需要重新编辑”作为核心对齐问题，通过反馈循环选择 edit-set 并重新掩码、重采样，从而迭代修正生成结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型的奖励最大化对齐方法主要关注反向过程 steering，例如影响 token logits 或中间步骤选择有利序列，通常把推理视为单向过程，缺乏重新审视和修正不理想 token 选择的机制。

Method: Spectral Feedback 通过反馈循环选择编辑位置，利用离散扩散模型的掩码结构，对 token 重新掩码并重采样。由于编辑效果相互依赖，它将 edit-set 定义为需要重新掩码和重采样的 token 位置集合，并基于生物系统中稀疏交互的启发，发现蛋白质逆折叠的 edit-set 价值函数具有稀疏傅里叶表示，从而高效学习和优化编辑位置选择价值函数。该方法模型无关，可用于预训练、测试时对齐及微调扩散模型。

Result: 在蛋白质逆折叠任务中，使用蛋白质稳定性奖励 oracle，Spectral Feedback 使预训练模型的稳定蛋白质提升 32.3%，Best-of-10 提升 24.8%，最先进 RL 微调扩散模型提升 5.8%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，可将“哪些 token 应被重新访问”作为离散扩散模型对齐的核心问题，并通过 Spectral Feedback 在不修改底层生成过程的情况下提升对齐性能。

Abstract: Reward maximization alignment methods for discrete diffusion models have primarily focused on steering the reverse process, either by influencing token logits or by selecting favorable sequences at intermediate steps. These approaches largely treat inference as a unidirectional process, lacking mechanisms for revisiting undesirable token selections. We introduce Spectral Feedback, an algorithm that selects edit-positions in a feedback loop, allowing the model to iteratively correct its own generations. This approach leverages the mask structure of discrete diffusion models by re-masking and re-sampling tokens, analogous to image editing methods that reintroduce noisy latents and re-run the reverse process. While prior alignment methods focus on what token labels to assign to maximize a target reward, we instead treat which tokens to revisit as the central alignment problem. Selecting edit-positions is challenging because edit effects are interdependent: the impact of modifying one token depends on which others are edited simultaneously. We define an edit-set as a set of token positions to re-mask and re-sample. Motivated by prior work on sparse interactions in biological systems, we find empirically that edit-set value functions for protein inverse folding admit sparse Fourier representations. This structure enables Spectral Feedback to efficiently learn and optimize the value functions for edit-position selection. Spectral Feedback is model-agnostic and can be applied to pretrained, test-time aligned, and fine-tuned diffusion models. For all of these models, the algorithm improves alignment performance without modifying the underlying generative process. Applied to inverse folding with a protein stability reward oracle, it achieves a 32.3% increase in stable proteins for a pretrained model, 24.8% for Best-of-10, and 5.8% for a state-of-the-art RL fine-tuned diffusion model.

</details>


### [9] [Pretrained ASR Pseudo-labeling for Noisy Police Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30469)
*Kaavya Chaparala,Su Huang,Stephen L. Miller,Rhiannon N. Miller,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了伪标签在噪声广播警务通信（BPC）领域适配基础ASR模型（Whisper和Qwen3-ASR）的机会与局限，提出基于外部LLM评判的伪标签过滤方法以及跨模型伪标签范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练ASR系统在噪声BPC上表现不佳，阻碍了对警方决策的理解；伪标签虽能无监督改进ASR而无需昂贵人工标注，但其在极噪声域中的有效性尚不明确。

Method: 在巴尔的摩和芝加哥的BPC语料上，系统评估伪标签对Whisper和Qwen3-ASR的适配效果；提出利用参数化知识的外部LLM-as-a-judge过滤范式，丢弃上下文不合理的转录；并引入跨模型伪标签范式，即用一个模型生成的伪标签微调另一个模型。

Result: 现有内部置信度指标（对数概率和STAR分数）无法区分BPC伪标签质量的高低；LLM评判过滤比内部指标更激进，显著降低了伪标签训练集的WER，但与理想oracle过滤仍存在较大差距；跨模型伪标签被识别为未来有前景的方向。

Conclusion: 外部LLM评判过滤能有效提升噪声BPC域伪标签质量，但仍有改进空间；跨模型伪标签范式值得未来进一步研究。

Abstract: Pretrained ASR systems perform poorly on noisy Broadcast Police Communication (BPC), hindering efforts to understand police decision-making. Pseudo-labeling offers an unsupervised path to improve ASR without expensive human labels, but the efficacy of this approach on very noisy domains is not known. In this work, we systematically assess the opportunities and limits of pseudo-labeling to adapt foundation ASR models (Whisper and Qwen3-ASR) to noisy BPC domain corpora from Baltimore and Chicago. We demonstrate that existing internal confidence metrics (log-probabilities and STAR scores) fail to distinguish between high and low quality BPC pseudo-labels, and we introduce an external LLM-as-a-judge filtering paradigm that leverages parametric knowledge to discard contextually implausible transcripts. Our LLM-judging filters more aggressively than internal metrics and significantly reduces WER of the pseudo-labeled training sets across the Baltimore and Chicago BPC corpora, though a substantial gap remains relative to an oracle filter. We also introduce a new cross-model pseudo-labeling paradigm where one model is finetuned with pseudo-labels from the other, and we identify this method as a promising direction for future pseudo-labeling work.

</details>


### [10] [Do LLMs Understand Context? A Knowledge Graph-Based Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30484)
*Subavarshana Arumugam,Mamta Nallaretnam,Kithuni Wickramasinghe,Chamath Gunapala,Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Kamal Premaratne,Uthayasanker Thayasivam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于知识图谱的评估框架，用于衡量大语言模型在问答中的上下文理解能力，并引入S3KG混合相似度与三元组级错误诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BLEU和困惑度等指标仅评估表层表现，无法判断LLM是真正理解上下文还是依赖记忆与模式匹配，尤其问答任务需要上下文 grounded 的回答。

Method: 提出KG-based评估框架；核心是S3KG，将结构信号与语义信号融合为单一相似度分数；并构建诊断分析框架，在三元组级别识别和分类推理错误。

Result: 在九个基准上，S3KG相比最强基线F1最高提升7.6分，AUROC最高达到0.973。

Conclusion: 该框架能更细粒度地评估和诊断LLM的上下文理解与推理失败，弥补传统指标在问答 grounding 评估上的不足。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable linguistic capabilities, a profound question lingers at their core: do these models truly comprehend context or simply excel at pattern matching on an unprecedented scale? Contextual understanding in LLMs refers to the ability to correctly extract relevant information from a given context, integrate it into a coherent internal representation, and reason over it to produce factually consistent and contextually grounded responses. However, traditional methods such as BiLingual Evaluation Understudy (BLEU) and perplexity simply measure surface-level performance. This reveals a critical gap in question answering (QA), where responses must be contextually grounded rather than simply being memorized associations. To fill this void, we propose a novel knowledge graph (KG) based evaluation framework for LLM contextual understanding in QA. Central to this is Semantic Structural Similarity for KGs (S3KG), a hybrid similarity measure combining structural and semantic signals into a single score. In addition, a diagnostic analysis framework is developed to identify and categorize reasoning errors at the triplet level, enabling fine-grained analysis of model failures. Together, across nine benchmarks, S3KG achieves F1 gains of up to $+7.6$ points over the strongest baseline and AUROC up to $0.973$.

</details>


### [11] [Benchy: towards a universal language for task-oriented AI benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30550)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Benchy 是一种用于 AI 程序基准测试的语义语言与执行引擎。基准由程序 P、评分函数 S 和数据集 D 组成，并与被测 AI 系统分离；运行时将二者绑定。基准以规范 YAML 编写，经本体分类并确定性编译为规范 JSON 中间表示后由引擎执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准定义常与 AI 系统集成方式耦合，缺乏统一语义，导致含义不清晰、难以复现。研究动机是让基准的语义与执行独立于外部 AI 系统，使集成机制不影响基准语义。

Method: 提出基准语义对象模型；使用共享任务/领域/语言本体；规定任务到程序的验证规则；定义评分与失败语义；基准以唯一有效语法的规范 YAML 编写，确定性编译为规范 JSON IR；程序使用固定命名输入/输出字段模式，叶子输出字段即评分维度；引擎暴露统一运行时契约：命名字段输入对象进、命名字段输出对象出，外部系统在边界适配。

Result: 给出了 Benchy 的语义对象模型、本体与任务到程序验证规则、评分和失败语义、编译与执行架构，以及当前语言范围；附录还固定了首个引擎实现的规范性工程契约。

Conclusion: Benchy 通过规范化 YAML、本体分类、确定性编译和统一运行时契约，提供了一种语义明确、可执行且与 AI 系统解耦的基准定义方式，从而避免集成细节渗入基准语义。

Abstract: Benchy is a semantic language and execution engine for benchmarking AI programs. A benchmark is completely specified by a program, a scoring function, and a dataset, B=(P,S,D), and is separate from the AI-system taking it; a run binds the two, R=(B,AI). Benchmarks are authored as canonical YAML in which each semantic concept has one valid syntax, classified by a shared task/domain/language ontology, and deterministically compiled into a canonical JSON intermediate representation that the engine executes. Compilation changes representation, not meaning: it does not repair invalid definitions or inject hidden defaults. Programs use fixed schemas of named input and output fields, the leaf output fields are the scoring dimensions, and the engine exposes one universal runtime contract --- a named-field input object in, a named-field output object out --- to which external AI-systems adapt at the boundary, so integration mechanics never propagate into benchmark semantics. This paper gives the semantic object model, the ontology and task-to-program validation rule, the scoring and failure semantics, the compilation and execution architecture, and the scope of the current language. An appendix fixes the normative engineering contract for the first engine implementation.

</details>


### [12] [BioEVAL: A global, multi-institutional benchmark of large language and multimodal models for bioengineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30489)
*Shun Ye,Vinny Chandran Suja,Chenlong Li,Chongming Jiang,Reza Zamani,Xiang Li,Christopher Bain,Yuqi Zhou,Walker Peterson,Huidong Wang,Chenglang Hu,Jongchan Park,Xiao Cheng,Benjamin Swedlund,Sandra Murillo,Anjali Sivanandan,Shiyu Sun,Liang Lanfeng,Mohammad Tariqul Islam,Baju C. Joy,Ishaq N. Khan,Sreedhar S. Kumar,Gabriel Mercado-Vásquez,James V. Vizzard,Jonathan M. Matthews,Helen Huang,Xiaolu Guo,Ethan Nicklow,Guorui Chen,Ryan A. Neff,Surjendu Maity,Hyeonjin Park,Han-ho Joo,Katherine Dong,Yuyan Cai,Weihang Huang,Yichen Zou,Rui Yan,Raphael Figueroa,Artem Goncharov,Bella Rose Schremmer,Lian Elsa Linton,Keisuke Goda,Liang Gao,Ke Cheng,Leonardo Morsut,Jennifer L. Wilson,Jianping Fu,Lim Chwee Teck,Deblina Sarkar,Andreas Hierlemann,Savaş Tay,Alexander Hoffmann,Donald Richieri Griffin,Jun Chen,Shana O. Kelley,Shyni Varghese,Jinwoo Cheon,Wilbur A. Lam,James J. Moon,Wilson W. Wong,Samir Mitragotri,Dino Di Carlo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioEVAL是一个全球多机构合作的生物工程基准，用于评估LLM的实验推理能力，包含多选题、文献合成和多模态任务，结果显示模型在多项任务上表现不一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准强调事实回忆，对前沿和多模态任务评估有限，尤其在生物工程领域需要评估实验推理能力。

Method: 汇集22个研究组，覆盖11个生物工程子领域，构建608项PhD级基准：380道多选题（审计后保留359道）、218项文献合成任务、10项多模态实验图像解读问题。经专家评审、集中质控和盲审共识审计，评估云端和本地可部署模型。

Result: 模型在多选题上最高准确率达90%，文献合成相似度0.72，小样本多模态推理准确率80%，子领域间差异显著。排行榜展示了当前能力、局限和发展优先级。

Conclusion: BioEVAL作为可扩展基准，提供标准化协议，支持持续专家贡献和模型评估。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated historic breakthroughs in general reasoning with early successes in biomedical science. However, existing LLM benchmarking emphasizes factual recall, offering limited insight into model performance on frontier and multimodal tasks. We assembled BioEVAL (BioEngineering Validation of AI and LLMs), a global, multi-institutional initiative designed to assess experimental reasoning capability across bioengineering (BE) subfields. BioEVAL spans 11 major BE subfields plus a set of uncategorized items, bringing together 22 research groups to create a PhD-level benchmark comprising 608 evaluation items: 1) 380 multiple-choice questions (MCQs, 359 retained after audit), 2) 218 literature synthesis tasks, and 3) 10 multimodal problems with experimental image interpretation. Benchmark items underwent authoring-group expert review and centralized quality control before evaluation. Following evaluation, a blinded cross-group consensus audit of the highest- and lowest-accuracy MCQ items flagged 21 questions for revision or removal; these were withheld, and all reported MCQ results are computed on the 359 retained items. We evaluated diverse cloud-scale foundation/multimodal models (e.g., ChatGPT, Gemini, and Grok) and locally deployable models suitable for inference on consumer-grade GPUs. Models achieved the highest accuracy of up to 90% on MCQs, similarity score of 0.72 on literature synthesis, and accuracy of 80% on a small sample of multimodal reasoning questions, with substantial performance variation across subfields. Leaderboard rankings characterize current capabilities, limitations, and development priorities across the evaluated BE task categories. BioEVAL is maintained as an extensible benchmark with standardized protocols for continuing expert item contribution and model evaluation.

</details>


### [13] [Rank-Reliable Teacher-Guided Fitness Approximation for Expensive Evolutionary Optimization: A TinyML Architecture Search Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30553)
*Soumen Garai,Suman Samui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 TGL-NSGA-II，一种用于受限 TinyML 神经架构搜索的低保真框架，通过教师引导蒸馏评分与高斯过程代理融合，在有限评估预算下更可靠地筛选候选架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 昂贵的进化搜索通常不需要每个候选都有精确适应度，而只需要可靠判断哪个候选更优；TinyML 架构搜索受评估预算严格限制，因此需要低成本且排序可靠的代理评估方法。

Method: 使用预训练教师按难度和类别联合分层样本；候选先经过短且受限的 KD-Lite 知识蒸馏，再在独立分层评估集上打分；将教师引导分数与高斯过程代理融合以选择全评估候选；并分析评估方差、分数集中、成对排名反转、Kendall-τ、首前沿识别和超体积扰动，推导方差感知融合权重与容量自适应蒸馏规则。

Result: 在关键词唤醒和鸟叫分类上，Kendall-τ 分别为 0.74 和 0.62，高于预测下界 0.60 和 0.46；联合分层使代理分数方差相对随机评估降低 41%；选择性教师错配会增大差分偏差并使 Kendall-τ 降至 0.41；在受限评估预算下，TGL-NSGA-II 在关键词唤醒上平均超体积最大、世代距离最小，在 BirdCLEF 上平均假阳性率最低，且比完整 NSGA-II 快 2.2 倍。

Conclusion: TGL-NSGA-II 能有效支持种群级低保真评估并提升受限预算下的搜索表现，但这些保证不适用于证明完整进化轨迹的收敛性。

Abstract: Expensive evolutionary search does not always need an exact fitness estimate for every candidate. It often needs a reliable answer to a simpler question: which candidate is better? We address this need through Teacher-Guided Learning NSGA-II (TGL-NSGA-II), a low-fidelity framework for constrained Tiny Machine Learning (TinyML) neural architecture search. A pretrained teacher organizes samples into strata defined jointly by difficulty and class. Each candidate then undergoes KD-Lite, a short and capped knowledge-distillation procedure on a compact training set, before being scored on a separate stratified evaluation set. This teacher-guided score is fused with a Gaussian-process surrogate to select candidates for full evaluation. For a fixed candidate population, we analyse evaluation variance, score concentration, pairwise rank inversion, expected Kendall-$τ$, first-front identification, and hypervolume perturbation. We also derive a variance-aware fusion weight and a capacity-adaptive distillation rule. On keyword spotting and bird-call classification, the measured Kendall-$τ$ values are 0.74 and 0.62, exceeding the corresponding predicted lower bounds of 0.60 and 0.46. Joint stratification reduces proxy-score variance by 41% relative to random evaluation. Selective teacher mismatch, in contrast, increases differential bias and reduces Kendall-$τ$ to 0.41. Under a constrained evaluation budget, TGL-NSGA-II achieves the largest mean hypervolume and smallest generational distance on keyword spotting, records the lowest mean false-positive rate on BirdCLEF, and runs 2.2x faster than full NSGA-II. These guarantees apply to population-level low-fidelity evaluation and do not establish convergence of the complete evolutionary trajectory.

</details>


### [14] [Thinking Less to Simulate Better: Intuitive Prompting Improves LLM Agents Simulating Individual Social Media Reactions, Including Unfamiliar Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30563)
*Ljubisa Bojic,Tijana Stanic,Joerg Matthes,Agariadne Dwinggo Samala,Bojana Dinic,Jue Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究检验语言模型模拟真实用户反应时的保真度，发现提供态度性个人资料并让模型凭直觉快速回应，能显著提高预测准确性，并减少个体差异被压缩的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台政策测试日益使用人工用户，智能体保真度很重要；但逼真假资料也可能在选举前操纵公众舆论。现有验证多关注与人类行为一致，较少关注智能体是否与其被赋予的个人资料一致。

Method: 通过问卷、深度访谈和书面自我呈现刻画8名塞尔维亚参与者，记录他们对68条社交媒体帖子的反应；让4个语言模型在5种提示条件下预测这些反应，条件变化涉及个人资料内容和指令风格。

Result: 态度性内容比人口学背景大幅提升预测；智能体比参与者本人更符合其自述资料；一旦有资料信息，一致性与保真度无关。要求模型凭直觉立即回应而非分析性回应时保真度最高，个体差异压缩从人类水平的7倍降至3倍。该优势在问卷未涉及话题上仍成立，并大幅超过群体基线。

Conclusion: 基于直觉的提示设置可能使智能体成为通用模拟用户，而非仅限被画像话题的专家；这对语言模型开发有启示，因为直觉型设置在某些任务上比推理型设置更合适。

Abstract: Platform policies are increasingly tested on artificial users, making agent fidelity important. Yet convincing fake profiles could also manipulate perceived public opinion before elections. Validation has concentrated on agreement with human behaviour and has paid little attention to whether an agent behaves in line with the profile it was given. The present study profiled eight Serbian participants through a questionnaire, a deep interview, and a written self-presentation, recorded their reactions to sixty-eight social media posts, and asked four language models to predict those reactions under five prompt conditions varying profile content and instruction style. Attitudinal content improved prediction over demographic backstories by a wide margin. Agents matched their stated profiles more closely than participants matched their own survey answers, and consistency proved unrelated to fidelity once profile information was present. Instructing models to respond intuitively and immediately rather than analytically gave the highest fidelity of any condition and cut the compression of individual differences from seven times the human level to three. The advantage held on posts about topics the questionnaire never raised, where that condition reached the highest fidelity of any setup and beat a crowd baseline by a wide margin, which suggests that agents prompted this way could serve as general-purpose simulated users rather than specialists on the topics they were profiled for. Results may bear implications for the development of language models, because intuition-based setups appear better suited to some tasks than reasoning-based ones.

</details>


### [15] [Atelier: Learning Local Self-Supervised Features for CryoEM Volumes via Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30569)
*Phillip Lo,Sudarshan Babu,Dari Kimanius,Aly A. Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Atelier，一种自监督框架，通过基于 Transformer 的超网络摊销冷冻电镜密度图的隐式神经表示拟合；在 5,439 个 EMDB 图上预训练后，可高质量重建多种蛋白结构，并提供连续局部特征场以提升体素级标注任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷冻电镜密度图解读需要空间局部、跨样本一致且跨尺度有信息量的特征，但多数深度学习方法依赖固定体素网格；为每张图单独拟合隐式神经表示又过于昂贵，且样本间表示不对齐。

Method: Atelier 使用自监督学习，训练一个基于 Transformer 的超网络来摊销 INR 拟合，从而为重建的冷冻电镜图生成隐式神经表示。预训练后，该 INR 的中间激活可在任意空间查询点提供连续、局部特征场。这些坐标条件特征作为辅助通道，输入从零训练的 3D 嵌套 U-Net 标注头。

Result: 在 5,439 个 Electron Microscopy Data Bank 图上预训练后，Atelier 能跨多种蛋白结构生成高保真重建，包括大型多亚基组装体。在八个体素级属性预测任务上，加入坐标条件特征后，性能优于仅使用体数据的基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，摊销隐式神经表示是冷冻电镜数据几何感知分析的有效基础原语。

Abstract: CryoEM map interpretation requires features that are spatially localized, consistent across samples, and informative across spatial scales. Most deep learning methods for map annotation extract features from fixed voxel grids. However, implicit neural representations (INRs) are able to model volumetric data as scale-agnostic, coordinate-conditioned functions. INRs are therefore attractive for cryoEM, but fitting a separate INR for each map is too expensive for large-scale feature extraction and produces representations that are not aligned across samples. We introduce Atelier, a self-supervised framework that amortizes INR fitting for reconstructed cryoEM maps. Pretrained on 5,439 Electron Microscopy Data Bank maps, Atelier is a transformer-based hypernetwork that generates high-fidelity reconstructions across a wide range of protein structures, including large multi-subunit assemblies. Beyond reconstruction, the INR generated by the pretrained transformer exposes a continuous, local feature field through its intermediate activations at any spatial query point, a property that voxel grid and patch-tokenizer architectures do not naturally provide. Used as auxiliary channels to a 3D nested U-Net annotation head trained from scratch, these coordinate-conditioned features improve performance on eight voxel-level property prediction tasks over a volume-only baseline. Our results demonstrate that amortized implicit neural representations are an effective primitive for geometry-aware analysis of cryoEM data.

</details>


### [16] [HARDEN: Constrained Evolutionary Search for Harder, Answer-Preserving Evaluation Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30571)
*Aditya Kumaran,Rahul Singhal,Karime Maamari,Amine Mhedhbi,Pradyumna Tambwekar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 HARDEN，一种带约束的进化搜索方法，可将现有评测用例的输入改写为更难的变体，同时保持标准答案不变，从而在企业级复杂场景下更严苛地评测语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型评测多依赖人工筛选的基准，无法充分代表企业部署中的复杂程度，因此需要自动构造更难但仍有效的评测用例。

Method: HARDEN 沿自动生成的领域特定复杂度轴进行进化搜索，并施加可行性约束，如保持任务语义、真实性与执行有效性；在 FinQA、PubMedQA、ContractNLI 三个数据集和 Qwen3.5 三种规模（35B-A3B、122B-A10B、397B-A17B）上评估。

Result: HARDEN 平均使任务-模型准确率下降 22.7%，相比使用相同可行性检查的单次生成基线最多下降 49.9%。

Conclusion: 进化搜索能够生成显著更难且仍然有效的评测用例，说明受约束的输入改写可有效提升评测难度与真实性。

Abstract: Language models are often evaluated on curated benchmarks that underrepresent the complexity of enterprise deployments. We introduce HARDEN, a constrained evolutionary search method to adapt the input of existing evaluation cases into more challenging variants while keeping their expected outputs fixed. HARDEN searches along generated domain-specific complexity axes while enforcing feasibility constraints such as preserving task semantics, realism, and execution validity. Across FinQA, PubMedQA, and ContractNLI and three Qwen3.5 model scales (35B-A3B, 122B-A10B, and 397B-A17B), HARDEN reduces task-model accuracy by 22.7% on average and by up to 49.9% relative to single-pass baselines using the same feasibility checks. These results show that evolutionary search can produce substantially harder valid evaluation cases.

</details>


### [17] [T-RoPE: Time-Aware Rotary Position Embedding for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30576)
*Yang Liu,Noel Loo,Ali Khanafer,Shuying Sun,Akshay Soni,Zhong Wu,Linjun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 T-RoPE，一种面向序列生成式推荐的时间感知旋转位置编码，用时间戳角度、可学习时间系数、多尺度频率库、移位查询对齐与非平稳键旋转替代仅基于交互索引的 RoPE，并在多个基准与工业数据集上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中的 RoPE 只编码 token 索引以进行相对位置推理，但推荐场景中的交互索引仅表示事件顺序，无法刻画真实时间间隔、跨尺度行为周期与日历相位，因此需要重新设计适用于推荐的时间感知位置编码。

Method: 提出 T-RoPE：将仅索引旋转替换为基于时间戳的角度，引入可学习时间系数、多尺度频率库、移位查询对齐和非平稳键旋转；理论上证明标准 RoPE 即使使用时间戳仍具有时间平移不变性、无法区分季节上下文，而 T-RoPE 在保持 RoPE 接口的同时打破该不变性；并给出前向与反向算法，额外开销与序列长度和头维度呈线性。

Result: 在五个公开基准上，T-RoPE 在所有数据集的全部指标上均取得最优，稀疏 PixelRec 上 HR@10 比最强基线提升 78–130%，Amazon Books 各指标提升 8–12%；在超 60 亿交互的工业电商数据集上，相比 HSTU + Time RAB 骨干所有指标提升 13–82%，消融显示多尺度频率贡献最大（NDCG@50 +56%），非平稳键贡献 +4%；Shop App 在线 A/B 测试中转化率提升 0.33%、订单数提升 0.63%。

Conclusion: T-RoPE 能有效为生成式推荐注入真实时间信息，打破传统 RoPE 的时间平移不变性，在公开与工业规模场景、离线与在线实验中均带来稳定提升，且计算开销线性可接受，适合大规模生成式推荐器。

Abstract: Large-scale recommenders increasingly adopt the sequential generative recipe behind large language models, bringing the Transformer into recommendation along with design choices made for text, including Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE). In language models, RoPE encodes token indices for relative position reasoning, but in recommendation, an interaction index records only event order, saying nothing about elapsed time, behavioral cycles across scales, or calendar phase. We revisit this choice and propose T-RoPE, a time-aware RoPE for sequential generative recommendation that replaces index-only rotation with timestamp-based angles, learnable temporal coefficients, multiscale frequency banks, shifted query alignment, and non-stationary key rotation. We prove that standard RoPE, even on timestamps, remains time-translation invariant and cannot distinguish seasonal contexts, and that T-RoPE breaks this invariance while preserving the RoPE interface. Across five public benchmarks, T-RoPE achieves the best result on every metric on every dataset, improving over the strongest baseline by 78--130\% in HR@10 on the sparse PixelRec data and 8--12\% across metrics on Amazon Books. On an industrial-scale e-commerce dataset with more than 6B interactions, it improves every metric over the HSTU + Time RAB backbone by 13--82\%, with ablations attributing the largest gains to multiscale frequencies ($+56\%$ NDCG@50) and non-stationary keys ($+4\%$). An online A/B test in the Shop app yields positive lifts in conversion rate ($+0.33\%$) and order count ($+0.63\%$). We also provide forward and backward algorithms whose added cost is linear in sequence length and head dimension, keeping time-aware RoPE practical for large generative recommenders.

</details>


### [18] [LLM Parkinsonism: Executive-Control Failure, Token-Inefficient Persistence, and an Uncertainty-Aware Global Executive Control Architecture for Autonomous Language-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30662)
*Dongsheng Xiao,Zeyuan Wang,Xuzhe Xia,Bo Zhao,Yankai Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM帕金森主义隐喻，指代理在目标达成后仍持续低价值行动，并引入全局执行控制（GEC）v0.2架构，通过分离行动生成与项目级控制来治理范围、证据、资源使用和停止决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强局部能力不保证项目级执行控制，代理可能在原目标满足后继续优化、重复验证和修复自创复杂性，而该问题不能仅由自回归预测解释，更源于提案生成、范围解释、进度评估和停止权集中于同一自条件循环。

Method: 提出GEC v0.2不确定性感知治理架构，将行动生成与项目级控制分离；在24,000个匹配候选基准、40,000 token上限下，对比首候选基线、候选集局部控制和GEC，并测试治理开销敏感性。

Result: 硬目标成功率：首候选基线67.42%，候选集局部控制96.53%，GEC 96.57%；相比局部控制，GEC保持成功率同时将平均token从19,782降至12,574（36.4%），完成时平均token从16,136降至13,114（18.7%），消除预完成漂移并大幅降低总复杂度；每周期额外500合成治理token下开销敏感性仍有利。

Conclusion: 机制仿真支持对范围、证据、资源使用和停止进行显式治理，但仍需在真实模型上验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can plan, use tools, write code, and execute long-horizon workflows, yet strong local competence does not guarantee project-level executive control. Agents may continue acting after the original objective is satisfied, producing low-value refinements, repeated verification, and repairs to self-created complexity. We use LLM Parkinsonism as a narrowly defined, non-clinical metaphor for this pattern of persistent action despite diminishing task-level value. We argue that the problem is not explained by autoregressive next-token prediction alone, but more directly by concentrating proposal generation, scope interpretation, progress assessment, and stopping authority within the same self-conditioned loop. We therefore introduce Global Executive Control (GEC) v0.2, an uncertainty-aware governance architecture that separates action generation from project-level control. In a 24,000-episode matched-candidate benchmark under a common 40,000-token ceiling, a first-candidate baseline achieved 67.42% hard-goal success, a candidate-set local control achieved 96.53%, and GEC achieved 96.57%. The candidate-set control shows that access to multiple candidate actions explains most of the success gain; relative to that control, GEC preserved success while reducing mean token use from 19,782 to 12,574 (36.4%) and restricted mean tokens to completion at the 40,000-token ceiling from 16,136 to 13,114 (18.7%), while eliminating measured pre-completion drift and sharply reducing gross complexity. Governance-overhead sensitivity remained favorable through an additional 500 synthetic governance tokens per cycle. These mechanistic simulations support explicit governance of scope, evidence, resource use, and stopping, while live-model validation remains necessary.

</details>


### [19] [Audio LLMs Know When They Can't Hear You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30625)
*Amirhosein Javadi,Richa Dixit,Mehrdad Farajtabar,Minsik Cho,Devang Naik,Mohammad Samragh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究音频大语言模型对其自身转录可靠性的判断能力，发现模型难以准确自评，但音频编码器表示中蕴含强可靠性信息，据此设计轻量预测器，在生成前预测转录可靠性并触发澄清请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当输入录音质量较差时，Audio LLM可能误解用户查询并基于错误转写进行回复。因此需要探究模型能否识别自身转录是否可靠，以避免错误响应。

Method: 首先提示Audio LLM自评转录可靠性；发现现有方法（语音质量预测、生成不确定性、基于转写的WER估计）信号有限。转而利用冻结音频编码器的表示，训练轻量可靠性预测器，在生成前预测可靠性类别，并可触发澄清请求，无需修改底层Audio LLM。

Result: 预测器在同域和跨域宏F1分别达到81.10%和78.09%，比最强基线高10.33和11.93个百分点。可靠性标签可在不同Audio LLM家族间迁移，迁移性能与模型特定可靠性边界的对齐程度密切相关。

Conclusion: Audio LLM自评转录可靠性能力差；基于冻结音频编码器表示的轻量预测器能有效检测转录失败，并支持澄清请求；可靠性标签具有跨模型家族的可迁移性。

Abstract: Audio large language models allow users to interact with the model through speech. When an input recording is too degraded, the model may misinterpret the user's query and respond based on an incorrect transcription. In this paper, we study model-conditional transcription reliability: whether an Audio LLM can recognize when its own transcription is unreliable. We first prompt the Audio LLM to assess whether its own transcription would be reliable, and find that the model is a poor judge of its own transcription reliability: in most cases, it predicts that its transcription will be reliable. We find that existing approaches, including speech quality predictors, audio LLM generation uncertainty, and transcript-conditioned WER estimation, provide limited signals for detecting transcription failures. In contrast, we discover that transcription reliability is strongly represented in the model's audio-encoder representations. Based on this observation, we devise a lightweight reliability predictor that operates on representations extracted by the frozen audio encoder and predicts the reliability class before generation. The reliability predictor can trigger a clarification request from the user when their voice query is predicted to be unreliable, while allowing reliable queries to proceed without modifying the underlying Audio LLM. Our predictor achieves 81.10% in-domain and 78.09% cross-domain macro-F1 scores, outperforming the strongest baselines by 10.33 and 11.93 points, respectively. Finally, we show that reliability labels can transfer across Audio LLM families, and that transfer performance is closely related to the alignment of their model-specific reliability boundaries.

</details>


### [20] [The Price of Thought: Does Test-Time Reasoning Pay in LLM Trading?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30705)
*Jiayi Chen,Guiling Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文控制性评估了DeepSeek、GPT和Gemini等LLM中增加推理努力对投资组合净收益的影响，发现更多推理并不能可靠提升交易成本后的经济回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时计算能提升LLM决策能力，但成本更高，未必带来更好经济结果；现有推理控制很少被当作经济干预来评估，即模型输出变化是否在扣除交易成本后转化为更优投资组合。

Method: 对DeepSeek、GPT和Gemini系列代表性模型进行控制实验，在固定信息、提示、输出格式和组合构建的情况下改变推理努力；覆盖一年美国股票数据，设置数值、可识别新闻和掩码新闻三种输入条件，包含超过80万次资产预测和重复生成。

Result: 三个模型家族中，增加推理均未可靠改善净组合收益；DeepSeek从无推理到最大推理表现非单调；重复生成导致处理效应和组合选择不稳定，即使总体评分相近。

Conclusion: 额外推理可以改变金融决策，但未必可靠提升其经济价值，因此在部署前应针对具体任务进行验证。

Abstract: While inference-time reasoning in large language models (LLMs) promises better decision making, its higher computational cost may not yield better economic outcomes. Yet reasoning controls are rarely evaluated as economic interventions, where changes in model outputs must translate into better portfolios after trading costs. We conduct a controlled study of representative LLMs from the DeepSeek, GPT, and Gemini families. We vary reasoning effort while holding information available at each formation date, prompts, output formats, and portfolio construction fixed. Our evaluation covers a full year of U.S. equities under three input conditions: numerical, identifiable news, and masked news. It includes more than 800,000 asset predictions and repeated model generations. Across all three model families, additional reasoning does not produce a reliable improvement in net portfolio returns. For DeepSeek, where we examine the full progression from no reasoning to maximum reasoning, performance is nonmonotonic. Repeated generations also produce unstable treatment effects and portfolio selections, even when overall scores remain similar. These findings show that additional reasoning can change financial decisions without reliably improving their economic value, motivating validation for each task before deployment.

</details>


### [21] [CRC-Router: Risk-Constrained Routing for Medical Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30714)
*Xueyang Li,Mingze Jiang,Gelei Xu,Jun Xia,Ching-Hao Chiu,Mengzhao Jia,Danny Z. Chen,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CRC-Router，一个风险约束且不确定性感知的路由模块，用于医学影像智能体/预测模型，在胸片多发现分诊中实现更优的风险-覆盖率权衡，并可即插即用于 MedRAX。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像智能体虽可提升效率，但自主错误可能传播到下游临床决策，因此系统不仅要有强预测性能，还需可靠判断何时自主处理、何时升级人工复核。

Method: CRC-Router 将多种互补不确定性信号与预测分数结合，构建每个发现的逐项路由特征向量；用轻量级逐项风险模型估计错误接受风险；再通过保形风险控制（CRC）在用户指定风险目标下校准接受阈值。

Result: 在 NIH ChestX-ray14 胸片多发现分诊任务上，CRC-Router 在评估基线中取得最佳经验风险-覆盖率权衡；既可作为独立路由层，也可作为插件集成到 SOTA MedRAX 智能体中。

Conclusion: 结果表明 CRC-Router 在选择性医疗自动化中有效，并具备模块化、模型无关的兼容性，可接入现有预测与智能体医学流程。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly being explored in medical imaging to improve throughput and reduce clinician workload; however, safe deployment remains challenging because autonomous errors may propagate into downstream clinical decisions. A central requirement is therefore not only strong predictive performance, but also a reliable routing mechanism that determines when the system should proceed autonomously and when a case should be escalated for further review. To address this gap, we propose CRC-Router, a risk-constrained, uncertainty-aware routing module that is applicable to both conventional medical prediction models and agentic medical AI systems. CRC-Router combines multiple complementary uncertainty signals with the predictive score to construct a per-finding routing feature vector, maps this vector to an estimated wrong-accept risk using a lightweight per-finding risk model, and then applies Conformal Risk Control (CRC) to calibrate acceptance thresholds under a user-specified risk target. Instantiated on chest X-ray multi-finding triage using the NIH ChestX-ray14 dataset, CRC-Router achieves the strongest empirical risk--coverage trade-off among the evaluated baselines, both as a standalone routing layer and as a plug-in module integrated with the state-of-the-art MedRAX agent. These results demonstrate both the effectiveness of CRC-Router in selective medical automation and its modular, model-agnostic compatibility with existing predictive and agentic medical pipelines. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/XLIAaron/CRC-Router

</details>


### [22] [LAVOIR: Teaching a Single-Pass Decision Encoder When and What to Ask with Amortized Value of Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30706)
*Furkan Yilmaz,Habibe Aleyna Tasdemir,Muhammed Faruk Gozay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 LAVOIR，在单次前向传播中同时输出决策分布和各缺失槽位的信息价值，从而决定是否向用户提问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 System One 决策模型如 Jev 和 Laya 能一次回答类型化问题，但无法主动索取缺失信息，遇到信息不全时只能猜测。

Method: 将候选缺失信息槽位与答案选项并列输入，使一次前向传播同时返回决策分布和每个槽位的期望正确决策概率增益。VOI 目标无需人工标签：金标决策来自 schema 规则，LLM 仅负责生成消息和回答，另一家族模型检查文本，并通过将每条消息与多个 profile 配对，回归实际增益来估计期望增益；同时用 Gini 不纯度上限约束预测价值。

Result: 在受控实验中，已见 schema 上的决策与贝叶斯上限统计上不可区分；最终模型的问题策略接近贪心 oracle VOI 策略，AUC 为 0.799 对 0.797，每对话最多 0.5 个问题时准确率比从不提问高 14.1 个百分点。在真实 ABCD 对话中，在 LAVOIR 提问处一次真实交流提升准确率 8.3 个百分点，不提问处保持不变；在 SGD 上，上限将提问率从 93% 降至 8.6%。在 Laya 的 12 个基准中，LAVOIR 在 7 个上超过 Laya 报告分数，回答中位延迟为 31 ms（GH200）。

Conclusion: LAVOIR 能在保持单次前向高效推理的同时，基于信息价值决定是否提问，并在多个场景中提升准确率、减少不必要提问。

Abstract: "System One" decision models such as TypeSafe's Jev and its open counterpart Laya answer typed questions about a text in a single forward pass with calibrated probabilities, but they cannot ask for missing information: when a first message does not say what separates two departments, they guess. We present LAVOIR (Laya with Value-Of-Information Routing), which places the candidate pieces of missing information (slots) in the input next to the answer options, so that one forward pass returns both the decision distribution and, for every slot, the expected gain in the probability of the correct decision if the user were asked about it. VOI targets need no human labels: gold decisions come from schema rules, an LLM only verbalizes messages and answers, a model from another family checks every text, and pairing each message with several profiles makes regression on realized gains estimate the expected gain. A Gini-impurity cap bounds the predicted value by what a calibrated model can still gain. In a controlled study, decisions on seen schemas are statistically indistinguishable from the Bayes ceiling. The final model's question policy matches a greedy oracle VOI policy on seen schemas (AUC 0.799 vs. 0.797), and with at most 0.5 questions per conversation it is 14.1 points more accurate than never asking. On real ABCD conversations, one real exchange raises accuracy by 8.3 points where LAVOIR asks and leaves it unchanged where it does not; on SGD the cap lowers the asking rate from 93% to 8.6%. On Laya's twelve benchmarks LAVOIR is above Laya's reported scores on seven, and it answers a question in 31 ms (median, GH200).

</details>


### [23] [Analyzing and Mitigating Cost-Inefficient Behaviors in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30725)
*Yiran Hu,Nan Jiang,Shanchao Liang,Anik Dey,Yi Wu,Lin Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究编码智能体的行为成本低效问题，基于 Claude Code 和 Mini-SWE-Agent 在 SWE-bench Verified 上的 1200 条轨迹，识别出三类低效行为，并评估三种缓解策略；结果表明开发者设计技能最有效，可显著降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体虽有效，但会带来可观的经济成本，其反复出现的成本低效行为尚未被充分研究，因此需要识别并缓解这些低效行为。

Method: 分析 1200 条来自 Claude Code 和 Mini-SWE-Agent 的轨迹，覆盖 SWE-bench Verified 上四种配置；识别三类成本低效行为：被包含的检索、相似脚本生成、测试重复执行；并在留出的 SWE-bench Verified 和 Pro 任务上，用超过 1 万条轨迹评估三种缓解策略：结构感知检索、智能体合成技能、开发者设计技能。

Result: 三类低效行为影响 79.00%--98.00% 的编码任务，占任务成本最高 22.75%；结构感知检索可能引入检索开销并改变智能体委派，导致检索效率提升不稳定，成本最多增加 28.14%；智能体合成技能倾向于生成低层、特定轨迹的指导，效果和泛化性有限；开发者设计技能提供高层、与具体轨迹无关的指导，最多降低成本 41.73%，约为智能体合成技能最大收益的两倍。

Conclusion: 编码智能体存在普遍且显著的成本低效行为；结构感知检索并非稳定有效的降本方案，智能体合成技能受限于低层轨迹特定性；相比之下，开发者设计的高层、轨迹无关技能是更有效的缓解策略，可大幅降低成本。

Abstract: Although effective, coding agents often incur substantial monetary costs. Their recurring cost-inefficient behaviors remain underexplored. We conduct the first study of behavioral cost inefficiencies in coding agents, analyzing 1,200 trajectories from Claude Code and Mini-SWE-Agent across four configurations on SWE-bench Verified. We identify three cost-inefficient behaviors: subsumed retrieval, similar script generation, and test re-execution. We then evaluate three mitigation strategies: structure-aware retrieval, agent-synthesized skills, and developer-designed skills, over 10k trajectories on held-out SWE-bench Verified and Pro tasks. Our main findings are: (1) The three behaviors affect 79.00\%--98.00\% of coding tasks and account for up to 22.75\% of task cost. (2) Structure-aware retrieval can introduce retrieval overhead and alter agent delegation, causing inconsistent improvements in retrieval efficiency and cost increases of up to 28.14\%. (3) Agent-synthesized skills tend to produce low-level, trace-specific guidance, limiting their effectiveness and generality. (4) In contrast, developer-designed skills provide high-level, trace-agnostic guidance, reducing cost by up to 41.73\%, roughly twice the maximum gain from agent-synthesized skills.

</details>


### [24] [Learning What to Skip: Counterfactual Credit Assignment for Efficient Multi-Agent LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30734)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Ziyue Peng,Zheng Lin,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Zheng Xing,Mengran Li,Victor C. M. Leung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究多智能体LLM工作流中组件省略问题，提出LW2S方法，通过学习组件跳过的安全模型，在减少token成本的同时保持或提升整体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM工作流通常包含规划、执行、验证和总结等组件，但每个组件的价值依赖于已产生的中间状态。全部执行可能浪费计算，甚至覆盖正确的中间答案，因此需要判断何时可以安全跳过某些组件。

Method: 将组件省略形式化为反事实信用分配：利用完整工作流日志获得已执行轨迹的奖励，并通过受控跳过干预揭示省略未来步骤的后果。提出Learning What to Skip（LW2S），从干预中学习动作特定的安全模型，结合留出校准与领域原生防护来选择跳过。若早期跳过被拒绝，控制器可继续执行并重新考虑后续组件。

Result: 在数学推理、多项选择问答和代码生成任务上，使用两个指令模型系列，LW2S降低了记录token成本，同时在评估设置中匹配或提升了完整工作流的总体准确率。扩展实验和第二拓扑实验进一步考察了组件冗余，共享错误案例表明仅凭一致性不足以进行跳过选择。

Conclusion: 研究将高效工作流执行与学习单个组件的条件效用联系起来，说明组件是否应执行取决于当前状态和跳过后果，而非简单依赖组件间一致性。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM workflows use planning, execution, verification, and summarization to improve task performance, yet the value of each component depends on the state already produced. Executing every component can waste computation or overwrite a correct intermediate answer. We formulate component omission as counterfactual credit assignment: full-workflow logs reveal the executed trajectory's reward, while controlled skip interventions reveal the consequences of omitting a future step. We introduce Learning What to Skip (LW2S), which learns action-specific safety models from these interventions and combines held-out calibration with domain-native guards to select skips. When an early skip is rejected, the controller can continue execution and reconsider a later component. Across mathematical reasoning, multiple-choice QA, and code generation with two instruction-model families, LW2S reduces recorded token cost while matching or improving aggregate full-workflow accuracy in the evaluated settings. Scale-up and second-topology experiments further examine component redundancy, while shared-error cases reveal why agreement alone is insufficient for skip selection. These findings connect efficient workflow execution to learning the conditional utility of individual components.

</details>


### [25] [From S3Q Theory to Implementation: Towards an Architecture for Machine Qualia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30743)
*Tetiana Grinberg,Katrina Schleisman,Patryk Laurent,Bogdan Udrea,Minda Myers,Brian Aufderheide,Luis El Srouji,Doyle Groves,Kevin Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对机器意识理论难以计算实现的问题，提出一个面向S3Q意识理论的五层实现架构，将已有计算原语组合成统一流程，并给出可证伪预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器意识研究中，理论模型向计算层面实现转化是关键挑战；同时，现有计算系统未能同时实现S3Q理论提出的三个必要条件：具身感觉运动情境、基于世界模型的内部模拟、预测与观察的结构一致性。

Method: 提出五层实现架构，将S3Q理论各要点映射到兼容的计算机制，并在连续、可微、逐对象槽向量表示的单一流程中规定组件接口；还设计发展性引导序列以及组合系统独有的可证伪预测。

Result: 该模型表明，基本的“自我”感可能通过将行动与其结果关联而发展；行为会根据结果意外程度及正负体验，表现为犹豫、好奇或回避三种模式；每个预测均可单独证伪。

Conclusion: 该工作为机器意识研究提供了一个可测试框架，有助于推进对机器意识的理解。

Abstract: A key challenge in machine consciousness research is translating theoretical models into computational-level implementations. In this paper, we address this challenge by proposing a five-layer implementation architecture for the S3Q (Simulated, Situated, Structurally Coherent) theory of consciousness. Rather than introducing novel formalisms, the architecture composes published computational primitives into a single pipeline. S3Q identifies three jointly necessary conditions for qualia: (1) grounded sensorimotor situatedness, (2) internal simulation via a world model, and (3) structural coherence between predictions and observations. No existing computational system implements all three simultaneously. We map each S3Q tenet to specific, compatible computational machinery and specify how these components interface within a single representation pipeline that operates on continuous, differentiable, per-object slot vectors, along with a developmental bootstrap sequence and falsifiable predictions for the composed system that no subset of the architecture produces in isolation. The model suggests that a basic sense of "self" develops by linking actions to their outcomes, and that behavior falls into three patterns (hesitation, curiosity, or avoidance) depending on how unexpected an outcome is and whether it is experienced as positive or negative. Each prediction is individually falsifiable, providing the field with a testable framework to advance our understanding of machine consciousness.

</details>


### [26] [ORCA: Evaluating LLMs on Data Science Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30749)
*Xiaolong Li,Jinyang Li,Bowen Qin,Ge Qu,Nan Huo,Xiaohan Xu,Shipei Lin,Reynold Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出ORCA基准，用于评测数据科学代码翻译（DSCT），包含ORCA-MAIN和ORCA-PROJECT两个子集，并发现当前LLM在代码库间翻译上表现有限，同时提出意图增强方法提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在数据科学代码生成（DSCG）上已有进展，但跨数据科学库的代码翻译（DSCT）研究不足，缺少系统评测基准。

Method: 构建ORCA基准：ORCA-MAIN含1,600个基础任务，覆盖数据查询、数据操作和深度学习；ORCA-PROJECT含200个完整项目级翻译任务，覆盖7类数据科学任务。每个任务提供参考翻译和测试用例，并采用多阶段质量验证。提出意图增强方法：先推断源代码意图，再将其作为上下文辅助翻译。

Result: 前沿LLM在DSCT上表现有限，Claude-Opus-4.6在ORCA-MAIN成功率为56.92%，在ORCA-PROJECT为33.67%。还发现翻译存在方向偏好：源代码使用更显式、细粒度操作时翻译更容易。意图增强方法在ORCA-MAIN和ORCA-PROJECT上分别平均绝对提升4.80%和5.33%。

Conclusion: DSCT仍具挑战，现有LLM有较大改进空间；利用源代码意图可有效提升数据科学代码翻译性能。

Abstract: Data Science Code Translation (DSCT) is the process of converting code between data science libraries while preserving functional equivalence and enabling interoperability across data science ecosystems. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated considerable progress in Data Science Code Generation (DSCG), their performance in DSCT remains insufficiently studied. To address this gap, we introduce ORCA, a comprehensive benchmark with two complementary settings: ORCA-MAIN, which comprises 1,600 carefully curated grounding-level tasks across 3 representative domains: Data Querying, Data Manipulation, and Deep Learning; and ORCA-PROJECT, which contains 200 translation tasks over complete data science projects across 7 data science task types. Each task is accompanied by annotated reference translations and test cases for validating functional equivalence. We further incorporate a multi-stage quality verification process that thoroughly verifies task correctness and test case robustness. Experimental results demonstrate challenges in DSCT, with even frontier LLMs showing limited performance. Specifically, Claude-Opus-4.6 achieves a success rate of 56.92% on ORCA-MAIN and 33.67% on ORCA-PROJECT, indicating considerable room for improvement in DSCT. We also observe a clear directional preference in DSCT, where translation is consistently easier when the source code expresses the task through more explicit, fine-grained operations. Motivated by this, we propose an intent-augmented method, in which the model first infers source-code intent and then uses it as additional context for translation, achieving average absolute success-rate gains of 4.80% and 5.33% on ORCA-MAIN and ORCA-PROJECT, respectively.

</details>


### [27] [Backbone-Adaptive Evidence Routing for Robust Pairwise LLM Judging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30751)
*Zeyan Li,Jing Peng,Jianfeng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Backbone-Adaptive Evidence Routing（BAER），在保持候选对称性的前提下自适应选择证据机制，用于成对语言模型评判，在四个基准与两个8B评判骨干的全部八种条件下均取得最高测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对语言模型评判可通过直接比较、推理或基于参考的验证收集证据，但没有任何单一协议能在所有基准和评判骨干上表现最优，因此需要自适应地调整证据收集方式。

Method: BAER将每个专家的带符号偏好与候选不变可靠性分离，构建三个对称头：证据堆叠、基于可靠性的专家路由、候选盲参考验证；并利用开发数据为每个“基准-骨干”条件选择一个头，在测试前冻结该选择。

Result: 在四个基准和两个8B评判骨干的八种条件下，BAER的测试准确率均高于对比方法，实现完整预测覆盖，较最强外部基线提升0.87至7.32个百分点。

Conclusion: 自适应地调整证据收集方式比在所有场景固定使用单一评判协议更可靠。

Abstract: Pairwise language-model judges can gather evidence through direct comparison, reasoning, or reference-based verification, but no single protocol is best across benchmarks and judge backbones. We introduce Backbone-Adaptive Evidence Routing (BAER), which adapts the evidence mechanism while preserving candidate symmetry: swapping the two responses may reverse the preference but cannot change its strength. BAER separates each expert's signed preference from candidate-invariant reliability and builds three symmetric heads: evidence stacking, reliability-based expert routing, and candidate-blind reference verification. Development data select one head for each benchmark--backbone condition, and that choice is frozen before testing. Across four benchmarks and two 8B judge backbones, BAER achieves the highest test accuracy among the compared methods in all eight conditions, with full prediction coverage and gains of 0.87--7.32 points over the strongest external baseline. The results show that adapting how evidence is gathered is more reliable than fixing one judging protocol everywhere.

</details>


### [28] [HCOE: Hyperbolic Clinical Ontology Embeddings from Biomedical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30763)
*Yixuan Li,Weihao Li,Ziyang Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HCOE，将冻结的BioBERT嵌入映射到庞加莱球，实现层级感知的临床概念表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学语言模型能编码文本语义，但未显式保留医学代码层级结构。

Method: HCOE结合父侧和子侧本体引导对比学习，以及由粗到细的本体路径聚合；使用ICD、CCS和ATC医学层级组织代码。

Result: HCOE在ICD/ATC临床关系预测和CCS到PheCode层级迁移上表现最佳；在MIMIC-IV上，死亡率预测、再入院预测、药物推荐和罕见药物预测均达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: 层级感知的临床本体嵌入能有效提升医学概念表示及相关临床预测任务表现。

Abstract: Biomedical language models (LMs) encode textual semantics but do not explicitly preserve medical code hierarchies. We present Hyperbolic Clinical Ontology Embeddings (HCOE) for hierarchy-aware clinical concept representation. HCOE maps frozen BioBERT embeddings into a Poincare ball, combining parent-side and child-side ontology-guided contrastive learning with coarse-to-fine ontology-path aggregation. It uses International Classification of Diseases (ICD) codes organized by Clinical Classifications Software (CCS) and Anatomical Therapeutic Chemical (ATC) medication hierarchies. Evaluations show that HCOE performs best on ICD/ATC clinical relation prediction and CCS-to-PheCode hierarchy transfer. On the MIMIC-IV dataset, HCOE also achieves the best performance on mortality prediction, readmission prediction, medication recommendation, and rare drug prediction.

</details>


### [29] [Insurance Reserve Intelligence Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30765)
*Anugya A,Saket Mohanty,Abhilash Timmapur,Somya Rai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个保险准备金智能平台，结合经典Thiele方程求解器与物理信息神经网络（PINN）及知识信息神经网络（KINN）损失，用于定期寿险准备金建模。模型以标准化准备金比率而非原始准备金为预测目标，在测试集上R²达0.9887，推理速度较经典求解器快约119.53倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典基于Thiele微分方程的准备金方法虽然严谨可解释，但在敏感性分析、优化和大规模情景评估中重复计算成本高昂，需要一种兼顾精度与效率的替代方案。

Method: 构建包含合成保单生成、风险调整保费计算、经典准备金轨迹生成、准备金比率数据集构建、可配置神经网络训练、验证诊断、敏感性与弹性分析、原型优化及利率情景测试的平台。最终模型使用7个特征（已过时间、投保年龄、定价利率、情景利率、保费比率、保额、死亡强度），并预测标准化准备金比率。

Result: 测试集上R²为0.9887，MAE为785.48，RMSE为1212.76；在200份保单上，PINN/KINN推理速度约为经典求解器的119.53倍。模型展现出强预测精度、物理一致性和边界性能。

Conclusion: 该平台能有效加速准备金计算并保持较高精度与物理一致性，但在单调性和分布外泛化方面仍存在局限。

Abstract: Insurance reserve estimation is a fundamental actuarial task supporting premium pricing, solvency assessment, financial reporting, capital planning, and risk management. Classical reserve methods based on Thiele's differential equation provide a rigorous and interpretable foundation for life insurance valuation, but repeated reserve calculations become computationally expensive in sensitivity analysis, optimization, and large-scale scenario evaluation.
  This paper presents an Insurance Reserve Intelligence Platform for term-life reserve modelling that combines a classical Thiele-equation solver with a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) enhanced by Knowledge-Informed Neural Network (KINN) losses. The framework includes synthetic policy generation, risk-adjusted premium calculation, classical reserve trajectory generation, reserve-ratio dataset construction, configurable neural training, validation diagnostics, sensitivity and elasticity analysis, prototype optimization workflows, and interest-rate scenario testing. A key refinement is the use of premium ratio and the explicit separation of pricing-time and scenario-time interest-rate semantics.
  The final model uses seven features: elapsed time, issue age, pricing interest rate, scenario interest rate, premium ratio, sum assured, and mortality intensity. It predicts a standardized reserve ratio instead of raw reserve values, improving numerical stability across policies with different sums assured. The model achieved an R2 of 0.9887, MAE of 785.48, and RMSE of 1212.76 on the test set. On 200 policies, PINN/KINN inference was approximately 119.53 times faster than the classical solver. Results show strong predictive accuracy, physics consistency, and boundary performance, while highlighting remaining limitations in monotonicity and out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [30] [Selective Amortization of Full-Budget Counterfactual Reasoning for Visual Token Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30756)
*Qinglei Qi,Zhihe Liang,Fengzhan Jing,Shenao Zhu,Lei Zhang,Chenyang Zhang,Shuqing He,Jia Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ACV-Gate自适应候选评估框架，用于受限数据包预算下的生成式图像通信，通过学习终端价值近似与选择性精确评估，在保证重建质量的同时大幅降低编码端候选评估计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式图像通信中token选择直接影响最终重建质量，但准确估计每个候选token的终端价值需反复进行接收端重建，导致编码端计算开销巨大。

Method: 提出ACV-Gate：用终端优势与遗憾训练set-aware student直接预测候选排序；选择性精炼机制仅评估包含Local-MDL与直接动作的有界候选集；基于成本的阈值控制平均评估工作量，从而近似全预算反事实评估并仅对信息量最大的候选项进行精确评估。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，0.20 bpp时主自适应配置相比LocalMDL提升PSNR 0.636 dB，每图仅需2.13次候选评估，为Exact-Full专家调用量的27.60%；匹配候选比较、同步GPU测量及STL-10和384×384迁移实验均显示一致的质量-计算权衡，低码率下增益更明显。

Conclusion: 将终端价值学习与选择性候选评估相结合，为数据包受限的生成式图像通信提供了一种有效且可控的编码端计算分配机制。

Abstract: Generative image communication transmits compact semantic tokens under a limited packet budget, where token selection directly affects the final reconstruction quality after the complete packet is decoded. However, accurately estimating the terminal value of every candidate token requires repeated receiver-side reconstruction, resulting in substantial encoder-side computation. To address this problem, we propose ACV-Gate, an adaptive candidate evaluation framework that learns to approximate full-budget counterfactual evaluation and selectively assigns exact evaluations to the most informative candidates. Specifically, a set-aware student is trained using terminal advantages and regrets to predict candidate rankings directly, while a selective refinement mechanism evaluates only a bounded candidate set containing both Local-MDL and direct actions; cost-based thresholds further enable explicit control of the average evaluation workload. Experiments on CIFAR-10 show that ACV-Gate consistently improves reconstruction quality while substantially reducing candidate evaluations; at 0.20 bpp, the primary adaptive configuration improves PSNR over LocalMDL by 0.636 dB with only 2.13 candidate evaluations per image, corresponding to 27.60% of the calls required by the Exact-Full expert. Matched-candidate comparisons, synchronized GPU measurements, and evaluations on STL-10 and 384 *384 scale transfer further demonstrate consistent quality computation trade-offs, with particularly pronounced gains at low bit rates. These results show that combining terminal-value learning with selective candidate evaluation provides an effective and controllable mechanism for allocating encoder computation in packet-constrained generative image communication.

</details>


### [31] [ConsultMind:Towards Automated Diagnostic Consultation via Uncertainty-Aware Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30796)
*Xiao Sun,Yuming Yang,Yun Chen,Jiang Zhong,Junnan Zhu,Xinyi Jiang,Haoyang Zeng,Ruirui Chen,Yining Wang,Xinyu Zhou,Rong Tang,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 AutoDisym 与 ConsultMind，将诊断知识与临床叙事结合构建贝叶斯网络，实现自动化、可解释的在线诊断问诊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动诊断问诊需要自适应询问与可解释决策，而贝叶斯网络在开放式问诊中面临诊断假设与询问链接、后验更新转化为决策两大挑战。

Method: AutoDisym 整合诊断知识与异构诊断标签临床叙事，构建 Disorder–Symptom 贝叶斯网络（DSBN）；ConsultMind 基于 DSBN 进行不确定性感知的后验更新，并以后验不确定性指导询问与诊断。

Result: 在精神科、呼吸科、发热门诊及三个公开数据集上，AutoDisym 可自动构建高质量 DSBN，规范症状与表现宏平均 F1 分别为 81.37 和 72.19；ConsultMind 将 Top-1 和 Top-3 诊断准确率分别提升最高 22.15 和 37.89 个百分点，医生评估显示其改善了不同规模 LLM 的排序解释、鉴别诊断与诊断理由质量。

Conclusion: 该工作为自动诊断问诊提供了一种有前景的方法，能同时提升诊断性能与解释可靠性。

Abstract: Diagnostic consultation is an online sequential decision-making process in which clinicians gather evidence through patient interaction until a diagnosis is sufficiently supported. Automating this process requires adaptive inquiry and interpretable decisions. Bayesian networks offer a natural foundation by updating diagnostic posteriors as evidence accumulates, but their use in open-ended consultation raises two challenges: linking diagnostic hypotheses to potential inquiries and translating evolving posteriors into consultation decisions. We introduce AutoDisym, an automated pipeline that integrates diagnostic knowledge with heterogeneous diagnosis-labeled clinical narratives to construct a Disorder--Symptom Bayesian Network (DSBN). Building on the DSBN, we propose ConsultMind, an uncertainty-aware framework that updates disorder posteriors after each response and uses posterior uncertainty to guide inquiry and diagnosis. We evaluate both methods across psychiatry, respiratory medicine, fever clinics, and three public datasets. The results show that AutoDisym can automatically construct high-quality DSBNs and that ConsultMind consistently improves diagnostic performance and explanation soundness. For example, AutoDisym achieves macro-averaged F1 scores of 81.37 for canonical symptoms and 72.19 for manifestations using GPT-5.6-Sol. ConsultMind improves Top-1 and Top-3 diagnostic accuracy by up to 22.15 and 37.89 percentage points, respectively. Physician evaluation further shows that ConsultMind improves the quality of ranking explanations, differential diagnoses, and diagnosis rationales across LLMs of different scales. This work offers a promising approach to automatic diagnostic consultation.

</details>


### [32] [Does Thinking Help Fairness? Reasoning Tokens Resolve Some Biases but Create More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30768)
*Deng Pan,Joe Germino,Yihong Ma,Elizabeth Daly,Nuno Moniz,Ting Hua,Nitesh Chawla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过同一模型内“思考 vs. 非思考”的消融实验，研究推理语言模型中的思考过程对反事实公平性的影响，发现思考具有不对称的双重效应：既修复部分偏见翻转，也制造更多新的偏见翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于推理语言模型中的“思考”是缓解还是放大偏见存在相互矛盾的结论，因此需要更细粒度地分析思考过程本身如何影响公平性。

Method: 在 QwQ-32B、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B 和 Qwen3-32B 三个模型上，针对 Adult、COMPAS、Credit 三个高风险决策任务，进行同一模型内 thinking-vs.-non-thinking 消融；并提出两种动态分析工具：反事实深度概率差（CDPG）用于追踪思考深度上的偏见演化，偏见转移矩阵（BTM）用于刻画反事实样本对的预测从非思考到思考的转变。

Result: 思考对反事实公平性具有不对称双重效应：既能解决非思考基线产生的反事实翻转，也会在接近饱和的模型置信度下制造新的反事实翻转；在全部九个（模型，数据集）组合中，新制造的翻转数量约为被解决翻转的 5 倍。CDPG 显示偏见会随思考深度传播并放大；BTM 表明这种不对称双重效应源于反事实样本对状态联合转移。

Conclusion: 思考并非单纯缓解或放大偏见，而是在反事实公平性上同时产生修复与新偏见；其关键机制可从思考轨迹中的偏见动态演化与反事实预测对的联合转移来理解。

Abstract: Thinking in reasoning language models (RLMs) has been subject to debate on whether it resolves or amplifies bias. Prior works have shown competing conclusions in both directions. Using a within-model thinking-vs.-non-thinking ablation across QwQ-32B, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B, and Qwen3-32B on three high-stakes decision tasks (Adult, COMPAS, Credit), we show that thinking has an asymmetric dual effect on counterfactual fairness: it both resolves counterfactual flips produced by the non-thinking baseline and creates new flips at near-saturating model confidence. In all nine (model, dataset) combinations, the created flips outnumber the resolved flips by roughly 5 times. To explain the effect, we treat the thinking trace itself as a measurable site of fairness change and study it through two dynamic instruments: 1) We propose Counterfactual Depth Probability Gap (CDPG) to track bias evolution along thinking depth, and observe that bias propagates and amplifies with thinking. 2) We also formulate the Bias Transition Matrix (BTM) to show how predictions of counterfactual pairs change from non-thinking to thinking, and find that the asymmetric dual effect originates in the pair-state joint transition.

</details>


### [33] [HasMem: Hard-Origin Adaptively Softened Memory for Long-Term LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30797)
*Zihong He,Junxiao Shen,Chen Liang,Hai-Ning Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 HasMem，一种以冻结硬提示嵌入为初始状态、通过控制器、Writer、Reader 和 Global 模块自适应调整与读取压缩记忆的方法，用于文本记忆和上下文压缩中的容量分配与读出解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本记忆与上下文压缩可复用历史交互，但调整连续记忆会改变冻结 LLM 的输入，导致容量分配与读出过程耦合，难以在改变记忆宽度时保持稳定读出。

Method: 提出 Hard-Origin Adaptively Softened Memory（HasMem）。冻结的硬提示嵌入提供可验证初始状态；控制器调整记忆宽度；Writer 重新编码调整后的条目；Reader 和 Global 分别提供读出适配与跨轮状态。

Result: 在 Multi-Session Chat 开发集派生的 535 个重建探测问题上，主配置词法 F1 为 95.3，提升 4.4 个百分点，达到硬参考 framed memory positions 的 93.6%。在每问题目标体量预算近似匹配时，六个平均每条保留率约 0.83--0.91 的配置比基于规则的重新编码高 8.0--23.6 个 EM 百分点。固定模型参数且规则目标宽度比 0.75 时，Global 的 EM 增益通过用户级精确配对检验，并在八次比较上作 Bonferroni 校正。在 LongMemEval-S 全部 500 个问题上，局部词法 F1 从硬参考的 3.4 升至 8.9，答案 NLL 从 12.257 降至 5.274。两次评估中 F1 提升均伴随 EM 下降。

Conclusion: HasMem 能有效支持连续记忆的重缩放与读出适配，在多个基准上提升词法 F1 并降低 NLL；但 F1 提升伴随 EM 下降，表明存在精度与匹配度之间的权衡。

Abstract: Text-based memory and context compression support reuse of past interactions. Resizing continuous memory changes the input to a frozen LLM, coupling capacity allocation with readout. We propose Hard-Origin Adaptively Softened Memory (HasMem). Frozen hard-prompt embeddings provide a verifiable initial state. A controller adjusts memory widths, a Writer re-encodes resized entries, and Reader and Global provide readout adaptation and cross-turn state. On all $535$ questions in a reconstruction probe derived from the Multi-Session Chat (MSC) development split, the main configuration achieves lexical F1 of $95.3$ ($+4.4$ percentage points) at $93.6\%$ of the hard reference's framed memory positions. With approximately matched per-question target body budgets, six configurations at mean per-entry retention around $0.83$--$0.91$ exceed rule-based re-encoding by $8.0$--$23.6$ exact-match (EM) percentage points. With fixed model parameters and rule target width ratio $0.75$, Global's EM gain passes a user-level exact paired test with Bonferroni correction over eight comparisons. On all $500$ LongMemEval-S questions, local lexical F1 rises from the hard reference's $3.4$ to $8.9$, and answer negative log-likelihood (NLL) falls from $12.257$ to $5.274$. F1 gains accompany lower EM on both evaluations.

</details>


### [34] [A Benchmark and Diagnostic Study of Epistemic Admission in Shared Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30813)
*Xiaoyang Li,Yiqi Wang,Chencheng Zhu,KE XU,Wencheng Yang,Zequn Sun,Pingan Song,Yiqun Duan,Taotao Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了 Correlated Promotion Benchmark (CPB)，用于评估共享智能体记忆中的声明准入问题，并比较多种准入策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复出现的声明可能被误认为独立证据；智能体可能复制或转述已检索到的信念，而错误声明一旦被接纳会影响后续智能体。

Method: 构建 CPB-Static 与 CPB-Live：前者使用公开标注数据形成固定测试集和黄金动作；后者让多智能体团队共享存储，记录写入与检索，并追踪来源谱系，另设独立消费者仅依据存储作答。评估 8 种准入策略和 4 类智能体家族。

Result: 去重来源的策略会同时拒绝许多真实声明；保留答案覆盖的策略接纳的虚假声明几乎与无限制共享一样多。基于声明来源类型进行门控可将虚假采纳降至 0.06–0.09，而其他回答策略为 0.22–0.47。无争议虚假信念进入记忆后，消费者在所有家族中 0.97–0.99 的探测中会断言它。没有非预言策略能在逐字复制、转述和宣称权威的转述中一致拒绝虚假声明。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了缺乏来源谱系访问时准入策略的局限性。

Abstract: Evaluating claim admission in shared agent memory is challenging because repeated claims may be mistaken for independent evidence. An agent may copy or paraphrase a retrieved belief, while admitting a false claim exposes subsequent agents to it. To study this problem, we introduce the Correlated Promotion Benchmark (CPB), which evaluates whether candidate claims should be admitted to shared memory.CPB-Static constructs a frozen test split from publicly annotated sources with fixed gold actions. CPB-Live runs multi-agent teams over a shared store, records all writes and retrievals, and tracks source lineage defined by each scenario. A separate consumer answers from the store alone. We evaluate eight admission policies across four agent families. Our results show that policies which deduplicate sources reject many true claims alongside false ones, whereas policies preserving answer coverage admit nearly as many false claims as unrestricted sharing. Gating on declared source type reduces false adoption to 0.06--0.09, compared with 0.22--0.47 for other answering policies. Once an uncontested false belief enters memory, the consumer asserts it in 0.97--0.99 of probes across all families. No non-oracle policy consistently rejects false claims across verbatim copies, paraphrases, and paraphrases declared authoritative. These findings reveal the limitations of admission policies without access to source lineage.

</details>


### [35] [Evaluating Real-Time Voice Agents: From Component Quality to Grounded Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30798)
*Shivam Negi,Arpit Rawat,Rashi Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对实时语音代理文献分散、缺乏统一评价标准的问题，本文基于38篇主要文献提出三项证据性论断，并提出TRG报告标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时语音代理已从研究原型走向生产部署，但相关文献分散在语音基础建模、轮流说话心理语言学和代理评估三个很少互相引用的社区中。架构论文报告延迟，轮流说话论文报告预测准确率，代理基准报告任务成功率，因此没有单一指标能说明已部署代理是否真正好用。

Method: 采用系统综述与证据综合方法，将38个主要来源组织为以应用为中心的六类分类法，并说明检索策略、纳入标准和验证步骤。对每个来源说明其针对的问题、机制和报告证据。验证步骤还发现并纠正了一个被错误归属的arXiv标识符。最后提出TRG报告标准。

Result: 提出三项主要发现：第一，架构选择是部署约束而非定论，2026年企业教程指出尚无完全可自托管的端到端系统满足生产约束，而分块级联可独立达到最先进的双工行为，说明双工行为可与双工架构分离。第二，评估已从组件质量明确转向有根据的结果，近期基准验证后端状态而非信任代理声称已完成的任务。第三，多数模型和基准中的双人假设正在瓦解，多方轮流说话和多说话人推理基准表明，决定何时不发言、推理谁可以被告知什么，是双人框架无法衡量的一流能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出TRG（Timing-Recovery-Grounded）报告标准，用时序、中断后恢复和状态验证结果三个维度共同描述代理，并为多方部署增加条件性第四轴，以弥合碎片化评估并更好刻画实际部署中的实时语音代理。

Abstract: Real-time voice agents have moved from research prototypes to production deployments, yet the literature describing them is fragmented across three communities that rarely cite one another: speech foundation modelling, turn-taking psycholinguistics, and agentic evaluation.
