Architecture papers report latency, turn-taking papers report prediction accuracy, and agentic benchmarks report task success, so no single number describes whether a deployed agent is actually good. We address that gap with three evidence-based claims, each traceable to a corpus of 38 primary sources organised into an application-centric taxonomy of six categories. First, architecture choice is a deployment constraint rather than a settled verdict: a 2026 enterprise tutorial reports that no fully self-hostable end-to-end system yet meets production constraints, while a chunked cascade independently reaches state-of-the-art duplex behaviour, showing duplex behaviour is separable from duplex architecture. Second, evaluation has shifted decisively from component quality toward grounded outcomes, with recent benchmarks verifying backend state rather than trusting what the agent claims to have done. Third, the dyadic assumption in most models and benchmarks is breaking down: multiparty turn-taking and multi-speaker reasoning benchmarks show that deciding when not to speak, and reasoning about who may be told what, are first-class capabilities two-participant framings cannot measure. For each source we state the problem it targets, its mechanism, and its reported evidence, alongside the search strategy, inclusion criteria, and a verification step that caught a misattributed arXiv identifier in circulation. We propose TRG (Timing-Recovery-Grounded), a reporting standard characterising an agent by timing, post-disruption recovery, and state-verified outcome together, with a conditional fourth axis for multiparty deployments.

</details>


### [36] [PTC-Decoder: Towards Intelligent SLMs on Offline Resource-Constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30836)
*Minghui Yu,Ke Mu,Gang Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PTC-Decoder，一种免训练、即插即用的解码器框架，用于在资源受限边缘设备上提升小语言模型多步工具编排智能体任务的规划执行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在遥感卫星等离线资源受限边缘设备上部署小语言模型时，其有限推理能力会阻碍需要复杂工具编排的多步智能体任务。现有多步规划求解范式依赖提示强制规划，但实验显示小语言模型几乎不遵守，弱模型甚至无法调用计划。

Method: 提出 PTC-Decoder，包含两部分：1）Plan-to-Act 范式，将规划提升为原子工具，并在首次推理步骤强制调用；2）TC-Decoder，使用确定性有限自动机对工具名施加 token 级硬约束，同时保留参数生成自由度，从而在不重训练的情况下维持小语言模型推理能力。

Result: 在 7 个小语言模型和 200 个真实遥感卫星任务上，PTC-Decoder 取得统计显著的总体得分平均提升 +1.21（p<0.01，95% CI [+1.13, +1.29]），并在不同模型和其他数据集上保持一致改善。消融去除 TC-Decoder 会导致所有质量指标显著下降且不降低计算成本，表明 TC-Decoder 是主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: PTC-Decoder 通过在推理时约束允许的输出词表来强制规划遵循，无需重训练，为提升步骤级可靠性提供了轻量且有效的方案；但最终答案准确性仍是开放挑战。

Abstract: Deploying small language models (SLMs) on offline, resource-constrained edge devices such as remote sensing satellites presents a fundamental challenge: their limited reasoning capacity hinders reliable execution of multi-step agent tasks requiring complex tool orchestration. Existing plan-solve paradigms rely on prompt-based enforcement, which our experiments show SLMs almost entirely disregard: weak models fail to invoke the plan. We propose PTC-Decoder (Plan-Tool Constrained Decoder), a training-free, plug-and-play decoder framework that combines (1) a Plan-to-Act paradigm, which elevates planning to an atomic tool and forces its invocation at the first inference step, and (2) TC-Decoder, a deterministic finite automaton that imposes token-level hard constraints on tool names while preserving freedom over parameter generation, thereby retaining SLM reasoning capability. Evaluated on 200 real remote-sensing satellite tasks across 7 SLMs, PTC-Decoder yields a statistically significant mean overall score gain of +1.21 (p<0.01), 95% CI [+1.13, +1.29]), with consistent improvements across models and other datasets. An ablation study that removes TC-Decoder causes substantial performance degradation across all quality metrics without reducing computational cost, confirming TC-Decoder as the primary driver. PTC-Decoder thus offers a lightweight yet effective solution for improving step-level reliability, with final-answer accuracy remaining an open challenge. In essence, we enforce plan adherence by constraining the permissible output vocabulary during inference, without requiring retraining.

</details>


### [37] [Why Jailbreaks Succeed in Diffusion Language Models: An Energy Landscape Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30841)
*Thong Bach,Dung Nguyen,Thao Minh Le,Truyen Tran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的安全对齐解释为塑造去噪能量景观，并基于此设计三种无需训练的检测信号，在多个dLLM上验证了互补性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对dLLM的攻击与防御仅针对特定漏洞，缺乏统一框架解释攻击为何成功。

Method: 将安全对齐视为能量景观，良性模型通过能量壁垒将有害查询导向安全输出；现有越狱攻击归为两类：初始时模糊查询安全倾向，或中途干预迫使去噪路径跨越壁垒。基于掩码扩散模型去噪时最小化动能的结果，推导出三个互补的无训练检测信号：读取初始logit分布的step-0比率，以及追踪logit空间互补子空间中动能的两个轨迹速度信号。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B、LLaDA-1.5、Dream-7B三种密集dLLM和LLaDA-MoE-7B稀疏混合专家dLLM上评估，证实了三个信号的互补性；对已知攻击的压力测试中，所有逃避检测的配置均无法生成有害内容。

Conclusion: 检测阈值与跨越能量壁垒的阈值难以分离，表明该框架能有效捕捉攻击本质。

Abstract: Existing attacks and defenses for diffusion-based large language models (dLLMs) target specific vulnerabilities but lack a shared framework explaining why attacks succeed. We propose one by interpreting safety alignment as shaping the denoising energy landscape: a well-aligned model routes harmful queries toward safe outputs through an energy barrier that separates the two regions. Current jailbreak attacks reduce to two strategies for circumventing this barrier: obscuring the query's safety disposition at initialisation, or intervening mid-trajectory to force the denoising path across the energy barrier. From this perspective and the result that masked diffusion models minimise kinetic energy during denoising, we derive three complementary, training-free detection signals: a step-0 ratio that reads the initial safety disposition from the logit distribution before generation begins, and two trajectory-velocity signals that track kinetic energy in complementary subspaces of the logit space. An attack must either reveal its intent at initialisation or expend kinetic energy to cross the barrier in at least one monitored subspace, so the three signals cover each other's blind spots in the energy budget by construction. Evaluation across three dense dLLMs (LLaDA-8B, LLaDA-1.5, Dream-7B) and a sparse mixture-of-experts dLLM (LLaDA-MoE-7B) confirms this complementarity. In stress tests of known attacks, every configuration that evades detection also fails to produce harmful content, suggesting that the detection and barrier-crossing thresholds are hard to separate.

</details>


### [38] [SkillEvoReg: Regularizing Agent Skill Evolution Against Overfitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30861)
*Guanyu Nie,Fangzhou Zhu,Shixiong Kai,Xiongwei Han,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究语言模型智能体将执行经验转化为可复用外部技能时出现的“技能演化过拟合”问题，并提出通用正则化框架 SkillEvoReg 来抑制技能膨胀与行为回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能反复更新本身构成一个学习过程：局部有用的编辑可能累积成冗余或过度任务特定的指令，而新的更新可能破坏此前正常工作的行为，因此需要显式正则化来稳定技能演化。

Method: 提出 SkillEvoReg 框架，受神经网络抗过拟合技术启发，融合三部分：训练时技能 dropout（扰动更新生成）、复杂度感知局部正则化（控制技能结构不必要增长）以及因果反例验证 CCV（对候选更新导致的行为回退进行针对性验证）。该框架可实例化到不同技能演化系统中，并保留各系统原生的技能演化器和任务评估器。

Result: 在 SkillOpt、SkillEvolBench 和 ContinualSkillBench 上，SkillEvoReg 在控制技能状态增长的同时保持了有竞争力的下游能力，改善了若干迁移与后期演化结果，并能识别出仅靠结构指标无法发现的更新级行为回退。

Conclusion: 显式正则化可作为日益强大的技能更新器的有效补充，用于缓解技能演化过拟合。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly improve by converting execution experience into reusable external skills. Yet repeated skill updates form a learning process of their own: locally useful edits can accumulate into redundant or task-specific instructions, while new updates can disrupt behavior that previously worked. We study this problem as skill-evolution overfitting and introduce SkillEvoReg, a general regularization framework for skill evolution inspired by anti-overfitting techniques in neural-network training. SkillEvoReg combines training-time skill dropout, which perturbs update generation, and complexity-aware local regularization, which controls unnecessary structural growth, with causal counterexample validation (CCV), which provides targeted behavioral validation of candidate-specific regressions. We instantiate the framework across heterogeneous skill-evolution systems while retaining each system's native skill evolver and task evaluator. Across SkillOpt, SkillEvolBench, and ContinualSkillBench, SkillEvoReg consistently controls skill-state growth while preserving competitive downstream capability, improves several transfer and later-stage evolution outcomes, and identifies update-level regressions that structural metrics alone cannot reveal. These results suggest that explicit regularization is a useful complement to increasingly capable skill updaters.

</details>


### [39] [TISD: On-Policy Self-Distillation with Trajectory Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30878)
*Taeckyung Lee,Rinat Amankos,Jeonghye Kim,Hyungjun Yoon,Woogyeol Jin,Sung-Ju Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出轨迹干预自蒸馏（TISD），通过让教师选择分支动作、由学生生成后续轨迹，再在特权上下文下蒸馏整条轨迹，以缓解同策略自蒸馏中教师偏好动作的后续上下文无法被监督的数据收集瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）只在学生采样到的轨迹上提供密集教师目标。当特权教师在已访问前缀偏好另一动作时，OPSD能监督分支决策，但无法监督该动作诱导的后续上下文，除非学生主动采样到它。诊断发现，分歧峰值处教师偏好的token虽能提升学生后续成功概率，但其局部纠正价值有限，因此师生分歧更适合用来提议轨迹分支，而非仅做局部修复。

Method: 提出简单分支-再生成-蒸馏算法TISD：强制执行教师选择的分支动作，将后缀生成交还给学生，并在特权上下文条件下用教师蒸馏完整轨迹。

Result: 在编程模型上，TISD相比SDPO平均Avg@4提升1.2个百分点；在科学领域，等步数预算下平均Avg@128提升0.8个百分点，等时间预算下提升0.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果支持将教师引导的分支作为暴露有用后继上下文以进行自蒸馏的一种有效方式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides dense teacher targets, but evaluates them only along student-sampled rollouts. When the privileged teacher favors an alternative action at a visited prefix, OPSD can provide a target for the branch decision but cannot supervise the successor contexts induced by that action unless the student samples it. This creates a training-time data-collection bottleneck and suggests a different role for teacher-student disagreement: proposing a trajectory branch rather than identifying a sufficient local repair. Our diagnostic framework using controlled token interventions reveals that a teacher-preferred token at peak disagreement can improve student continuation success, while its local corrective value is limited. Motivated by this finding, we introduce a simple branch-regenerate-distill algorithm, Trajectory-Intervention Self-Distillation (TISD). TISD forces a teacher-selected branch action, returns suffix generation to the student, and distills the full trajectory under the privileged-context-conditioned teacher. Across the coding models, TISD improves average Avg@4 over SDPO by 1.2 percentage points. Across the science domains, it improves average Avg@128 by 0.8 points under an equal-step budget and by 0.3 points under an equal-time budget. These results support teacher-guided branching as a way to expose useful successor contexts for self-distillation.

</details>


### [40] [EXAONE Demand 1.0: A Time Series Foundation Model for Demand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30880)
*Seunghan Lee,Sangjun Han,Jun Seo,Junhyeok Kang,Jaehoon Lee,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 EXAONE Demand，面向需求时间序列的基础模型，通过需求专用语料和需求感知适配器提升预测性能，并在 22 个留出数据集上优于 36 个时间序列基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用时间序列基础模型多在多领域数据上预训练，需求序列占比很小；而需求数据具有短历史、频繁零值、缺货截断和未记录外生事件等特殊性质，现有语料难以充分覆盖。

Method: 构建包含 73 个来源、1130 万条序列和 484 亿观测的需求专用语料，并用合成生成器补充公开需求数据中不足的行为；在冻结的通用域骨干网络上附加低秩分支，分别对应平滑、间歇、波动和块状四类需求，并用基于 8 个尺度无关统计量的路由器决定各分支贡献；训练了真实+合成和仅合成两个版本。

Result: 在 22 个留出数据集上，两个版本均优于 36 个时间序列基础模型；加入真实需求数据相比仅使用合成数据还能带来额外提升。

Conclusion: 针对需求数据的专用语料和需求感知适配器能有效提升需求时间序列建模与预测表现，且真实需求数据对模型性能有补充价值。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are pretrained on series from diverse domains, where demand series make up only a small fraction. Demand data has properties that such corpora rarely contain: Short histories, frequent zeros, censoring by stock-outs, and exogenous events that the series does not record. To this end, we propose EXAONE Demand, built on 1) a demand-specific corpus and 2) a demand-aware adapter. For the corpus, we assemble 11.3M series and 48.4B observations from 73 sources, and a synthetic generator supplies the behaviour that open demand data under-represents. For the adapter, we attach low-rank branches to a frozen general-domain backbone, one for each of the four demand classes (smooth, intermittent, erratic, and lumpy), and a router that reads eight scale-free statistics of the input series decides how much each branch contributes. We build EXAONE Demand in two versions, one trained on real-world and synthetic demand together and one trained on the synthetic corpus alone. On 22 held-out datasets, both versions outperform 36 TSFMs, and real-world demand adds a gain over synthetic data alone.

</details>


### [41] [From Tapping to Hopping: Augmenting Mobile GUI Agents with App-Native Deeplinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30887)
*Yuchen Sun,Chenglin Cai,Gongjie Zhang,Tianyu Xia,Quyu Kong,Panrong Tong,Zhengwen Zeng,Long Chen,Steven Hoi,Chongyang Zhang,Yue Wang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动 GUI 代理通常依赖逐步点击和滑动，跨应用通用但导航效率低；单次 deeplink 调用可替代多步屏幕级 GUI 操作。

Method: 通过静态分析发现候选 deeplink，在真实设备上验证并描述其落地界面，构建经验证且带落地屏描述的 deeplink 目录；基于该目录实现 GUI-Hopper，混合使用 deeplink 导航与 GUI 操作及回退。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents complete tasks using GUI actions like taps and swipes. These actions are broadly applicable across applications, but reaching a navigation interface. A single deeplink call can replace a sequence of screen-by-screen GUI actions. We therefore introduce hybrid interaction, using deeplinks for direct navigation and GUI actions for other on-screen operations and fallback. To enable this, we discover candidate deeplinks through static analysis, validate them on real devices, and describe their observed landing screens. This process creates a verified and grounded deeplink catalog that pairs each working deeplink with a description of its landing screen. Using this catalog, we introduce GUI-Hopper, a improves task success in commercial applications on real devices, further demonstrating the benefits of hybrid interaction.

</details>


### [42] [Training Graph Foundation Models on The Web Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30894)
*Ryoma Sato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Acacia，一个在网页图（web graph）上训练的图基础模型，无需额外训练即可支持任意特征维度与语义、多种图任务，并具备上下文学习能力，且不依赖预训练大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型通常需要额外训练分类头或特征投影器来适配新图或新标签，并且常通过拼接预训练大语言模型来获得能力；作者希望构建一个更通用、无需任务特定适配、也不依赖大语言模型的图基础模型。

Method: Acacia 在 Common Crawl 网页图上从零开始训练，不借助预训练大语言模型；设计上支持任意特征维度和语义，并覆盖节点分类、链接预测、节点聚类和图生成等多类任务。

Result: Acacia 无需额外训练即可支持多种任务和任意特征语义，并具备上下文学习能力；同时证明仅使用图数据从零训练也能获得类似大语言模型的涌现能力。

Conclusion: 图基础模型可以像大语言模型一样从零训练中获得涌现能力；Acacia 为无需额外分类头、特征投影器或预训练大语言模型的通用图基础模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce Acacia, a graph foundation model, trained on the web graph. Acacia (i) supports arbitrary feature dimensionalities and semantics without additional training, (ii) supports a wide range of tasks, including node classification, link prediction, node clustering, and graph generation, without additional training, (iii) has in-context learning capabilities, and (iv) does not rely on pretrained LLMs. In particular, existing graph foundation models often require training additional classification heads or feature projectors to accommodate new graphs or new labels, whereas Acacia does not. Moreover, existing graph foundation models often gain their capabilities by being stitched together with pretrained LLMs, whereas Acacia is trained from scratch using only the Common Crawl web graph. This is also an important result because it provides evidence that graph models can acquire emergent capabilities from scratch like LLMs.

</details>


### [43] [JevSoup: System-One Routing for Training-Free LoRA Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30922)
*Xiuying Wang,Jiahua Cheng,Shuotian Li,Yufan Cheng,Bowen Deng,Zhexuan Bai,Yichen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 JevSoup，一个无需训练的框架，将专家路由与执行分离，通过选择两个 LoRA 专家并正交化合并来提升多任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可适应 AI 系统需要协调不同任务的专用能力。LoRA 可实现模块化专家，但现有路由方法往往需要辅助数据、额外训练或自回归解码。

Method: JevSoup 将 System One 专家路由与 System Two 执行分离。仅利用输入和专家描述，Jev 通过结构化概率选择两个专家；保留领先专家的更新，将第二个专家投影到第一个更新行空间的正交补上，并以等权重合并。

Result: 在 14 个 PorTAL 任务和三种 Qwen3 规模上，相比最强外部基线，任务宏平均准确率绝对提升最高 1.19%，样本微平均准确率绝对提升最高 1.21%。

Conclusion: JevSoup 是一种有效的免训练 LoRA 专家路由与合并方法，能提升多任务准确率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Building adaptable AI systems requires effective coordination of specialized capabilities across diverse tasks. Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) enables modular expertise, but existing routing approaches may require auxiliary data, additional training, or autoregressive decoding. We propose JevSoup, a training-free framework separating System One expert routing from System Two execution. Using only the input and expert descriptions, Jev selects two experts through structured probabilities. JevSoup retains the leading expert's update, projects the second onto the orthogonal complement of the first update's row space, and combines them with equal weights. Across 14 PorTAL tasks and three Qwen3 scales, JepSoup achieves absolute gains of up to 1.19\% in task-macro and 1.21\% in sample-micro accuracy over the strongest evaluated external baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/Leowang980/JevSoup.

</details>


### [44] [Self-Play Search Distillation for Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30936)
*Lorenzo Molfetta,Wai-Chung Kwan,Giacomo Frisoni,Luca Ragazzi,Gianluca Moro,Pavlos Vougiouklis,Jeff Z. Pan,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Self-Play Search Distillation（SPSD），利用棋类自我博弈搜索生成高质量、超人类的思维链数据，以提升大语言模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升大语言模型推理能力需要高质量数据，其中应包含困难决策、竞争性选项及其后果；但合成数据质量低且人工标注成本高，导致数据稀缺。

Method: SPSD 在可执行环境中训练类似 MuZero 的网络进行自我博弈，将搜索过程转化为结构化推理问题；在每个状态记录专家偏好决策、合理替代方案、合理对手回应和价值估计，并将自我博弈搜索记录转换为超人类思维链，用环境接地监督训练大语言模型。

Result: 尽管只在自我博弈搜索记录上训练，SPSD 仍能迁移到未见数学任务。在 Qwen3-4B-Base 上，六个数学基准的平均分从 24.1 提升到 36.6，留出棋类游戏的胜率从 15% 提升到 45%。

Conclusion: SPSD 提供了一种标注高效的方法来生成高质量合成数据，从而提升大语言模型在推理任务上的表现。

Abstract: Improving reasoning abilities in Large Language Models (LLMs) requires high-quality data that exposes difficult decisions, competing alternatives, and their consequences. Data scarcity is driven by the low quality of synthetic data and the cost of human labeling. We introduce Self-Play Search Distillation (SPSD), a framework for generating superhuman synthetic data via self-play of MuZero-like networks trained on board games. SPSD uses executable environments to turn search into structured reasoning problems. At each state, the expert identifies a preferred decision, plausible alternatives, plausible opponent replies, and value estimates. By converting the self-play search records into superhuman chains-of-thought, we train LLMs with environment-grounded supervision. Although trained only on self-play search records, SPSD transfers to unseen mathematics. On Qwen3-4B-Base, it raises the mean over six mathematics benchmarks from 24.1 to 36.6 while increasing the held-out-game win rate from 15% to 45%. SPSD offers an annotation-efficient way to create high-quality synthetic data for improving LLM performance in reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [45] [MACBT: A Multi-Agent Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Decision Support System with Longitudinal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30939)
*De Jiang,Shuo Zhang,Weiwei Liao,Jianying Zhang,Chuanhui Yu,Hongen Liao,Kehong Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种面向临床医生的AI决策支持系统，结合多智能体CBT框架（MACBT）和纵向记忆模块（CD Memory），用于提升抑郁症认知行为疗法的可扩展性和会话质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBT作为抑郁症一线循证治疗，其规模化受限于临床医生在会前准备、会后记录和纵向认知病理追踪上耗费的大量时间。

Method: 构建MACBT框架，将五阶段CBT工作流（评估、苏格拉底提问、认知重构、行为实验、治疗监测）编码为五个协作智能体；设计CD Memory模块，跨会话追踪认知扭曲类型、频率、严重度和重构效果，生成会前病理报告和干预优先级建议；通过双角色大语言模型模拟构建中文CBT对话语料，采用监督微调和直接偏好优化训练Qwen3-14B模型。

Result: GPT-4评判显示，MACBT在专业性和临床真实性上分别达到2.62和2.25，优于MeChat、SoulChat、PsyChat和CPsyCounX；完整记忆增强系统将会话质量提升12.6%，在跨会话连续性、干预进展和个性化方面取得2.29的纵向平均分。

Conclusion: 该多智能体与记忆增强系统能有效辅助临床医生，提升CBT会话质量与纵向连续性，为规模化CBT治疗提供可行方案。

Abstract: Cognitive behavioral therapy (CBT) is an evidence-based first-line treatment for depression, yet its scale is constrained by the time clinicians spend on pre-session preparation, post-session documentation, and longitudinal cognitive-pathology tracking. We present a clinician-facing AI decision-support system that combines a multi-agent CBT framework (MACBT) with a CBT-specific longitudinal memory module (CD Memory). MACBT encodes the five-stage CBT workflow (assessment, Socratic questioning, cognitive restructuring, behavioral experiments, and treatment monitoring) into five collaborative agents. CD Memory tracks cognitive-distortion type, frequency, severity, and restructuring efficacy across sessions to generate pre-session pathology reports and intervention-priority recommendations. We construct a Chinese CBT dialogue corpus via dual-role large language model simulation and train a Qwen3-14B backbone with supervised fine-tuning and direct preference optimization. Evaluation with GPT-4 judges shows MACBT outperforms MeChat, SoulChat, PsyChat, and CPsyCounX in professionalism (2.62) and clinical authenticity (2.25). The full memory-augmented system further improves session quality by 12.6% and achieves a longitudinal mean of 2.29 on cross-session continuity, intervention progression, and personalization.

</details>


### [46] [Financial Fragility in Societies of LLM Agents: Coordination Failures and Stabilizing Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30940)
*Zhenhao Fu,Ruipeng Xu,Qibing Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM智能体在金融决策中的集体脆弱性，提出FRAIL实验框架，在银行挤兑、债务展期和奖励众筹三类动态金融环境中测试七个主流LLM，发现即使无人被指示破坏系统，仍普遍出现集体失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体在金融决策中承担更大角色，金融AI安全不能只关注单个智能体，还必须关注多个智能体共同形成的系统层面风险；个体理性或保护性决策可能导致可避免的集体失败。

Method: 提出FRAIL受控实验框架，将LLM智能体置于银行挤兑、债务展期和奖励众筹三类动态金融环境中，使个体决策改变他人面临的金融条件；测试七个领先LLM，并比较三种互动机制：补偿性承诺、集中式承诺协议和参与者主导联盟。

Result: 基线中77%的银行挤兑回合和83%的债务展期回合以失败告终；三种互动机制均改善总体结果，但没有一种机制在所有金融结构中表现最佳；成功稳定具有共同时间模式：广泛承诺在防御行为自我强化前尽早形成。

Conclusion: 个体能力强的智能体不会自动形成安全的金融系统；系统级评估和互动设计是金融AI安全的核心问题。

Abstract: Individually protective decisions can produce avoidable collective failures. As large language model (LLM) agents take on greater roles in financial decision-making, financial AI safety must therefore be considered not only at the level of individual agents, but also at the level of the systems they jointly create. We study this problem with FRAIL, a controlled experimental framework that places LLM agents in three dynamic financial environments---bank runs, debt rollover, and reward crowdfunding---where agents' decisions reshape the financial conditions faced by others. Across seven leading LLMs, we find widespread collective fragility even when no agent is instructed to destabilize the system: 77\% of baseline bank-run episodes and 83\% of debt-rollover episodes end in failure. We then compare three interaction mechanisms based on compensated commitments, centralized commitment agreements, and participant-led coalitions. All three improve aggregate outcomes, but no single mechanism performs best across all financial structures. Across mechanisms, successful stabilization shares a common temporal pattern: broad commitment forms early, before defensive behavior becomes self-reinforcing. Our findings show that individually capable agents do not automatically form safe financial systems, highlighting system-level evaluation and interaction design as central problems for financial AI safety. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/FinFrail-CF26.

</details>


### [47] [LogicTree-RAG: Logic Tree-guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Long-form Patent Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30943)
*Jiaqi Zhu,Naili Xing,Hexiang Pan,Haotian Gao,Jianwei Yin,Xiaokui Xiao,Beng Chin Ooi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LogicTree-RAG，一种以逻辑树为引导的检索增强生成框架，用于专利起草等长文本技术文档生成，无需专家先验大纲即可实现全局结构化与可控制生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文本技术文本生成需要全局一致的逻辑结构和可靠的技术推理，而现有大模型常缺乏全局组织能力；专利起草是典型场景，现有方法多聚焦局部章节或依赖人工大纲，难以实现可扩展自动化。

Method: 构建层次化逻辑树作为全局组织骨架，每个节点表示一个技术要素，并通过证据引导的递归生成构建；随后使用混合遍历机制将逻辑树映射到专利章节，实现可控且章节均衡的生成。

Result: 大量实验表明，LogicTree-RAG 在内容质量和语言合规性上持续优于强 LLM 基线，并能生成更长的结构化文本，同时保持高 token 效率。

Conclusion: 以逻辑为中心的生成方法能有效应对复杂技术文档起草任务，为长文本技术生成提供可扩展的自动化路径。

Abstract: Long-form technical text generation underpins knowledge-intensive workflows, yet remains challenging for large language models (LLMs) due to the need for globally consistent logical structuring and faithful technical reasoning beyond local coherence. Patent drafting is a canonical instance of this challenge, demanding holistic generation of a legally compliant and technically exhaustive document through sustained multi-expert collaboration. Existing approaches often focus on partial section generation or rely on manually crafted outlines, limiting scalable automation in realistic settings. In this work, we propose LogicTree-RAG, a logic tree-guided retrieval-augmented generation framework that induces a hierarchical logic tree as a global organizational backbone to organize and ground technical disclosures, without relying on expert-defined drafting priors. Each node in the logic tree represents a technical element and is constructed through evidence-guided recursive generation. A hybrid traversal mechanism then maps the logic tree into patent sections, enabling controllable and section-balanced generation. Extensive experiments show that LogicTree-RAG consistently improves content quality and language conformity over strong LLM-based baselines and achieves longer structured generation with high token efficiency, demonstrating the effectiveness of logic-centric generation for complex technical document drafting.

</details>


### [48] [MoMHa: Multi-Objective Optimization of LLM Harnesses over Accuracy, Safety, and Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30967)
*Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Md Mehrab Tanjim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将LLM的Python外围代码（harness）视为一等设计对象，其质量是多目标的。作者构建Meta-Harness系统，用Claude Code作为智能提议器，在准确率、行为安全性和token成本三个目标上进行搜索，发现单阶段联合奖励提议器MoMHa在17个领域上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM改进工作大多只关注准确率，但harness（构建提示、路由调用、解析输出的代码）的质量本质上是多目标的：一个拒绝不了不安全请求的准确harness，或token消耗高一个数量级的harness，都不是好harness，因此需要将harness设计作为多目标搜索问题来研究。

Method: 将harness设计建模为对三个域内目标（准确率、行为安全性、token成本）的搜索；由具备完整文件系统访问权限的智能提议器Claude Code，基于先前harness源码、执行轨迹和评分产物进行提议；对比单阶段联合奖励提议器MoMHa与两阶段“先准确率后token”消融、纯标量反馈、仅准确率基线等；在17个领域（7个合成能力套件、7个真实公开基准、3个U-SafeBench衍生用户特定安全域）和12个模型（4个家族）上评估。

Result: MoMHa在合成赛道联合均值为0.482，优于十个基线的0.198-0.422，在7/10个域列中获胜；真实赛道得分0.461，优于最强基线DSPy的0.377，在5/7列中获胜；在12个目标模型中的8个上无需重训练即可迁移到未见基准；取得最高行为安全复合分（U-SafeBench 0.781），且每例比两阶段方案少用95个token。

Conclusion: 单阶段联合奖励的harness搜索优于两阶段、标量反馈和仅准确率方法，表明harness策略可跨模型和基准迁移，并能在准确率、安全性和成本之间取得更好平衡；作者将公开所有harness代码、评估基础设施和跨模型日志。

Abstract: Most work on improving large language models treats accuracy as the sole objective. We argue that the harness, the Python code surrounding the model that constructs prompts, routes calls, and parses outputs, is a first-class design surface whose quality is inherently multi-objective: an accurate harness that refuses no unsafe request, or that consumes an order of magnitude more tokens, is not a good harness. We present Meta-Harness, a system that casts harness design as search over three per-domain objectives (accuracy, behavioural safety, and token cost) solved by an agentic proposer (Claude Code) with full filesystem access to prior harness source, execution traces, and scoring artifacts. Our central finding is that a singlephase joint-reward proposer (MoMHa) outperforms every alternative, including a two-phase "accuracy then tokens" ablation, scalar-only feedback, and an accuracy-only baseline. We evaluate on seventeen domains: seven synthetic capability suites, seven real-world public benchmarks (HumanEval, MBPP, Spider, FEVER, MMLU-Pro, LawBench, NuminaMath), and three U-SafeBench-derived user-specific safety domains, using a 12-model fleet spanning four families. On the synthetic track MoMHa achieves a joint mean of 0.482 versus 0.198-0.422 for ten baselines, winning $7 / 10$ per-domain columns; on the real-world track it scores 0.461 versus 0.377 for the strongest baseline (DSPy), winning 5/7 columns, demonstrating that harness strategies transfer to unseen benchmarks without retraining on 8 of 12 target models. MoMHa attains the highest measured behavioral safety composite (U-SafeBench, 0.781) and uses 95 fewer tokens per example than the two-phase alternative. We will release all harness code, evaluation infrastructure, and crossmodel logs.

</details>


### [49] [FTB Graph: Determining and Validating First-token Broadcasters and Language-Identity Head Circuits in Multilingual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30954)
*Arjun Pillai,Christian Hoang,Anjelo Laroza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究对六种模型架构进行端到端结构电路分析，追踪多语言大模型首token语言身份决策的因果回路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型必须在生成早期确定目标响应语言，但控制首token语言身份决策的因果电路尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 使用Edge Attribution Patching（EAP）与FP16 active clamping，随后用精确激活修补验证，设置2000候选边搜索上限，提取驱动首token语言广播的有向无环图；覆盖GPT-2、BLOOM-560M、Pythia-1B/2.8B、Qwen2.5-1.5B Base/Instruct。

Result: 独立模型中发现深层或中深层广播枢纽，Pythia-2.8B和BLOOM-560M证据最强；GPT-2和Pythia-1B图外头较少，Qwen2.5-1.5B两个变体反转必要性检验。Pythia-1B到2.8B扩展增加节点参与但验证边预算相近，拓扑更稀疏。Qwen2.5-1.5B base与instruct电路Jaccard相似度84.7%，含Layer 27枢纽。EAP分数与精确修补增量相关性弱。

Conclusion: 首token路由主要在预训练阶段建立，并被指令微调保留；线性梯度近似在FP16中可能偏离因果干预，因此可靠电路发现需要精确修补验证。

Abstract: Large language models operating in multilingual contexts must resolve target response languages early in generation, yet the causal circuitry governing first-token language identity decisions remains poorly mapped. We present an end-to-end structural circuit analysis across six model architectures spanning four families: GPT-2, BLOOM-560M, Pythia-1B/2.8B, and Qwen2.5-1.5B Base/Instruct. Using Edge Attribution Patching (EAP) with FP16 active clamping, followed by exact activation patching verification with a 2,000-candidate-edge search ceiling, we extract directed acyclic graphs driving first-token language broadcasting. Across the standalone models, we observe deep or mid-to-deep broadcasting hubs, though the evidence is strongest for Pythia-2.8B and BLOOM-560M because GPT-2 and Pythia-1B leave few out-of-graph heads for comparison, while both Qwen2.5-1.5B variants invert the necessity check. Scaling from Pythia-1B to 2.8B expands node participation while maintaining a similar verified edge budget, producing sparser topology. The Qwen2.5-1.5B base and instruct circuits retain 84.7% Jaccard similarity, including the Layer 27 hub, indicating that first-token routing is largely established during pretraining and preserved by instruction tuning. Finally, EAP scores correlate weakly with exact patching deltas across most models, showing that linear gradient approximations can diverge from causal interventions in FP16 and motivating exact-patching verification for reliable circuit discovery.

</details>


### [50] [SciHorizon-eLab: An Agentic Protocol-to-Task Compiler for Scalable Benchmarking of Scientific Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30971)
*Maokai Qin,Chuan Qin,Qi Zhang,Dianyu Liu,Zirui Liu,Hongting Niu,Yuanchun Zhou,Hengshu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SciHorizon-eLab，将科学实验协议编译为可执行、可验证的具身任务，并构建包含 300 个认证任务的基准；最强策略平均成功率仅 49.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真实验室基准依赖人工任务工程，难以规模化、系统化地将多样科学协议编译为可执行且可验证的具身任务，制约了具身智能体在自动化科学实验中的进展。

Method: 将科学具身任务构建视为编译问题：输入自然语言实验协议，通过语义接地、可执行任务合成、多阶段仿真认证，逐步生成语义环境、可执行操作程序和步骤级成功规范，并支持专家演示与执行轨迹的可复现生成。

Result: 构建了包含 300 个认证任务、覆盖多种实验室操作的即用基准，支持 HIL 任务执行、可复现专家演示生成和有序步骤级评估；代表性任务上最强策略平均成功率仅为 49.7%，并暴露出人类与具身智能体协同的明显弱点。

Conclusion: SciHorizon-eLab 为具身科学实验提供了可扩展的协议到任务编译与评估方案，并公开代码、基准数据和评估工具包以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Embodied agents offer a promising route to automating scientific experimentation, yet their progress is constrained by the lack of reliable and systematic evaluation environments. Existing simulation-based laboratory benchmarks rely heavily on manual task engineering, making it challenging to systematically compile diverse scientific protocols into executable and verifiable embodied tasks at scale. To address this challenge, we introduce SciHorizon-eLab, an agentic protocol-to-task compiler that formulates scientific embodied task construction as a compilation problem. Given a natural-language protocol of scientific experiments, SciHorizon-eLab progressively compiles laboratory protocols into semantic-preserving embodied tasks through semantic grounding, executable task synthesis, and multi-stage simulation-based certification. The system generates semantically grounded environments, executable manipulation programs, and step-level success specifications, while enabling reproducible generation of expert demonstrations and execution traces. Using this pipeline, we further construct \BenchName, a ready-to-use benchmark comprising 300 certified tasks across diverse laboratory operations. It supports HIL task execution, reproducible expert-demonstration generation, and ordered step-level evaluation. Across representative tasks, the strongest policy attains an average success rate of only 49.7%, with further evaluations revealing pronounced weaknesses in human and embodied agent coordination. We publicly release the code, benchmark data, and evaluation toolkit at https://github.com/SciHorizon-elab/SciHorizon-elab.

</details>


### [51] [Factorized axis convolutional gated recurrent unit with dynamic adaptive pooling for remaining useful life prediction of rolling bearings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30972)
*Hanbyeol Park,Jungho Choo,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种分解轴卷积门控循环单元模型，用于基于振动信号时频图的滚动轴承剩余使用寿命预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CNN的各向同性卷积核难以捕捉时频图中沿频率或时间轴方向对齐的退化结构，且全局平均池化会平均各轴信息，可能掩盖显著激活的位置与集中程度。

Method: 采用多尺度各向异性卷积和双轴卷积块注意力模块增强方向特征并突出显著时频区域；使用动态自适应池化聚合时频轴信息；通过GRU捕获潜在表示中的时间动态；利用蒙特卡洛dropout进行预测不确定性估计。

Result: 在两个公开轴承数据集上，所提模型在不同工况下优于现有RUL预测方法。消融实验表明，分解轴设计比各向同性卷积核具有更低平均误差；动态自适应池化在一个数据集上明显提升，在另一个数据集上与全局平均池化相当。

Conclusion: 各向异性特征提取和自适应特征聚合对基于时频表示的剩余使用寿命预测具有重要作用。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNN) are widely used to predict the remaining useful life (RUL) of rolling bearings from time-frequency representations (TFRs) of vibration signals. However, during degradation, characteristic structures in TFRs align predominantly along the frequency or time axis, making it challenging for conventional CNN isotropic kernels to capture directional structure. Furthermore, global average pooling (GAP) averages across axes, potentially obscuring the locations and concentrations of salient activations. This study introduces a factorized-axis convolutional gated recurrent unit (GRU) that employs multiscale anisotropic convolution and dual-axis convolution block attention module to enhance directional features and highlight salient time-frequency regions. Dynamic adaptive pooling (DAP) adaptively aggregates the time-frequency-axis information from the extracted feature maps, whereas a GRU captures temporal dynamics in the latent representations and Monte Carlo dropout enables predictive uncertainty estimation. Experiments on two public bearing datasets demonstrate that the proposed model outperforms existing RUL prediction methods across operating conditions. Ablation experiments demonstrate that the factorized axis-wise design achieves lower mean errors than convolutional isotropic kernels. DAP yields clear improvements on one dataset while matching GAP on the other, highlighting the importance of anisotropic feature extraction and adaptive feature aggregation for TFR-based RUL prediction.

</details>


### [52] [Same Text, Different Numbers: The Divergence of LLM-Based Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31013)
*Hamid Boustanifar,Sasan Mansouri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验基于大语言模型的公司文本变量是否对模型选择具有不变性，发现不同LLM生成的文本测量结果差异显著，并会显著影响后续实证推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者越来越多地使用生成式大语言模型将公司文本转化为实证变量，但尚不清楚这些测量是否依赖具体模型，以及模型选择是否会改变研究结论。

Method: 使用13种文本测量指标（如情感、管理层清晰度、不确定性、回答具体性、气候与政治风险），让来自不同提供商的7个LLM对S&P 500公司财报电话会议记录进行评分，并比较跨模型相关性、方差分解及下游回归推断。

Result: 跨模型排名相关性平均仅为0.52；跨提供商共同的记录层面差异仅解释总得分变异的34%。跨模型分歧不能预测后续分析师或市场分歧，表明存在显著的模型特有成分。模型选择会显著影响下游推断，系数大小、符号和统计显著性随模型变化。跨提供商平均可提高多数构念的排名稳定性，但得分水平仍对纳入集成的模型敏感。

Conclusion: LLM生成的变量应被视为依赖于模型的测量，并应在不同提供商模型间进行验证，以避免模型选择驱动研究结论。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use generative large language models (LLMs) to convert corporate text into empirical variables. We examine the extent to which LLM-based textual measures are invariant to model choice using thirteen measures, including sentiment, management clarity, uncertainty, answer specificity, and climate and political risk. Seven LLMs from different providers score earnings call transcripts of S&P 500 companies on these constructs. Cross-model rank correlations average only 0.52, and transcript-level differences common across providers account for only 34% of total score variation. Cross-model disagreement does not predict subsequent analyst or market disagreement, consistent with a substantial model-specific component rather than common ambiguity in the underlying disclosure. Model choice significantly affects downstream inference, with coefficient magnitudes, signs, and statistical significance varying substantially across models. Averaging across providers makes transcript rankings more stable for most constructs, but score levels remain sensitive to the models included in the ensemble. LLM-generated variables should therefore be treated as model-contingent measurements and validated across providers.

</details>


### [53] [Cheap, open agents make LLM pollution harder to mitigate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31054)
*Raluca Rilla,Anne-Marie Nussberger,Rui Mata,Dirk U. Wulff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究比较了九种智能体配置（从完全开源到闭源商业）在调查问卷中的表现与可检测性，发现完全开源智能体可本地免费运行且表现与商业替代品相当，构成LLM污染的新风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型污染指合成回答污染用于捕捉人类行为的数据；此前自主调查智能体因部署成本高而风险有限，但开放权重模型与开源智能体框架可能消除了这一障碍。

Method: 比较九种智能体配置（从完全开放到闭源商业），让每个智能体自主完成含多种回答类型的调查，并设置多种检测检查。

Result: 完全开放智能体可本地运行、无使用费，表现与商业替代品有竞争力；开放与商业智能体在不同检查中失败，没有单一检查能可靠检测所有智能体，但开放文本回答在区分智能体与人类方面效果最好。

Conclusion: 完全开放智能体是LLM污染的一项独特风险，支持采用强调开放文本分析的多层检测策略。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) pollution occurs when synthetic responses contaminate data intended to capture human behavior. High deployment costs have so far limited the risk posed by autonomous survey agents. However, open-weight models paired with open-source agentic frameworks may have removed this barrier. We compared the performance and detectability of nine agent configurations, ranging from fully open variants to closed commercial ones. Each agent autonomously completed a survey containing multiple response types yielding various detection checks. Fully open agents ran locally without usage fees and performed competitively with commercial alternatives. Open and commercial agents failed different sets of checks, and no single check reliably detected all agents, but open-text responses discriminated best between agents and humans. These findings identify fully open agents as a distinct risk for LLM pollution and support multilayered detection strategies emphasizing open-text analysis.

</details>


### [54] [Governed Deduction: Policy-Grounded Premise Authorization Beyond Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31029)
*Wesley Shu,Hsi-Ching Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Governed Deduction 形式化授权约束，基于 RBAC 增强的 Spider 基准构建匹配授权对，测试线性控制器能否学习策略敏感的授权推理。结果发现模型依赖角色名捷径，去除捷径后仅达随机水平，而符号策略 oracle 仍完美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理系统通常把前提使用视为相关性问题：事实可用且有用即可用于推理。但授权施加不同约束：前提可能被表示且逻辑上可用，却不被允许用于特定局部转换。因此需要区分可用性与局部许可性。

Method: 形式化 Governed Deduction，引入 transition-local admission predicate admit(p, tau, S)。从独立生成的 RBAC-augmented Spider 基准构建 4,461 个匹配授权对，使同一查询前提和策略状态分别支持允许与拒绝的消费转换。训练 premise/state-only、transition-only 和 joint 线性控制器，并进行角色名排列去捷径、留出评估、单侧控制与泄漏审计。

Result: 初始联合控制器在留出集达 99.19% 准确率，但 transition-only 控制达 100%，暴露角色名捷径。经过冻结、标签无关的上下文局部角色排列去除该捷径后，premise/state-only、transition-only 和 joint 线性控制器在 1,856 条留出边上均恰好为 50%，而符号策略 oracle 保持 100%。

Conclusion: 这是一个受控的负面发现：基准确实实例化了超越相关性的、以策略为基础的授权关系，但冻结线性表示无法恢复该关系。因此，评估学习到的策略敏感推理时，匹配单侧控制和泄漏审计至关重要。

Abstract: Reasoning systems usually treat premise use as a question of relevance: if a fact is available and useful, it may be selected for inference. Authorization imposes a different constraint: a premise may be represented and logically usable but not permitted for a particular local transition. We formalize this distinction as Governed Deduction (GD), with a transition-local admission predicate admit(p, tau, S). From an independently produced RBAC-augmented Spider benchmark, we construct 4,461 matched authorization pairs in which the same query premise and policy state support permitted and denied consuming transitions. An initial joint controller reaches 99.19% held-out accuracy, but a transition-only control reaches 100%, exposing a role-name shortcut. After a frozen, label-independent context-local role permutation removes that shortcut, premise/state-only, transition-only, and joint linear controllers all score exactly 50% on 1,856 held-out edges, while a symbolic policy oracle remains at 100%. The result is a controlled negative finding: the benchmark instantiates policy-grounded authorization beyond relevance, but the frozen linear representation does not recover the relation. Matched one-sided controls and leakage audits are therefore essential for evaluating learned policy-sensitive reasoning.

</details>


### [55] [Neuralyzing the Trace: Selective Representation-Level Unlearning with Contrastive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31056)
*Itai Zehavi,Fanny Jourdan,Ulrich Aivodji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCALPEL，一种对比稀疏自编码器，用于细粒度机器遗忘，通过选择性提取目标相关的内部特征，在TOFU基准上优于NMF和标准SAE，并与Gradient Difference和RMU相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的隐私删除请求（如欧盟GDPR）往往只针对单个人等狭窄目标。仅靠行为遗忘可能不足，需要直接干预模型内部表示；但标准机制可解释性提取器对这类目标选择性差。

Method: 识别出基于重建的提取存在能量偏差，即偏向主导背景结构而忽略低能量目标特定成分。提出SCALPEL：一种对比稀疏自编码器，学习更具选择性的遗忘特征。理论上证明对比训练能促进目标选择性特征，且选择分数可控制预期背景知识扰动。

Result: 在TOFU基准上对Qwen、Llama和Gemma进行实验，SCALPEL显著优于NMF和标准SAE干预，并与Gradient Difference和RMU具有竞争力。

Conclusion: SCALPEL将机制可解释性与细粒度机器遗忘结合起来，为更精准地删除窄目标信息同时保留模型其他能力提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted information while preserving a model's other abilities. In realistic settings, such as privacy requests under the EU GDPR, the target may be narrow, for example information associated with a single person. Behavioral forgetting alone may be insufficient, motivating interventions directly on internal representations. However, standard mechanistic-interpretability extractors are poorly selective for such targets. We identify an energy bias in reconstruction-based extraction, which favors dominant background structure over low-energy target-specific components. We introduce SCALPEL, a contrastive sparse autoencoder designed to learn more selective forget features. We show theoretically that contrastive training promotes target-selective features and that our selection score controls expected background knowledge perturbation. We validate SCALPEL experimentally on TOFU across Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, where it substantially improves over NMF and standard SAE interventions and is competitive with Gradient Difference and RMU, bridging mechanistic interpretability and fine-grained unlearning.

</details>


### [56] [Externalized CPDAG Summaries Improve LLM Causal Deduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31071)
*Wentao Sun,João Paulo Nogueira,Dominique Verchere,Mathieu Acher,Alonso Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究 Corr2Cause 任务，即判断因果断言是否在所有与观测相关性和条件独立性兼容的 DAG 中成立。作者将其视为潜在对象推理问题，并提出 Structured Thinking 两轮流程：先显式输出受模式约束的 CPDAG 摘要，再基于该图状态作答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由形式的思维链常把马尔可夫等价类问题退化为局部模式匹配，难以可靠完成 CPDAG 查询。因此需要将定义标签的潜在对象外化并约束其形式，再检验下游答案是否真正使用该对象。

Method: 提出 Structured Thinking：两轮流水线。第一轮生成类型化、模式约束的 CPDAG 摘要；第二轮基于该图状态回答因果问题。与强 PC 指令基线、PC 脚手架两轮散文控制、以及其他模型和扰动实验进行对比。

Result: 在 Corr2Cause 全测试集上，Structured Thinking 将 Qwen3.5-27B 的 Yes F1 从 73.0 提升到 86.4，提升 13.4 个百分点，McNemar p=2.4e-6，bootstrap 95% CI [+8.4, +18.6]；三个全 ID 随机种子平均增益 +8.1±5.3 个百分点。PC 脚手架两轮散文控制仅 67.6 F1。在 Qwen3.6-27B、Paraphrase-OOD 和 GPT-5.4-mini 上趋势一致。打乱生成的 CPDAG 会损失 12.0 F1；全划分审计与参考 CPDAG 高度一致，ID skeleton F1 0.960，精确匹配 75.9%。

Conclusion: 结果支持一个有边界的设计原则：外化定义标签的潜在对象，约束其形式，并检验下游答案是否使用它。

Abstract: Corr2Cause asks whether a causal claim holds in every DAG compatible with observed correlations and conditional independencies. We frame this as latent-object reasoning: the label is defined by a CPDAG query, but free-form chain-of-thought often collapses the Markov-equivalence-class problem into local pattern matching. We propose Structured Thinking, a two-turn pipeline that first externalizes a typed, schema-constrained CPDAG summary and then answers against that graph state. On the Corr2Cause full test, Structured Thinking raises Qwen3.5-27B from $73.0$ to $86.4$ $F_1$(Yes) over a strong PC-instruction baseline in the primary paired run ($+13.4$ pp; McNemar $p=2.4\times 10^{-6}$; bootstrap $95\%$ CI [$+8.4$, $+18.6$]); across three full-ID seeds, the mean gain is $+8.1 \pm 5.3$ pp. A PC-scaffolded two-turn prose control reaches only $67.6$ $F_1$, indicating that a detailed PC scaffold plus a schema-free prose intermediate is not sufficient. The same pattern holds on Qwen3.6-27B, Paraphrase-OOD, and GPT-5.4-mini. Scrambling the emitted CPDAG costs $12.0$ pp $F_1$, and a full-split audit shows close agreement with the reference CPDAG (ID skeleton $F_1$ $0.960$; exact match $75.9\%$). These results support a bounded design principle: externalize the latent object that defines the label, constrain its form, and test whether downstream answers use it.

</details>


### [57] [Up and Down the Abstraction Ladder: Code-Based Skills for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31076)
*Bartłomiej Cupiał,Jens Tuyls,Maciej Wołczyk,Davide Paglieri,Martin Klissarov,Benjamin Eysenbach,Piotr Miłoś,Karthik R. Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文系统研究代码化动作抽象对语言智能体的性能、推理成本和训练学习的影响。在NetHack中构建CodeHack技能库，比较仅用原始动作、仅用语义技能以及技能与原始动作结合的智能体，并在零样本提示、监督微调和强化学习三种设置下评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在需要长序列低级动作的环境中难以行动和学习。代码化抽象可让智能体调用可复用技能，提高生产力，但抽象存在泄漏，技能能力之外的情况仍需回退到原始动作。因此需要研究代码化动作抽象在生产力与灵活性之间的权衡。

Method: 在NetHack环境中使用CodeHack：一个带自然语言描述的代码技能库。比较三类智能体：仅使用原始动作、仅使用语义技能、技能与原始动作结合。评估设置包括零样本提示、监督微调和强化学习。

Result: 零样本评估中，相比原始动作，技能使游戏进展接近三倍，并将每回合推理成本降低86%。技能与原始动作结合可保留大部分收益，同时保留回退到低级动作的路径。强化学习中，基于技能的智能体学习显著更快，在相同训练预算下地牢层数平均增益是原始动作智能体的7.2倍。

Conclusion: 提供的技能库能同时提升语言智能体的性能、效率和可学习性；保留原始动作则在技能库不足时提供灵活性。论文发布了CodeHack及训练和评估代码。

Abstract: Language agents struggle to act and learn in environments that require long sequences of low-level actions. Code-based abstractions can make these agents more productive by letting them invoke reusable skills instead of repeatedly selecting individual actions. The code handles recurring local decisions, while the language model decides which skills to use and how to combine them. Yet abstractions are leaky, and situations beyond a skill's capabilities may require a return to primitive actions. Motivated by this tradeoff between productivity and flexibility, we systematically study how code-based action abstraction affects the performance, inference cost, and learning of language agents. We study this in NetHack, a challenging, long-horizon game environment, using CodeHack, our library of code-based skills with natural-language descriptions. We use this library to compare agents restricted to primitives with those using semantic skills alone or in combination with primitives. We evaluate these agents in three settings: zero-shot prompting, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning. Across a broad zero-shot evaluation on NetHack, we find that compared with primitives, skills nearly triple game progression, while reducing inference cost per episode by 86%. Combining skills with primitives retains much of this benefit while preserving a path back down to low-level actions. Finally, in RL, we find that skill-based agents learn significantly faster than agents acting on primitives, achieving a 7.2x larger average gain in dungeon level over the same training budget. These results show that a supplied skill library can improve performance, efficiency, and learning, while retaining primitives provides flexibility when the library is insufficient. We release CodeHack together with training and evaluation code.

</details>


### [58] [OmouAI: Argumentative Human-AI Policy Deliberation with Simulated Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31078)
*Stylianos Loukas Vasileiou,Antonio Rago,William Yeoh,Georgina Curto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmouAI，一个结合大语言模型与计算论辩的交互式、包容性审议系统，用于高风险公共政策审议。它通过模拟角色生成论点、构建共享论辩框架，并允许人类用户参与修订，最终用确定性论辩语义对照外部目标（如联合国可持续发展目标）评估论点，以减少谄媚并保证解释忠实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由大语言模型驱动的智能体辩论应用广泛，但在涉及人类的高风险场景（如公共政策审议）中，存在谄媚、缺乏忠实解释等问题，因此需要更可靠的人机审议机制。

Method: 提出 OmouAI 系统：让人类用户与模拟角色（如利益相关者、领域专家、魔鬼代言人）就政策主张进行审议；每个角色生成论点，所有论点形成共享论辩框架；用户可质疑、添加和修改论点，提供人类监督；再使用确定性论辩语义，对照外部目标对论点进行评估。

Result: OmouAI 能够支持交互式、包容性的政策审议，通过多角色论点和共享论辩框架降低谄媚，并通过确定性论辩语义提供忠实解释；外部目标的推进或恶化可作为政策建议的指标。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型与计算论辩相结合，有助于在高风险公共政策审议中缓解谄媚和解释不忠实问题，并通过人类监督与外部目标评估提升政策建议的可解释性和可信度。

Abstract: Debates amongst agents driven by large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated vast potential in various applications, but when these interactions include humans and take place in high-stakes environments, e.g., in public policy deliberations, they are beset with issues such as sycophancy and a lack of faithful explanations. To tackle these issues, we present OmouAI, an interactive and inclusive deliberation system that uses LLMs in combination with computational argumentation, a field which excels in representing and reasoning within debates. OmouAI allows a human user to deliberate policy claims for real-world challenges with simulated personas, e.g., representing stakeholders, domain experts or devil's advocates, towards reducing sycophancy. Each persona generates its own arguments, and the arguments of all parties form a shared argumentation framework. Users can then contest, add and revise arguments, providing crucial human oversight. Then, arguments are evaluated using deterministic argumentative semantics against external goals, such as the UN Sustainable Development Goals, guaranteeing faithful explanations. The advancement or worsening of the goals thus serve as indicators for the policy recommendations.

</details>


### [59] [Monitor Jailbreaking: Evading Chain-of-Thought Monitoring Without Encoded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31121)
*Julian Schulz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究让推理模型同时执行主任务和副任务，并在监控器检测到副任务推理时进行惩罚。结果模型学会了“监控器越狱”：不隐藏推理，而是通过措辞和格式让监控器无法标记，但人类仍能完全理解。这种现象跨模型、监控器和任务出现，并能泛化到未见过的监控器。释义（paraphrasing）是一种有效防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链监控有望在模型行动前检测问题推理，但担忧模型会通过编码推理隐藏真实思路，而强化学习中监控器的优化压力可能驱动这种编码行为。

Method: 训练推理模型执行主任务和副任务，当监控器检测到副任务相关推理时给予惩罚；分析模型学到的规避策略，并测试其在不同模型规模、监控器、任务、未见监控器及监控提示间的泛化性；最后检验释义作为防御手段的效果。

Result: 模型并未学会编码推理，而是学会调整思维链的措辞和格式，使监控器无法标记副任务推理，同时推理对人类读者完全透明，即“监控器越狱”。该现象跨模型规模、监控器和任务出现，可泛化到训练中未见过的更弱或更强监控器，并跨监控提示迁移。越狱策略看似简单，但手动复现不能可靠欺骗监控器。释义被越狱的思维链后，同一监控器能正确标记，且模型仍能完成两个任务。

Conclusion: 监控器越狱是思维链监控中一种现实且泛化性强的失败模式，不依赖编码推理。释义可作为一种有效防御，在保留任务能力的同时恢复监控器对问题推理的检测。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a promising safety technique for reasoning models, enabling detection of problematic reasoning before models act. A key concern is encoded reasoning, where models hide their true reasoning in ways that monitors and humans cannot interpret. Optimization pressure from CoT monitors during reinforcement learning is considered a likely driver of such behavior. We investigate this by training reasoning models to perform a main task and a side task, while penalizing them when a monitor detects reasoning about the side task. Surprisingly, models learn to evade monitors without encoding their reasoning. Instead, they learn to phrase and format their chains of thought such that monitors fail to flag side task reasoning, while the reasoning remains completely transparent to human readers. We call this phenomenon monitor jailbreaking. We find that monitor jailbreaking arises across different model sizes, monitors, and tasks. Jailbreaks generalize to monitors not seen during training, including both less and more capable monitors, and transfer across different monitor prompts. While jailbreaking strategies appear simple, manually replicating them does not reliably fool monitors. Finally, we show that paraphrasing is an effective defense: paraphrasing a jailbroken CoT allows the same monitor to correctly flag it, while still allowing the model to perform both tasks.

</details>


### [60] [AtomWorld-Mem: Memory-Restored World States for Long-Horizon Atomistic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31133)
*Tian Luo,Ruge Zhang,Haozhi Han,Yifrng Chen,Yunquan Zhang,Yunxin Liu,Ting Cao,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 AtomWorld-Mem，将长时程原子尺度演化视为基于记忆的世界状态恢复问题，通过短期事件记忆和长期结构记忆恢复瞬时快照缺失的隐藏动力学状态，用于在 KMC 约束下优先选择合法空位事件，实现高效、高保真且可迁移的合金演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瞬时原子构型快照存在歧义：局部相似构型可能对应不同隐藏动力学背景、未来事件偏好和等待时间尺度，因此仅凭当前构型难以进行长时程高保真演化。

Method: 将演化合金视为 AtomWorld；空间编码器从稠密局部拓扑和稀疏长程缺陷上下文写入多尺度原子关键帧；短期事件记忆与长期结构记忆跨时间整合关键帧，恢复可预测未来的演化状态；该状态在单事件 KMC 约束下对合法空位介导事件排序，事件合法性、物理执行和驻留时间更新仍由底层模拟器决定。

Result: 在固定微观事件预算下，AtomWorld-Mem 提升长时程原子演化进度，并在能量、结构和空位输运观测指标上保持高保真演化；还能零样本迁移到未见过的多种合金-温度 AtomWorld。

Conclusion: 记忆恢复式世界状态建模有望实现高效、物理合理且可迁移的原子尺度演化，表明其学到的是可复用的隐藏状态推断原则，而非特定体系的局部能量启发式。

Abstract: High-fidelity atomistic evolution over long timescales requires more than observing the current crystal configuration. Instantaneous atomistic snapshots are often incomplete: locally similar configurations can correspond to different hidden dynamical contexts, future event preferences, and waiting-time scales. We argue that this snapshot ambiguity makes long-horizon atomistic evolution fundamentally a memory-based world-state restoration problem. To address this, we introduce AtomWorld-Mem, a memory-restored atomistic world model that recovers the latent world state missing from instantaneous crystal snapshots. AtomWorld-Mem treats the evolving alloy as an AtomWorld: spatial encoders write multi-scale atomistic keyframes from dense local topology and sparse long-range defect context, while short-term event memory and long-term structural memory integrate these keyframes across time to restore a future-predictive evolutionary state. The restored state is used to prioritize legal vacancy-mediated events under single-event Kinetic Monte Carlo (KMC) constraints, while event legality, physical execution, and residence-time updates remain governed by the underlying simulator. Empirically, AtomWorld-Mem improves long-horizon atomistic progress under fixed microscopic event budgets while maintaining high-fidelity evolution across energetic, structural, and vacancy-transport observables. It further transfers zero-shot across diverse unseen alloy-temperature AtomWorlds, suggesting that the learned memory-restoration mechanism captures reusable principles of hidden-state inference rather than a system-specific local energy heuristic. These results position memory-restored world-state modeling as a promising route toward efficient, physically grounded, and transferable atomistic evolution.

</details>


### [61] [Can Linguistic Reasoning Vectors Enhance Multimodal Reasoning Ability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31140)
*Ziyi Wang,Li Li,Aolin Zhou,Yankun Shen,Chonghan Liu,Shuxia Lin,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出LIFT方法，通过向量干预将基座LLM的推理能力迁移到对齐后的VLM，无需重新训练，从而部分恢复多模态扩展中受损的语言推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数VLM由预训练LLM扩展视觉模块与多模态对齐而来，但多模态扩展常损害基座LLM原有的语言推理能力；对齐后的VLM自身难以可靠访问该能力，因此从基座LLM迁移推理能力比仅依靠VLM更有效。

Method: LIFT定义推理向量为“有显式推理轨迹的Reasoner路径”与“无推理轨迹的Solver路径”之间答案token隐藏状态的差异，并将其注入目标VLM的语言侧激活中；同时支持可学习的向量适配，而VLM主干保持冻结。

Result: 在两个VLM和六个推理基准上，按匹配协议比较来自基座LLM与对齐VLM的推理向量，结果显示LLM导出的向量一致优于VLM导出的向量；LIFT通过轻量语言侧干预部分恢复了受损的推理能力。

Conclusion: 基座LLM是恢复VLM推理能力的更有效来源；LIFT以轻量、无需重训练的方式部分恢复推理，且推理向量影响中间推理行为而非仅改变最终答案。

Abstract: Most Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are built by extending pretrained Large Language Models (LLMs) with visual modules and multimodal alignment. However, this multimodal scaling often degrades the language-side reasoning ability originally encoded in the base LLM. While the base LLM retains usable reasoning after scaling, the aligned VLM itself cannot reliably access this ability. Therefore, recovering the degraded reasoning capability in VLMs would benefit more from seeking help from the base LLM than from the VLM alone. Motivated by this, we propose LIFT (Language-side reasonIng Facilitation and Transfer), a lightweight vector-intervention method that transfers reasoning capability from the base LLM to the VLM without retraining the backbone. LIFT defines Reasoning Vectors as answer-token hidden-state differences between a Reasoner path with an explicit reasoning trace and a Solver path without it, and injects these vectors into language-side activations of the target VLM. LIFT further supports learnable vector adaptation while keeping the VLM backbone frozen. We evaluate LIFT on two VLMs across six reasoning benchmarks, comparing Reasoning Vectors extracted from the base LLM and from the aligned VLM under matched protocols. Results show that LLM-derived vectors consistently outperform VLM-derived vectors, confirming that the base LLM is a more effective source for recovering reasoning. LIFT partially recovers degraded reasoning through lightweight language-side interventions. Further analyses show that Reasoning Vectors influence intermediate reasoning behavior rather than merely altering final answers. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [62] [Toward AI-Augmented Cooperative Engineering Workflows: Requirements and Architecture the European Rover Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31136)
*Ahmed R. Sadik,Frank Joublin,Mariusz Bujny,Antonello Ceravola,Joan Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究AI如何支持流程级协作工程工作流，以欧洲漫游车挑战赛（ERC）学生团队为背景，通过调查识别 teamwork 瓶颈，并提出AI增强协作工程流程的需求和初始助手系统架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI工具多用于编码、文档、信息检索等孤立任务，较少关注流程级协作，即团队如何协调需求、任务、沟通、知识转移和子系统集成。ERC团队在单学期内设计并集成复杂漫游车系统，时间紧、子系统高度耦合，适合研究该问题。

Method: 对ERC 2025团队开展角色自适应40题问卷调查，共获得14支团队104份回复。调查涵盖团队结构、知识转移、任务管理、集成实践、沟通模式及当前AI使用情况，并据此推导AI增强协作工程工作流需求，提出初始助手架构。

Result: 发现反复出现的工作流瓶颈：文档有限、需求不清晰、沟通碎片化、任务监控非正式、集成返工严重。提出初始助手架构，连接用户界面、凭证管理、服务选择、专用AI服务和外部工程工具，支持任务澄清、需求与合规管理、沟通摘要、集成风险检测和持续知识捕获。

Conclusion: 论文为混合人-AI团队场景下的AI增强协作工程工作流提供了经验需求和架构方向。

Abstract: The growing availability of Artificial Intelligence (AI) tools creates new opportunities to support engineering design processes, yet their current use often remains limited to isolated tasks such as coding, documentation, or information retrieval. Less attention has been given to how AI can support cooperative engineering workflows at the process level, where teams must coordinate requirements, tasks, communication, knowledge transfer, and subsystem integration. This paper investigates this challenge in the context of the European Rover Challenge (ERC), where student teams design and integrate complex rover systems within a single academic cycle under strict time constraints and high subsystem interdependence. We conducted a role adaptive 40 question survey with ERC 2025 teams, yielding 104 responses from 14 teams. The survey examined team structure, knowledge transfer, task management, integration practices, communication patterns, and current AI usage. The results reveal recurring workflow bottlenecks, including limited documentation, unclear requirements, fragmented communication, informal task monitoring, and substantial integration rework. Based on these findings, we derive requirements for AI augmented cooperative engineering work-flows and propose an initial assistant system architecture that connects user facing interfaces, credential management, service selection, specialized AI services, and external engineering tools. The proposed architecture aims to support task clarification, requirement and compliance management, communication summarization, integration risk detection, and continuous knowledge capture. In doing so, the paper contributes empirical requirements and an architectural direction for AI augmented cooperative engineering workflows in hybrid human AI team settings.

</details>


### [63] [Learning to Stop without Learning to Stop: Self-Supervised Confidence Training Improves Reasoning Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31619)
*Parsa Hosseini,Akasha Tigalappanavara,Sumit Nawathe,Chenrui Fan,Sourya Basu,Genta Indra Winata,Anirban Das,Soheil Feizi,Nima Chitsazan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种自监督置信度微调方法，仅用600道训练题让推理模型在自身推理轨迹中间点预测答案置信度。推理时仍使用标准生成流程，不加入置信度引导或早停机制，却能减少最多25%的生成token并保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型常生成很长的推理链，导致推理计算成本高。现有提效方法主要依赖推理时早停或训练时显式奖励短推理，而作者希望探索一种不同监督信号，即置信度，是否能自然带来推理效率提升。

Method: 采用自监督过程，在模型自身推理轨迹的中间点预测其对最终答案的置信度，仅使用600个训练问题微调。损失函数不包含推理长度、效率或停止目标。推理阶段使用标准生成流程，不进行置信度提取或早停。

Result: 在Gemma、Qwen、Nemotron和GPT-OSS等模型上，于数学、科学和编程推理基准中，自监督置信度微调在匹配准确率下最多减少25%生成token，效率提升与显式优化短推理的方法相当。分析还表明，该方法大体保留基模型的高层推理组成，而非选择性抑制某些行为。

Conclusion: 高效推理可能作为学习元认知信号的下游结果自然出现，而无需被直接优化。置信度监督为提升推理效率提供了一种不依赖长度惩罚或推理时干预的新途径。

Abstract: Reasoning models often generate very long reasoning traces, making inference computationally expensive. Existing approaches typically improve efficiency either through inference-time early-stopping mechanisms or by explicitly encouraging shorter reasoning during training, for example through reinforcement learning with length penalties. We show that substantial efficiency gains can instead emerge from a different kind of supervision: \textit{confidence}. Using a self-supervised procedure, we fine-tune reasoning models to predict their confidence in the answer at intermediate points along their own reasoning trajectories using only 600 training problems. Confidence is used only as a training target: the loss contains no objective for reasoning length, efficiency, or stopping. At inference, the fine-tuned models use the standard generation procedure, with no confidence elicitation or early-stopping mechanism. Despite this, self-supervised confidence fine-tuning makes reasoning more efficient, reducing generated tokens by up to 25\% at matched accuracy across Gemma, Qwen, Nemotron, and GPT-OSS models on mathematical, scientific, and coding reasoning benchmarks, with efficiency gains comparable to methods that explicitly optimize for shorter reasoning. Analysis of reasoning episodes further shows that confidence supervision largely preserves the base models' high-level reasoning composition rather than selectively suppressing particular behaviors. Our results suggest that efficient reasoning may emerge as a downstream consequence of learning metacognitive signals, without being directly optimized.

</details>


### [64] [The Right Information Extraction Pipeline Depends on the Document: Accuracy-Energy Trade-offs for Small, Local Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31341)
*Christoph Walser,Mauricio Fadel Argerich,Jonathan Fürst*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在隐私约束下，使用本地部署的小型（≤8B参数）纯文本模型和视觉语言模型进行信息抽取时，应处理页面图像还是解析文本，并同时评估准确率与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息抽取流程选择页面图像还是解析文本取决于文档类型，且答案会随版面复杂度变化；同时隐私敏感文档不能使用云端服务，需要在本地小模型上兼顾能耗与准确率。

Method: 在输入表示、模型家族和推理配置构成的设计空间上，对小型纯文本模型与视觉语言模型进行基准测试，评估准确率和能耗；使用近纯文本的Kleister-NDA合同和版面复杂的VRDU表单。

Result: 批处理是主导性能耗杠杆，每页能耗降低38-85%且不损失准确率；FP8量化在单请求时节省27-32%，但批处理后每页节省不足1mWh（9-19%）。预处理占剩余能耗主导：神经OCR每页能耗是经典OCR的17倍，且从未进入帕累托前沿。表示选择随文档类型翻转：版面复杂文档适合视觉语言模型，近纯文本文档适合小型纯文本模型加廉价解析器，后者比任何视觉语言配置更准确且更便宜。

Conclusion: 研究给出了面向节能、隐私合规的本地信息抽取的具体指南，强调批处理、按文档类型选择表示方式，并避免高能耗的神经OCR。

Abstract: Whether an information extraction pipeline should process page images or parsed text depends on the document, and the answer flips across the layout spectrum. We study this trade-off under a constraint that rules out (closed) cloud services: privacy-sensitive documents processed on-premise by small ($\le 8\mathrm{B}$ parameter) text-only and vision--language models, evaluated on both accuracy and energy over a design space spanning input representation, model family, and inference configuration. Benchmarking on the near-plain-text Kleister-NDA contracts and the layout-rich VRDU forms, we find that batching is the dominant energy lever, cutting energy per page by 38-85% at no cost in accuracy, while FP8 quantization saves 27-32% when requests are served one at a time but less than 1mWh per page (9-19%) once batching is applied. Preprocessing dominates what remains: neural OCR costs $17\times$ more energy per page than classical OCR and never reaches the Pareto frontier. Which representation wins flips with the type of document: vision--language models on layout-rich documents and small text-only models with a cheap parser on near-plain text, where they are both more accurate and cheaper than any vision--language configuration. Our work yields concrete guidelines for energy-efficient, privacy-compliant local information extraction.

</details>


### [65] [Momentum-Guided Federated Split Distillation for Personalized Temporal Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31159)
*Ahmed-Rafik Baahmed,Jean-François Dollinger,Amine Brahmia,Mourad Zghal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动量引导的联邦拆分蒸馏框架，用于实现个性化、高效且自主的时间边缘智能。该框架包含TeRR-SAtt和AMGF两种创新方法，并在真实智能建筑数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于边缘设备上实现个性化、高效且自主的时间序列智能的需求，以及现有方法在延迟、内存和CPU使用方面的不足。

Method: 方法包括：1）动量引导的联邦拆分蒸馏框架；2）TeRR-SAtt：结合固定储备池表示、轻量级时间学生模型和个性化输出模块；3）AMGF：通过习得动量对客户端聚类并生成专门的教师更新。

Result: 在真实智能建筑数据上，TeRR-SAtt将边缘训练延迟降低65.50%，推理延迟降低44.70%，训练内存使用降低18.40%，推理CPU使用降低33.10%。AMGF相比全局更新将局部学习的RMSE最多提高35.31%。

Conclusion: 该框架能够实现高效且个性化的时间边缘智能，显著降低资源消耗并提升学习性能。

Abstract: We propose a momentum-guided federated split distillation framework for personalized, efficient, and autonomous temporal edge intelligence. We introduce TeRR-SAtt, our novel temporal reservoir student attention design that combines fixed reservoir representations, a lightweight temporal student, and personalized output modules. We also present AMGF, our anticipatory momentum-guided fusion mechanism that clusters clients through learning momentum and derives specialized teacher updates. On real-world smart-building data, TeRR-SAtt reduces edge training latency by 65.50%, inference latency by 44.70%, training memory usage by 18.40%, and inference CPU usage by 33.10% over the considered baselines. At the same time, AMGF improves local learning by up to 35.31% in RMSE compared to global updates.

</details>


### [66] [Semantic Navigation for Issue Localization in Code Repository](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31176)
*Yunxiang Wei,Zhenyu Lei,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SemNav，一个用于仓库级 issue 定位的框架：先用确定性检索生成广泛候选集，再由 LLM 智能体基于证据迭代精化与排序，从而提升文件/函数定位效果并改善下游问题解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体在迭代定位 issue 相关代码时，环境支持不足：难以按需解析未解决的关系目标、需从原始源码重建实体语义、且候选修订缺乏证据依据。

Method: SemNav 结合确定性检索与 LLM 智能体，包含三个核心组件：语义导航图，通过语言服务器按需解析程序关系并跨文件导航；面向 issue 的语义卡片，提供紧凑且基于源码的实体角色与相关性解释；持久候选工作区，记录候选及其证据依据，支持有依据的验证、修订和排序。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Lite 和 PLocBench 上，SemNav 优于现有基线；使用 Gemma 4B 时 File Hit@10 从 68.33% 提升至 82.67%。组件消融与轨迹分析表明三个组件具有互补作用，语义卡片相比完整源码阅读减少 48.2% 工作上下文负载。SemNav 在 SWE-Explore 的全部七项证据质量指标上排名第一，并将下游 issue 解决率从 44.00% 提升至 52.33%。

Conclusion: SemNav 通过确定性检索提供初始覆盖，并用 LLM 智能体进行证据引导的迭代修订，有效提升仓库级 issue 定位与下游问题解决性能，同时降低上下文负担。

Abstract: Repository-level issue localization aims to identify and rank the files and functions relevant to resolving a reported issue. LLM agents approach this task iteratively: they identify a set of potentially relevant locations, inspect the corresponding code, and revise their judgments about these candidates as new evidence is acquired. Existing environments, however, provide limited support for this loop: agents must search for unresolved relation targets, reconstruct entity semantics from raw source code, and revise candidates without evidential basis. To address these limitations, we present SemNav, a framework that leverages deterministic retrieval to seed a broad candidate set and an LLM agent to continually refine that set, thereby combining initial coverage with evidence-guided revision. SemNav supports this process through three key components. A Semantic Navigation Graph resolves program relations on demand through a language server, enabling direct navigation to related entities across files. Issue-conditioned Semantic Cards provide compact, source-grounded interpretations of each entity's role and relevance to the issue. A persistent Candidate Workspace records each candidate together with its evidential basis, enabling grounded verification, revision, and ranking. Across SWE-bench Lite and PLocBench, SemNav outperforms existing baselines, improving File Hit@10 from 68.33\% to 82.67\% with Gemma 4B. Component ablations and trajectory analysis support the complementary roles of all three components, while Semantic Cards reduce working-context load by 48.2\% relative to full-source reading. SemNav further ranks first on all seven evidence-quality metrics on SWE-Explore and improves downstream issue resolution from 44.00\% to 52.33\%.

</details>


### [67] [Neural State Prediction: Obstructing Shortcut Learning in EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31167)
*Kieren Yu,Ziyang Liu,Chang Huang,Jintai Chen,Kaishun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出神经状态预测（NSP）框架，通过约束预测目标和可用上下文，提升EEG基础模型的迁移表征学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型常用掩码预测，但优化该目标不能保证可迁移的神经表征；稳定的位置线索和局部相关性使掩码区域无需整合分布式神经上下文即可被预测。

Method: NSP使用EMA更新的目标编码器定义潜在监督，通过身份残差化去除通道身份和相对时间的加性效应，并采用拓扑分离上下文排除即时时空邻域。在TUEG的220万EEG片段上预训练。

Result: 在30个下游数据集上评估，覆盖临床诊断、睡眠分期、情绪识别、运动想象、事件相关电位、认知状态解码和语言检索。在EEG-FM-Bench全参数多任务微调下，14个数据集平均宏平衡准确率达63.94%，超最强基线2.35个百分点；消融实验验证各机制贡献。

Conclusion: 联合设计潜在目标及其上下文，为从分布式信号结构学习的EEG基础模型提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: EEG foundation models increasingly use masked prediction to learn from unlabeled recordings, but optimizing this objective does not ensure transferable neural representations. A central challenge is that stable positional cues and local correlations can make masked regions predictable without integrating distributed neural context. To reduce this reliance on low-information prediction paths, we introduce Neural State Prediction (NSP), a latent-predictive framework that constrains both the prediction target and the available context. NSP uses a Target Encoder updated by an exponential moving average (EMA) to define latent supervision. Identity residualization removes additive effects associated with channel identity and relative time from the targets, while topology-separated context excludes their immediate spatial and temporal neighborhood from the visible input. We pretrain NSP on 2.2 million EEG segments from TUEG and evaluate it across 30 downstream datasets spanning clinical diagnosis, sleep staging, emotion recognition, motor imagery, event-related potentials, cognitive-state decoding, and language retrieval. Under full-parameter multi-task fine-tuning on EEG-FM-Bench, NSP achieves 63.94 macro balanced accuracy across 14 datasets, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 2.35 percentage points. Controlled component ablations assess the contribution of each mechanism, while matched context controls and held-out interventions characterize the role of context geometry, signal content, and positional information. Jointly designing latent targets and their context offers a promising direction for EEG foundation models that learn from distributed signal structure.

</details>


### [68] [SPO: Discovering Adaptive Large Neighborhood Search Operators via Stackelberg Program Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31179)
*Xinyi Ke,Kai Li,Junliang Xing,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SPO，一种基于LLM的框架，用于发现自适应可执行的destroy-repair程序，将二者建模为Stackelberg交互，并在TSP和CVRP上优于强基线且具有泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LNS的效果关键取决于destroy和repair算子，而现有算子需要适应不断变化的LNS状态，并处理destroy与repair之间非对称的依赖与交互关系。

Method: SPO以紧凑的LNS状态作为条件来指导算子决策，将destroy-repair发现组织为程序空间上的Stackelberg博弈：destroy为领导者，repair为条件跟随者；使用角色特定信用评估，并结合LLM生成器学习与基于种群的进化搜索进行耦合优化。

Result: 在旅行商问题和带容量约束车辆路径问题上，SPO在广泛设置中优于强基线，并能从发现规模泛化到更大实例和基准集；行为分析表明算子具有状态依赖行为，且发现过程中destroy-repair得到耦合改进。

Conclusion: SPO能够通过LLM和Stackelberg程序优化发现自适应、相互耦合的destroy-repair算子，从而提升LNS性能并展现良好泛化性。

Abstract: Large neighborhood search (LNS) relies critically on destroy and repair operators, whose effectiveness depends on both adaptation to the evolving LNS state and interaction between the two roles. We introduce Stackelberg Program Optimization (SPO), an LLM-based framework for discovering adaptive executable destroy-repair programs. SPO conditions operator decisions on a compact LNS state, allowing state-dependent behavior to emerge through program discovery, and organizes destroy-repair discovery as a Stackelberg interaction over program space that reflects their asymmetric dependency. Role-specific credits evaluate destroy programs as leaders and repair programs as conditional follower responses, guiding a coupled optimization process that combines LLM generator learning with population-based evolutionary search over programs. Experiments on the traveling salesperson problem and capacitated vehicle routing problem show that SPO outperforms strong baselines across a broad range of settings and generalizes beyond the discovery scale to larger instances and benchmark sets. Behavioral analyses further demonstrate state-dependent operator behavior and coupled destroy-repair improvement during discovery.

</details>


### [69] [Accounting for Bias Enables Sustainable LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31184)
*Harshita Katoch,David Antony Selby,Gerrit Großmann,Sebastian Vollmer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出当前LLM-as-a-judge排行榜通过增加比较次数来补偿系统性偏差，既不严谨又浪费计算；作者提出统一的潜变量框架，显式校正位置、冗长、评委严厉、自我增强等偏差，从而用更少比较获得可靠排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge已成为可扩展主观评估的事实标准，但现有排行榜依赖大量比较来抵消测量偏差。其根源是测量模型不完整，把LLM评委当作中立、可互换的工具，忽视了位置偏差、冗长偏差、评委严厉度和自我增强等已知偏差，而增加数据量无法消除这些偏差。

Method: 提出一个统一的潜变量框架，联合建模成对比较数据和序数数据，并显式校正上述混淆因素，以在更少比较下恢复可靠排名。

Result: 该框架能够从显著更少的比较中恢复可靠排名；其拟合成本相对于单轮LLM推理可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 偏差校正不仅比单纯增加比较更具统计严谨性，也是更可持续、更值得信赖的评估方法。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge has become the de facto standard for scalable, subjective evaluation, yet current leaderboards compensate for systematic measurement bias by running ever more comparisons, an approach that is both statistically unsound and computationally wasteful. The root cause is an incomplete measurement model, treating LLM judges as neutral, interchangeable instruments ignores documented biases like position bias, verbosity bias, judge severity, and self-enhancement, that no volume of additional data can eliminate. We propose a unified latent variable framework that jointly models pairwise and ordinal data while explicitly correcting for these confounders, recovering reliable rankings from substantially fewer comparisons. Because fitting this model costs negligible compute relative to a single round of LLM inference, bias correction is not only more statistically rigorous but also a more sustainable approach to trustworthy evaluation.

</details>


### [70] [Evolutionary Safety of Recursive Self-Improving AI: Taxonomy, Risk Discovery, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31186)
*Chang Gong,Jingping Bi,Di Yao,Xinjian Liang,Chao Xiang,Ruijie Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“演化安全”视角，研究AI在持续且递归自我改进（RSI）过程中，安全属性如何变化、保持、积累与传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI能力快速提升并开始参与自身改进，递归自我改进日益现实；当系统、经验积累乃至产生后继系统的过程持续变化时，如何维持安全成为根本性问题。

Method: 引入演化安全视角；刻画意图漂移、错误累积、经验污染、安全属性侵蚀、评估器漂移、风险传播等反复出现的表现；构建涵盖持续智能体状态、模型状态、评估与环境反馈、计算基底、元层更新机制的分类体系；跨状态、更新、轨迹和谱系考察演化风险的发现与评估；推导修改、选择、授权、溯源和恢复等治理原则。

Result: 提出演化安全的分析框架、风险表现清单与分类体系，并给出跨状态、更新、轨迹和谱系的发现与评估思路，以及修改、选择、授权、溯源、恢复等治理原则；同时列出开放问题并提供项目资源与拟议评估系统。

Conclusion: 随着AI系统愈发持久、自适应和递归自我改进，需要从演化安全角度维护安全保证，关注安全属性在演化中的变化、持续、积累与传播，并推动相应评估与治理体系发展。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is advancing rapidly, with increasingly capable systems taking larger roles in reasoning, decision-making, scientific discovery, and autonomous development. As AI begins to participate in its own improvement, from model training and experience accumulation to agent evolution and automated AI development, the prospect of recursive self-improvement (RSI) is becoming increasingly relevant. This transition raises a fundamental safety question: how can safety be maintained when the system, its accumulated experience, and even the process producing its successors continue to change?
  We introduce Evolutionary Safety as a perspective for studying safety under persistent and recursive self-improvement. It concerns not only whether an AI system is safe at a particular moment, but how safety properties change, persist, accumulate, and propagate throughout evolution. We characterize recurring manifestations, including intent drift, error accumulation, experience contamination, safety-property erosion, evaluator drift, and risk propagation. We then develop a taxonomy spanning persistent agent state, model state, evaluation and environmental feedback, computational substrate, and meta-level update mechanisms. Building on this taxonomy, we examine how evolutionary risks can be discovered and evaluated across states, updates, trajectories, and lineages, and derive governance principles for modification, selection, authorization, provenance, and recovery. Finally, we outline open problems toward maintaining safety guarantees as AI systems become increasingly persistent, adaptive, and recursively self-improving. Project resources and proposed evaluation systems are available at https://chaunceykung.github.io/evolutionary-safety-rsi.

</details>


### [71] [Samples, Sources, Space: Decomposing Data Scale in Spatially Structured Representation Learning of Human Brain Microarchitecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31201)
*Christian Schiffer,Mathis Bode,Thomas Lippert,Katrin Amunts,Timo Dickscheid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将数据缩放视为分配问题，分离样本数量、来源多样性与空间覆盖，并在全脑组织学图像上用对比学习模型进行93次受控预训练实验，发现样本数、空间覆盖、算力和模型容量提升性能，但在固定样本预算下增加受试者来源并无明显收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统缩放研究通常只用样本总数表示训练数据，但对于具有层次和空间结构的数据，相同样本数可能来自少数或许多来源，且空间分布不同，因此需要研究样本数、来源多样性和空间覆盖对表征学习的独立影响。

Method: 在显微全脑组织学数据中，将单个大脑视为来源、特定空间位置的图像块视为样本；使用基于空间邻近监督的对比模型，在来自21个人脑的1160万个空间锚定图像块上，对数据分配、计算量和模型容量进行93次受控预训练实验。

Result: 性能随独特样本数、空间覆盖、计算量和模型容量增加而提升；在固定样本数下，将样本分配到1至18个受试者中没有检测到改进，尽管表征对预训练中见过的受试者泛化明显更好；受试者间变异强烈影响泛化，但增加来源数量在固定样本预算下无收益。

Conclusion: 样本数量、来源多样性和空间覆盖是空间结构化表征学习中彼此不同的数据缩放轴，固定样本预算下简单增加来源多样性并不必然带来性能提升。

Abstract: Scaling studies typically represent training data by a single count of samples. For hierarchically and spatially structured data, however, the same number of samples can be drawn from few or many sources and distributed differently across the underlying domain. We therefore study data scaling as an allocation problem, separating unique sample count, source diversity, and spatial coverage. We study this decomposition in microscopic whole-brain histology, where a source is an individual brain, and a sample is an image patch at a specific spatial location.
