Across 93 controlled pretraining runs of a contrastive model that uses spatial proximity for supervision, we vary data allocation, compute, and model capacity over 11.6 million spatially anchored image patches from 21 human brains. Performance improves with more unique samples, broader spatial coverage, additional compute, and larger model capacity. At fixed sample count, distributing samples across one to 18 subjects produces no detectable improvement, even though representations generalize substantially better to subjects encountered during pretraining. Inter-subject variation therefore strongly affects generalization, but additional subjects provide no benefit when a fixed sample budget is distributed across more sources. These results establish sample count, source diversity, and spatial coverage as distinct axes of data scaling in spatially structured representation learning.

</details>


### [72] [Which Influence Are We Estimating? The Role of Counterfactual Specifications in Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31214)
*Zhe Li,Wei Zhao,Peixin Zhang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出数据影响估计器排名不一致的根本原因常是“规格不匹配”，而非单纯近似误差，并主张将影响定义为反事实估计量，系统分析行为、干预与反事实训练过程等规格选择对归因结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练样本影响估计对数据调试、估值和归因很重要，但现有影响估计器常给出不兼容的排名，通常被归因于近似误差；作者认为更深层原因是不同估计器隐含的规格不同。

Method: 将影响形式化为反事实估计量，区分固定估计量下的近似误差与不同估计量间的规格不匹配；按隐含规格组织代表性估计器；推导局部分解以刻画行为信号、训练信号和反事实参数响应之间的交互；并在受控实验、噪声标签检测和LLM归因中验证。

Result: 受控实验表明，不同规格下的精确估计量可产生不同排名，而近似误差会随扰动远离线性化点而增大；在噪声标签检测和LLM归因中，规格选择显著影响归因质量，尤其是行为代理变量的选择；与行为对齐的规格能识别被默认损失或相似度规格掩盖的目标特定训练样本。

Conclusion: 规格分析应成为解释和比较数据影响估计器的必要第一步，影响估计结果高度依赖于所归因的行为、施加的干预及反事实训练过程等规格设定。

Abstract: Estimating the influence of training examples on model behavior is essential for data debugging, valuation, and attribution. Existing influence estimators often produce incompatible rankings, which are commonly ascribed to approximation error. We argue that a more fundamental source of disagreement is specification mismatch: influence depends on the behavior being attributed, the intervention applied to each training example, and the counterfactual training process that maps the intervention to a model response. These choices are especially important when the target behavior requires a tractable surrogate, such as query loss, a logit, or a margin. We formalize influence as a counterfactual estimand, distinguish specification mismatch across estimands from approximation error in estimating a fixed estimand, and organize representative estimators by their implied specifications. We further derive a local decomposition that exposes how behavior signals, training signals, and counterfactual parameter responses interact. Controlled experiments show that exact estimands under different specifications can induce different rankings, whereas approximation error grows as perturbations move farther from their linearization points. Experiments on noisy label detection and LLM attribution show that specification choices significantly affect attribution quality, especially for the choice of behavior surrogate. Behavior-aligned specifications can identify target-specific training examples obscured by default loss-based or similarity-based specifications. These results establish specification analysis as a necessary first step for interpreting and comparing data influence estimators.

</details>


### [73] [DIAL: Position-Debiased LLM Judges with Adaptive Human Preference Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31215)
*Zesheng Cai,Yingqi Fan,Sichang Chen,Jin-Hong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 DIAL 统一框架，结合大量 LLM 比较与少量人类比较，分离并消除评判位置效应，学习去偏后的共享 LLM 偏好结构，并自适应校准到人类偏好目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 作为评判者虽可扩展评测，但其判断易受回答顺序影响；即便去除位置效应后，仍可能系统性地偏离人类偏好。

Method: DIAL 框架融合丰富的 LLM 比较和有限的人类比较，分离评判者特定的位置效应，学习位置去偏的 LLM 偏好共享结构，并自适应校准到人类偏好。理论上研究潜变量偏好、位置效应与人类校准的识别，平衡 LLM 锚定与有限人类证据的自适应估计，以及校准后人类偏好的固定权重不确定性量化。实证上在受控模拟和三个人类偏好基准上评估，并收集 21 个 LLM 评判者在两种展示顺序下的超 41 万条判断。

Result: DIAL 对不平衡回答顺序保持稳健，在有限标签下实现强人类对齐排序，并在 LLM 信息不完美时自适应利用人类证据；真实数据研究为 LLM 评判偏差、异质性和人类对齐研究提供资源。

Conclusion: DIAL 能有效去偏 LLM 评判并利用有限人类标签提升人类偏好对齐，同时所构建的大规模双顺序判断数据支持未来相关研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) as a judge enable scalable evaluation, but their judgments can be sensitive to response order and, even after removing such position effects, can still diverge systematically from human preferences.We introduce DIAL, a unified framework that combines abundant LLM comparisons with limited human comparisons to separate judge-specific position effects, learn shared structure in position-debiased LLM preferences, and adaptively calibrate that structure toward the human preference target. Theoretically, we study three aspects of DIAL: (i) identification of latent LLM preferences, position effects, and human calibration; (ii) adaptive estimation that balances LLM anchoring against limited human evidence; and (iii) fixed-weight uncertainty quantification for the calibrated human preference. Empirically, we evaluate position debiasing and human alignment separately in controlled simulations and on three human-preference benchmarks, showing that DIAL remains robust to unbalanced response order, achieves strong human-aligned rankings with limited labels, and adapts toward human evidence when LLM information is imperfect. Our real-data study collects over 410K judgments from 21 LLM judges in both display orders, providing a resource for future studies of LLM-judge bias, heterogeneity, and human alignment.

</details>


### [74] [Purin: A Biology-inspired Mechanism for Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31235)
*Zishu Liu,Chunbo Luo,Christos Grecos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Purin，一种受生物启发且兼容人工神经网络的机制，将突触效能调制引入卷积神经网络，以建模短期和长期突触效能变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 ANN 在一个训练批次中使用固定可训练权重表示神经传递，忽略了突触效能的短期变化；而离散时间步模拟又需要额外的时间处理，许多常规 ANN 架构并不采用。

Method: Purin 使用基于时间间隔的神经活动抽象，无需离散时间步即可引入短期和长期突触效能变化；引入有界因子表示临时突触效能变化，并使用两个权重矩阵表示输入侧和输出侧效能，这些矩阵通过反向传播更新并解释为长期突触效能变化。

Result: 在去除 AlexNet、VGG11 和 GoogLeNet 架构中的混杂因素后，Purin 在所有评估数据集上均提升了这三个模型的分类准确率。

Conclusion: Purin 是一种生物学启发且与常规 CNN 兼容的机制，能够在不依赖离散时间步的情况下引入短/长期突触效能变化，并有效提升图像分类性能。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks (ANNs) usually represent neural transmission with fixed trainable weights during a training batch, which omits short-term changes in synaptic efficacy. In addition, the discrete time-step simulation requires additional temporal processing that many conventional ANN architectures do not use. To overcome these challenges, we propose Purin, a biology-inspired and ANN-compatible mechanism, that introduces synaptic efficacy modulation into conventional convolutional neural networks. Purin uses a time-interval-based abstraction for neural activities, which allows Purin to introduce short- and long-term synaptic efficacy changes without using discrete time-steps. Purin introduces a bounded factor to represent temporary synaptic efficacy changes, together with two weight matrices that represent input-side and output-side efficacy. The weight matrices are updated by backpropagation and interpreted as the long-term synaptic efficacy changes. Experimental results show that after removing the confounding factors in the AlexNet, VGG11, and GoogLeNet architectures, Purin improves the classification accuracies in all three models across the evaluated datasets.

</details>


### [75] [MA-WAM: Multi-Agent World-Action Model for Test-Time Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31281)
*Guowei Zou,Haitao Wang,Guoxin Wang,Beiwen Zhang,Zhiquan Chen,Guojie Wang,Hejun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MA-WAM，一种面向多智能体流策略的测试时世界模型规划框架，通过建模联合动作间的跨智能体依赖来预测团队回报，并在多种离线多智能体任务中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协作任务要求智能体同时执行联合动作，且每个智能体的动作会影响其他智能体的观测与响应。直接将单智能体世界模型逐步应用于各智能体动作，无法捕捉多个智能体同时动作之间的依赖关系，因此需要专门的多智能体世界模型来预测联合动作带来的团队回报。

Method: MA-WAM 是一种测试时规划框架，使冻结的多智能体流策略能够评估候选联合动作的未来结果。它根据跨智能体依赖预测每个联合动作的后果，并实现高效的候选动作评分。

Result: 在 MAMuJoCo、SMAC 和 MPE 的30个离线多智能体强化学习设置中，MA-WAM 相对直接执行平均提升22.0%，相对均匀动作选择平均提升25.6%。在 A100 GPU 的标准评估协议下，MA-WAM 仅增加12.1 ms，占生成与评分时间的2.5%。

Conclusion: MA-WAM 是首个面向多智能体流策略的测试时世界模型规划器，能够有效利用跨智能体依赖评估候选联合动作，在多种离线 MARL 任务中取得显著性能提升，同时计算开销较小。

Abstract: Multi-agent cooperative tasks require different agents to execute a joint action simultaneously, and each agent's action affects both the observations and responses of the other agents. Hence, a world model is needed to predict the team return resulting from the joint actions of all agents. A naive extension directly applies a single-agent world model to each agent's action when predicting the team return step by step. However, such an extension fails to capture the dependencies among the simultaneous actions of multiple agents. We propose Multi-Agent World-Action Model (MA-WAM), a test-time planning framework that enables a frozen multi-agent flow policy to evaluate futures of candidate joint actions. To our knowledge, MA-WAM is the first test-time world-model planner for multi-agent flow policies. MA-WAM predicts the consequences of each joint action according to cross-agent dependencies and enables efficient candidate scoring. Across 30 offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) settings on MAMuJoCo, SMAC, and MPE, MA-WAM achieves mean relative gains of 22.0% over direct execution and 25.6% over uniform action selection. Under the standard evaluation protocol on an A100 GPU, MA-WAM adds 12.1 ms, accounting for 2.5% of the measured generation-and-scoring time.

</details>


### [76] [Mutable Transcripts: Mitigating Context Pollution through Editable Conversation State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31354)
*Dan Barry,Andrew Hines*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“可变对话记录”（mutable transcripts）交互范式，允许用户通过自然语言编辑请求修改先前对话轮次，从而让对话历史可更新而非仅追加。通过原型系统、17人用户研究和对话记录分析，初步验证其能提升交互体验并减少过时上下文影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM聊天系统将对话历史视为不可变的轮次序列，但用户意图会通过纠正、细化和约束变化而动态演变。静态记录与动态意图不匹配会导致上下文污染，使过时或无关信息持续影响后续回复。

Method: 提出可变对话记录范式，将对话记录从被动记录转变为可编辑的对话状态表示；实现集成到标准聊天界面的工作原型；通过控制用户研究（n=17）和代表性交互场景的对话记录分析评估可行性。

Result: 用户显著更偏好可变对话记录，在清晰度、信心和易用性上评分更高，且更少意图重新开始对话。对话记录分析表明，可变对话记录可缩短对话长度并消除已过时的保留上下文。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，用户驱动的对话历史修訂可以提升交互质量，并帮助系统维持对用户当前意图的更准确表示。

Abstract: Contemporary large language model (LLM) chat systems treat conversation history as an immutable sequence of turns that defines the model's working context. However, user intent in real interactions is not static: it evolves through correction, refinement, and shifting constraints. This mismatch between dynamic intent and static transcripts can result in context pollution, where outdated or irrelevant information persists and continues to influence subsequent responses. We introduce mutable transcripts, a new interaction paradigm that enables users to revise prior turns through natural language edit requests, allowing the conversation history itself to be updated rather than appended. This reframes the transcript from a passive record into an editable representation of conversational state. We present a working prototype that integrates transcript-level revision into a standard chat interface and evaluate its feasibility through a controlled user study (n=17) and an illustrative transcript analysis of representative interaction scenarios. Participants significantly preferred mutable transcripts over standard chat across measures of clarity, confidence, and ease of use, with reduced intent to restart conversations. Transcript analysis of representative user study conversations shows that mutable transcripts can reduce conversation length and eliminate obsolete retained context. These findings provide initial evidence that user-driven revision of conversational history can improve interaction quality and help maintain a more current representation of user intent. The source code and prototype can be accessed at https://github.com/QxLabIreland/ReChat

</details>


### [77] [Programs-of-Layers in LLMs through the Lens of Cortical Areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31360)
*Justus Westerhoff,Stephan Olbrich,Hatem Oraby,Matthew Evan Larkum,Felix Alexander Gers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对PoLar方法进行了复现与扩展分析：将Transformer层视为可调用函数库，通过自适应跳过或重复连续层块来动态路由输入，并在5个模型上验证其效果与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM推理采用固定深度、固定顺序的前向传播，不根据输入难度调整计算；而人脑以丘脑为中心灵活路由信息。PoLar提出让Transformer层像函数库一样被动态组合，本文旨在更细致地复现并检验该思路。

Method: 重新实现并详细诊断PoLar的MCTS搜索过程，在5个模型上评估；分析跳过、重复及二者组合的程序效果；检验其学习到的单次推理路由器；并进一步研究通用程序、程序结构及鲁棒性。

Result: 复现了部分发现：跳过优于标准前向，重复优于跳过，二者结合优于单独使用；简单问题需更短程序，难题需更多层重复。但未能复现其学习路由器的主结论：单次推理中最高排名预测退化为标准前向，尽管top-k程序整体有真实准确率提升。此外，少量通用程序即可解决大多数问题；纠错程序非常脆弱，单次编辑通常即破坏纠错效果。

Conclusion: PoLar在动态层路由上具有潜力，类似丘脑-皮层协调机制，但其学习路由器在单次推理中的可靠性不足；通用程序与程序鲁棒性分析揭示了该方法的优势与脆弱性，相关代码已公开。

Abstract: Inference in LLMs is conventionally a fixed-depth, fixed-order forward pass through every layer, regardless of how difficult the input is. The human brain does not work this way: using the thalamus as a central hub, it routes information flexibly to all regions of the cortex according to demand. Li et al. (2026) recently showed, with a system they call program-of-layers (PoLar), that transformers can be given an analogous flexibility if their layers are treated as a library of functions rather than a fixed sequence. Performance improves over the standard forward pass when each input is dynamically routed through an adaptive sequence of skipped or repeated contiguous layer blocks. We reconstructed PoLar's diagnostic MCTS in more detail than the original paper and applied it across 5 models. We reproduced several of PoLar's findings: skipping outperformed the standard pass, repeating outperformed skipping, and combining both outperformed either alone. Shorter programs sufficed for easier questions, while harder questions required more layer repeats. However, we failed to replicate the main claim regarding their learned router for single-shot inference: its top-ranked prediction consistently collapsed back to the standard pass, even though its top-k predicted programs, taken together, did show a real accuracy gain. Beyond reproduction, we find that a small number of generic programs are enough to solve most of the questions. We also provide a much deeper analysis of these programs' structure and robustness: for example, we found that programs that correct errors are highly brittle: undoing even a single edit inside a program typically breaks the correction. Connecting this to the brain's routing mechanisms, PoLar mirrors principles of thalamo-cortical coordination between cortical-area-like transformer layers. We publicly release the code at https://datexis.github.io/RE-PoLar/

</details>


### [78] [Segment-Level Agentic Topic Modeling for Improved Data Exploration and Resource Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31460)
*Myeongjun Erik Jang,Antonios Georgiadis,Sae Young Moon,Fran Silavong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SeLATM 框架，通过段落级主题生成和基于智能体反馈循环的主题精炼，解决现有 LLM 主题模型在主题分配过程中的资源消耗高、主题粒度不佳及无法生成文档主题分布等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的主题模型虽能生成更自然、可读的主题，但主题分配过程存在无法输出文档主题分布、主题过宽或过窄、资源消耗随文档数量和长度增加而显著上升等缺陷，难以满足工业级大规模文档的高质量深度分析需求。

Method: SeLATM 采用段落级主题生成，并利用智能体反馈循环进行主题精炼，以避免传统主题分配过程的弊端。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，SeLATM 相比基于主题分配的方法显著减少了 LLM 资源消耗，同时保持了更优的性能。

Conclusion: SeLATM 能有效应对工业应用中大规模文档处理和高品质主题分析的需求，在降低资源成本的同时提升主题建模效果。

Abstract: Topic modeling is an effective technique for discovering hidden themes within documents and is widely used in text mining and data analysis across a variety of industry sectors. Recently, large language model (LLM)-based topic models have been emerged that prompt LLMs to generate topics then assign the topics to documents, producing more natural and human-readable topics than conventional topic modeling algorithms. However, the nature of topic assignment process causes certain drawbacks, such as the incapability to produce topic distributions over a document, too broad or narrow topics, and high resource consumption, which increases with the number and length of of documents being assigned topics. These issues are particularly critical for industrial applications, which require high-quality, in-depth analysis and the processing of large volumes of documents. In this context, this paper introduces a framework called SeLATM, which addresses these concerns by employing segment-level topic generation and topic refinement through agentic feedback loops. Experimental results on various datasets demonstrate that SeLATM significantly reduces the LLM resources compared to methods based on topic assignment process, while maintaining superior performance.

</details>


### [79] [G2MAF: Test-Time Gradient Guidance for Multi-Agent Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31286)
*Guowei Zou,Haitao Wang,Guoxin Wang,Zhiquan Chen,Beiwen Zhang,Guojie Wang,Hejun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对离线多智能体强化学习中冻结策略单次部署易陷入次优联合动作的问题，提出G2MAF测试时优化框架，通过全局归一化投影评论家梯度引导所有智能体修正动作，在保持动作可行且接近冻结策略的同时提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线多智能体强化学习从固定数据集中学习协作策略，部署时策略冻结，通常只提出并执行单个联合动作，这种一次性部署容易选择次优方案，即使存在与行为数据一致的更优近邻替代方案。

Method: 提出梯度引导多智能体流（G2MAF）框架，在测试时优化联合策略。它应用一个全局归一化的投影评论家梯度来指导和协调所有智能体的修正，同时保持动作既可行又接近冻结策略的提议。

Result: 在24个MPE和SMAC设置中，其规范变体改进了20个冻结设置，MPE上平均相对增益9.2%，SMAC上8.9%，模型推理延迟仅增加约6%。

Conclusion: G2MAF能有效提升离线MARL部署性能，且计算开销小。

Abstract: Offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) learns cooperative policies from fixed datasets without further environment interaction and a learned policy is frozen at deployment. Such a frozen policy typically proposes a single joint action and executes it directly at deployment time. However, this one-shot deployment often commits to a suboptimal proposal, even when better nearby alternatives remain consistent with the behavior data. To address this issue, we propose Gradient Guided Multi Agent Flow (G2MAF), a refinement framework for optimizing joint policies at test-time. G2MAF applies one globally normalized, projected critic gradient to guide and coordinate all agents' corrections while keeping the action both feasible and close to the frozen policy proposal. Across 24 MPE and SMAC settings, its canonical variant improves 20 frozen settings, with mean relative gains of 9.2% on MPE and 8.9% on SMAC, with model inference latency increased by about 6% only.

</details>


### [80] [Compress What You See, Not What You Say: Anchored Context Distillation for Latent-Observation Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31430)
*Zhensheng Zou,Guoqing Wang,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 LOHA 与 ACD，用于压缩软件工程智能体的长上下文：将旧工具观察压成软 token，同时保留关键文本与行为，从而降低上下文成本并维持任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具观察占据智能体上下文的主要部分，长交互历史维护成本高；现有压缩方法可能丢失后续动作所需信息，而适配软 token 表示又可能损害原始行为。

Method: 结合 Latent Observations, Hard Actions (LOHA) 与 Anchored Context Distillation (ACD)。LOHA 将较旧工具观察压缩为软 token，保留智能体自身轮次和最近 K 条观察文本；ACD 将基座模型全文预测蒸馏到潜在视图，并以纯文本输入锚定行为，减少行为漂移。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 上，K=3 使每次调用上下文减少 43%（Qwen3-4B）和 57%（SWE-Master-4B-RL），解决率为 12.1% 和 21.8%，未压缩基座为 14.5% 和 27.5%。K=8 时单次 recency sweep 达 14.4% 和 23.0%。在 32K token 限制下，Qwen3 K=3 在 199 实例子集上解决率 21.1%，高于同一适配智能体全文输入的 11.1%；单 GPU 并发服务吞吐为全文智能体的 1.9 倍。

Conclusion: LOHA+ACD 可在保留动作关键信息和智能体行为的同时显著压缩上下文；压缩窗口越大通常越利于任务表现，但较小 K 可提升上下文效率与吞吐。

Abstract: Tool observations dominate the context of software-engineering agents, making long interaction histories costly to maintain. Existing context compression methods can discard information needed by later actions, while adapting agents to soft-token representations can compromise their original behavior. To reduce context while preserving action-critical information and agent behavior, we combine Latent Observations, Hard Actions (LOHA), a context layout that separates compressed history from text needed for exact reference, with Anchored Context Distillation (ACD), a training method that enables latent reading while constraining behavioral drift. LOHA compresses older tool observations into soft tokens while retaining the agent's own turns and the last K observations in text, providing compact access to historical information and exact access to recent content. To enable the agent to use this representation, ACD distills the base model's full-text predictions into the latent view while anchoring its behavior on plain-text inputs to the same base model. On SWE-bench Verified, K=3 reduces context per call by 43% for Qwen3-4B and 57% for SWE-Master-4B-RL, with resolve rates of 12.1% and 21.8% versus 14.5% and 27.5% for their uncompressed bases. A single-run recency sweep reaches 14.4% and 23.0% at K=8, with larger windows generally favoring task performance over compression. Under a 32K-token limit, Qwen3 with K=3 resolves 21.1% of a 199-instance subset versus 11.1% for the same adapted agent using full text. In concurrent single-GPU serving, it achieves 1.9 times that full-text agent's instance throughput.

</details>


### [81] [Completed Pairs Hide Capped Failures: A ReVerPi Case Study of Selective Context Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31381)
*Guangzhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过 ReVerPi 扩展考察上下文投影在工具观察归档中的权衡：投影用紧凑可寻址摘录替代旧观察以减少重复输入，但可能增加证据检索轮次；论文重点分析评估中停止规则、未执行配对和资源汇总带来的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文投影可能降低重复输入，却可能引入额外检索请求，需要量化其对成功率和 token 消耗的影响；同时现有评估若在首个分支失败后停止并抑制配对分支，会遗漏边界运行，导致结论偏差。

Method: 构建 ReVerPi（Pi 扩展），包含归档观察和匹配的全量/投影续写。开展 86 次源阅读实验、641 次模型请求，得到 15 个完整配对，并分析额外边界运行。比较两臂成功率、投影减全量的成功差界、逻辑 token、后缀请求，并区分选择器拟合与评估数据。

Result: 15 个完整配对中两臂成功率相同，均为 12/15。恢复 27 个边界运行后，投影减全量的成功差界为 -9 到 +1 个任务。一个被选择器选中的投影续写虽成功检索归档，但耗尽 12 次请求，而全量对照仅用 3 次。11 个共同正确配对中，投影使聚合逻辑 token 降低 25%，但使配对中位数 token 增加 29%，后缀请求从 35 增至 55。分离拟合与评估后，选择器在 13 个可比运行中多 1 次失败、逻辑 token 多 8.6%。

Conclusion: 这是一项方法学案例研究，结论仅适用于所记录的活动，并非对无限制 Pi 的总体非劣效或优越性断言。评估应保留每个干预边界，独立执行两个分配臂而不依赖第一臂完成情况，并同时报告完成率、交互次数和 token 消耗。

Abstract: Context projection replaces older tool observations with compact, addressable excerpts, reducing repeated input while potentially adding evidence-retrieval turns. We study this trade-off in ReVerPi, a Pi extension with archived observations and matched full/projected continuations. In an 86-run source-reading campaign with 641 model requests, the 15 completed pairs show identical success: 12/15 per arm. Twelve further boundary runs stop, with the runner suppressing the companion whenever the first arm fails to complete. Restoring all 27 boundary runs bounds projected-minus-full success between $-$9 and +1 tasks. One omitted, selector-chosen projected continuation successfully retrieves archive text yet exhausts twelve requests; its full counterpart answers in three. The eleven jointly correct pairs form a fully observed success stratum within this recorded frame: projection reduces aggregate logical tokens by 25%, while increasing the median pair's tokens by 29% and total suffix requests from 35 to 55. Separating fitting from evaluation changes the selector's apparent tie: outside its four fitting pairs, it incurs one extra failure and 8.6% more logical tokens over thirteen comparable runs. This methodological case study connects stopping rules, known bounded failures, unexecuted companions, and resource aggregation. Its findings concern the recorded campaign, rather than population noninferiority or superiority over unrestricted Pi. Evaluations should retain every intervention boundary, execute both allocated arms independently of the first arm's completion, and report completion alongside interaction and token expenditure.

</details>


### [82] [Game Arena: Strategic LLM Evaluation in Competitive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31473)
*Bovard Doerschuk-Tiberi,Yao Yan,Justin Chiu,Hann Wang,Timothy Chung,Martyna Plomecka,John Schultz,Jon Lipovetz,Clayton Drazner,Yuchen Zhuang,Jaimie Hwang,Nate Keating,Riley Jones,Andrew Lee,Oran Kelly,Ian Gemp,Michael Aaron,Laurel Prince,Kate Larson,Jeff Moser,Harrison Jobe,Chad Woodford,Siqi Liu,Andrew Wang,Bo Chang,Christopher D'Mello,Diane Chaleff,Addison Howard,Johnny Yip,Chuck Sugnet,Antonio Gulli,Meghan O'Connell,Will Cukierski,Nenad Tomasev,Dima Yeroshenko,Kinjal Parekh,Roxanne Daniel,Marc Lanctot,Domino Weir,Elsa Dong,Daniel Hennes,Melissa Nalubwama,Robert Fraser,Ryan Trostle,Jun Peng,Tom Mason,Lloyd Hightower,Chiamaka Chukwuka,Yuexiang Zhai,Phoebe Kirk,Yi Su,Yuting Han,Jie Ren,Chris Prichard,Sahand Sharifzadeh,Karim Hakimzadeh,DJ Sterling,Meg Risdal,Kate Olszewska,Ya Xu,Orhan Firat,Minmin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 介绍 Kaggle Game Arena：一个通过竞技游戏动态评估大语言模型的开放平台，并报告国际象棋、扑克和狼人杀三个试点环境的竞赛设计与结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准容易随模型能力提升而饱和，难以持续评估模型在策略规划、适应性和不确定性下的鲁棒性，因此需要更动态、可扩展的评测方式。

Method: 构建开放且持续扩展的游戏竞技场基础设施，让模型在结构化环境中进行头对头对战；选取国际象棋、扑克和狼人杀覆盖完全信息、不完全信息和多人博弈场景，并定义评估指标、运行完整竞赛。

Result: 完成了 Game Arena 基础设施和三个试点游戏环境的搭建；针对每个游戏给出环境说明、评估指标及跨模型完整竞赛结果；通过大规模基于真实对局的评估支持可复现、透明和可泛化的评测。

Conclusion: Game Arena 能避免静态基准的性能饱和问题，系统研究模型的策略、适应与不确定性鲁棒性，并可扩展至新游戏和变体，提供可复现、透明且通用的 LLM 评估框架。

Abstract: We introduce Kaggle Game Arena, an open and ever-expanding platform to evaluate large language models (LLMs) through competitive games. Different from static benchmarks, game arena enables models to play head-to-head matchups in structured environments where the gameplay strength naturally increases as models evolve, preventing performance saturation. This technical report details the infrastructure behind Game Arena and describes the three pilot game environments: Chess, Poker, and Werewolf. These environments span perfect information, imperfect information, and multiplayer game settings, enabling a systematic study of models' strategic planning, adaptation, and robustness under uncertainty. For each game, we provide a detailed description of the environment, evaluation metrics, and results from running full competitions across models. Through robust infrastructure and large-scale ground-truth based evaluation, Game Arena ensures reproducibility, transparency and generalizability to new games and variants over time.

</details>


### [83] [Prompt Minimization: Reducing Input Redundancy Without Sacrificing Output Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31505)
*Marius F. R. Juston,Kevin A. Karim,Jonathan Gao,Kevin C. Li,Rudhi Bashambu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨提示最小化，即在保持输出效果的前提下，将提示压缩为最小且信息密度最高的形式，并提出三种框架来识别和评估最小提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的提示设计仍主要依赖启发式且临时性的方法；更短的提示可降低计算开销和推理延迟，尤其当提示中包含不必要的大段上下文时；更长提示还可能损害模型的推理与准确性。此外，多个提示可产生等效输出，说明输入空间存在高度冗余，因此需要研究哪些信息对激发特定模型行为是必要的。

Method: 提出三种变体框架，用于识别和评估最小提示，并比较最小提示与更长提示的输出效果。

Result: 最小提示通常能产生与更长对应提示相当或可比的输出。

Conclusion: 这些发现为高效提示工程提供了新方向，并加深了对大语言模型输入压缩的理解。

Abstract: Despite the growing capabilities of large language models (LLMs), prompt design remains largely heuristic and ad hoc. This project will explore $\textit{prompt minimization}$, the process of reducing prompts to their smallest, most information-dense form while preserving output fidelity. Practically, shorter prompts reduce computational overhead and inference latency, especially when large contexts, such as entire documents or codebases, are included unnecessarily. Further, longer prompts can damage LLM reasoning and accuracy. Theoretically, the existence of multiple prompts yielding equivalent outputs suggests a high degree of redundancy in the input space, raising fundamental questions about what information is essential to elicit specific model behaviors. We propose three variant frameworks to identify and evaluate minimal prompts and demonstrate that minimal prompts often produce outputs comparable to those of their longer counterparts. These findings suggest new directions for efficient prompt engineering and deepen our understanding of input compression in LLMs.

</details>


### [84] ["AI is (not) the new...": A Diagnostic Analogy Framework for Generative AI's Cultural Impacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31482)
*Rida Qadri,Vinodkumar Prabhakaran,Remi Denton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个诊断框架，用于分析生成式AI如何改变认知与文化实践，通过三个坐标分解干预，并区分结构性文化后果与偶然性后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学者和政策制定者常用印刷机、蒸汽机、电力等历史类比来理解生成式AI，但这些类比往往不精确地说明技术哪一属性被类比，可能导致针对错误系统属性的治理干预。

Method: 将每种干预分解为三个坐标：技术作用的认知场所、组织其对象的治理逻辑、实现该逻辑的技术机制；并将该框架应用于信息发现与知识综合。

Result: 框架揭示从索引性到推断、从编辑权威到统计共识的转变，产生具体且可追溯的文化效应，并显示整体类比所掩盖的治理杠杆；同时可区分由机制本身导致的结构性文化后果与仍可由设计和制度选择决定的偶然后果。

Conclusion: 要精确诊断生成式AI的认知与文化影响，需要按属性与机制分解分析，而非依赖模糊历史类比，从而为更精准的治理提供依据。

Abstract: Generative AI is reshaping the cultural infrastructures through which knowledge is found, synthesized, and held accountable. To make sense of this shift, scholars and policymakers reach for historical analogies of technologies such as the printing press, steam power or electricity. But these comparisons are typically imprecise about which property of the technology carries the comparison, and imprecise analogies produce imprecise governance by designing interventions against the wrong property of the system. This paper offers a diagnostic framework for analyzing how generative AI can transform epistemic and cultural practice. This paper offers a diagnostic framework for analyzing how generative AI can transform epistemic and cultural practice. We decompose each intervention into three coordinates: the epistemic site at which a technology acts, the governing logic by which it organizes its object, and the technical mechanism through which the logic is instantiated. This framework allows us to distinguish between structural cultural consequences, which follow from the mechanism itself, from contingent ones, which remain open to design and institutional choice. Applying the framework to information discovery and knowledge synthesis, we show how the shift from indexicality to inference and from editorial authority to statistical consensus produces specific, traceable cultural effects and reveals governance levers that gestalt analogy obscures.

</details>


### [85] [A Flow Matching Framework for Neural Representational Dissimilarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31544)
*Zeyuan Ye,Xue-Xin Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出用深度生成模型中的流匹配框架统一神经表征差异度量，将多种距离视为不同速度约束下的 Jeffreys 散度，并用于估计与设计新度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经表征差异度量对比较不同刺激、脑区、任务和模型下的神经编码很重要，但现有距离度量假设不同且估计方法各异，缺乏统一理解与原则化设计。

Method: 采用流匹配框架：将多种常用距离统一为不同速度约束下的 Jeffreys 散度，并用流匹配估计涉及复杂分布和连续变量的距离，进而指导新距离度量的设计。

Result: 多种距离度量可在流匹配框架下统一；流匹配在复杂分布和连续变量的距离估计上具有优势；该框架能够以原则化方式设计新的神经表征差异度量。

Conclusion: 流匹配为神经表征差异度量的理解、估计和设计提供了统一方法。

Abstract: Neural representational dissimilarity quantifies differences between neural response distributions, and is essential for comparing neural codes across stimuli, brain areas, tasks, and models. Commonly used distance metrics involve different assumptions and are estimated with separate methods. Here, we show that a variety of distance metrics can be unified under a flow matching framework developed in deep generative models. That is, these distances arise as Jeffreys divergences under different velocity constraints. We find that flow matching has advantages for estimating distances involving complicated distributions and continuous variables. Furthermore, this framework enables the design of new distance metrics in a principled way. Together, flow matching provides a unified approach for understanding, estimating, and designing neural representational dissimilarity metrics.

</details>


### [86] [UQ-LOB: Uncertainty-Aware Limit Order Book Mid-Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31491)
*Derrick Gilchrist Edward Manoharan,Eljas Linna,Kestutis Baltakys,Hao Dong,Juho Kanniainen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 UQ-LOB，一种轻量、编码器无关的不确定性量化模块，可附加到任意预训练 LOB 编码器上，为短期中间价预测输出校准概率与标量置信度，从而支持选择性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度 LOB 预测器多为点预测，只输出方向或位移，却不说明哪些预测可信；算法交易需要可靠的不确定性估计，以过滤低质量信号。

Method: 受 attentive neural processes 启发，将每个预测条件于最近已完成窗口的上下文集。UQ-regression 输出未来 tick 位移的校准高斯分布；UQ-classification 输出下跌/上涨/平稳的类别分布；两者均提供标量置信度（预测信噪比或类别概率）。

Result: 在 7 种加密货币、52 亿 LOB 事件及 5、10、15 秒预测期限上，UQ-regression 达到接近名义的 68% 区间覆盖率；仅保留最自信 10% 预测时，方向 macro F1 提升 0.11-0.15（UQ-regression）和 0.05-0.11（UQ-classification）。5 秒期限大波动上，最紧置信层方向 F1 达 0.88（下跌）和 0.83（上涨）。

Conclusion: UQ-LOB 能为 LOB 预测提供有效校准和置信度，显著改善选择性预测表现，尤其在高置信、大波动场景下具有实用价值。

Abstract: Forecasting short-horizon mid-price movements from limit order book (LOB) data is central to algorithmic trading, yet most deep LOB forecasters are point predictors: they output a direction or a displacement, but never indicate which of their forecasts can be trusted. We introduce UQ-LOB, a lightweight, encoder-agnostic uncertainty quantification module that attaches to any pretrained LOB encoder and, in the spirit of attentive neural processes, conditions each forecast on a context set of recently completed windows whose outcomes are already realised. The UQ-regression variant outputs a calibrated Gaussian over the future tick displacement, while the UQ-classification variant outputs a categorical distribution over down/up/stationary. Both expose a scalar confidence (predicted signal-to-noise ratio or class probability) that supports selective prediction. On 5.2 billion LOB events across seven cryptocurrency assets and horizons of 5, 10 and 15 seconds, UQ-regression attains near-nominal 68% interval coverage, and restricting to the most confident 10% of predictions raises directional macro F1 by 0.11-0.15 for UQ-regression and 0.05-0.11 for UQ-classification, at every horizon. On large, economically meaningful moves, the tightest confidence tier reaches a directional F1 of 0.88 (down) and 0.83 (up) at the 5-second horizon.

</details>


### [87] [DeepEdu-v1: Efficient and Scalable Agentic LLMs for Vietnamese Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31568)
*Quang Nguyen,Hieu Nguyen,Hien Hoang,Toan Pham,Cong Tran,Nam Vu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepEdu-v1，一个基于SCALE框架的越南教育AI辅导系统，通过长上下文推理引擎和自改进智能体层，在消费级GPU上实现更快响应、更低内存占用和更高准确性，同时保障数据主权并减少对西方中心知识的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越南等发展中地区急需AI辅导提升学习效果，但云助手（如ChatGPT）违反数据主权法律且知识偏离本地教材，自托管开源模型则面临长上下文导致的内存溢出、响应慢以及地区性内容幻觉问题。

Method: 基于SCALE框架：1）长上下文推理引擎，将token选择从每子块粒度摊销到每簇粒度，减少检索调用；2）自改进智能体层，从历史交互中持续整理验证过的playbook，而非微调模型。

Result: 长上下文检索调用比最先进选择性注意力基线少7.7倍，首token延迟（TTFT）降低约35%，任务准确率持平或提升；部署配置下，TTFT比标准vLLM服务快近2倍，复杂任务智能体准确率从70.0%提升至79.5%，在金融推理和交互式智能体基准上提升最大。

Conclusion: DeepEdu-v1在消费级GPU上实现了高效、准确且符合数据主权要求的AI辅导，通过自改进机制逐步减少对主导语言先验的依赖，为发展中地区本地化教育AI提供了可行方案。

Abstract: AI tutoring could markedly improve learning outcomes for students in developing regions such as Vietnam, yet the two obvious paths both fall short. Cloud assistants such as ChatGPT route sensitive student data to foreign servers---violating data-sovereignty laws such as Vietnam's Decree 53---and, pre-trained on Western-centric corpora, are not organized around the national textbook curriculum, so their knowledge of local content is unsystematic and frequently hallucinated. Self-hosting an open model keeps data on-premise but hits a two-fold wall: post-training quantization (AWQ, GPTQ) tames the static weight footprint, yet the dynamic KV cache and prefill latency of long tutoring contexts still cause out-of-memory failures and slow responses on consumer GPUs, while the model keeps hallucinating on region-specific material. We present DeepEdu-v1, an AI-tutoring system for Vietnamese education built on SCALE (Self-improving Context-Aware Learning Engine), a framework with two innovations. First, a long-context inference engine amortizes token selection from per-sub-chunk to per-cluster granularity; on long-context retrieval it issues x7.7 fewer retrieval calls than a state-of-the-art selective-attention baseline, cutting prefill latency (TTFT) by roughly 35% while matching or improving task accuracy. Second, a self-improving agentic layer continuously curates a verified playbook from past interactions instead of fine-tuning, a design intended to progressively reduce reliance on dominant-language priors as trustworthy local knowledge accumulates. In its deployed configuration, DeepEdu achieves a nearly x2 TTFT speedup over standard vLLM serving and lifts agentic accuracy from 70.0% to 79.5% on complex tasks, with the strongest per-track gains across financial-reasoning and interactive-agent benchmarks.

</details>


### [88] [Multi-agent Scaling Across Disjunctive and Compensatory Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31563)
*Carolina Fortuna,Blaz Bertalanic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体LLM系统随团队规模扩大时的性能变化，发现其 scaling 行为强烈依赖任务结构，并受成员输出聚合机制影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统常被期望通过增加团队规模来提升性能，但实际效果可能取决于任务类型。作者希望系统分析不同任务结构下团队扩展的收益与局限。

Method: 引入Steiner群体任务分类法作为分析框架，重点研究分离型（disjunctive）和补偿型（compensatory）任务；将独立采样的智能体建模为给定项目下的条件独立，推导大团队极限；在多个基准、13个开放权重模型、最多30个智能体团队上，比较多数投票、平均聚合、多轮修订以及模型家族组合等机制。

Result: 在分离型任务上，至少一个智能体正确的概率随团队规模增加5-20个百分点，但多数投票几乎无法实现这一潜力，平均仅差0.5个百分点以内；多轮修订能显著提升准确率，但1个同伴与29个同伴的增益几乎相同。在Fermi估计这类补偿型任务上，扩展收益很小：模型样本间共享的项目级偏差解释了约87%的平方误差，平均聚合仅降低约6%误差；组合模型家族有助于Fermi估计，但在分离型任务上未能超过最强单个成员。

Conclusion: 任务结构以及组合成员输出的机制，是决定多智能体LLM团队扩展效果的根本因素；单纯增加团队规模并不必然带来相应性能提升。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems are often expected to improve as team size increases, yet the scaling behavior may depend on task structure. Our central contribution is to introduce Steiner's taxonomy of group tasks as a framework for analyzing multi-agent LLM scaling and focusing the analysis on disjunctive and compensatory tasks. We model independently sampled agents as conditionally independent given the item, which yields their large-team limits: plurality voting converges to the model's modal answer, and averaging converges to the model's item-level bias. Across selected representative benchmarks, 13 open-weight models, and teams of up to 30 agents, we find qualitatively different scaling behavior. On disjunctive tasks, the probability that at least one agent is correct grows by 5-20 points with team size, but plurality voting over agents that answer directly realises almost none of this potential, as the model predicts to within 0.5 points on average. Multi-round revision raises accuracy considerably, yet the gain is nearly the same with one peer as with 29. In contrast, scaling provides little benefit on Fermi estimation, despite its natural suitability for aggregation: item-level biases shared across the samples of a model account for about 87% of the squared error, so averaging reduces error by only about 6%. Combining model families helps on Fermi estimation but does not surpass the strongest member on disjunctive tasks. These results show that task structure, together with the mechanism combining member outputs, is a fundamental determinant of team scaling.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [AlphaEarth distinguishes cities but compresses urban variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30356)
*Andrew Renninger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文审计以AlphaEarth为代表的卫星基础模型对城市表征的几何与地理特性，发现其可支持跨区域比较，但年度图层差异尚未能可靠用于时间比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市在建成形态、土地覆盖和开发历史上差异很大，使跨地点、跨时间比较困难；现有卫星基础模型虽提供统一数值表征，但其训练任务和目标通常不聚焦城市，缺乏全球一致的城市功能标签，且土地覆盖/土地利用分类常将建成环境压缩为少数类别。

Method: 聚焦AlphaEarth，并推广至其他地球嵌入，对162个国家1000个城市区域的嵌入进行几何与地理探测；分析城市在超球面上的位置、洲际与气候对城市中心平均方向变异的解释力、城市化程度对城市内部变异的贡献、残余变异的方向结构，以及国家发展水平与嵌入离散度的关系；还考察年度表征变化与像素重绘、Sentinel-1B失效的关系。

Result: 城市位于超球面上偏移但重叠的区域，距全局平均方向62.7°；洲际和气候可解释排除国家后城市中心平均方向变异的24.3%。城市内部，城市化程度携带8.9%的变异，残余变异共享方向但局部取向变化，不存在单一普适城市化轴。残余变异不均：国家发展水平每增加1个标准差，城市中心内离散度高14.1%，调整人口、面积和洲先后仍成立。进一步控制表明，发展中国家城市植被与纹理对比度较低，离散度随该对比度变化，完全调整后梯度最多剩6.4%。年度上，城市表征移动量约为像素重绘解释量的近8倍，并在2022年Sentinel-1B丢失一个过境方向处收缩。

Conclusion: AlphaEarth表征支持跨区域比较，但其年度图层之间的差异尚未被验证可用于跨时间比较。

Abstract: Cities differ in built form, land cover and development history, complicating comparison across places and time. Satellite foundation models map Earth's surface onto common numerical representations. Yet the tasks and targets used to shape them typically do not focus on cities: globally consistent labels for urban function do not exist, and many datasets - especially land cover and land use classifications - collapse the built environment into few classes. Here we audit the representation, focusing on AlphaEarth but with broader applicability to other Earth embeddings, by probing the geometry and geography of embeddings for 1,000 urban areas in 162 countries. We find that cities occupy a shifted but overlapping region on the hypersphere, 62.7° from the global mean direction, and continent and climate predict 24.3% of variation among the mean directions of urban centres in excluded countries. Inside cities, degrees of urbanisation carry 8.9% of the variation, and what they leave holds shared directions whose local orientation varies, not one universal axis of urbanisation. Retained variation is itself unequal: dispersion within urban centres is 14.1% greater per standard deviation of national development, even after adjusting for population, land area and continent. Further controls suggest cities in developing countries present less contrast in vegetation and texture, and dispersion follows that contrast: full adjustment for it leaves at most 6.4% of the gradient. Annually, a city's representation moves nearly eight times more than redrawing its own pixels explains, and contracts where the 2022 loss of Sentinel-1B removed a pass direction. AlphaEarth's representations therefore support comparison across regions, while the differences between its annual layers are not yet validated for comparison over time.

</details>


### [90] [LiTe-GS: Oracle-Efficient Next Best View Selection for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30393)
*Vivek Pandey,Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiTe-GS 是一种用于 3D Gaussian Splatting 中下一最佳视角选择的 oracle 高效方法，通过随机子集评估候选视角，显著减少信息增益 oracle 调用次数，同时保持重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 3D Gaussian Splatting 中，选择信息量高的相机视角对高效训练和自适应细化至关重要，但现有基于信息增益的视角选择策略在候选视角增多时需反复评估昂贵的 oracle，计算成本高。

Method: 提出 LiTe-GS，对候选视角进行随机子集评估而非全量穷举评分；在理论上实现期望 O(M log(1/ε)) 的 oracle 复杂度，其中 M 为候选视角数，且与选择基数 K 无关，并通过 ε 显式权衡 oracle 效率与近似质量。

Result: 在 Blender 和 Mip-NeRF 360 数据集上的实验表明，LiTe-GS 能保持与基于 Fisher 信息的基线相当的重建质量，同时在多种采集设置下大幅减少 Fisher-oracle 评估次数。

Conclusion: LiTe-GS 为 3D Gaussian Splatting 的下一最佳视角选择提供了一种高效且可理论保证的 oracle 节约方案，在精度与计算效率之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Selecting informative camera views is critical for efficient training and adaptive refinement in 3D Gaussian Splatting, where each observation significantly influences model parameters. However, information-driven view-selection strategies can require repeated evaluations of expensive information-gain oracles as the number of candidate views increases. We propose LiTe-GS, an oracle-efficient method for next best view selection in 3D Gaussian Splatting. LiTe-GS reduces the number of information-oracle evaluations by performing randomized subset evaluation of candidate views rather than exhaustively scoring the full candidate pool. The resulting approach achieves expected $O(M\log(1/ε))$ oracle complexity with respect to the number of candidate views $M$, independent of the selection cardinality $K$, while providing an explicit trade-off between oracle efficiency and approximation quality through $ε$. We provide theoretical guarantees on oracle complexity and approximation performance under the proposed selection scheme. Experiments on Blender and Mip-NeRF 360 demonstrate that LiTe-GS maintains reconstruction quality comparable to Fisher-information-based baselines while substantially reducing the number of Fisher-oracle evaluations across different acquisition settings.

</details>


### [91] [CSCWD: Cross-Scale Channel-wise Knowledge Distillation for Lightweight Tiny Object Detection on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30395)
*Amir Zamani,Zeinab Ghasemi-Naraghi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨尺度通道知识蒸馏（CSCWD），将高分辨率空间表征从YOLO11m-P2教师迁移到轻量YOLO11n学生，在不改变推理结构下提升航拍微小目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航拍图像中微小目标空间证据弱，轻量检测器丢失高分辨率细节，实时检测困难；需低延迟、计算高效的检测方案。

Method: 训练时框架CSCWD，特征对齐后教师P2监督学生P3，深层保持同尺度蒸馏；在Drone-vs-Bird七序列协议验证，并在DUT-Anti-UAV零样本测试；部署于Raspberry Pi 5 NCNN-FP16。

Result: YOLO11n-CSCWD达50.17% mAP@0.5、59.73%召回，比CA-YOLO11n基线提升2.92和3.55个百分点；跨尺度对齐比同尺度通道蒸馏提升2.09点；零样本DUT-Anti-UAV mAP@0.5从48.29%到50.06%；树莓派5上2.58M参数，50.32% mAP@0.5，82.32 ms，12.15 FPS，运行时和内存与基线相当。

Conclusion: 跨尺度蒸馏能在不增加推理复杂度下提升微小目标检测性能。

Abstract: Real-time tiny object detection in aerial imagery is constrained by the weak spatial evidence of very small objects and the loss of high-resolution detail in lightweight detectors. This study presents Cross-Scale Channel-wise Knowledge Distillation (CSCWD), a training-time framework that transfers high-resolution spatial representations from a YOLO11m-P2 teacher to a compact YOLO11n student without altering the student's inference architecture. Unlike conventional same-scale feature distillation, CSCWD transfers supervision from teacher P2 to student P3 after feature alignment while retaining same-scale distillation at deeper pyramid levels. Under the unified seven-sequence Drone-vs-Bird validation protocol, YOLO11n-CSCWD achieves 50.17% mean average precision at an intersection-over-union threshold of 0.5 (mAP@0.5) and 59.73% recall, improving the matched CA-YOLO11n baseline by 2.92 percentage points in mAP@0.5 and 3.55 points in recall. Cross-scale alignment further increases mAP@0.5 by 2.09 points over the corresponding same-scale channel-wise distillation configuration. In zero-shot evaluation on DUT-Anti-UAV, mAP@0.5 increases from 48.29% to 50.06% without target-domain fine-tuning. This domain was included because its challenging small targets make low-latency, computationally efficient detection particularly relevant. On Raspberry Pi 5 using NCNN-FP16 at 640x640 resolution, the 2.58-million-parameter student achieves 50.32% mAP@0.5 at 82.32 ms mean wall-clock latency, or 12.15 frames per second, while retaining essentially the same runtime and memory requirements as the matched baseline. The results support cross-scale distillation for improving tiny-target detection without increasing inference-time model complexity.

</details>


### [92] [What Improves Multimodal Misinformation Detection? Answers from a Large-Scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30402)
*Akshit Sharma,Prashant W. Patil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对多模态虚假信息检测中的设计选择进行大规模研究，在三个基准数据集和多种预训练视觉与语言骨干上开展超过3375次实验，系统比较并分析鲁棒性，回答4个关键研究问题，为设计更可靠检测系统提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态虚假信息 increasingly 通过文本主张与看似证明的图像配对来增强说服力，而构建有效检测器常依赖少数未经受控检验的设计选择，因此需要系统研究这些选择的影响。

Method: 在三个基准数据集和广泛预训练视觉、语言骨干模型上开展超过3375次实验，进行系统比较和针对性鲁棒性分析，围绕4个研究问题评估多模态检测流程中的关键设计选择。

Result: 提炼出哪些设计选择有帮助、何时会静默失败，以及流程中哪些方面最强烈影响模型行为，并回答了4个关键研究问题。

Conclusion: 研究为设计更强、更可靠的多模态虚假信息检测系统提供了可靠基础，并对更广泛的研究社区有所贡献。

Abstract: Multimodal misinformation is increasingly crafted to look convincing by pairing a textual claim with an image that appears to "prove" it. Yet in practice, building effective detectors often hinges on a small set of design choices that are rarely examined in a controlled way. In this paper, we conduct a large-scale study of multimodal design choices for misinformation detection with over 3,375 experiments- spanning three benchmark datasets and a broad range of pre-trained vision and language backbones. Through systematic comparisons and targeted robustness analyses, we distill practical guidance on which design choices help, when do they fail silently, and what aspects of the pipeline most strongly shape model behavior, answering 4 key Research Questions (RQs). We aim to provide a reliable foundation for designing stronger and more dependable multimodal misinformation detection systems, thus contributing to the broader research community.

</details>


### [93] [ProCAP: Probabilistic Cross-Attentive Prompt Learning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30434)
*Hiwa Azeez Abbas,Fatemeh Daneshfar,Moloud Abdar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ProCAP，一种概率化交叉注意力提示学习框架，在不更新CLIP权重的前提下，通过学习视觉与文本提示token并利用双向多头交叉注意力增强跨模态交互，结合高斯参数化与正则化、对称InfoNCE对齐，提升少样本与分布偏移场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉语言模型（如CLIP）虽可通过提示识别新类别，但在标注数据稀缺或测试分布偏移时表现不佳；现有提示学习方法仅调整少量参数且视觉与文本分支耦合较弱，在少样本场景下容易脆弱、不稳定。

Method: ProCAP学习视觉和文本提示token，通过堆叠的双向多头交叉注意力在提示深度上让两分支互相细化；为缓解过拟合，将提示token参数化为高斯均值与方差，并加入轻量KL和L2正则；此外增加紧凑的对称InfoNCE头，在共享低维空间中对齐交叉注意力后的图像特征与类别级文本表示；全程冻结CLIP主干。

Result: 在11个数据集上的少样本基类到新类泛化、跨数据集迁移以及ImageNet分布偏移基准的域泛化实验中，ProCAP取得了较强的基类到新类聚合性能和具有竞争力的迁移性能，同时保持CLIP主干不变。

Conclusion: ProCAP通过增强跨模态交互和训练稳定性，在不更新CLIP权重的情况下有效提升了少样本与分布偏移场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Pre-trained vision-language models such as CLIP can recognize new categories via prompting, but they often struggle when labeled data are scarce or the test distribution shifts. Prompt learning adapts only a small set of parameters while keeping the backbone frozen, yet many existing multimodal prompt learners couple the visual and textual branches weakly and can be brittle in low-shot regimes. We propose ProCAP, a probabilistic cross-attentive prompt learning framework that improves cross-modal interaction and training stability without updating any CLIP weights: it learns both visual and textual prompt tokens and links them through stacked bidirectional multi-head cross-attention so the two branches refine each other across prompt depth. To reduce overfitting under limited supervision, we parameterize prompt tokens with Gaussian means and variances and regularize them with lightweight KL and L2 penalties, and we further add a compact symmetric InfoNCE head that aligns cross-attended image features with class-level text representations in a shared low-dimensional space. Across few-shot base-to-novel generalization on 11 datasets, cross-dataset transfer, and domain generalization on ImageNet shift benchmarks, ProCAP achieves strong aggregate base-to-novel performance and competitive transfer performance while keeping the CLIP backbone unchanged.

</details>


### [94] [LensDesigner: A Self-Improving Agent for Optical Lens Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30450)
*Lei Sun,Haoran Liang,Dannong Xu,Yao Gao,Yuyu Geng,Jinjin Gu,Kaiwei Wang,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LensDesigner自主智能体框架，通过光学感知检索、物理仿真交互和课程引导的自演化机制，解决光学镜头设计中的非凸优化难题，并在LensArena基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学镜头设计是复杂的非凸优化问题，严重依赖人类经验与直觉；现有基于优化的自动设计方法需精细手动调参，难以在庞大参数空间中有效搜索，且面临冷启动问题。

Method: 构建LensLib100K光学镜头库，采用Optics-Aware Retrieval提供物理有效的结构种子；在交互式物理仿真环境中，智能体执行宏观编排并接收即时光学反馈；引入课程智能体引导的持续自演化机制，通过逐步增加难度的设计任务迭代，自主提取、积累和复用设计启发式知识。

Result: 在包含120个多样化及极端配置设计任务的LensArena标准化评测基准上，LensDesigner显著优于公开基线算法，实现了更高的成功率和优化效率。

Conclusion: 该工作为智能光学这一新兴领域提供了新思路，代码将公开。

Abstract: Optical lens design is a complex, non-convex optimization challenge that relies heavily on human experience and intuition. Existing optimized-based automatic lens design methods struggle to navigate this vast parameter space without meticulous manual tuning. In this paper, we present LensDesigner, an autonomous agent framework that mirrors the problem-solving workflow of expert opticians. To overcome the initial cold start problem, we construct LensLib100K, an extensive optical lens library, and employ Optics-Aware Retrieval to supply physically valid structural seeds. Within an interactive physical simulation environment, the agent executes macroscopic orchestration while receiving immediate optical feedback. Furthermore, we introduce a continuous self-evolving mechanism guided by a curriculum agent. By iteratively solving design tasks with progressively increasing difficulty, the agent autonomously extracts, accumulates, and reuses design heuristics, effectively evolving its optical lens design expertise over time. At the evaluation level, we introduce LensArena, a standardized evaluation benchmark comprising $120$ diverse optical design tasks, covering extreme configurations. Extensive experiments on this benchmark demonstrate that LensDesigner significantly outperforms publicly available baseline algorithms, achieving superior success rates and optimization efficiency. We hope this work sheds light on the emerging field of intelligent optics. The code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [95] [The Shape of Events: Edge-Based Inductive Biases via Cross-Domain Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30478)
*Soshun Kihara,Shunsuke Yasuki,Masato Taki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过从事件域到RGB域的知识蒸馏，系统研究了事件相机数据为视觉模型带来的归纳偏置，发现其能诱导颜色不变性、形状偏置和高频噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ImageNet上训练的卷积神经网络偏好局部高频纹理，导致对现实环境中的分布偏移鲁棒性脆弱。事件相机仅记录场景亮度变化，擅长捕捉轮廓信息，但事件域缺乏诊断基准，其归纳偏置尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用从事件域到RGB域的知识蒸馏，借助RGB域丰富的评估工具包，系统剖析事件数据诱导的归纳偏置。通过分析早期层颜色和空间信息处理的变化，以及频谱权衡，验证其机制假设。

Result: 蒸馏使RGB域模型获得颜色不变性、形状偏置和对高频噪声的鲁棒性。机制在于模型抑制对高频纹理的依赖，转而更强依赖基于边缘的物体形状。频谱权衡表现为：对高频成分缺失鲁棒，但对依赖频段的污染和几何结构破坏脆弱。该归纳偏置不同于现有鲁棒化方法，并可作为形状和轮廓信息相关下游任务的有用先验。

Conclusion: 从事件域蒸馏到RGB域所诱导的归纳偏置与现有鲁棒化方法不同，能作为有用先验用于多种下游任务，其中形状和轮廓信息与其他线索共同发挥作用。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks trained on ImageNet are known to exhibit a strong preference for local high-frequency texture, an inductive bias that translates into fragile robustness against distribution shifts in real-world environments. Event cameras, in contrast, record only changes in scene brightness and are therefore well suited to capturing contour information; however, due to the absence of diagnostic benchmarks in the event domain, the inductive bias that event-camera data instills in vision models has remained underexplored. In this work, we use knowledge distillation from the event domain to the RGB domain so as to exploit the rich evaluation toolkit available in the RGB domain and systematically dissect this inductive bias. Our experiments show that distillation from the event domain induces, in the RGB domain, color invariance, shape bias, and robustness to high-frequency noise. We identify the underlying mechanism as the model suppressing its dependence on high-frequency texture while acquiring a stronger dependence on edge-based object shape. This hypothesis is supported by changes in how color and spatial information are processed at the early layers, together with a spectral trade-off in which robustness to the absence of high-frequency components coexists with vulnerability to contamination of the relied-upon frequency bands and to disruption of geometric structure. We further show that this inductive bias differs from existing robustification methods and that it functions as a useful prior for diverse downstream tasks in which shape and contour information contribute alongside other cues. The code is available at https://github.com/snskysk/event2rgb-distillation .

</details>


### [96] [Learning Polarization Image Restoration with General Restoration Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30728)
*Chenggong Li,Jinhao Liu,Caiyun Wu,Yidong Luo,Junchao Zhang,Degui Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向多种及复合退化的全合一偏振图像复原框架，通过归一化Stokes表示分离强度与偏振信息，并采用双分支架构与跨域知识蒸馏实现偏振复原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中的偏振图像采集常受多种耦合退化影响，而现有方法多针对特定退化，且偏振数据规模和质量有限，难以支撑实用偏振视觉中的图像复原。

Method: 研究不同偏振表示对复原性能的影响，选择归一化Stokes表示；设计强度与偏振双分支架构。强度分支利用预训练通用复原先验和混合专家扩展处理复合退化，并通过跨域特征变换将复原知识自适应蒸馏到对称的偏振分支；同时构建复合退化偏振基准。

Result: 在公开数据集和所提基准上的大量实验验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的全合一偏振复原框架能够有效应对多样和复合退化，为偏振视觉中的实用图像复原提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Polarization imaging captures distinctive surface and geometric cues that benefit a wide range of vision tasks. However, real-world polarization acquisition is often affected by multiple coupled degradations, making image restoration essential for practical polarization vision. Existing methods are largely tailored to specific degradations and remain constrained by the limited scale and quality of polarization data. To address these limitations, we develop an all-in-one polarization restoration framework for diverse and composite degradations. We first study the impact of different polarization representations on restoration performance and identify the normalized Stokes representation as an effective choice for separating intensity and polarization information. Accordingly, we devise a dual-branch architecture that separates intensity and polarization modeling. To overcome the limitations of polarization-specific training, the intensity branch leverages pretrained general restoration priors and a mixture-of-experts extension for composite degradations, while its restoration knowledge is adaptively distilled into the symmetric polarization branch via a cross-domain feature transform. In addition, we establish a composite-degradation polarization benchmark to support all-in-one restoration research. Extensive experiments on public datasets and our proposed benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [97] [Atlases Are Already Inside: Recovering Population Templates from Pretrained Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30566)
*Jian Shi,John Femiani,Peter Wonka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种扩散模型推理时采样器，无需重新训练即可从预训练模型中生成人群的“内在图谱”，并在多种数据集上验证其作为配准模板的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人群图谱构建通常依赖配准和额外训练，流程复杂且成本高。作者希望利用扩散模型已学习到的人群结构，把图谱构建转化为生成模型的自然副产品。

Method: 设计新的推理时采样器，使任意随机种子收敛到人群中心解剖结构，即内在图谱；无需重新训练、无需可变形配准，单次推理即可获得图谱，并支持多领域和年龄等子人群条件生成。

Result: 方法适用于脑MRI、胸部X光、人脸和3D形状；年龄条件模型可生成训练范围内任意年龄图谱，并再现健康衰老中的脑脊液扩张。作为配准目标，内在图谱在每个数据集上达到最佳或次佳，并在留出脑MRI队列中是最中心模板。

Conclusion: 图谱构建可被重新定义为生成建模的副产品：扩散模型是人群结构的学习表示，而图谱正是其已经包含的内容。

Abstract: We present a new inference-time sampler for diffusion models that gives a pretrained model a capability it was never trained for: constructing the atlas of the population it synthesizes. The sampler converges from every random seed to the population's central anatomy, which we call the \emph{intrinsic atlas}. The advantage is threefold. (1) It requires no retraining. A diffusion model that has already learned a coherent population, including the released ones, yields its atlas in a single inference pass without involving deformable registration. (2) It applies to multiple domains, such as brain MRI, chest X-ray, faces, and 3D shapes. (3) It extends to subpopulations. One age-conditioned model gives an atlas at any age in its training range, and the resulting family reproduces the CSF expansion of healthy aging. Evaluated as a registration target, the intrinsic atlas is best or second-best on every dataset against classical and learned templates, and the most central template on held-out brain MRI cohorts. Atlas construction can be reframed as a byproduct of generative modeling: a diffusion model is a learned representation of population structure, and the atlas is what it already contains.

</details>


### [98] [Action Forcing: Training World Models on Unsupervised Video by Recovering Underlying Egomotion Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30595)
*Ashish Sundar,Tiankuo Hou,Zhong Fan,Chunbo Luo,Xiaoyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出从无标注普通视频中恢复由数据驱动的自运动基，从而获得有物理依据的油门-偏航控制信号，并用其训练可控世界模型；同时提出新的无参考评估指标，揭示传统视频生成指标忽略的失控问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控世界模型需要同步动作标注，但这类数据稀缺。现有方法依赖仪器化平台、昂贵人工标注或缺乏物理依据的潜在动作模型，因此作者希望直接从无标注视频中提取 grounded 控制信号。

Method: 通过跟踪帧间像素位移并利用自运动引起的重复相干结构，仅用 PCA 等简单方法恢复自运动基，得到有符号、可缩放、可组合的油门-偏航控制。为防止视频 DiT 利用像素级监督，使用在线 latent critic 蒸馏冻结的 decoder-tracker-PCA 教师，且不通过 decoder 或 tracker 反传。另提出无参考评估：可控性、合理性、凭空造物、几何完整性。

Result: 多数基线能沿常见动作方向运动，但难以反向或保持静止；该模型能处理这些情况并保持组合控制与生成质量。尽管反向动作不足训练数据 1%，模型仍学会反向、线性缩放响应，并将油门与转向组合。

Conclusion: 从无标注视频中恢复的 grounded 动作空间可有效训练可控世界模型，并支持组合控制；传统视频生成指标不足以评估世界模型，需引入能检测失控与几何崩坏的无参考指标。

Abstract: Synchronised action annotations are needed to train controllable world models and these datasets remain elusive. Existing approaches make use of instrumented platforms with calibrated sensors, costly manual annotation, or latent-action models which lack grounding. We instead turn ordinary unlabelled video into action-supervised training data by recovering (without training) a data-derived egomotion basis. We track pixel displacements across frames and exploit the recurring coherent structure induced by egomotion to obtain grounded control signals directly. Using a method as simple as principal components analysis perform this, we find that the leading components provide signed, scalable, and composable throttle--yaw controls, although the method can recover only motion axes represented in the data. To prevent a high-capacity video DiT from exploiting pixel-level supervision, an online latent critic distils a frozen decoder--tracker--PCA (Principal Components Analysis) teacher without backpropagating through the decoder or tracker. Finally we critique the use of video generation metrics to evaluate WMs and introduce an example of an alternative, reference-free evaluation method. We measure \textit{controllability}, \textit{plausibility}, \textit{conjuring} (creating objects out of thin air) and \textit{geometric integrity}, revealing failures that conventional video metrics miss. We show that most baselines follow familiar action directions but struggle to reverse or remain stationary. Our model handles both while retaining compositional control and generation quality. Despite backwards actions being less than $1\%$ of our training data, we find that the model learns to reverse, scale its response linearly, and compose throttle with steering, all simply by learning through a grounded action space.

</details>


### [99] [MVAgent: Multi-Agent Video Generation via Consistent Condition Construction and Shot-Level Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30609)
*Xiangyu Kong,Wenjie Zhou,Fengping Tian,Lihua Fang,Haoqin Sun,Chenyang Lyu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MVAgent，将多镜头智能体视频生成重新定义为条件构建问题，通过多智能体协作与 Trunk-GDPO 强化学习，在冻结生成器和评判器的情况下提升跨镜头一致性与叙事规划质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多镜头智能体视频生成需要角色外观一致、跨视角空间布局稳定以及镜头间角色状态连续，但每个镜头若单独请求冻结生成器，仅重复文本无法决定外观、布局或状态，因此需要显式构建条件。

Method: MVAgent 是一个多智能体流水线，智能体通过类型化条件输入协作：Spatial Grounding 从生成的相机运动片段中采样视角，并将镜头锚定到匹配构图；Observer 记录每个镜头结束状态到连续性记忆；Transition 为下一镜头构建角色动作与空间参考；Orchestrator 组合输入形成请求。由于请求效果渲染后才可见，采用 Trunk-GDPO 智能体强化学习，在每个镜头比较渲染候选并保留最佳作为主干。

Result: 在生成器与评判器冻结条件下，MVAgent 在 ViMax-Bench 上取得相比对比方法最高的跨镜头一致性和叙事规划质量，并在人类评估中优于最强智能体基线。

Conclusion: 将多镜头视频生成视为条件构建，并通过多智能体协作与逐镜头强化学习优化，可有效提升跨镜头一致性与叙事质量，且无需微调生成器。

Abstract: Multi-shot agentic video generation requires consistent character appearance, stable spatial layout across camera angles, and continuous character state between shots. When every shot is a separate request to a frozen generator, repeated text does not determine appearance, layout or state. We therefore recast the problem as condition construction and present MVAgent, a multi-agent pipeline whose agents collaborate through typed conditioning inputs. Because an environment image shows one viewpoint, a Spatial Grounding agent samples views from generated camera-traversal clips and anchors each shot to the view matching its framing. As generated shots drift from the plan, an Observer records how each shot ends in a continuity memory, from which a Transition agent builds character action and spatial references for the next shot. An Orchestrator composes these inputs into each request. Since a request reveals its effect only after rendering, we train it by agentic reinforcement learning with Trunk-GDPO, which compares rendered candidates at every shot rather than once per video and continues the best as the trunk. With generator and judges frozen, MVAgent attains the highest cross-shot consistency and narrative-planning quality among the compared methods on ViMax-Bench and is preferred over the strongest agentic baseline in human evaluation.

</details>


### [100] [MedTokenBudget: Lesion-Preserving Token Routing for Dermoscopic Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30613)
*Zhexiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MedTokenBudget，一种基于病灶掩码监督的后骨干 token 路由框架，用 LATS 模块在预算下选择病灶富集的 patch，并在 ISIC 2019 上优于 Random 和 ToMe。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤镜 Vision Transformer 通常均匀处理所有图像块，但诊断证据主要集中在病灶区域；现有 token 剪枝多依赖通用显著性/相似性，较少验证保留的子集是否仍包含病灶。

Method: 提出 Lesion-Aware Token Scoring（LATS），融合注意力熵、特征范数和局部特征对比度，通过可学习打分器在目标预算下路由 top-K patch；训练采用预算课程学习、多样性正则、注意力蒸馏和病灶掩码监督。

Result: 在 ISIC 2019 上，掩码监督的 LATS 在主要预算设置下持续优于 Random 和 ToMe，同时保留显著更多病灶 patch；使用病灶保留率直接衡量真实病灶证据在 token 预算下的留存。

Conclusion: 将病灶掩码监督引入 token 路由，可在有限 token 预算下构建紧凑且病灶富集的表示，并提供可复现代码与补充材料中的完整结果。

Abstract: Dermoscopy classifiers built on Vision Transformers process all image patches uniformly, although diagnostic evidence is concentrated in the lesion region. Existing token pruning methods reduce tokens using generic saliency or similarity signals, but rarely ask whether the retained subset still contains the lesion. This paper introduces MedTokenBudget, a supervised post-backbone token routing framework that learns to construct compact lesion-enriched representations when auxiliary lesion masks are available. Its Lesion-Aware Token Scoring (LATS) module fuses attention entropy, feature norm, and local feature contrast through a learned scorer, then routes the top-$K$ patches under a target budget. LATS is trained with budget curriculum learning, diversity regularization, attention distillation, and lesion-mask supervision. The trained router is evaluated with a lesion retention rate that directly measures how much ground-truth lesion evidence survives the token budget. On ISIC 2019, mask-supervised LATS consistently outperforms Random and ToMe at headline budgets while retaining substantially more lesion patches. Code is provided for reproducibility, and complete tabulated results are included in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [101] [StarWM: Self-Supervised Trained Attention Routing for Robust World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30667)
*Zeqiang Zhang,Fabian Wurzberger,Maximilian Otte,Daniel Schmid,Sebastian Gottwald,Arne Peter Raulf,Daniel Alexander Braun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StarWM，一种选择性重建的世界模型：利用自监督动力学交叉注意力决定重建区域，并通过双流解码器和停止梯度隔离重建与预测目标，从而在保留任务相关状态的同时丢弃干扰信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型需在忠实捕捉环境动力学与抽象掉无关内容之间取得平衡。基于重建的方法按像素面积分配表征容量，易被任务无关内容主导；无重建方法虽避免该偏差，却可能丢弃潜在相关信息。

Method: StarWM使用基于自监督动力学训练的交叉注意力模块判断何处进行重建，并用双流解码器将重建限制在注意力区域；通过停止梯度屏障防止重建目标与动力学预测目标相互干扰。

Result: 在DeepMind Control动态视频背景任务中，默认无奖励StarWM在随机帧干扰下表现最佳，在顺序视频下显著优于基于重建的基线；加入奖励后，其在顺序视频上匹配或超过无重建方法，并在所有干扰模式下取得最高总回报。机制探测表明，StarWM在长时程想象中近乎完美保留状态属性，同时系统性丢弃干扰因素。

Conclusion: 由动力学相关性引导的选择性重建能有效平衡忠实性与抽象性，提升世界模型在动态干扰环境中的鲁棒性和任务表现。

Abstract: A robust world model must strike the balance between faithfully capturing environmental dynamics and abstracting away from irrelevant content. While reconstruction-based world models ensure faithful supervision, they misallocate representational capacity by pixel area rather than dynamics relevance for visual tasks, which can cause task-irrelevant content to dominate the learned representation. Alternatively, reconstruction-free methods avoid this bias but risk discarding possibly relevant information. We propose StarWM, which uses a cross-attention module trained on self-supervised dynamics to decide where reconstruction applies. A dual-stream decoder then restricts reconstruction to the attended regions, with stop-gradient barriers preventing interference between the two objectives. These components allows reconstruction to supervise the visual content of attended regions without contaminating the latent with non-predictive information. On DeepMind Control with dynamic video backgrounds, default (reward-free) StarWM achieves the strongest performance under random-frame distractors and substantially outperforms reconstruction-based baselines under sequential video. In addition, its reward-augmented variant matches or exceeds reconstruction-free methods on sequential video, achieving the highest overall return across all distractor regimes. Mechanistic probing confirms StarWM preserves state attributes with near-perfect fidelity through long-horizon imagination while systematically discarding distractors.

</details>


### [102] [Conditional Predictive Sufficient Statistics for Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30647)
*Yuzhou Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将有用的视觉表示形式化为条件预测充分统计量（CPSS）：保留与未来共享的潜在因子并丢弃图像块私有噪声；用共享因子模型和余弦损失/ von Mises-Fisher 模型分析预测信息，并通过小型因果 Transformer 在 MNIST/CIFAR-10 上做诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有用的视觉表示应保留与未来共享的潜在因子、丢弃块私有噪声，但这一要求缺乏形式化，且 stop-gradient 等常见做法是否真能选出充分统计量尚不清楚。

Method: 定义 CPSS；在图像块共享因子模型下将过去与下一块之间的互信息与共享因子信息联系；用余弦损失预测下一块嵌入，作为 von Mises-Fisher 方向模型的极大似然与预测信息的可处理代理；分析 stop-gradient 仅阻断实现常数解的对称梯度；使用浅层嵌入回归目标；在 MNIST 和 CIFAR-10 上训练小型因果 Transformer 进行诊断。

Result: MNIST 上未来偏移和 stop-gradient 可使探针准确率变化数十个百分点，CPSS 读出在输出层之前达到峰值；CIFAR-10 短预算无增强时各目标都接近像素上的线性分类器；几何上 CPSS 输出比最佳中间块读出更差，下一像素回归无此惩罚，移除 stop-gradient 会使嵌入有效秩崩溃，即使 pretext loss 看似完美。

Conclusion: stop-gradient 本身不足以选出充分统计量；CPSS 更可能出现在中间块，输出层因浅层嵌入目标而受限。MNIST 诊断支持推导，但 CIFAR-10 结果有限，说明该方法/目标在复杂数据上仍难以显著超越像素基线。

Abstract: A useful visual representation is a statistic of the observed past that retains the latent factors shared with the future and discards patch-private noise. We formalize this requirement as a conditional predictive sufficient statistic (CPSS). Under a shared-factor model of image patches, the mutual information between the past and the next patch equals the information the past carries about the shared factor, up to a remainder that the next patch itself fails to reveal. Predicting the next patch embedding with a cosine loss is maximum likelihood for a von Mises-Fisher model of that embedding's direction, and is therefore a tractable surrogate for the predictive information. The same population loss is also minimized by a constant embedding, so stop-gradient does not by itself select the sufficient statistic; it only blocks the symmetric gradient that implements the constant solution in one step. The regression target is a shallow embedding, which forces the network output back into that shallow range and leaves the sufficient statistic in intermediate blocks. Small causal Transformers on MNIST and CIFAR-10 are used as diagnostics, not as a leaderboard. On MNIST the future shift and the stop-gradient move probe accuracy by tens of points, and the CPSS readout peaks before the output. On CIFAR-10, with the same short budget and no augmentation, every objective lands near a linear classifier on pixels. What still matches the derivation is the geometry: the CPSS output is a worse readout than its best intermediate block, next-pixel regression does not pay that penalty, and removing the stop-gradient collapses the effective rank of the embedding even when the pretext loss looks perfect.

</details>


### [103] [Structure-Guided Masked Autoencoders for Ultra-High Resolution Scientific Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30682)
*Enzhi Zhang,Du Wu,Rui Zhong,Cong Ma,Isaac Lyngaas,Amir Koushyar Ziabari,Xiao Wang,Peng Chen,Tao Luo,Toshio Endo,Fumiyoshi Shoji,Kento Sato,Kentaro Uesugi,Takayuki Nonoyama,Ryuji Kiyama,Masahiro Yoshida,Masaru Tezuka,Tetsuya Ishikawa,Satoshi Matsuoka,Masaharu Munetomo,Mohamed Wahib*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SGMA 是一种面向超高分辨率科学图像的结构引导掩码自编码框架，通过自适应四叉树分词和结构条件掩码，使 ViT/MAE 能高效处理千兆像素图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 MAE 难以用于千兆像素科学图像：随机掩码不适合科学数据中结构化、多尺度形态，均匀分词又会产生过长序列，使 O(N^2) 注意力不切实际。

Method: SGMA 包含两部分：内容自适应四叉树分词器，将千兆像素图像压缩为固定长度序列；结构条件掩码过程，将重建偏向空间信息丰富区域。并引入阻尼累积（DA），跨尺度聚合信号相关响应为结构画布以指导掩码。

Result: 在电子显微镜、全切片光学显微镜和 X 射线 CT 数据集上，SGMA 均优于 MAE。在 8K x 8K x 28K SpringXCT 上达到 95.68% Dice，比同架构 MAE 提升 13.00 个点；在 32K^2 WSI PAIP 上达到 83.21% Dice，提升 16.84 个点，并实现最高 24.8 倍推理加速。

Conclusion: SGMA 在保留细微微观结构的同时，与标准 ViT 编码器和 MAE 式重建兼容，为超高分辨率科学图像提供了有效且可扩展的自监督预训练方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised pre-training with Vision Transformers, including Masked Autoencoders (MAE), is difficult to apply to gigapixel scientific images. Random masking is poorly matched to the structured, multi-scale morphology of scientific data, while uniform tokenization produces prohibitively long sequences that make $O(N^2)$ attention impractical. We propose SGMA, a structure-guided masked autoencoding framework for ultra-high-resolution scientific images. SGMA couples two components: a content-adaptive quadtree tokenizer that compresses gigapixel images into a fixed-length sequence, and a structure-conditioned masking process that biases reconstruction toward spatially informative regions. To stabilize this process across scales, we introduce Damped Accumulation (DA), which aggregates signal-dependent responses across the tree into a structure canvas used to guide masking. The resulting pre-training task preserves fine microstructure while remaining compatible with standard ViT encoders and MAE-style reconstruction. Across electron microscopy, whole-slide optical microscopy, and X-ray CT datasets, SGMA consistently outperforms MAE baselines. It achieves 95.68% Dice on the 8K x 8K x 28K SpringXCT dataset, improving over the same-architecture MAE baseline by +13.00 points, and 83.21% Dice on the 32K^2 WSI PAIP dataset, improving by +16.84 points, while providing up to a 24.8x inference speedup.

</details>


### [104] [MM-VeriAgent: Learning to Use Extensive Tools to Verify Multimodal Misinformation with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30698)
*Peipei Li,Shuhan Xia,Shengyang Liu,Zekun Li,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出MM-VeriAgent，用于学习使用工具验证混合来源的多模态虚假信息。作者构建了专用工具包MM-VeriTools，并通过强化学习训练LVLM智能体调用工具；同时引入Tool-Execution Cache以提升训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的多模态虚假信息常涉及混合伪造来源，需要样本特定的检测策略。现有工具增强方法依赖预定义工作流或推理时规划，适应性有限或增加推理成本。

Method: 首先构建MM-VeriTools工具包，通过对文本、视觉和跨模态伪造分析子任务上的候选模型与方法进行基准测试，选择最强方法封装为统一接口的可调用工具；然后在工具包之上用强化学习训练LVLM智能体，使其学会使用工具解决混合来源检测；此外提出Tool-Execution Cache，预先执行候选工具调用并在训练中复用缓存输出，减少在线工具执行。

Result: 在MMFakeBench上的实验表明，该方法相较基础模型取得显著准确率提升，且推理时无需显式工具搜索。消融与效率分析验证了学到的工具使用策略，并显示Tool-Execution Cache减少了训练期间的在线工具执行次数。

Conclusion: MM-VeriAgent能够学习样本特定的工具使用策略，有效验证混合来源多模态虚假信息，在提升检测准确率的同时提高训练效率，并避免依赖预定义流程或推理时规划带来的适应性与成本问题。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal misinformation often involves mixed forgery sources, requiring sample-specific detection strategies. Existing tool-augmented methods rely on predefined workflows or inference-time planning, limiting adaptability or increasing inference cost. To address this issue, we introduce \textbf{MM-VeriAgent}, which learns to verify mixed-source multimodal misinformation with tools. We first build \textbf{MM-VeriTools}, a specialized toolkit for misinformation detection agents. By benchmarking various candidate models and methods on the sub-tasks required by mixed-source detection, we select the strongest for textual, visual, and cross-modal forgery analysis and encapsulate them as callable tools with a unified interface. On top of this toolkit, we train the LVLM agent with reinforcement learning to teach it how to use these tools to better solve mixed-source detection. Since many of the tools are specialized models whose online execution at every rollout severely limits RL efficiency, we further introduce \textbf{Tool-Execution Cache}, which pre-executes candidate tool calls and reuses their cached outputs during training. This preserves multi-step rollouts while reducing online tool execution, largely improving the training efficiency.Experiments on MMFakeBench demonstrate substantial accuracy gains over the base model without explicit tool search at inference time. Ablation and efficiency analyses further validate the learned tool-use policy and show that Tool-Execution Cache reduces online tool executions during training.

</details>


### [105] [SAGE: Source-Anchored Guidance via Frequency Equalization for Hierarchical RGB-T Alignment and Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30703)
*Timing Li,Yiming Sun,Boan Tao,Xiyuan Gao,Haifang Cao,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAGE统一框架，通过频率均衡、层次对齐与子带融合实现RGB-T图像对齐与融合，在真实和合成错位数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-T融合中空间错位和跨模态差异常导致鬼影、结构模糊和内容失衡；现有方法将外观适应、几何对齐和信息融合解耦，限制了阶段间的依赖传播。

Method: SAGE采用可逆联合编码和源特定低频调制获取结构与增益引导，同时保留源信息；通过层次频率协同对齐从低频近似估计全局仿射几何，并将几何与上下文线索传递到高频相关性推理进行可靠性感知残差精炼；引导子带融合在传播的源与对齐引导下聚合对齐的频率系数，协调高低频互补信息，经逆小波变换重建融合图像。

Result: 在真实和合成错位的RGB-T数据集上大量实验表明，SAGE在对齐和融合任务上持续具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 验证了源锚定引导对弱配准RGB-T图像的有效性。

Abstract: Spatial misregistration and cross-modal discrepancies often cause ghosting, structural blurring, and content imbalance in RGB-T fusion. Existing methods typically decouple appearance adaptation, geometric alignment, and information fusion, limiting dependency propagation across stages. We propose Source-Anchored Guidance via Frequency Equalization for Hierarchical RGB-T Alignment and Fusion (SAGE), a unified framework integrating frequency equalization, hierarchical alignment, and subband fusion. SAGE employs invertible joint encoding and source-specific low-frequency modulation to derive structural and gain guidance while preserving source information. Hierarchical frequency collaborative alignment estimates global affine geometry from low-frequency approximations and transfers geometric and contextual cues to high-frequency correlation reasoning for reliability-aware residual refinement. Guided subband fusion jointly aggregates the aligned frequency coefficients under propagated source and alignment guidance, coordinates complementary low- and high-frequency information, and reconstructs the fused image through the inverse wavelet transform. Extensive experiments on RGB-T datasets with real-world and synthetic misalignments demonstrate consistently competitive performance in alignment and fusion, validating the effectiveness of source-anchored guidance for weakly registered RGB-T images.

</details>


### [106] [VLALight: Lightweight Vision-Language-Action Models for Emergency-Aware Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30709)
*Kemou Jiang,Maonan Wang,Xingchen Zou,Jiayue Zhu,Yuhang Fu,Sicheng Wang,Xi Chen,Yirong Chen,Zhiyong Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VLALight，一个轻量级端到端视觉-语言-动作框架，直接将路口多视角视觉观测与信号相位信息映射为离散信号动作，实现实时交通信号控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的交通信号控制方法存在模块间松耦合、信息反复转换导致细粒度视觉细节丢失，以及顺序推理带来高延迟的问题。

Method: 将多个方向摄像头视图融合为统一视觉输入，并用文本指令建立其与交通流向和信号相位的对应关系；使用0.5B参数紧凑模型直接预测动作，无需中间图像到文本描述或手工交通状态表示。

Result: 在对比方法中紧急车辆服务最优，相比级联的VLMLight，汇总紧急等待时间减少21.1%，可在本地硬件实时运行，并能泛化到未见过的路口拓扑和交通流模式。

Conclusion: VLALight通过端到端轻量级视觉-语言-动作框架，有效解决细节丢失与高延迟问题，实现实时且泛化性强的交通信号控制。

Abstract: Traffic signal control (TSC) is essential for mitigating urban congestion. Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) enable richer interpretation of intersection scenes, opening new opportunities for visual-context-aware TSC. However, the loose coupling and repeated information conversion between modules can lead to the loss of fine-grained visual details, while sequential inference introduces substantial latency. To address these limitations, we propose VLALight, a lightweight end-to-end vision-language-action framework that directly maps intersection observations and signal-phase information to discrete signal actions. To handle the multi-view nature of TSC, VLALight combines multiple directional camera views into a unified visual input and uses textual instructions to establish their correspondence with traffic movements and signal phases. This design enables direct action prediction with a compact 0.5 B-parameter model, without intermediate image-to-text descriptions or handcrafted traffic-state representations. Experiments show that VLALight delivers the best emergency-vehicle service of all compared methods, reducing pooled emergency waiting time by 21.1% over the cascaded VLMLight while running in real time on local hardware and generalizing to unseen intersection topologies and traffic-flow patterns.

</details>
