Result: 在 ImageNet-256 和 DINOv3-L 上，单个 FuseReg 解码器无需重训练即可从全层、稀疏和单层融合中重建，PSNR 高于针对固定融合专门训练的解码器。仅替换解码器即可在保持 RAEv2 DiT-XL 生成器不变时，将无引导 gFID 降低 27%；联合正则化在 DiT-Base 上将无引导 gFID 降低 29%。

Conclusion: 训练下游模型具备层融合鲁棒性，可在不修改预训练编码器的情况下缩小重建与生成之间的差距。

Abstract: Representation autoencoders (RAEs) reuse features from a pretrained visual encoder as reconstruction and diffusion latents, integrating strong visual representations into image generation. However, RAEs still need to decide which encoder layers form the shared latent space for the generator and pixel decoder. This choice involves a trade-off. Shallower layers tend to preserve fine pixel details better, while deeper layers tend to yield better generation metrics. A fixed heuristic layer fusion therefore couples two stages that benefit from different information. We introduce FuseReg, which replaces heuristic feature selection with training over random subsets of encoder layers. We theoretically analyze the underlying mechanism: subset sampling explicitly penalizes sensitivity to cross-layer disagreement. On ImageNet-256 with DINOv3-L, a single FuseReg decoder reconstructs from full, sparse, and single-layer fusions without retraining, achieving higher PSNR than decoders specialized to fixed fusions. This flexibility also benefits generation: decoder replacement alone reduces unguided gFID by 27% with an unchanged RAEv2 DiT-XL generator. The same regularization principle extends to diffusion training, with joint regularization of both stages reducing unguided gFID by 29% on DiT-Base. These results show that training downstream models for layer-fusion robustness narrows the reconstruction-generation gap without modifying the pretrained encoder.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [A Mechanistic Study of AI-Text Detection Neurons in Frozen BERT: Sparse Probing and Activation Patching on RAID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30287)
*Paweł Blicharz,Miłosz Grunwald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究冻结的 BERT-base-uncased 编码器中哪些神经元支持 AI 生成文本检测。作者在 RAID 基准的六种生成器上使用稀疏探针定位稳定神经元，并通过激活修补验证其因果作用，发现检测信号冗余分布，且指令微调模型在最后一层留下可迁移的神经足迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 AI 生成文本检测器在标准基准上准确率很高，但驱动这些预测的内部表征仍不清楚。研究希望理解检测器依赖哪些内部神经元，以及这些神经元是否稳定、因果相关并可跨生成器迁移。

Method: 使用 RAID 基准，覆盖六种生成器，包括纯基础模型和指令微调模型。对冻结的 BERT-base-uncased 的 9,216 个 CLS 隐藏状态维度（12 层 × 768）应用 Gurnee 等人的 L1-to-L2 稀疏探测协议。通过双向激活修补检验因果性，并进行均值消融、跨生成器分析和留一模型家族评估。

Result: 每个生成器可恢复出少于 1% 的稳定神经元，且跨折、跨随机种子一致；仅用该神经元集合的探针可保留大部分全特征检测准确率。双向激活修补显示，该集合翻转预测的频率比同规模随机集合高一个数量级；均值消融后准确率基本不变，说明信号冗余分布。跨生成器分析呈现二分结构：指令微调生成器将 30–36% 的稳定神经元集中在 BERT 最后一层，而两个基础生成器均低于 14%，与后训练对齐的第 12 层足迹一致。留一模型家族评估中，所选神经元在未见生成器家族上保留全特征上限的 86–94%。

Conclusion: AI 文本检测可由少量稳定且因果相关的神经元支持，且该信号在模型中冗余分布。检测器可在小型固定子空间上运行，而无需针对每个生成器重新识别神经元；指令微调模型最后一层的神经足迹还表明后训练对齐会影响检测特征。

Abstract: AI-generated text detectors achieve high accuracy on standard benchmarks, yet the internal representations that drive these predictions remain poorly understood. We study which neurons in a frozen BERT-base-uncased encoder support AI-text detection, using the RAID benchmark across six generators spanning pure-base and instruction-tuned models. We apply the L1-to-L2 sparse-probing protocol of Gurnee et al. (2023) to all 9,216 CLS hidden-state dimensions (12 layers x 768), which we call neurons. The procedure recovers a stable set of under 1% of neurons per generator, consistent across folds and seeds; a probe restricted to that set retains most of the full-feature detection accuracy. Bidirectional activation patching confirms this set's causal relevance: in both directions it flips predictions an order of magnitude more often than size-matched random sets. Mean-ablating the same neurons leaves accuracy largely intact; the signal is therefore redundantly distributed. Cross-generator analysis reveals a bipartite structure: instruction-tuned generators concentrate 30-36% of stable neurons in BERT's final layer while both base generators fall below 14%, consistent with a layer-12 footprint of post-training alignment. Leave-one-family-out evaluation shows the selected neurons retain 86-94% of the full-feature ceiling on unseen generator families, so a detector can operate on a small fixed subspace without re-identifying neurons per generator.

</details>


### [177] [Manifold Projection and Iterative Autoencoder Refinement for Masked Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30288)
*Narges Mokhtari,Farzan Haddadi,Ebrahim Rezaii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需注意力机制的 Transformer 替代方案：用低秩瓶颈自编码器模块进行 token 间信息混合，并在掩码位置采用迭代细化。它在 C4 上预训练，以约 1.9 倍更少 FLOPs 达到注意力模型的大部分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力是 Transformer 掩码语言模型的主要上下文混合机制，但计算开销大；无注意力混合器虽用固定或超网络生成的 MLP 替代注意力，但仍有局限。作者希望以低秩瓶颈自编码器获得动态、内容相关的加权混合能力，同时降低计算量。

Method: 用自编码器混合模块替换注意力，包括局部邻域、全序列和跨注意力头三类混合模块；每个模块通过瓶颈压缩并重建输入，瓶颈宽度是超参数。掩码位置采用迭代细化：先“拉近”步骤将嵌入拉向邻居加权平均，再“纠正”步骤通过自编码器投影回学习到的流形。并使用频率感知训练调度，对稀有 token 进行非均匀过采样。

Result: 在 C4 上预训练并与参数匹配的 BERT 基线比较，模型以约 1.9 倍更少 FLOPs 达到注意力性能的显著部分；在使用频率感知训练调度后，在最稀有 token 频率区间上等于参数匹配的 BERT 和 TinyBERT 基线。

Conclusion: 低秩瓶颈自编码器混合与迭代细化可以为掩码语言建模提供有效的无注意力替代方案，在显著降低计算成本的同时保持竞争力，尤其通过频率感知训练改善稀有 token 表现。

Abstract: In Transformer-based masked language models, attention is the primary mechanism for context mixing, but there are other ways to mix data across tokens. Recent attention-free mixers replace attention with fixed or hypernetwork-generated MLPs, alternating their dynamic, content-dependent weighting for computational simplicity. We build an alternative that gets the same property from a low-rank bottleneck autoencoder. We replace attention with a stack of autoencoder-based mixing modules, one operating over local neighborhoods, one over the full sequence, and one across attention heads, each compressing and reconstructing its input through a bottleneck, and its width is a hyperparameter rather than a training effect. In masked positions, we introduce an iterative refinement procedure that has two distinct steps. A pulling step that pulls an embedding representation toward a weighted average of its neighbors, and a correcting step that projects the result back to the learned manifold via an autoencoder. Our architecture achieves a significant portion of attention's performance at about $1.9 \times$ fewer FLOPs when pretrained on C4 and evaluated with parameter-matched BERT baselines. Our model equals parameter-matched BERT and TinyBERT baselines on the rarest-token frequency bucket using a frequency-aware training schedule that samples rare tokens more than uniformly for the masking tasks.

</details>


### [178] [Not All Memories Are Equal: Hierarchical Collaborative Memory for Validity-Aware Retrieval in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30289)
*Yufei Shi,Rujing Yao,Ang Li,Yang Wu,Zhuoren Jiang,Xiaozhong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 HiCoMER，一个面向 LLM 智能体团队协作的分层协作记忆管理与有效性感知检索框架，用于避免检索过时或冲突记忆，并提升基于记忆的问答质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队协作中记忆异构且持续演化：团队记忆包含共识与协议，个体记忆包含成员观察与执行轨迹。现有记忆增强系统通常将所有记忆视为扁平池，仅按语义相关性、重要性或时间排序，未建模层级结构与动态有效性，因此常检索到语义相关但过时或与团队共识冲突的个体记忆，影响协作 LLM 智能体回答的可靠性。

Method: 提出 HiCoMER 框架，包含三个组件：分层记忆冲突更新器、有效性感知记忆检索器、基于记忆的答案生成器。HiCoMER 先维护团队与个体记忆的有效性，再仅检索仍然有效的记忆，而非直接从所有存储记忆中检索。同时构建了两个用于协作场景下基于记忆问答的新数据集。

Result: 在两个新数据集上的实验表明，HiCoMER 持续优于强基线，能够减少过时记忆检索、保留当前团队共识，并提升下游问答质量。

Conclusion: 有效性感知的分层协作记忆管理能够有效解决团队协作中记忆冲突与过时问题，为协作 LLM 智能体提供更可靠、当前有效的记忆基础。

Abstract: In team collaboration scenarios, memory is heterogeneous and continually evolving. Team memories capture collective decisions, protocols, and current consensus, while individual memories preserve member-specific observations, execution traces, and intermediate progress. Existing memory-augmented systems typically retrieve from all stored memories as a flat pool, ranking them by semantic relevance, importance, or recency without modeling hierarchical structure or evolving validity. As a result, they often surface semantically relevant but outdated or conflicting memories, especially individual memories that no longer align with current team consensus, instead of prioritizing currently valid memories. This is particularly problematic when collaborative LLM agents answer user questions, since their responses should be grounded in valid memories. We propose HiCoMER, a framework for hierarchical collaborative memory management and validity-aware retrieval in LLM agents. HiCoMER first maintains the validity of team and individual memories and then retrieves memories that remain valid, rather than retrieving directly from all stored memories. It consists of three components: a Hierarchical Memory Conflict Updater, a Validity-Aware Memory Retriever, and a Memory-Grounded Answer Generator. To evaluate HiCoMER, we construct two new datasets for memory-grounded question answering in collaborative settings. Experiments on both datasets show that HiCoMER consistently outperforms strong baselines by reducing outdated retrieval, preserving current team consensus, and improving downstream QA quality.

</details>


### [179] [Auditing and Repairing LLM-as-Judge Failures in a Production Text-to-SQL Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30290)
*Haowei Liu,Hsin-Tai Wu,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 生产 text-to-SQL 流水线中的 LLM 评审与人类标注的一致性从未被验证。作者审计发现 gpt-4o-mini 评审的 Cohen's kappa 仅 0.04（富分歧集）和 0.42（随机抽查），过度标记 77.1% 的人类忠实案例，主要源于“评分幻觉”。自托管 Qwen3.6-27B（kappa=0.72）与 Claude Opus 4.7（0.71）相当且成本约 1/300；集成无免费收益，三强评审一致路由可达 0.79，自动覆盖率 89.7%；跨域审计将 BIRD-financial 的 25.5% 专家金标 SQL 标为候选问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产 text-to-SQL 流水线普遍以 LLM-as-judge 收尾，但其与人类标注者的一致性从未被实际测量，可能导致不可靠的评估与部署决策。

Method: 审计已部署的 gpt-4o-mini 评审，在富分歧集和均匀随机抽查上计算 Cohen's kappa；识别导致过度标记的 GRADE-HALLUCINATION 机制；比较自托管 Qwen3.6-27B 与 Claude Opus 4.7；测试集成策略（弱+强评审配对、三个强评审一致路由）；将审计配方跨域应用于 BIRD-financial。

Result: gpt-4o-mini 评审 kappa 仅 0.04（富分歧集）和 0.42（随机抽查），过度标记 77.1% 人类忠实案例；Qwen3.6-27B 达 kappa=0.72，与 Opus 4.7（0.71）相当，成本约 1/300；弱+强配对集成降低一致性，三强评审一致路由达 kappa=0.79、自动覆盖 89.7%；跨域审计将 BIRD-financial 的 25.5% 专家金标 SQL 标为候选问题。

Conclusion: 部署的弱 LLM 评审与人类一致性极低，且存在可识别的评分幻觉机制；可改用更便宜且一致性相当的强评审；集成需谨慎设计，三强一致路由有效；该审计配方可跨域发现金标 SQL 问题；代码与预注册已公开。

Abstract: Production text-to-SQL pipelines often end with an LLM-as-judge whose agreement with human annotators has never actually been measured. When we checked ours, the deployed gpt-4o-mini judge agreed with two-author gold at only Cohen's kappa = 0.04 on a disagreement-enriched set and 0.42 on a uniform-random spot-check, over-flagging 77.1% of the human-FAITHFUL cases in the enriched set. Most of its over-flags trace back to a single mechanism we call GRADE-HALLUCINATION. A self-hosted Qwen3.6-27B replacement (kappa = 0.72) lands in the same range as Claude Opus 4.7 (kappa = 0.71); the head-to-head is underpowered at n = 96, but for the deployment decision that hardly matters, since Qwen costs roughly 1/300 as much per call. Ensembling does not help for free. Pairing the weak judge with a stronger one degrades agreement, whereas three strong judges under unanimity routing reach kappa = 0.79 at 89.7% auto-coverage. Applied out-of-domain, the same audit recipe flags 25.5% of BIRD-financial's expert-authored gold SQLs as candidate gold-SQL issues under our annotation protocol. Code and pre-registration are at https://github.com/JamesL404/synca-audit.

</details>


### [180] [A Survey on Fake Review Detection: From Pre-trained Language Models to Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30292)
*Fanji Yang,Huiyao Chen,Xi Yu,Meishan Zhang,Xiaohong Xiao,Mingsen Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从信息融合视角综述2018年至2026年初的211项虚假评论检测研究，梳理文本、情感、评分行为、时间元数据、用户-商品图、多模态内容、外部知识及大模型生成信号等证据来源与融合层级，并总结从传统机器学习、深度学习到预训练语言模型和大语言模型的方法演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线评论影响消费者决策、平台治理与企业声誉，虚假评论会污染评分系统、推荐流程和公众信任；同时大语言模型既能生成更流畅、上下文感知的欺骗性评论，也为检测提供更强的语义表示，因此需要系统梳理该领域的发展与挑战。

Method: 采用信息融合视角进行综述，按证据来源和融合层级组织文献，覆盖评论文本、情感、评分行为、时间元数据、用户-商品图、多模态内容、外部知识及大模型生成信号；追踪传统机器学习、深度学习、基于预训练语言模型和基于大语言模型的方法，并分析不同方法如何融合文本、行为、结构和多模态证据。

Result: 总结了在Amazon、Yelp和OpSpam等基准数据集上的性能趋势，同时指出标签构建过程、数据划分和评估协议差异带来的可比性限制，并归纳了对抗生成、跨域迁移、不确定性感知融合、缺失来源鲁棒性、可解释性以及面向AI生成欺骗内容的可信评估等开放问题。

Conclusion: 虚假评论检测正从单一文本检测转向多源证据融合与预训练/大语言模型驱动的方法；未来需在对抗鲁棒性、跨域泛化、不确定性建模、缺失源处理、可解释性和可信评估等方面取得进展。

Abstract: Online reviews shape consumer decisions, platform governance, and corporate reputation.Fake reviews compromise this information channel by injecting deceptive evidence into rating systems, recommendation pipelines, and public trust mechanisms.The rise of large language models, or LLMs, has changed the problem in two directions.LLMs can generate fluent and context-aware deceptive reviews, while pre-trained language models, or PLMs, and LLMs also provide stronger semantic representations for detection.This survey reviews fake review detection from an information fusion perspective, covering 211 studies published from 2018 to early 2026.We organize existing work by evidence source and fusion level, covering review text, sentiment, rating behavior, temporal metadata, user-product graphs, multimodal content, external knowledge, and LLM-generated signals.We trace the development from traditional machine learning and deep learning to PLM-based and LLM-based methods, and examine how different approaches combine textual, behavioral, structural, and multimodal evidence.We also analyze reported performance trends on widely used Amazon, Yelp, and OpSpam benchmark families, while noting the limitations caused by different label construction procedures, data splits, and evaluation protocols.Finally, we identify open problems in adversarial generation, cross-domain transfer, uncertainty-aware fusion, missing-source robustness, interpretability, and trustworthy evaluation for AI-generated deceptive content.

</details>


### [181] [Cartograph: Federated Tool Discovery with Operator-Attested Retrieval for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30293)
*Justice Owusu Agyemang,Michael Agyare,Kwame Opuni-Boachie Obour Agyekum,Kwame Agyeman-Prempeh Agyekum,Francisca Adoma Acheampong,Jerry John Kponyo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Cartograph 是一种联邦式 MCP 代理，将 AI 代理可见的工具发现从全目录 O(n) 遍历变为 O(k) 渐进披露，通过能力卡、Rift 混淆簇分析和两阶段检索降低工具定义加载开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 MCP 连接的工具目录增大，代理若加载全部工具定义会变得非常昂贵，因此需要减少代理可见的工具定义数量，同时保持可发现性。

Method: 提出 Cartograph，包含三项机制：1）由部署运营方控制的 Ed25519 签名能力卡；2）Rift 三层可混淆簇分析，包括密度聚类、查询边界分析和 token 诊断；3）先检索服务器再检索工具的两阶段检索。

Result: 在 22 个服务器、374 个工具的部署中，Cartograph 仅暴露 3 个代理工具而非 374 个定义。49 查询基准上 R@5 为 0.816，高于 Jaccard 关键词基线的 0.592；top-5 发现交互仅用 475 tokens，而全目录计账为 42,450 tokens。Rift 识别出 49 个可混淆簇，其中 4 个为 HIGH 风险。网关相比直接 stdio MCP 调用平均增加 5ms 延迟（0.8%）。

Conclusion: Cartograph 能有效降低工具发现成本并提升检索效果，且与代码执行方法互补：它控制哪些工具描述被呈现，并记录每次查询排序所用描述的来源。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) enables AI agents to discover and call tools, but loading every definition becomes expensive as connected catalogs grow. We present Cartograph, a federated MCP proxy that changes agent-visible tool discovery from $O(n)$ catalog traversal to $O(k)$ progressive disclosure. Cartograph combines three mechanisms: (1) operator-attested capability cards, Ed25519-signed descriptions generated under the deploying operator's control rather than ranked publisher copy; (2) Rift, a three-layer confusable-cluster analysis comprising density clustering, query-margin analysis, and token diagnosis; and (3) two-stage retrieval, which ranks servers before tools. On a 22-server, 374-tool deployment, Cartograph exposes three proxy tools instead of 374 definitions. A 49-query author-constructed benchmark yields R@5 of 0.816, compared with 0.592 for a Jaccard keyword baseline, while a measured top-5 discovery exchange uses 475 tokens rather than 42,450 under the stated full-catalog accounting. Rift identifies 49 confusable clusters, including four HIGH-risk clusters in bootstrap-generated cards. An exploratory comparison of 119 LLM-generated descriptions removes the observed zero-distance cluster but shows that mixing card-generation regimes can reduce R@5. Gateway measurements over ten trials add 5ms mean latency (0.8%) relative to direct stdio MCP calls. Cartograph is complementary to code-execution approaches: it controls which tool descriptions are surfaced and records the provenance of the descriptions used for ranking for each query.

</details>


### [182] [SlideLab: Audience-Centered Scientific Slide Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30294)
*Vidushee Vats,Karun Sharma,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SlideLab，一个无需训练的多智能体框架，可将研究论文生成连贯的科学演示文稿；同时提出面向听众的评测框架 ConfArena。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学演示不只是论文摘要，还需要连贯的叙事、清晰的主旨表达，并帮助听众跟上内容。

Method: SlideLab 先规划演示叙事，再通过内容规划、视觉生成、布局优化和事实 grounding 验证等智能体，构建并迭代优化共享幻灯片；ConfArena 则模拟会议室，逐页评估演示文稿。

Result: 在盲测人类偏好研究中，SlideLab 在 77% 的论文上优于开源和商业系统，且推理 token 约为最强开源基线的四分之一；ConfArena 与人类系统排名一致，并能检测篡改数字、劣化图表、丢失幻灯片和打乱顺序等问题。

Conclusion: SlideLab 能有效生成高质量科学演示文稿，ConfArena 可为演示文稿提供以听众为中心的可靠评估。

Abstract: Scientific presentations are more than summaries of research papers. They need to present the work in a coherent sequence, explain the main ideas clearly, and help the audience follow the presentation. We present SlideLab, a training-free multi-agent framework for generating scientific presentations from research papers. SlideLab first plans the presentation narrative, then builds and iteratively refines a shared slide deck using agents for content planning, visual generation, layout refinement, and grounding verification. In a blind human preference study, SlideLab was preferred over both open-source and commercial systems on 77% of papers while using roughly 4 times fewer inference tokens than the strongest open-source baseline. We also introduce ConfArena, an audience-oriented evaluation framework that simulates a conference room and assesses presentations slide by slide. ConfArena matches human system rankings and detects injected presentation problems, including falsified numbers, degraded figures, dropped slides, and shuffled slide order.

</details>


### [183] [SignTrace: Describe a Sign, Find the Word](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30295)
*Zengji Tu,Xingye Zhu,Ningjing Wang,Tingyi Huang,Yangjunfeng Zhu,Dai Wan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SignTrace是一个面向中文手语词典的自然语言反向查询系统，用户可通过描述动作来查找不熟悉的手语词条，在500条基准查询上达到94.0% Hit@1和0.9540 MRR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语学习者常记得手语动作，却不知道其含义或正式特征编码，导致难以查找不熟悉的手语，存在反向查找难题。

Method: 系统整合基于大语言模型的词典增强、动作抽取、词典风格改写、七通道检索和候选重排序，覆盖6699个词条，并已部署进行用户试用。

Result: 在词典衍生的500条动作描述查询基准上，Hit@1为94.0%，Hit@9为97.4%，MRR为0.9540；重排序将Hit@1从71.8%提升至94.0%；六并发查询的中位处理时间为13.37秒。

Conclusion: SignTrace将日常动作描述与已有手语词条及含义连接起来，为识别不熟悉手语提供了实用工具，但基准采用词典衍生措辞和预先选择，限制了向用户独立表述的泛化能力。

Abstract: Identifying an unfamiliar sign is difficult when a learner remembers its movement but does not know its meaning or formal feature codes. SignTrace addresses this longstanding reverse-lookup problem through natural-language access to a Chinese sign-language dictionary. The system integrates LLM-based dictionary enrichment, action extraction, dictionary-style rewriting, seven-channel retrieval, and candidate reranking over 6,699 entries. It has been deployed for user trials and has received positive informal feedback. Evaluation on a dictionary-derived benchmark of 500 movement-description queries yields 94.0% Hit@1, 97.4% Hit@9, and a mean reciprocal rank of 0.9540. Reranking increases Hit@1 from 71.8% to 94.0%, while component analyses show the contribution of enriched entry descriptions. Median query-processing time is 13.37 seconds with six concurrent queries. By connecting everyday movement descriptions to documented signs and meanings, SignTrace provides a practical tool for identifying unfamiliar signs. Dictionary-derived wording and prior selection within the benchmark limit generalization to descriptions independently produced by users.

</details>


### [184] [Bootstrapping Conversational Recommendation Agents At Spotify: Synthetic Data Generation and Self-Improvement Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30297)
*Enrico Palumbo,Alexandre Tamborrino,Victor Ode,Ben Lacker,Adrià Casas Escoda,Jeremy Hopple,Marcus Better,James Leoni,Hugo Galvão,Hugues Bouchard,Mounia Lalmas,José Luis Redondo García,Abenezer Abebe,Ann Clifton,Anton Blomberg,Henrik Lindström,Dani Doro,Christine Doig Cardet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一套用于对话式推荐代理的合成数据生成与自我改进流程，以优化工具选择、排序和调用规划，并在 Spotify 生产环境中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式推荐代理需理解复杂自然语言意图，但冷启动阶段缺乏真实用户交互数据，导致代理规划（工具选择、排序和调用）难以优化。

Method: 构建多轮合成数据生成流水线，将单轮提示转化为真实多轮对话；结合基于方差的对比优化与编码代理迭代精炼，自动识别并修复规划及工具使用错误。

Result: 在高度优化的人工提示基础上质量提升 +8%；系统已生产化，加速 Spotify 对话式推荐代理上线迭代；线上 A/B 测试显示用户收听 +14%、周活跃用户 +5%、跳过率降低 5%（相比仅支持会话精炼的旧体验）。

Conclusion: 该工作为工业界加速开发对话式推荐代理提供了实用框架，表明合成数据与自我改进循环可在冷启动下有效提升代理规划与线上表现。

Abstract: Conversational recommendation agents are a new paradigm for content discovery, enabling users to express complex intents through natural language (e.g., "recommend Italian indie artists I haven't heard before"). A central challenge in building such agents is optimizing agent planning -- deciding how to select, sequence, and invoke tools -- particularly in cold-start settings where real user interactions are not yet available. We introduce a pipeline for multi-turn synthetic data generation and a self-improvement loop to address this challenge. The synthetic data pipeline transforms single-turn prompts into realistic multi-turn conversations, enabling systematic evaluation before launch. The self-improvement loop combines variance-based contrastive optimization with iterative refinement through a coding agent, automatically identifying and fixing planning and tool-use errors. Our approach improves quality by +8% on top of a highly optimized manual prompt. The system has been productionized and significantly accelerated iteration cycles for the launch of a conversational recommendation agent at Spotify. Online A/B tests demonstrate its effectiveness, with +14% user listening, +5% increase in weekly active users, and a 5% reduction in skip rate compared to a prior experience supporting only session refinement. This work provides a practical framework for accelerating the development of conversational recommendation agents in industry.

</details>


### [185] [A Benchmark Framework for Screening Automation in Systematic Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30298)
*Gauransh Kumar,Luciano Marchezan,Guillaume Genois,Kévin Delcourt,Eugene Syriani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对系统综述筛选阶段耗时耗力的问题，构建了用于评估大语言模型筛选性能的基准数据集，并提出考虑类别不平衡的评估框架和PromptSR工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统综述的文献筛选工作繁重，大语言模型有望辅助文章相关性分类以减轻负担，但现有评估多依赖传统指标，在高度不平衡的筛选数据集上可能产生误导。

Method: 构建包含45,064条标注条目、覆盖32项精选二次研究的基准数据集；提出考虑类别不平衡的评估框架，以反映排除文章与纳入文章的自然 prevalence；开发PromptSR工具，支持提示词实验、实验管理和结果分析；并给出一个应用SRBench和PromptSR的用例。

Result: 论文提供了大规模标注基准数据集、面向类别不平衡的评估框架以及PromptSR工具，并通过用例展示了SRBench与PromptSR在LLM系统综述筛选评估中的应用。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于大语言模型的系统综述筛选提供了更贴近真实数据分布的基准、评估方法和实验工具，有助于更可靠地衡量和推进LLM辅助文献筛选。

Abstract: Systematic reviews (SR) are essential for evidence-based research, but their screening phase is highly time-consuming and labor-intensive. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising opportunity to reduce this workload by assisting with article relevance classification. However, existing evaluation approaches often rely on traditional metrics that may be misleading for highly imbalanced SR screening datasets.This paper presents a benchmark dataset of $45\,064$ labeled entries for evaluating LLM performance in SR screening across 32 curated secondary studies. It proposes an evaluation framework that accounts for class imbalance, i.e., the natural prevalence of excluded articles relative to included articles in SRs. It also introduces PromptSR, a tool designed to support prompt experimentation, experiment management, and result analysis for LLM-based screening. We also present a use case demonstrating the application of SRBench and PromptSR.

</details>


### [186] [A Unified Account of Concepts and Chunks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30414)
*Karthik Singaravadivelan,Pat Langley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文综述了Cobweb这一分类与概念形成计算模型，并提出整合“组块”（chunks）及其习得的扩展理论，同时实现Trellis系统，在上下文无关文法上验证其学习、解析和生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知心理学中关于概念/类别的研究与关于组块/熟悉元素模式的研究几乎彼此独立，这给统一认知理论带来挑战。

Method: 回顾Cobweb模型；提出纳入组块及其习得的扩展理论，该理论不限定模态，适用于可分解为元素及关系的经验；实现Trellis系统；以涉及概念类与组块类元素的上下文无关文法作为测试平台，并在三个合成文法上开展实验。

Result: 实验表明系统能够表示句法知识，利用其解析和生成句子，并能从样例解析中学习组合结构。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了概念与组块的相关工作，并指出未来研究方向，以推动该领域的统一认知理论发展。

Abstract: Cognitive psychology has studied how people encode, use, and learn concepts that describe categories, and how they represent, recognize, and acquire chunks for familiar patterns of elements. The literatures on these two topics are nearly disjoint, which poses a challenge for unified theories of cognition. In this paper, we review Cobweb, a computational account of categorization and concept formation, and propose an extended theory that incorporates chunks and their acquisition. The theory makes no commitments about modality, applying to any experience that decomposes into elements and relations among them. We also present \trellis/, an implementation of this theory, and illustrate its application to learning context-free grammars, which we adopt as a testbed because they involve both concept-like and chunk-like elements. In addition, we report experimental results on three synthetic grammars that demonstrate the system's ability to represent syntactic knowledge, use it to parse and generate sentences, and learn compositional structures from sample parses. We conclude by discussing related work on concepts and chunks, along with directions for future research in the area.

</details>


### [187] [All In Good Time: Causality-Aware Framework for LLM-Based Simultaneous Speech-to-Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30416)
*Amir Hussein,Enas Albasiri,Travis M. Bartley,Nourchene Ferchichi,Ke Hu,Harishchandra Dubey,Myungjong Kim,Zhehuai Chen,Oluwatobi Olabiyi,Sanjeev Khudanpur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种因果感知的同步语音到语音翻译框架FAST-CAP，并配套生成高保真、因果对齐训练数据的新流程，在低资源场景下提升翻译质量、说话人保真度并显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在低资源离线翻译中表现良好，但扩展到同步语音到语音翻译仍很困难，主要因为缺乏具有高跨语言说话人保真度的因果对齐训练数据；同时现有方法依赖固定翻译策略或置信度启发式，导致质量次优且延迟较高。

Method: 提出因果感知的Simul-S2ST框架，包含：新数据流水线生成高保真、因果对齐且语音迁移更好的片段；分解式S2ST架构FAST；因果感知自适应策略CAP；以及因果感知延迟指标。

Result: 在CVSS西班牙语、德语和法语上，FAST-CAP持续改善质量-延迟权衡，相比固定策略最高提升+1.2 BLEU，并实现26%的相对延迟降低；尽管训练数据远少于现有系统，仍达到语音翻译质量和说话人保真度的SOTA，延迟相对降低最高达38.8%。

Conclusion: FAST-CAP表明，通过因果感知的数据构建、自适应策略和延迟度量，可以在较少训练数据下实现更优的同步语音到语音翻译质量、说话人保真度与延迟权衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance in low-resource offline translation; however, extending them to simultaneous speech-to-speech translation (Simul-S2ST) remains challenging due to the scarcity of causally aligned training data with high cross-lingual speaker fidelity. In addition, existing approaches rely on fixed translation policy or confidence heuristics, leading to suboptimal quality and higher latency. We propose a causality-aware Simul-S2ST framework with a novel data pipeline that generates high-fidelity, causally aligned segments with improved voice transfer. The framework introduces (i) a factorized S2ST architecture (FAST), (ii) a causality-aware adaptive policy (CAP), and (iii) causality-aware latency metric. Experiments on CVSS Spanish, German, and French show that FAST-CAP consistently improves the quality-latency trade-off, achieving up to +1.2 BLEU and a 26% relative latency reduction over a fixed policy. Despite using substantially less training data than existing systems, FAST-CAP achieves state-of-the-art results in speech translation quality and speaker fidelity while yielding up to a 38.8% relative reduction in latency.

</details>


### [188] [Inference-Time Target Speaker Unlearning in LLM-Based Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30439)
*Bo Su,Yueru Yan,Thai Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出目标说话人遗忘ASR（TSU-ASR）任务：在多说话人ASR与说话人日志中，给定多说话人语音及一组不愿被转写的退出说话人，系统需转写其他说话人，同时仍标明退出说话人何时活跃。作者提出可附加于冻结双流语音LLM的轻量Enrollment-Conditioned Gating（ECG）模块，实现推理时动态遗忘新退出说话人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频会议中，说话人可能希望动态退出AI自动转写，但不应被迫离开会议；因此需要一种隐私保护方案，在保留其他说话人转写和说话人活动指示的同时，抑制对指定退出说话人的转写。

Method: 定义TSU-ASR任务，并在全端到端多说话人ASR与diarization框架下解决。提出轻量ECG模块，附加到冻结的双流语音LLM上，通过注册条件门控，使系统在推理阶段可动态支持训练时未见过的新的opt-out说话人。

Result: 在AMI（英语）和AliMeeting（普通话）数据集上，对应opt-out词/字的转写准确率分别从72.3%降至48.2%、从73.6%降至27.3%，而保留说话人的转写错误率基本不变。

Conclusion: 该方法为视频会议平台提供实用方案，让说话人无需强制离开会议即可动态退出自动AI转写，实现隐私保护接口，有望服务每日数百万在线会议。

Abstract: We introduce target-speaker unlearning ASR (TSU-ASR) task in a fully end-to-end framework for multi-speaker ASR and diarization. Given a multi-speaker utterance and a set of opt-out speakers who do not wish to have their speech transcribed, the task requires an ASR system to transcribe all speakers except the opt-out ones, while still indicating when those speakers are active. As a first step towards tackling this task, we introduce a novel, light-weight Enrollment-Conditioned Gating (ECG) module attachable to a frozen dual-stream speech LLM that enables ASR for new opt-out speakers dynamically during inference, even those who were not seen during initial ECG training phase. Our experiments on both AMI (English) and AliMeeting (Mandarin) datasets show that speech transcription accuracy for corresponding opt-out words or characters falls from 72.3% to 48.2% and from 73.6% to 27.3%, respectively, while retained speakers' transcription error rates maintain more or less the same. Our approach provides a practical solution for modern video conferencing platforms, allowing speakers to dynamically opt-out from automated AI transcriptions without forcefully leaving the meeting sessions, enabling a privacy-preserving interface for potentially millions of online meetings daily.

</details>


### [189] [Where Does Retrieval-Based Open-Ended Evaluation Fail? Automatic Taxonomy Induction from Long-Form Medical Answer Factuality Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30467)
*Heyuan Huang,Jirui Dai,Alexandra DeLucia,Sonal Joshi,Mahsa Yarmohammadi,Jie Gao,Bernal Jiménez Gutiérrez,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对医学RAG事实性评估，提出检索与验证失败分类法及自动标注流程，并压力测试多种检索和验证模型，发现现有retrieve-then-verify范式存在根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学事实核查系统多用F1等聚合指标，无法揭示失败发生在哪里以及为何失败；同时现有RAG诊断依赖金标准答案或标注证据，而开放式医学场景中并不具备这些条件。

Method: 基于MedExpert开放式数据集和3个封闭数据集构建两套分类法：将检索阶段错误按5个质量维度分解，将验证器推理错误按6个连续步骤分解；采用LLM-as-Judge的自动模式归纳流水线，大规模标注证据质量并分类验证推理错误；在4种检索方法和6个前沿验证模型上进行压力测试。

Result: 扩大模型规模、增加推理努力、扩展权威网络来源以及医学微调均不能解决这些失败模式；这些失败模式是开放式医学场景下retrieve-then-verify范式的根本限制，而非旧系统缺陷。

Conclusion: 医学RAG事实性评估需要超越聚合指标并重新审视检索-验证范式；作者发布代码和数据以保证结果可复现。

Abstract: Retrieval-based factuality evaluation, where LLM-generated claims are verified against evidence from authoritative medical corpora, has become the dominant paradigm for scalable hallucination detection in high-stakes clinical settings. Despite the urgency of reliable and transparent medical fact verification, most systems measure performance with aggregate metrics like F1, which obscure where and why failures occur. Existing RAG diagnostics require gold answers or annotated gold evidence, neither of which exists in this regime. We introduce two comprehensive taxonomies, grounded in a case study on the open-ended MedExpert dataset and 3 closed-ended datasets, decomposing failures into retrieval-stage errors along five quality dimensions, and verifier-reasoning errors into six consecutive steps. We adapt an automatic pattern induction pipeline using LLM-as-Judge to label evidence quality and classify verifier reasoning errors at scale, and then stress-test our findings across 4 retrieval methods and 6 frontier verifier models. Our analysis reveals that scaling model size, adding reasoning effort, expanding to authoritative web sources, and applying medical fine-tuning do not resolve these failure modes, demonstrating that they represent fundamental limitations of the retrieve-then-verify paradigm in open-ended medical settings rather than artifacts of outdated systems. We release our code and data at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Medical_RAG_eval-4AB5 for the full reproducibility of our results.

</details>


### [190] [CARGO: Context-Aware Retrieval-Gated Evaluation of Agentic AI in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30471)
*Mukul Chhabra,Shail Patel,Luigi Medrano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出参考实例分歧（RID）问题：在动态实体场景下，基于参考答案的LLM评判会把正确但实体不同的回答误判为错误。为此提出CARGO框架，将参考视为流程范例、基于实时上下文做三分类判断并仅惩罚矛盾，同时用检索置信度门控评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署中的智能体系统常处理动态实体（如工单、资产、账户），最接近的参考答案往往是对不同实体应用正确流程，导致字面匹配的评判器将不同的标识符、日期和状态误判为错误或幻觉，即RID失效模式。

Method: 提出CARGO框架：1）将检索到的参考作为程序性范例，将事实判断锚定在实时实例的观测上下文中；2）为每个主张分配三种状态（支持、矛盾、不可验证），仅惩罚矛盾；3）通过检索置信度门控评估，将生产评估转化为选择性预测。同时构建CARGO-Bench，基于扰动的诊断套件，通过构造保证真值，区分宽松与判别能力。

Result: 在CARGO-Bench（246项、两个评判模型、7,872次判断）上，标准参考评判器对100%的正确实体替换答案施加惩罚，且无信息量（判别指数DI约0）；仅提供实时事实而不重新定位也无改善。CARGO消除了这些误罚（0/50），同时保持近乎完整的矛盾召回（50/50和49/50），将DI提升至0.58 [0.48, 0.68]；评分标准交换控制表明大部分效果归因于基于上下文的维度定义。CARGO也暴露自身局限：保护实体值的宽松性抑制了对流程性错误的检测（召回率20%），事后修复未能弥补，LLM标注研究也显示相同盲点。

Conclusion: CARGO能有效解决RID导致的误判并提升判别能力，但存在对流程性错误检测不足的盲点。作者发布了预注册协议，以将评估扩展到专家一致性、风险-覆盖率和生产流量成本。

Abstract: Reference-based LLM-as-a-judge evaluation assumes the reference answer is the target. In deployed agentic systems that operate over dynamic entities (support cases, assets, accounts), the closest available reference typically applies the correct procedure to a different entity, so a literal judge penalizes different identifiers, dates, and statuses as errors or hallucinations. We name this failure mode reference-instance divergence (RID). We propose CARGO, a framework that (i) treats retrieved references as procedural exemplars and grounds factual judgments in the live instance's observed context, (ii) assigns each claim a three-way status (supported, contradicted, unverifiable) and penalizes only contradictions, and (iii) gates evaluation by retrieval confidence, casting production evaluation as selective prediction. We introduce CARGO-Bench, a perturbation-based diagnostic suite with ground truth by construction that separates leniency from discrimination. On CARGO-Bench (246 items, two judge models, 7,872 judgments), the standard reference-based judge penalizes 100% of correct entity-transplanted answers and is uninformative (discrimination index DI ~ 0); supplying the live facts without reframing changes nothing. CARGO eliminates these false penalties (0/50) while retaining near-complete contradiction recall (50/50 and 49/50), raising DI to 0.58 [0.48, 0.68]; a rubric-swap control attributes most of the effect to context-grounded dimension definitions. CARGO also exposes a limitation of its own design: the leniency that protects entity values suppresses detection of procedural corruptions (20% recall). A post-hoc fix does not close the gap, and an LLM-as-annotator study with written guidelines and adjudication shows the same blind spot. We release a preregistered protocol for extending the evaluation to expert agreement, risk-coverage, and cost on production traffic.

</details>


### [191] [Breaking Homogeneity: Diversifying Persona Sets for Creative LLM Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30492)
*Sang Bin Moon,Nicole Cho,Daniel Borrajo,Sumitra Ganesh,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文将人格多样化视为集合级条件问题，研究选择或生成人格、空间填充或前沿探索多样性两种设计选择，并实例化四种方法。评估表明，进化人格生成能显著提升开放式任务中的响应多样性和创造力，且可与提示优化互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在开放式任务中常产生同质化响应，这种同质性可能导致群体思维，使想法趋同于单一且可能次优的决策，因此需要研究如何通过人格多样化来促进发散输出。

Method: 将人格多样化形式化为集合级条件问题，考察两个正交设计维度：选择人格 vs. 生成人格，空间填充多样性 vs. 前沿寻求多样性。据此实现四类方法：覆盖和分散子集选择、均匀覆盖采样、进化人格生成。在 Alternative Uses Task、Infinity-Chat 和 Divergent Association Task 上评估多样性、原创性、灵活性、整体创造力与有效性等指标。

Result: 在 AUT 上，进化人格生成相比仅任务提示使响应多样性提升 78.8%、原创性提升 26.1%、灵活性提升 49.5%、整体创造力提升 13.9%，并保持 98.5% 有效性；在 Infinity-Chat 上，其人格诱导的响应分离度接近随机人格的两倍；与创造力优化提示结合后，进一步使响应多样性提升 18.6%、创造力提升 6.3%。

Conclusion: 人格集合几何是一种任务无关的机制，可用于激发大语言模型的发散输出；人格多样化可作为提示优化的可复用补充，用于缓解输出同质化并提升创造力。

Abstract: Language models often produce homogeneous responses to open-ended tasks; such homogeneity can spawn groupthink-the convergence of ideas toward a singular and potentially suboptimal decision. We formulate persona diversification as a set-level conditioning problem and study two orthogonal design choices: selecting versus generating personas, and space-filling versus frontier-seeking diversity. We instantiate this design space with four methods spanning coverage and dispersion subset selections, uniform-coverage sampling, and evolutionary persona generation. Evaluations on the Alternative Uses Task (AUT), Infinity-Chat, and Divergent Association Task (DAT) show the benefits of the proposed methods across tasks and creativity objectives. On AUT, evolutionary persona generation increases response diversity by 78.8%, originality by 26.1%, flexibility by 49.5%, and holistic creativity by 13.9% over task-only prompting, while maintaining 98.5% validity; on Infinity-Chat, it nearly doubles persona-induced response separation relative to random personas. Moreover, evolutionary personas compose with creativity-optimized prompting, further increasing its response diversity by 18.6% and creativity by 6.3%. These results establish persona-set geometry as a task-agnostic mechanism for eliciting divergent LLM outputs, and support persona diversification as a reusable complement to prompt optimization.

</details>


### [192] [Inquesto Score: A reliability Protocol For Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30514)
*Massa Baali,Bhiksha Raj*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Inquesto Score（IS）协议，用固定版本评估人群中成功完成呼叫目标且未发生功能失败或更严重问题的通话百分比来衡量语音代理可靠性，并发布协议、参考实现和评估记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音代理越来越多用于失败会影响交易、访问等后果性结果的工作流，因此需要可复现、可解释的可靠性评估方法。

Method: 定义显式失败事件和严重级别，直接评估已部署语音流水线；从音频测量抢话、响应延迟等时序失败，用场景谓词、工具轨迹和固定开源模型裁判评估语义与状态相关失败；诊断视图不并入总分。v0.1 评估30个场景、3种声学条件、4个说话人群体、每代理306次通话，覆盖参考系统的13种配置。

Result: 评估表明，可靠测量需要转录文本之外的证据，显式处理部署条件，并验证用于判定结果的评估器。

Conclusion: 发布协议、参考实现和评估记录，为语音代理可靠性提供可复现、可解释的评估基础。

Abstract: Voice agents are increasingly deployed in workflows where failed interactions can affect transactions, access, and other consequential outcomes, creating a need for reproducible and interpretable evaluation. We introduce Inquesto Score (IS), a protocol for measuring voice-agent reliability as the percentage of calls in a fixed, versioned evaluation population that achieve the caller's goal without a functional failure or worse. Rather than combining heterogeneous metrics, IS defines explicit failure events and severity levels and evaluates the deployed voice pipeline. Timing failures, including talk-over and delayed responses, are measured directly from audio, while semantic and state-dependent failures are evaluated using scenario predicates, tool traces, and a pinned open-model judge. Diagnostic views of behavior, acoustic robustness, identity handling, and speaker groups accompany the score without being combined into it. Inquesto Score v0.1 evaluates 30 scenarios, three acoustic conditions, four speaker groups, and 306 calls per agent across 13 configurations of a reference voice-agent system. Our evaluation shows that reliable measurement requires evidence beyond transcripts, explicit treatment of deployment conditions, and validation of the evaluators used to determine outcomes. We release the protocol, reference implementation, and evaluation records.

</details>


### [193] [Feeding BabyLMs Macaroni: Code-Switching Curricula Cause Cross-Lingual Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30535)
*Dries Rooryck,Alex Cai,Yonatan Belinkov,David Alvarez-Melis,Kianté Brantley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探索用代码转换文本预训练小型解码器语言模型，以促进多语言表示对齐。结果表明，词级到句级代码转换课程学习能提升多语言预训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言社区中儿童常进行代码转换，即在单个话语中使用多种语言；作者希望探究能否通过代码转换文本训练来诱导语言模型中的跨语言对齐。

Method: 在英语、荷兰语和中文BabyBabelLM混合而成的1亿词多语言语料上，预训练小型解码器Transformer；并用LLM生成词级和句级代码转换语料。进一步采用从词级代码转换、到句级代码转换、再到单语文档的学习课程。

Result: 基于代码转换数据的训练能对齐平行文本的表示，尤其是跨不同文字系统时，且这种对齐在后续单语文档训练中仍能保持；采用代码转换课程学习的模型在BabyLM评测套件上优于未使用代码转换数据的基线。

Conclusion: 代码转换课程学习是一种有效的多语言预训练数据增强方法。作者公开了代码、数据和模型。

Abstract: Children in multilingual communities often code-switch, using multiple languages in a single utterance. Can we induce cross-lingual alignment in language models by training on code-switched text? We pretrain small decoder-only transformers on two 100M-word multilingual corpora: a base corpus formed by mixing the English, Dutch, and Chinese BabyBabelLM datasets, and a corpus generated from it by inserting word- and sentence-level code-switching using an LLM. We find that training on code-switched data aligns the representations of parallel text, particularly across different scripts, and that this alignment persists through training on monolingual documents. Under a learning curriculum that progresses from word-level code-switching, to sentence-level code-switching, to monolingual documents, models trained on code-switched data outperform baselines trained without it on the BabyLM evaluation suite. Our work characterizes code-switching curriculum learning as an effective data augmentation method for multilingual pretraining. We release our code, data, and models at https://github.com/drooryck/multilingual-macaroni.

</details>


### [194] [REALMS: An AI-Assistant Conversational System for Real-Time Exact Audience Sizing over High-Dimensional Nested Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30547)
*Haixu Ma,Aditya Bansal,Shubham Lohiya,Sumit Ranjan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了REALMS，一个基于LLM的对话式受众规模精确估算系统，已在企业客户数据平台生产部署，支持自然语言查询百万级画像和数千属性，秒级返回精确计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统受众规模估算方法（如骨架受众、抽样或预测建模）存在显著延迟、估计误差和高维数据可扩展性差的问题，无法满足实时精确查询大规模画像数据的需求。

Method: REALMS包含三个关键组件：（1）基于嵌入向量搜索的分类属性检索机制，动态识别相关模式属性；（2）基于LLM的NL2SQL流水线，采用模板化上下文学习，在复杂嵌套模式上生成准确查询；（3）模式标准化，支持跨行业部署。

Result: 在真实企业数据上的评估显示，属性检索召回率高，SQL执行准确率高，延迟低，能够实现实时交互式受众洞察，而此前方法需要数小时。

Conclusion: REALMS能够通过自然语言在秒级内精确估算大规模画像受众，实现实时交互式受众洞察，并具备跨行业部署能力。

Abstract: Audience sizing is a critical component of digital marketing. It enables precise resource allocation, campaign planning, and performance optimization. Traditional approaches using skeleton audiences, sampling, or predictive modeling suffer from significant delays, estimation errors, and poor scalability over high-dimensional profile data. We present REALMS (Real-time Exact Audience sizing via LLM-based Multi-attribute Search), a conversational system for exact audience sizing deployed in production on an enterprise customer data platform. REALMS enables marketers to query massive profile stores with millions of profiles and thousands of attributes using natural language and receive precise counts in seconds. The system introduces three key components: (1) a categorical attribute retrieval mechanism using embedding-based vector search to dynamically identify relevant schema attributes without manual configuration; (2) an LLM-powered NL2SQL pipeline with template-based in-context learning for accurate query generation over complex nested schemas; and (3) schema standardization enabling industry-agnostic deployment across diverse enterprise environments. Evaluation on real enterprise data demonstrates strong recall for attribute retrieval, high SQL execution accuracy, and low latency, which enables real-time interactive audience insights where prior methods required hours.

</details>


### [195] [Probing Stability-Plasticity Tradeoffs in Agent Memory through Cognitive Experimental Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30558)
*Jiaqi Ding,Guorong Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 MemProbe，一个受认知科学启发的诊断框架，用于评估智能体记忆系统中的稳定性-可塑性权衡，将记忆评测从最终答案准确率拆解为可解释的行为画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统评测常将记忆行为简化为最终答案准确率，无法揭示系统在何时应更新、保留或对记忆持不确定态度等关键差异；认知记忆研究表明记忆是重构性的，并受干扰、来源可靠性、强化和再激活影响。

Method: MemProbe 将认知科学洞见转化为四个可复用实验范式：干扰、错误信息、巩固强度和再巩固窗口；并将正确性分解为更新、保留、归因和时间组织等行为画像。作者在 56 个 episode 的诊断套件中实例化这些范式，并在统一协议下评估六种增量记忆系统。

Result: 总体得分相近的系统表现出明显不同的行为画像，说明聚合指标会掩盖记忆维护策略上的差异。

Conclusion: MemProbe 提供了诊断视角，可将智能体的聚合性能转化为随时间变化且可解释的记忆维护画像，用于揭示稳定性-可塑性权衡。

Abstract: Agent memory systems are increasingly used to maintain long-term user preferences, task states and evolving facts, but current evaluations often collapse memory behavior into final-answer accuracy. We introduce MemProbe, a cognitive-science-inspired framework for diagnosing stability-plasticity tradeoffs in agent memory. The framework is motivated by a core insight from cognitive memory research: memory is reconstructive and shaped by interference, source reliability, reinforcement, and reactivation. MemProbe turns this insight into four reusable experimental paradigms (interference, misinformation, consolidation strength, and reconsolidation window) that manipulate when a memory should be updated, preserved, or treated as uncertain. It further decomposes correctness into behavioral profiles that reveal how systems update, preserve, attribute, and temporally organize information. We instantiate these paradigms in a 56-episode diagnostic suite and evaluate six incremental memory systems under a unified protocol. Results show that systems with similar aggregate scores exhibit distinct behavioral profiles. MemProbe provides such a diagnostic lens, turning aggregate performance into interpretable profiles of memory maintenance over time. Code is available at https://github.com/jq-ding/MemProbe.

</details>


### [196] [The Hard Part Comes After Search: Benchmarking Web Agents on Synthesizing, Organizing, and Displaying Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30604)
*Alexander Gill,Md Farhan Ishmam,Xuyen Nguyen,Neha Bhat,Parker Henry DeYoung,Fateme Hashemi Chaleshtori,Nathan Stringham,Kenneth Marino,Ana Marasović*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 KNOWS 基准，用于评估计算机使用智能体作为助手完成开放式、复杂浏览器任务的能力，任务最终需产出文档、演示文稿或电子表格等工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体基准未充分评估智能体作为助手的能力。有用助手需在复杂多步工作流中检索信息、综合成工件，并操作程序界面，这要求推理、任务分解以及视觉空间理解。

Method: 构建 KNOWS 基准，包含开放式、复杂、基于浏览器的任务，每个任务以生成工件为目标。开发任务设计准则与协议来确保任务质量，并为每个任务配备评估器，结合确定性检查和 LLM 判断。评估前沿计算机使用智能体与浏览器框架。

Result: 前沿智能体在部分成功指标上得分中等，但最佳者在少于 3% 的复杂长时程任务中完全成功。视觉步骤失败会导致工件不可用，即使智能体完成超过 50% 的其他评估步骤。

Conclusion: 当前智能体尚不能胜任端到端助手任务，暴露了工具使用、视觉理解和长时程推理方面的局限，亟需进一步进展。

Abstract: Existing computer-use agent benchmarks do not fully evaluate agents acting as assistants. A useful assistant retrieves information across complex, multi-step workflows, synthesizes it into artifacts (documents, presentations, spreadsheets), and navigates program interfaces to produce a coherent final product. Such workflows demand reasoning and synthesis, decomposition of complex tasks, as well as visual and spatial understanding. To study agents on workflows like these, we introduce KNOWS, a benchmark of open-ended, complex, browser-based tasks that jointly evaluate these capabilities, with each task culminating in a produced artifact. To write tasks, we develop a task design rubric and a protocol for ensuring that tasks meet the requirements. Each task is paired with an evaluator, a program that combines deterministic checks with LLM judgments to balance the richness, reliability, and automation tradeoff inherent to agent evaluation. We evaluate and analyze frontier computer-use agents and browser-based harnesses. They achieve moderate scores on partial-success metrics, but the best performer fully succeeds in fewer than 3% of our complex, long-horizon tasks. Failures on visual steps render the resulting artifacts unusable, even when agents complete more than 50% of other evaluation steps. Our results expose limitations of current agents acting as end-to-end assistants, and call for progress on tool use, visual understanding, and long-horizon reasoning.

</details>


### [197] [Recursive Self-Improvement via On-Policy Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30652)
*Shangjian Yin,Zehao Zhao,Kavosh Asadi,Rui Liu,Yuchen Lu,Shike Mei,Hang Cui,Luke Simon,Zhouxing Shi,Hamed Firooz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对在线策略自蒸馏中冻结教师无法吸收学生改进的问题，提出动态共同进化（DCE）与自精炼简洁学习（SRCL）的递归框架，并在多个数学基准上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD无需外部教师，但冻结特权教师虽有助于训练稳定，却无法随学生进步而更新，限制蒸馏效果；同时更强的修订可能使回答冗长、过度自我批评。

Method: 在OPSD中让特权教师与学生共同进化（DCE），使每轮学到的修订指导下一轮；并额外训练模型自身在线策略响应的更短、已验证重写（SRCL），作为互补目标以控制冗长。

Result: 在多种模型规模和四个竞赛级数学基准上，DCE+SRCL优于OPSD。在Qwen3-8B上达到65.97% Average@12，比OPSD高35.62个百分点，且相比仅DCE平均输出长度减少7.80%。

Conclusion: 动态共进化与自精炼简洁学习能有效突破冻结教师限制，在提升数学推理性能的同时缓解冗长问题。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model by having it generate trajectories, then matching its next-token predictions with an external teacher's next-token predictions. This provides dense, token-level supervision to the student. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) eliminates the need for the external teacher. Specifically, a second frozen copy of the student model, now given the ground truth in its context, serves as the teacher. The student model only receives the problem and learns to mimic the privileged teacher model, while the teacher remains frozen throughout training. Previous work showed that freezing the teacher is useful for training stability, but we argue that this can prevent the teacher from incorporating the improvements learned by the student during training. Our primary contribution is to address this limitation with a recursive framework built around two complementary components. First, we let the privileged teacher co-evolve with the student so that revision learned in one round can guide the next, a process we refer to as Dynamic Co-Evolution (DCE). Second, because stronger revision can also make responses too verbose and self-critical, we additionally train on shorter, verified rewrites of the model's own on-policy responses. We call this complementary objective Self-Refined Concise Learning (SRCL). Overall, our comprehensive evaluations show that DCE+SRCL outperforms OPSD across multiple model scales and four competition-level mathematics benchmarks. Specifically, on Qwen3-8B, DCE+SRCL reaches 65.97% Average@12, outperforming OPSD by 35.62 percentage points while reducing mean output length by 7.80% relative to DCE alone.

</details>


### [198] [TRACE: Temporal Audit and Condition-aware Evaluation of Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30670)
*Yibo Ma,Qianqian Zhang,Peng Liu,Tiancheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TRACE条件感知基准与评估框架，用于流式视频理解，显式建模证据时序、历史维护和响应触发，并将性能报告为执行条件下的系统行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前流式视频理解评估常只报告任务分数，未说明证据何时有效、视觉历史如何维护、响应如何触发，导致相似分数可能对应不同工作负载、失效模式和运行行为。

Method: TRACE结合时间审计视觉任务、证据时序和指令相关触发标注；采用统一因果Core-Adapter协议控制信息可用性，并记录实际历史处理与响应事件；进行答案质量、及时性、响应选择、工作负载、完成度和可靠性等多维报告。在517个视频的1240条记录上，评估8个公开模型或系统的8种配置。

Result: 近乎相同的QA准确率可能掩盖完成度、答案有效性和生成工作负载的显著差异；主动性能可分解为响应质量、响应延迟、误报和漏检目标窗口。

Conclusion: 流式视频性能应被理解为执行条件约束下的系统行为，而非单一分数。基准和代码已开源。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires models to interpret evidence as it arrives, yet current evaluations often report task scores without specifying when evidence becomes valid, how visual history is maintained, or how responses are triggered. As a result, similar scores may correspond to different workloads, failure modes, and operational behavior. We introduce TRACE (Temporal Audit and Condition-aware Evaluation), a condition-aware benchmark and evaluation framework that makes these factors explicit. TRACE combines temporally audited visual tasks with evidence timing and instruction-dependent trigger annotations, a unified causal Core--Adapter protocol that controls information availability while recording actual history processing and response events, and multidimensional reporting of answer quality, timeliness, response-selection behavior, workload, completion, and reliability. On 1,240 records from 517 videos, we evaluate eight publicly available models or systems in eight configurations. We find that nearly identical QA accuracy can mask substantial differences in completion, answer validity, and generation workload, while proactive performance separates into response quality, response delay, false alarms (responses emitted while no target window is currently valid and a later one remains), and missed target windows. These results show that streaming-video performance should be interpreted as execution-conditioned system behavior rather than a single score. Our benchmark and code can be accessed at \href{https://github.com/om-ai-lab/trace-bench}{https://github.com/om-ai-lab/trace-bench}.

</details>


### [199] [Words Speak Louder Than Order: A Behavioral Evaluation of Gemma 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30716)
*Amanda Fitch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过反平衡行为实验测试 Gemma 4-e4b 在收到相互冲突文档时如何取舍，发现来源框架比阅读顺序影响更大，且首因效应受表面措辞和结构重复显著调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型面对两份冲突文档时，是依赖来源框架还是文档呈现顺序来决定优先采用哪一份信息。

Method: 在 Google 预训练 Gemma 4-e4b 模型上进行行为测试，使用 n=13 个项目、784 次前向传播、短单轮上下文和完全反平衡实验设计，以数学上分离来源框架与阅读位置效应，并抵消模型自然词汇偏差。

Result: 十个测试条件显示：1）来源框架显著压过阅读位置；2）模型偏好先读到的文档，但首因效应强度会因表面措辞变化至少 5 倍；3）位置偏差主要由整体结构重复驱动，而非“is [Answer]”等短复制线索；当两份文档逐字模板一致时首因效应增强，措辞变化则减弱位置偏差。

Conclusion: 模型对冲突文档的优先级判断更受语义来源框架影响，而非单纯呈现顺序；首因效应存在但不稳定，且与文档间结构重复程度密切相关。

Abstract: When a language model receives two conflicting documents as input, how does it decide which one to prioritize? Does it rely on how the sources are framed or the presentation order of the documents? We evaluated this behavior on Google's pre-trained Gemma 4-e4b model across a targeted behavioral suite (n = 13 items, 784 forward passes in short, single-turn contexts) using a completely counterbalanced experimental design. This setup allowed us to mathematically isolate the specific effects of source framing and reading position, while ensuring the model's natural vocabulary biases were canceled out.
  Across ten test conditions, we discovered the following:
  1. Source framing heavily overpowers reading position. When directly competing, the semantic framing of a source (such as presenting it as an official guideline or a fresh update) had a significantly stronger impact on the model's final answer than the presentation order of the document.
  2. The model favors the first document it reads, but this bias is highly variable. While the model consistently demonstrated a primacy effect (preferring the first document presented), the actual strength of this bias fluctuated by at least a factor of 5 based solely on the surface wording.
  3. Overall structural repetition, not short copy-cues, drives positional bias. The model's preference for the first document is not a mechanical reaction to short, repetitive trigger phrases, such as "is [Answer]". However, the primacy effect does increase significantly when the two competing documents are structurally identical, using word-for-word verbatim templates. Introducing variation in the overall wording between the two sources reduces this positional bias.

</details>


### [200] [SEA-CLIP-Tiny: Efficient Multilingual Text-Vision Embedding for Southeast Asian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30739)
*Puja Ahmad Habibi,Faiz Assabil Firdaus,Ashvanth S,Ekapol Chuangsuwanich,Pume Tuchinda,Peerat Limkonchotiwat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SEA-CLIP-Tiny，一个面向东南亚语言、参数少于50M的紧凑多语言图文嵌入模型；在7种东南亚语言上取得所评估学生模型中最强的平均检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言图文嵌入模型对跨语言图文检索很重要，但东南亚语言因语言多样性高、数据和算力资源有限而支持不足。

Method: 将 CLIP-KD 式框架适配到东南亚多语言场景，通过区域数据整理和多语言教师指导，训练紧凑的学生模型 SEA-CLIP-Tiny。

Result: 在7种东南亚语言上，平均检索性能达到 R@1 12.9%、R@5 31.5%、R@10 42.2%；相比 MobileCLIP2，平均 R@10 提升12.1个百分点，参数减少38.4%，CPU延迟也更低。

Conclusion: 区域感知训练对东南亚高效多语言图文模型至关重要。

Abstract: Multilingual text-vision embedding models are essential for cross-lingual image-text retrieval, but Southeast Asian languages remain poorly supported due to the region's linguistic diversity and limited data and computing resources. In this paper, we introduce SEA-CLIP-Tiny, a compact multilingual text-vision embedding model for Southeast Asia with fewer than 50M parameters. Our model adapts a CLIP-KD-style framework to Southeast Asian multilingual settings through regional data curation and multilingual teacher guidance. Experiments across seven Southeast Asian languages show that SEA-CLIP-Tiny achieves the strongest average retrieval performance among the evaluated student models, reaching 12.9%, 31.5%, and 42.2% at R@1, R@5, and R@10, respectively. Compared with MobileCLIP2, it improves average R@10 by 12.1 points while using 38.4% fewer parameters and lower measured CPU latency. These results highlight the importance of region-aware training for efficient multilingual text-vision models in Southeast Asia.

</details>


### [201] [Beyond Mean Attention: Diversity-Aware, Layer-Wise Scoring for KV Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30738)
*Tianfang Xie,Wei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文研究 KV cache 淘汰中的 token 排序评分，在均值注意力上加入注意力离散度与已选 token 冗余项，并检验相关性-多样性平衡是否应随层深变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SnapKV、PyramidKV 等方法仅用小观察窗的均值注意力排序 token，忽略注意力分散和 token 间冗余，且可能不同层需要不同的相关性-多样性权衡。

Method: 提出统一评分 μ_i+λ1σ_i+λ2corr(i,S)；当 λ2<0 时惩罚与已选 token 的相似性，类似 MMR，且无需额外前向。比较固定全局系数与三段、二次深度 profile，仅在开发集上用 sinh 重参数化搜索深度 profile，并在 Mistral-7B 与 16 个英文 LongBench 数据集上测试。

Result: 每层预算 64 时，单一全局多样化常数在 16 个数据集中提升 13 个，macro +1.1；预算 32 仍有效，128 时增益收窄。逐数据集搜索在多数数据集中未发现明显层结构，但在 passage retrieval 上发现显著中层符号翻转：奖励相似性，预算 64 比 baseline +9.6，预算 128 比全局常数 +13.2。消融表明增益主要来自冗余项，重放所有被接受的搜索状态可区分真实结构与调参噪声。

Conclusion: 相关性-多样性平衡通常用单一全局常数即可获得稳定收益，深度自适应主要在 passage retrieval 等特定任务中显著有效；冗余项是关键，且需通过重放实验区分真实层结构与调参噪声。

Abstract: KV cache eviction methods such as SnapKV and PyramidKV rank tokens solely by mean attention over a small observation window. We study a unified score, $μ_i+λ_1σ_i+λ_2\mathrm{corr}(i,S)$, adding attention dispersion across window queries and redundancy relative to selected tokens. For $λ_2<0$, the score penalizes similarity to selected tokens as in maximal marginal relevance (MMR), without extra forward passes. To test whether this relevance-diversity balance should vary with depth, we compare fixed global coefficients with three-segment and quadratic profiles. Only these depth profiles are searched on a development split under a $\sinh$ reparameterization. On all 16 English LongBench datasets with Mistral-7B at a budget of 64 entries per layer, a single global diversification constant improves 13 of 16 datasets (macro +1.1); the gain holds at budget 32 and narrows at 128. Per-dataset search finds no detectable layer structure on most datasets; on passage retrieval it finds a large one: a mid-layer sign flip that rewards similarity and is worth +9.6 over the baseline at budget 64 and, without re-tuning, +13.2 over the global constant at budget 128. Ablations attribute the gain to the redundancy term; replaying every accepted search state on the held-out test set separates genuine structure from tuning noise.

</details>


### [202] [Understanding the Role of Prompt Template in Knowledge Distillation for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30802)
*Anjila Budathoki,Manish Dhakal,Benjamin M. Ampel,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了知识蒸馏中提示模板选择对学生模型已有安全对齐的影响，发现使用聊天模板会显著削弱安全对齐，而非聊天模板能更好保留原有安全表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明SFT阶段提示模板会影响后续安全对齐鲁棒性，但知识蒸馏中教师到学生模板选择对已有安全对齐的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 作者在不同模板配置下进行知识蒸馏，分析对学生模型安全对齐的影响，并在LLaMA、Gemma和Qwen三个模型家族及多个安全基准上评估，同时比较内部表示变化。

Result: 安全对齐在已对齐的指令微调基础模型上出现显著退化；聊天模板比非聊天模板使模型更易顺从有害查询，且该现象在三个模型家族中一致；非聊天模板蒸馏更好保留基础学生模型的内部表示，聊天模板则导致更大表示偏移。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏中的提示模板选择会显著影响学生模型的安全对齐和内部表示，非聊天模板更有利于保持原有安全对齐。

Abstract: Prior research has demonstrated that the choice of prompt template during Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) significantly impacts the robustness of safety alignment afterwards. However, the influence of template selection during Knowledge Distillation (KD) from teacher to student remains largely unexplored. Thus, we fill this gap by analyzing how different template configurations influence the pre-existing safety alignment of the student. We observe a significant degradation of safety alignment present in the aligned base instruct-tuned model. Specifically, we find that utilizing chat templates renders the model more compliant with harmful queries compared to a non-chat template. These findings are consistent across three models: LLaMA, Gemma and Qwen model families and are evaluated across multiple safety benchmarks. We further show that using a non-chat template during distillation better preserves the base student's internal representations, while chat template distillation induces a larger representational shift. Code: https://github.com/anjilab/role-of-prompt-template-in-kd

</details>


### [203] [Learning Natural Conversational Behavior in Tandem Speech-to-Speech Models with Randomized Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30773)
*Manato Yaguchi,Yotaro Kubo,Hikaru Asano,So Kuroki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出随机中间引导方法，用于训练KAME这类串联式语音到语音模型，使其在真实对话数据上无需模拟后端LLM即可学习利用后端信息，从而兼顾回复质量与自然对话行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 串联式语音到语音架构中，LLM后端会在用户说话时向前端提供候选回复作为引导。但普通对话录音只包含最终回复，不包含后端在用户说话期间应提供的引导；若用模拟LLM生成这些引导，会带来大量数据准备开销。

Method: 提出随机中间引导：不模拟后端LLM，而是直接从对话语料中构造引导。训练时，目标回复提供有信息量的引导，随机采样的回复则提供可能不相关的更新，以此促使语音前端学会有选择地利用后端信息。

Result: 在合成对话上，采用该训练方法的KAME回复质量与LLM生成引导、相似度引导基线相当；在3.8k小时真实对话上训练后，相比仅用合成数据训练的KAME，改善了轮流说话的流畅性和音频评判的自然度，同时相对Moshi仍保持回复质量优势。

Conclusion: 随机引导是一种实用路径，可将串联模型的回复质量优势与从真实语音中学到的自然对话行为结合起来。

Abstract: Tandem speech-to-speech architectures couple a responsive speech frontend with an asynchronous text backend. In KAME, a large language model (LLM) serves as the backend, supplying candidate responses as guidance to the speech frontend while the user is still speaking. Ordinary conversation recordings capture the eventual response but not the guidance the backend would supply during the user's utterance. Generating the missing guidance with a simulator LLM adds substantial data-preparation overhead when training on real conversations. We propose randomized intermediate guidance, which derives guidance directly from the conversation corpus rather than simulating backend LLM behavior. During training, target responses provide informative guidance, while randomly sampled responses provide potentially irrelevant updates during the utterance. This combination aims to teach the frontend to use backend information selectively. On synthetic dialogues, KAME trained with this recipe achieves response quality comparable to that of the LLM-generated and similarity-based baselines. Training on 3.8k hours of real conversations improves smooth turn-taking and audio-judge naturalness over synthetic-data KAME while retaining a response-quality advantage over Moshi. These results show that randomized guidance offers a practical route to combining the response-quality benefits of tandem models with natural conversational behavior learned from real speech.

</details>


### [204] [Enhancing Assessment of Self-Consistency in LLM Explanations using Perturbation Strength](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30849)
*Phuong Q. Le,Kemal Kurniawan,Jey Han Lau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出用LLM作为评判者来统一衡量输入扰动和思维链扰动的强度，并在受控强度下评估不同LLM生成解释的自洽性，发现输入扰动通常比思维链扰动影响更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作使用表面级扰动方法研究LLM生成解释的自洽性，但未明确测量和控制扰动强度，导致不同扰动类型之间难以公平比较。

Method: 提出一种基于LLM-as-a-judge的扰动强度度量方法，统一评估输入扰动和CoT扰动的强度；在控制扰动强度的条件下，比较多种LLM生成解释的自洽性。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的基于LLM的扰动强度度量优于基于嵌入和概率的方法；输入扰动通常比CoT扰动对LLM的影响更强。

Conclusion: 关于模型自洽性的判断只有在相同扰动类型下才公平，跨扰动类型的自洽性比较需要控制扰动强度。

Abstract: Prior work has examined the self-consistency of LLM-generated explanations using surface-level perturbation methods. However, the strength of these perturbations is not explicitly measured and controlled. In this work, we propose an LLM-as-a-judge approach to measure perturbation strength in a unified manner across input and CoT perturbations. We then evaluate the self-consistency in explanations generated from various LLMs under controlled strength conditions, ensuring a fair comparison across perturbation types. Experiments show that our proposed LLM-based perturbation strength measure outperforms other embedding- and probability-based approaches and that input perturbations generally affect LLMs more strongly than CoT perturbations. Our work suggests that judgments about a model's self-consistency is fair only within the same perturbation type.

</details>


### [205] [I-Parakeet: Integer-Only Conformer ASR on Mobile NPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30846)
*Taichi Nishimura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 I-Parakeet，这是 NVIDIA Parakeet-CTC（0.6B 参数）的纯整数实现，可在智能手机 NPU 上运行，无需任何浮点算子或 CPU 回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 Conformer ASR 模型因体积大而难以部署到边缘设备，且量化模型在数值敏感操作上仍会回退到浮点，无法充分利用移动 NPU 等整数加速器。

Method: 作者提出三项贡献：1）为 Conformer 核心的相对位置自注意力推导整数形式，将两个不同量化尺度的得分分支与相对位移融合为纯整数操作；2）引入最小化最大误差的 minimax 优化 Swish 近似；3）通过逐层激活范围分析，对 BatchNorm 输出采用 INT16 网格，并对重尾的预编码器激活采用百分位校准。

Result: I-Parakeet 在 LibriSpeech test-other 上达到 4.97% WER，在 Qualcomm NPU 上实时因子为 0.048，比 CPU 基线快 7.5 倍。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过整数化注意力、激活近似和范围校准，Conformer ASR 模型可以完全以纯整数形式部署到移动 NPU 上，并保持较高识别精度与显著加速。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose I-Parakeet, an integer-only implementation of NVIDIA's Parakeet-CTC (0.6B parameters) that runs on a smartphone NPU without any floating-point operator or CPU fallback. Modern Conformer ASR models are hard to deploy on edge devices because of their size, and quantized models still fall back to floating point for numerically sensitive operations. This prevents them from fully exploiting integer accelerators such as mobile NPUs. To achieve this, our contributions are threefold. First, we derive an integer formulation of the relative-positional self-attention at the core of the Conformer. We fuse its two score branches with different quantization scales and the relative shift into integer-only operations. Second, we introduce a minimax-optimized Swish approximation that minimizes the maximum error of the Swish output. Third, a layer-wise range analysis of activations yields two targeted remedies: an INT16 grid for the BatchNorm output and percentile calibration for the heavy-tailed pre-encoder activations. I-Parakeet achieves 4.97% WER on LibriSpeech test-other, running on a Qualcomm NPU at a real-time factor of 0.048, 7.5x faster than a CPU baseline.

</details>


### [206] [Evidence-Grounded Auditing of Identification Assumptions in Climate-Policy Causal Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30867)
*Yonghong Zhang,Yong Xie,Isabel M. Parra,Ricardo Correia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出ARGUS，一个结构化语言模型流程，用于审计双重差分（DID）气候政策研究中的识别假设证据。它基于11维“假设-含义-证据”标准，在无法检索到相关证据时弃权，并生成与证据关联的风险报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DID广泛用于评估气候政策，但其识别假设是否得到证据支持难以评估。现有方法（如关键词流程）检测能力有限，因此需要更系统、可追溯的审计工具。

Method: 构建ARGUS语言模型流程，按11维“假设-含义-证据”评分标准审计报告证据，并在证据不可检索时弃权。评估包括：注入缺陷基准、26篇经济学论文，以及由两名标注者协调标签的5篇论文试点。

Result: 在11类缺陷基准中，ARGUS检测出73%的植入缺陷，关键词流程为18%。在26篇经济学论文中，约40%的“论文-维度”评估因无法检索证据而弃权。在5篇试点中，完成的33项评估里有25项给出的风险等级高于标签；预先固定的规则可在样本内消除大部分差异，但加权一致性仍较低。

Conclusion: ARGUS可提供证据关联的风险报告，定位潜在薄弱点以供专家审查，但不裁决因果主张。

Abstract: Difference-in-differences (DID) studies are widely used to evaluate climate policy, but assessing the evidence supporting their identification assumptions remains challenging. We introduce ARGUS, a structured language-model pipeline that audits reported evidence against an eleven-dimension assumption-implication-evidence rubric and abstains when relevant evidence cannot be retrieved. We evaluate ARGUS using injected flaws, economics papers, and a small pilot with reconciled labels. On the 11-flaw benchmark, ARGUS detects 73% of planted flaws, compared with 18% for a keyword-based pipeline. Across 26 economics papers, ARGUS abstains on about 40% of paper-dimension assessments for lack of retrievable evidence. In a five-paper pilot with labels reconciled by two annotators, it assigns a higher risk level than the labels on 25 of the 33 assessments it completes. A rule fixed before the labels arrived removes most of this in-sample; weighted agreement stays low. ARGUS provides evidence-linked risk reports that localize potential weaknesses for expert review, without adjudicating causal claims. Code and data: https://github.com/yonghongzhang-io/ARGUS

</details>


### [207] [Persistent Negatives for Adversarial Black-Box On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30864)
*Haixu Ma,Saad Lahrichi,Weiwei Li,Kevin Han,Weiqiang Wu,Peggy Yang,Dongzhuo Li,Ruiyi Li,Serena Li,Gedi Zhou,Mingze Gao,Abhishek Kumar,Xiangjun Fan,Lizhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出持久负样本对抗蒸馏，用于黑盒在线策略蒸馏。它通过维护历史教师-学生比较池来缓解判别器负样本分布随策略更新而变化的问题，并在多个基准上取得更好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒在线策略蒸馏中，教师只提供采样回复而不提供token概率。对抗蒸馏用判别器区分教师与学生回复，并将分数作为策略奖励，但每步从最新学生采样负样本会使奖励依赖不断变化的负分布，形成移动目标问题。

Method: 提出persistent-negative adversarial distillation：在每个判别器批次中用历史、提示匹配的教师-学生比较替换一部分样本；GRPO仍使用新鲜学生回复保持on-policy。理论分析表明贝叶斯最优奖励是教师到负样本的对数密度比，并在显式假设下说明持久负样本可锚定判别器、降低奖励估计MSE。

Result: 在两个学生家族、三个评判器和四个对话基准上，匹配判别器计算量时，该方法一致优于现有方法；同时产生更平滑的新策略判别器轨迹，低于随机概率的下降更少。

Conclusion: 判别器的负样本分布是黑盒在线策略蒸馏中的一个重要设计维度，持久负样本对抗蒸馏能有效提升黑盒OPD性能。

Abstract: Black-box On-Policy Distillation (OPD) seeks to improve a student from its own generations when the teacher provides sampled responses but not token probabilities. Adversarial distillation offers one route: it learns a discriminator over prompt-matched teacher and student responses and uses its score as the policy reward. However, sampling discriminator negatives from the latest student at each step couples the learned reward to a negative distribution that changes after every policy update. We address this moving-target problem with persistent-negative adversarial distillation, a live-pool method that replaces a fraction of each discriminator batch with historical, prompt-matched teacher--student comparisons. Under matched discriminator compute, historical comparisons train the discriminator, while GRPO remains on-policy with fresh student responses. Our analysis identifies the Bayes-optimal reward as a teacher-to-negative log-density ratio and, under explicit assumptions, shows how persistent negatives anchor the discriminator and reduce reward-estimation MSE relative to fresh-negative training. Across two student families, three judges, and four judged-chat benchmarks, persistent-negative adversarial distillation consistently improves performance over current methods at matched discriminator compute. It also yields smoother fresh-policy discriminator trajectories, with fewer below-chance dips. These findings identify the discriminator's negative distribution as an important design axis in black-box on-policy distillation.

</details>


### [208] [Effects of Transcript Compression on LLM-based Medical Misinformation Detection in Japanese YouTube Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30882)
*Yuya Wake,Sho Tsugawa,Toshiyuki Amagasa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究考察转录文本压缩如何影响LLM对日本医学YouTube长视频的真伪分类。比较完整转录、摘要、RAPTOR-RAG和筛选四种输入设计，发现完整转录效果最好，压缩输入会增加假视频被误判为真的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于评估长时医学视频，但其效果可能取决于提供完整转录还是经摘要、检索或句子筛选压缩后的转录，因此需要研究转录压缩对医学虚假信息检测的影响。

Method: 使用74个标注为Real或Fake的日本医学YouTube长视频，比较四种转录输入：完整转录、LLM生成摘要、基于RAPTOR的检索增强生成（RAG）、提取候选医学健康句子的Screening。评估分类性能，并用J-LIWC、模糊表达及机构/技术术语分析语言变化。

Result: 完整转录基线表现最佳；所有压缩输入都增加假阴性，即Fake视频更易被误判为Real。摘要导致最大性能下降；Screening在压缩输入中最好，但仍遗漏许多医学相关句子。语言分析显示错误并非简单由确定性增加解释；摘要减少情感、社会、时间、认知和会话线索，摘要与RAG使机构和技术术语更突出。

Conclusion: 转录压缩可能使Fake视频表现为更连贯、更权威的输入，从而削弱虚假信息检测所需的线索。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to assess long-form medical videos, but their effectiveness may depend on whether transcripts are provided in full or compressed through summarization, retrieval, or claim screening. This study examines how such transcript compression affects LLM-based veracity classification of Japanese medical YouTube videos. We compare four transcript input designs: full transcripts, LLM-generated summaries, RAPTOR-based retrievalaugmented generation (RAG), and Screening, which extracts candidate medical and health-related sentences. Using 74 long-form videos labeled as Real or Fake, we evaluate classification performance and analyze linguistic changes using J-LIWC, hedge expressions, and institutional or technical terms. The full-transcript Baseline achieved the best performance, whereas all compressed inputs increased false negatives, meaning that Fake videos were more likely to be misclassified as Real. Summary caused the largest performance drop, while Screening performed best among the compressed inputs but still omitted many medically relevant sentences. Linguistic analyses showed that these errors were not explained by a simple increase in certainty. Instead, Summary reduced affective, social, temporal, cognitive, and conversational cues, while Summary and RAG made institutional and technical terms more salient. These findings suggest that transcript compression can represent Fake videos as more coherent and authoritative inputs, thereby weakening cues needed for misinformation detection

</details>


### [209] [ToolSearcher: Optimizing Tool Selection at Scale via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30906)
*Zhenlong Dai,Xujie Song,Zitong Wang,Tong Niu,Jian liu,Weiqiang Wang,Xiu Tang,Sai Wu,Chang Yao,Jingyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文关注大规模工具选择问题，提出面向智能体强化学习的 ToolSearcher 框架，以提升大语言模型在多轮搜索与复杂工具组合中的工具选择能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具学习通常假设工具集合较小或预先定义，而真实工具库规模庞大且工具功能多样。大语言模型受上下文长度限制，难以有效搜索、区分和组合工具，因此大规模工具选择成为智能体强化学习的新挑战。

Method: 提出 ToolSearcher 强化学习框架，包含三项关键设计：类别约束的工具区分，提升模型区分功能相似工具的能力；事件级搜索建模，显式优化多轮搜索中目标工具的发现；轨迹对齐的信用分配，为搜索-选择过程的不同阶段提供细粒度奖励信号。

Result: 在大规模工具选择基准上的大量实验表明，在涉及迭代搜索和复杂工具组合的挑战性设置中，ToolSearcher 持续优于多种强基线方法。

Conclusion: ToolSearcher 能够有效应对大规模工具选择中的搜索与区分难题，说明面向多轮搜索和细粒度优化的强化学习方法在构建可行动智能体方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at natural language processing but struggle to interact with external environments. Tool learning provides a promising way to extend LLMs into actionable agents, where tool selection is a critical prerequisite for successful tool use. Existing work often assumes a small or predefined set of tools, leaving large-scale tool selection underexplored. Real-world repositories contain a vast and diverse array of tools, making it difficult for LLMs to effectively search, distinguish, and compose tools under context-length constraints. We identify large-scale tool selection as a new challenge for agentic reinforcement learning, highlighting that existing RL methods for knowledge-based question answering are inadequate for selecting tools while considering compatibility. To address this challenge, we propose ToolSearcher, a novel RL framework for effective multi-turn search and fine-grained optimization in large-scale tool selection. Specifically, we introduce category-constrained tool discrimination to improve the model's ability to distinguish functionally similar tools, event-level search modeling to explicitly optimize the discovery of target tools during multi-turn search, and trajectory-aligned credit allocation to provide fine-grained reward signals for different stages of the search-selection process. Extensive experiments on large-scale tool selection benchmarks demonstrate that ToolSearcher consistently outperforms a set of strong baselines in challenging settings involving iterative search and complex tool composition.

</details>


### [210] [From annotation to reasoning: Culture in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30897)
*Daniel Hershcovich,Alexander Conroy,Jens Bjerring-Hansen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文探讨如何评估语言模型在存在多种合理解释时的文化理解能力，主张以文学阐释为场景，通过证据驱动基准、保留学术分歧的评估及模型开发实验，推动超越传统指标的文化稳健性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文化基准多考察事实知识、与调查答案一致或识别预设含义，无法检验模型能否解释特定文本中的文化指涉、用证据支持解读并在批评后修正，即缺乏对‘阐释深度’的评估。

Method: 提出以文学阐释特别是文化指涉与再利用为研究对象，关联证据中心基准、保留学者分歧的评估方法，以及在文学数据、语境资源和学者反馈上的模型开发实验；以丹麦文学为具体起点。

Result: 论文是一项立场/框架性研究，未报告具体实证结果；其核心主张是文学学者可在不同意某解读的同时承认其证据支持质量，并据此设计评估。

Conclusion: 目标是发展超越常规基准指标的替代评估策略，引导模型开发走向AI系统的文化稳健性，并可推广至其他语言和领域。

Abstract: How should we evaluate language models when more than one interpretation can be right? Cultural benchmarks often test factual knowledge, agreement with survey responses, or recognition of a predefined meaning. These tasks leave open whether a model can explain how a cultural reference works in a particular text, support a reading with evidence, or revise it after criticism. This is a question of interpretive depth, complementary to the breadth of cultural coverage. We argue that literary interpretation offers a useful setting for studying these capabilities. We focus on cultural referencing and reuse: how texts invoke, repeat, and transform earlier expressions across historical and linguistic contexts. Our central claim is that literary scholars can disagree about an interpretation while recognizing the quality of its support. We propose linking evidence-centered benchmarks, evaluation that preserves scholarly disagreement, and model-development experiments on literary data, contextual resources, and scholarly feedback. Danish literature provides a concrete starting point, with implications for other languages and domains. The aim is to develop alternative evaluation strategies that go beyond conventional benchmark metrics and guide model development toward cultural robustness in AI systems.

</details>


### [211] [Training-Free Pronunciation Transcription via Text-Constrained Acoustic Rescoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30924)
*Hikaru Asano,Yotaro Kubo,So Kuroki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的语音-文本到发音（ST2P）流水线，在推理时结合词汇与声学信息，通过文本约束生成候选并利用冻结的预训练S2P模型进行贪心搜索，显著降低发音转写错误率且效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模TTS训练数据需要准确高效的发音转写。现有方法各有局限：G2P仅用文本，S2P仅用语音，而ST2P虽结合两者却依赖昂贵的发音标注数据。

Method: 提出免训练ST2P流水线：利用词汇资源和G2P工具生成文本约束的候选发音；使用冻结的预训练S2P模型计算整个序列的负对数似然，并通过从左到右的贪心搜索选择最佳候选；同时采用级联策略提升效率。

Result: 在三个日语语料上，CER从纯文本基线的0.60–1.40%降至参考转录下的0.04–0.17%、ASR转录下的0.64–1.58%，优于所有基线（包括训练过的ST2P模型和商业多模态大模型）。贪心搜索在相近CER下比束搜索快3–3.5倍，级联比直接解码快2倍。在西班牙语、法语和初步英语上也超过四个开源多模态大模型和最佳传统方法。

Conclusion: 该免训练ST2P流水线无需发音标注数据，即可实现高准确率和高效率的发音转写，并具备跨语言泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient pronunciation transcription is essential for preparing text-to-speech training data at scale. Existing approaches have different limitations: grapheme-to-pronunciation (G2P) and speech-to-pronunciation (S2P) methods each capture only partial information, using only text or only speech, while speech-and-text-to-pronunciation (ST2P) methods use both but require costly pronunciation-annotated data. To address this problem, we propose a training-free ST2P pipeline that integrates both lexical and acoustic information at inference time. Lexical resources and G2P tools generate text-constrained candidates, and a left-to-right greedy search selects the best one using whole-sequence negative log-likelihoods from frozen pretrained S2P models. On three Japanese corpora, our method reduces Character Error Rate (CER) from 0.60--1.40\% (text-only baseline) to 0.04--0.17\% with reference transcripts, and 0.64--1.58\% with ASR transcripts. It outperforms all baselines, including a trained ST2P model and commercial multimodal LLMs. Our greedy search method is 3--3.5$\times$ faster than beam search at similar CER, and the cascade is 2$\times$ faster than direct decoding ensuring the efficiency and accuracy. In Spanish, French, and preliminary English, it also surpasses four open multimodal LLMs and the best traditional methods.

</details>
