### [212] [Estimating and Orthogonalizing Unknown Pre-training Gradients for Continual Fine-tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30935)
*Bing Wang,Changchun Li,Xin-Qiang Cai,Lin Yuanbo Wu,Ximing Li,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 EoupCT 框架，在持续微调大语言模型时估计并正交化未知的预训练梯度，以缓解灾难性遗忘并保留通用知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续微调对 LLM 适应真实环境至关重要，但会导致对旧任务和预训练通用知识的灾难性遗忘。现有正交梯度投影方法依赖预训练数据和梯度，而这些对预训练 LLM 通常未知且高度多样，因此难以真正保留通用知识。

Method: EoupCT 通过带 Gumbel-Softmax 的可学习软提示动态生成对新任务最易遗忘的伪数据，以估计预训练梯度；并构建多目标优化问题，引入一阶高效 Pareto 优化器联合优化 LLM 参数和软提示，使新任务更新与估计的预训练梯度正交。

Result: 在多个 LLM 上的大量实验表明，EoupCT 能有效保持任务特定能力和预训练通用知识，成功缓解灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: EoupCT 为持续 LLM 微调提供了一种有效方案，通过估计和正交化未知预训练梯度，在适应新任务的同时减少对旧知识和通用能力的遗忘。

Abstract: Continual fine-tuning is essential for large language models (LLMs) to dynamically adapt to real-world environments, yet it inevitably suffers from catastrophic forgetting, particularly the performance degradation of previous tasks and LLMs' general-purpose knowledge. Although existing methods, such as orthogonal gradient projection, mitigate the forgetting across various fine-tuning tasks, they fundamentally fail to preserve pre-training LLMs' inherent general-purpose knowledge because the original data and gradients of off-the-shelf pre-training LLMs required by these methods are strictly unknown and highly diverse. To bridge this critical gap, we propose EoupCT, a novel framework designed to Estimate and Orthogonalize Unknown Pre-training gradients for Continual LLM fine-Tuning. Specifically, EoupCT estimates pre-training gradients by dynamically generating pseudo data that is most susceptible to forgetting for new tasks through a learnable soft prompt equipped with Gumbel-Softmax relaxation. Furthermore, we formulate a multi-objective optimization problem and introduce a first-order efficient Pareto optimizer that jointly optimizes LLM parameters and the soft prompt, rigorously enforcing orthogonality between new task updates and the estimated pre-training gradients. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs demonstrate that EoupCT effectively preserves both task-specific proficiency and inherent general-purpose knowledge, successfully mitigating the catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [213] [FAVoR: Measuring and Mitigating Author-Style Homogenization in Federated Personalized Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30968)
*Lu Han,Jingyao Zhang,Katy Ilonka Gero,Nguyen H. Tran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究多作者个性化写作助手场景下联邦参数高效微调（PEFT）导致的作者风格同质化问题，并提出 FAVoR 方法来保留作者个人写作风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为个性化写作助手时，多作者适配可能使模型偏向共享语域，从而削弱个体写作风格；联邦 PEFT 虽保护数据本地性，但标准聚合可能仍导致作者风格难以区分。

Method: 定义并诊断“作者风格同质化”；使用基于 Angular Style Classification Encoder (ASCE) 的指标及外部作者身份验证；在 BlogText 与 Mythos-Reddit 上评估；提出 FAVoR（Federated Authorial Voice Retention），采用共享-私有适配器设计，客户端上传共享适配器更新，并将作者特定残差修正保留在本地。

Result: 标准联邦 PEFT 基线能保持语义/续写效用，但会平均掉作者特定信号、降低风格可区分性；FAVoR 在 BlogText 和外部 Mythos-Reddit 验证中相比标准及个性化联邦 PEFT 基线提升了作者风格保留，代价是轻微续写效用下降；组件消融、外部验证和冷启动迁移支持该结论。

Conclusion: 联邦 PEFT 中需显式处理作者风格同质化；FAVoR 的共享-私有残差机制可在数据本地约束下更好保留作者个人声音，尽管存在小幅效用权衡。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as personalized writing assistants, but adapting a model across many authors can compromise individual writing style by pulling author-specific signals toward a shared register. Federated parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) offers a data-local setting for this multi-author adaptation problem: clients keep author text local while sharing compact adapter updates. However, we show that standard aggregation can preserve continuation utility while making different authors' generations less distinguishable in style space, a failure mode we define as author-style homogenization. We evaluate author-style retention with Angular Style Classification Encoder (ASCE)-based diagnostics on our main BlogText benchmark and ASCE-independent external authorship verification. Using this protocol, we find that common federated PEFT baselines can preserve semantic utility while averaging out author-specific signals. To address this homogenization, we instantiate FAVoR (Federated Authorial Voice Retention), an author-style residual mechanism for federated PEFT. FAVoR uses a shared-private adapter design: clients upload shared-adapter updates while retaining author-specific residual corrections locally. Across BlogText and external Mythos-Reddit validation, FAVoR improves author-style retention over standard and personalized federated PEFT baselines. These gains come with small continuation-utility trade-offs and are supported by component ablations, external verification, and cold-start transfer.

</details>


### [214] [THA: Weighted Finite-State Text Normalization and Inverse Text Normalization for Khmer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30984)
*Seanghay Yath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Tha，一个基于加权有限状态转换器的高棉语文本规范化与逆文本规范化开源工具包，用于 TTS 和 ASR 的文本/口语形式转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高棉语在文本转语音和语音识别两个方向都缺乏持续维护的开源工具；同时高棉文没有空格分词，且数字词会出现在普通词内部，使文本规范化更加困难。

Method: 构建 Tha 工具包，使用加权有限状态转换器；通过一次最短路径搜索完成整行的分词和分类，并用第二个转换器拒绝高棉语音节内部的 token 边界。

Result: 在 Google 高棉语测试集上，Tha 对全部 274 个基数词与参考一致，仅有一个拼写变体差异；在 2,906 条真实 TTS 提示中，它重写的 158 个句子有 153 个正确。

Conclusion: Tha 是有效的开源高棉语文本规范化与逆文本规范化工具，采用 Apache 2.0 许可证发布。

Abstract: Text-to-speech needs written text in spoken form, and speech recognition output needs the reverse. For Khmer, neither direction has a maintained open-source tool, and the script makes both harder: words are not separated by spaces, and number words occur inside ordinary words. We present Tha, a Khmer text normalization and inverse text normalization toolkit built from weighted finite-state transducers. It segments and classifies a whole line in one shortest-path search, and a second transducer rejects token boundaries inside a Khmer syllable. On Google's Khmer test suite, Tha agrees with the reference on all 274 cardinals up to one spelling variant, and on 2,906 real TTS prompts, 153 of the 158 sentences it rewrites are correct. Tha is open source under the Apache 2.0 license.

</details>


### [215] [Coupled Usage-Sense Processes: Temporal and Attributable Lexical Semantic Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30974)
*Haruka Ezoe,Ryohei Hisano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出耦合用法-义项过程（CUSP），从保持边际分布的时间过程中同时刻画词汇语义变化的幅度、时间、机制、成分运动、变化模式及文本证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法仅用不同时期独立采样分布之间的标量距离衡量词汇语义变化，只能说明“变了多少”，无法揭示“何时变、通过哪些机制与成分运动、哪些用法支持归因”。

Method: CUSP 通过分层耦合将上下文分布与潜在用法成分关联，并用马尔可夫组合保证相邻及长跨度对应一致；引入位移算子量化变化幅度与时间，将变化拆分为成分中心移动和成分内部重组，并归因到被输运的成分对；用词局部模式识别不同变化方向及其时间活动，并用代表性文本段落提供证据。在高斯混合特化下证明算子和平方距离的参量可恢复性。

Result: 合成实验支持理论预测的收敛速率；在英语和德语 DWUG 上具竞争力，并能恢复受控 Janus 变化剖面且保持组合一致的输运；在美国法院意见大语料的无标注自然文本中展示了转变、模式和段落归因。

Conclusion: CUSP 将幅度、时间、机制、运动、模式与文本证据统一为同一词汇历史的相容视角。

Abstract: Lexical semantic change is usually summarized by a scalar distance between independently sampled period distributions. This measures how much a word changed, but does not reveal when it changed, which mechanisms and component movements carried the change, or which usages support the attribution. We introduce Coupled Usage--Sense Processes (CUSP), which derives these answers from a single marginal preserving temporal process. A hierarchical coupling relates contextual distributions through latent usage components, while Markov composition makes adjacent and longer span correspondences compatible. Displacement operators quantify change magnitude and timing, split variation exactly between movement of component centers and reorganization within components, and attribute it to transported component pairs. Word-local modes resolve distinct directions of change and their activity over time, while representative passages from attributed components ground the analysis in text. Under a Gaussian mixture specialization, we prove parametric recovery of the operators and squared distances. Synthetic experiments support the predicted rate. CUSP remains competitive on English and German DWUG and recovers controlled Janus profiles while maintaining compositionally coherent transport. A large corpus of US court opinions demonstrates transition, mode, and passage attribution in unlabeled natural text. CUSP thus makes magnitude, timing, mechanism, movement, modes, and textual evidence compatible views of one lexical history.

</details>


### [216] [Evaluating Sycophancy in Chinese Large Language Models on Factual Questions Derived from Online Search Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30986)
*Geng Liu,Feng Li,Mengxiao Zhu,Francesco Pierri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析中文事实问答中大语言模型的谄媚行为，覆盖DeepSeek、Qwen、Doubao三个模型，发现避免附和用户错误信念并不等于保持事实准确性，需从答案转变层面评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型日益成为信息获取中介，事实准确和独立答案至关重要。但模型可能迎合用户错误信念，将错误信息包装为独立验证，强化用户对虚假主张的信心。既有研究尚未明确：引入用户信念是使正确答案变错或不确定，还是使不确定答案变为迎合信念的错误答案；抗谄媚干预是保持/恢复事实准确性，还是仅将回答转向不确定。

Method: 研究使用中文是非事实核查问题，覆盖三个中国前沿大模型（DeepSeek、Qwen、Doubao）对12,165个来自真实中文搜索查询的事实问题的364,941条回答。实验在有/无推理条件下，采用基线、用户信念条件和抗谄媚提示，追踪正确答案、错误答案和不确定答案之间的匹配转变。在用户信念错误时，区分“信念一致错误”与“事实信心丧失”（原本正确变为不确定）。

Result: 结果因模型和推理设置而异：推理并非一致的保护因素；抗谄媚指令可减少错误附和，但会增加不确定性。在中文事实问答中，避免附和错误信念不等同于保持事实准确性，凸显转变层面评估的价值。

Conclusion: 此类行为可能通过强化错误信息或削弱用户对正确答案的信心，损害LLM中介信息获取的可靠性，因此需要采用转变层面的评估方法来衡量谄媚与事实准确性。

Abstract: As large language models increasingly mediate information access, factually accurate and independent answers are critical. However, these models can exhibit sycophancy by aligning their responses with users' stated beliefs even when those beliefs are incorrect, potentially presenting misinformation as independently verified and reinforcing users' confidence in false claims. Prior work leaves unresolved whether introducing user beliefs causes correct responses to become incorrect or uncertain, or causes uncertain responses to become belief-aligned incorrect answers. It also remains unclear whether anti-sycophancy interventions preserve or restore factual accuracy or merely shift responses toward uncertainty. We analyze factual sycophancy in Chinese-language information seeking using yes/no fact-checking questions. Our analysis covers 364,941 responses from three frontier Chinese-based LLMs (DeepSeek, Qwen, and Doubao) to 12,165 factual questions derived from real-world Chinese search queries. We evaluate the models with and without reasoning across baseline, belief-conditioned, and anti-sycophancy prompting, tracing matched shifts among correct, incorrect, and uncertain responses. Under incorrect user beliefs, we distinguish belief-aligned errors from losses of factual confidence, in which initially correct answers become uncertain. Patterns vary across models and reasoning settings: reasoning is not a consistent safeguard, and anti-sycophancy instructions can reduce incorrect agreement while increasing uncertainty. In Chinese-language factual question answering, avoiding agreement with false beliefs is therefore not equivalent to preserving factual accuracy, highlighting the value of transition-level evaluation. Such behavior may undermine the reliability of LLM-mediated information access by reinforcing misinformation or weakening users' confidence in factually correct answers.

</details>


### [217] [G$^2$PTQ: Improving LLM Post-Training Quantization with Generalized Gradient Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31009)
*Ruikang Liu,Haoli Bai,Yuxuan Sun,Qian Zhang,Wenzheng Cai,Yanqi Hao,Feiyu Wang,Weidong Zhong,Zhuang Wang,Tong Yang,Xiangsheng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出G2PTQ，一种统一的训练后量化（PTQ）框架，通过广义梯度补偿在全局监督的块级优化目标中融合一阶和二阶信息，并在量化每个Transformer块前刷新梯度和Hessian估计，避免过往全局方法的陈旧性问题，实验表明其优于现有最优基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后量化能减少大语言模型的内存和计算开销且无需重训练，但现有基于GPTQ的方法存在互补局限：局部逐层目标缺乏全局监督；全局目标方法在开始时固定Hessian估计且忽略一阶梯度，导致量化推进时指导信息变得陈旧。

Method: 提出G2PTQ框架，采用广义梯度补偿，在全局监督的块级优化目标下整合一阶与二阶信息。量化每个Transformer块前刷新梯度与Hessian估计，避免陈旧性。引入信赖域缩放机制动态限制梯度步长，防止权重更新爆炸。并推导了块级Hessian近似与精确梯度补偿的高效实现。

Result: 在多种模型族和不同位宽上的实验表明，G2PTQ能更好地与全精度模型对齐，性能超越当前最优基线。

Conclusion: G2PTQ是一种有效的统一PTQ框架，通过融合一阶和二阶信息并动态刷新估计，显著提升了量化后模型与全精度模型的对齐程度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is a practical approach to reducing the memory and computational footprint of large language models (LLMs) without retraining. GPTQ-based methods have become the de facto standard, yet they suffer from two complementary limitations. Methods with local, layer-wise objectives lack global supervision; while methods with global objectives fix their Hessian estimates at the start and ignore first-order gradients, so their guidance grows stale as quantization proceeds. This paper presents G$^2$PTQ, a unified PTQ framework with Generalized Gradient Compensation that integrates both first- and second-order information under a globally supervised, block-wise optimization objective. By refreshing gradient and Hessian estimates before quantizing each Transformer block, G$^2$PTQ avoids the staleness of prior global methods. Furthermore, to stabilize the exact first-order compensation, we introduce a trust-region scaling mechanism that dynamically bounds the gradient step to prevent exploding weight updates. Finally, we derive efficient implementations for block-wise Hessian approximation and exact gradient compensation. Experimental results on various model families and bit-widths demonstrate that G$^2$PTQ enables better alignment with the full-precision model, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. Code is available at: https://github.com/G2PTQ/G2PTQ.

</details>


### [218] [ZooWork-ShopRanker: An Open, Preference-Aligned E-Commerce Reranker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31002)
*Siqiao Xue,Shuxuan Liu,Ning Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ZooWork-ShopRanker系列电商重排序模型（0.6B、4B、8B），利用多家族推理LLM评委标注偏好数据训练，并发布约1万对私有流量的双格式基准ShopRank-Bench，在电商排序与MTEB上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用网页检索的开源重排序器迁移到电商场景效果不佳，因为电商排序不仅依赖主题相关性，还涉及用户偏好、商品约束和商品间比较；真实搜索流量虽提供真实查询和候选，但缺乏干净的成对偏好标签，难以大规模监督。

Method: 由不同家族的推理LLM组成偏好评委，进行位置去偏判断并划分一致性层级，生成训练对；据此训练重排序器；8B旗舰模型作为蒸馏教师，指导4B和0.6B模型拟合其分数并在判断对上锐化；同时构建ShopRank-Bench，包含约1万条私有流量偏好对、双文本格式，并按评委家族数量分层。

Result: ZooWork-ShopRanker-8B和-4B显著优于最强开源重排序基线；各模型均显著优于自身未对齐基座；0.6B优于同规模模型；提升在两种文本格式和常见MTEB基准上均成立。

Conclusion: 发布模型和双格式ShopRank-Bench，以促进电商重排序及偏好对齐的进一步研究。

Abstract: Open rerankers trained for general web retrieval transfer imperfectly to e-commerce, where ranking decisions depend not only on topical relevance but also on user preferences, product constraints, and comparative product fit. These preference signals are difficult to supervise at scale: real search traffic provides authentic queries and candidates but no clean pairwise labels. We present ZooWork-ShopRanker, a family of e-commerce rerankers (0.6B, 4B, and 8B) aligned to judge-labeled shopping preference. Training pairs are labeled by a panel of reasoning large language models (LLMs) from different families acting as a preference oracle, with position-debiased judgments and agreement tiers, and the rerankers are trained on these labels. The aligned 8B flagship then serves as a distillation teacher for the efficient 4B and 0.6B models, which are fit to its scores and sharpened on judged pairs. To measure progress, we introduce ShopRank-Bench, a contamination-limited benchmark of ~10,000 private-traffic preference pairs in both text formats, tiered by how many judge families committed to each label. ZooWork-ShopRanker-8B and -4B significantly outperform the strongest open reranker baseline, every model significantly beats its own un-aligned base, and ZooWork-ShopRanker-0.6B beats its size peer; the gains hold in both formats and extend to common MTEB benchmarks. We release the models and the dual-format ShopRank-Bench to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [219] [CG-Probes: Recovering Guardrail Directions from Patient Query Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31062)
*Marko Řeháček,Vítězslav Dušek,Martin Rusinko,Vít Nováček*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出临床护栏探针（CG-Probes），用于度量患者向AI助手提问中的医疗与心理风险，并在捷克肿瘤学查询上验证其可行性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向患者的AI助手虽有支持价值，但患者查询可能带来医疗风险，因此需要建立可操作的风险护栏。

Method: 与肿瘤科医生合作定义三个有序风险轴：医疗紧迫性、心理紧迫性和主题敏感性。在冻结嵌入器的归一化嵌入空间中，用差值均值法将每个轴视为线性方向进行探测。训练时，用BERTopic聚类79,658条捷克肿瘤学搜索查询，并通过少样本提示生成风险水平对比的查询对。评估使用200条查询（90条真实、110条合成），每条由两位肿瘤科医生评分，并与两个开源权重LLM和一个前沿LLM比较。

Result: 紧迫性相关轴可作为线性方向恢复；探针在二次加权kappa上与开源权重LLM无显著差异，但延迟显著更低。每个轴输出一个可被临床医生检查的标量分数，并可用于设置升级阈值。

Conclusion: 该流程仅需搜索日志、轴定义和嵌入模型的黑盒访问，表明其可能迁移到其他医疗领域；但在新查询和新轴上的稳健验证仍是未来工作。

Abstract: Patient-facing AI assistants promise valuable support to patients, but incoming queries can pose medical risks. To create guardrails, we work with oncologists to define three ordinal risk axes: Medical Urgency, Psychological Urgency, and Topic Sensitivity. We propose Clinical Guardrail Probes (CG-Probes) to measure the risks from query embeddings. We probe for each axis in the normalized embedding space of frozen embedders via the difference-in-means method, treating each axis as a potential linear direction. To train the probes, we cluster 79,658 Czech oncology search queries with BERTopic and use these clusters to generate pairs of queries with contrastive risk levels via few-shot prompting. We evaluate the approach on 200 queries (90 real, 110 synthetic), each graded by two oncologists, against two open-weight LLMs and a frontier LLM. We find that urgency-based axes are recoverable as linear directions, and the probes are competitive with open-weight LLMs (no significant differences in quadratic-weighted kappa) at a fraction of the latency. Each axis yields a scalar score that clinicians can inspect and use to set escalation thresholds. The pipeline requires only search logs, axis definitions, and black-box access to the embedding model, suggesting transferability across healthcare domains. Robust validation on new queries and axes remains future work.

</details>


### [220] [Modeling Student Sensemaking with LLMs and Knowledge-Graph-Guided Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31046)
*Özge Alacam,Zübeyde Demet Kirbulut Güneş,Funda Ekici,Nurcan Turan-Oluk,Dilay Dinçdemir,Hakkı Kadayıfçı,Sevinç Nihal Yeşiloğlu,Burcu Işık,Halil Tümay,Sinem Gencer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估指令微调大语言模型在协作意义建构多维分析中的表现，发现推理提示和知识状态诊断能改善对不成功案例的识别，但无单一配置在所有维度上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作科学学习需要对学习者对话进行细致解读，以刻画其识别知识缺口、构建解释和解决问题的过程，这种理论驱动的分析劳动密集且难以规模化。

Method: 评估两个中等规模LLM在23个专家标注片段上的表现，变化提示条件（定义性支架、推理模式、轮次结构），并引入知识状态诊断信息。

Result: 无推理时模型过度预测成功意义建构；启用推理改善不成功案例识别；知识状态诊断提高检测不成功意义建构的能力并与专家标注更一致；无单一配置在所有维度上最佳。

Conclusion: 任务具有多维性，没有单一配置在所有维度上最优。LLM可支持协作意义建构分析，但结构化知识状态信息能提供额外帮助。

Abstract: Collaborative science learning requires nuanced interpretation of student dialogue to characterize how learners identify knowledge gaps, build explanations, and work toward resolution - a theory-driven analysis that is labor-intensive and difficult to scale. We investigate whether instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) can support multidimensional analysis of collaborative sensemaking without task-specific training, and whether structured knowledge-state information improves model inference. We evaluate two mid-size LLMs on 23 richly annotated, expert-labeled episodes across prompting conditions that vary definitional scaffolding, reasoning mode, and turn structure. Without reasoning, models tend to overpredict successful sensemaking; reasoning-enabled prompting improves identification of unsuccessful cases. Knowledge-state diagnostics provide additional grounding, improving detection of unsuccessful sensemaking and increasing agreement with expert annotations. No single configuration performs best across all sensemaking dimensions, underscoring the multidimensional nature of the task.

</details>


### [221] [LocUS: Head Selection and Subspace Projection for Targeted Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31122)
*Irene Tallini,Lorenzo Basile,Valentino Maiorca,Francesco Locatello,Alberto Cazzaniga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LocUS方法，将激活引导约束在模型输出词表子空间，并稀疏作用于部分注意力头，在多个控制任务上匹配或超越现有方法，同时仅干预不到6%参数并更好保留通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准激活引导从对比数据估计每层方向并作用于整个表示空间，可能耦合无关属性并损害模型的其他能力。

Method: 在unembedding矩阵中识别属性特定的线性子空间，通过几何约束将引导变换限制在该子空间，并定位到稀疏的注意力头子集上应用。

Result: 在三个模型家族上进行毒性缓解、情感重定向和谄媚抑制评估，LocUS匹配或超越最先进基线，干预参数少于6%，且更好地保持通用能力。

Conclusion: LocUS是一种参数高效且有效的激活引导方法，通过输出词表子空间定位减少对无关能力的副作用。

Abstract: Activation steering is a powerful training-free paradigm for controlling large language models at inference time. However, standard approaches estimate a per-layer steering direction from contrastive data and apply it on the layer's entire representation space, which may couple the intervention to off-target properties present in the contrastive data and degrade unrelated capabilities. To mitigate this issue, we introduce LocUS (Localized Unembedding Steering), a method which grounds activation steering to the model's own output vocabulary subspace. By identifying a property-specific linear subspace within the unembedding matrix, LocUS enforces a geometric constraint that restricts the steering transformation to a specific subspace and at the same time localizes its application to a sparse subset of attention heads. Extensive evaluations across three model families on toxicity mitigation, sentiment redirection and sycophancy suppression show that LocUS matches or outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while intervening on under 6% of parameters and better preserving general capability.

</details>


### [222] [Do we need to answer that question? Salience and Answerability of Potential Questions in Naturalistic Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31130)
*Amandine Decker,Maxime Amblard,Ellen Breitholtz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文实证检验自然对话中基于QUD（讨论中的问题）的建模，研究生成潜在问题的显著度能否预测其后续是否被回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在验证QUD模型在自然对话中的适用性，特别是问题显著度与后续解决/回答之间的关系，并与独白文本中的表现进行比较。

Method: 基于Wu et al. (2024)，从英国国家语料库的话语及前文中自动生成7,124个问题，并标注显著度和可回答性；随后进行相关性与标注者一致性分析。

Result: 对话中显著度与可回答性呈稳健但较低的正相关，显著问题更可能被处理；该效应明显弱于独白文本，且结构化互动中标注者一致性高于组织较松散的对话。

Conclusion: QUD显著度能预测对话中的问题解决，但预测力有限，说明会话结构更难预测；同时结构化互动有助于提高标注一致性。

Abstract: We empirically investigate Question Under Discussion based modelling in naturalistic dialogue by studying whether the salience of generated potential questions predicts their subsequent resolution. Building on Wu et al. (2024), we construct a dataset of 7,124 questions automatically generated from utterances and preceding context from the British National Corpus, and annotated for salience and answerability. We find a robust but low positive correlation between salience and answerability in dialogue, indicating that more salient questions are more likely to be addressed. However, this effect is markedly weaker than in monologic text, suggesting that conversational structure is less predictable. We further observe that structured interactions exhibit stronger alignment between annotators than less organised dialogues.

</details>


### [223] [Improving Visual Sensitivity of LLMs on Multimodal Machine Translation with Metric-based Loss Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31169)
*Paweł Mąka,Piotr Andruszkiewicz,Yusuf Can Semerci,Jan Scholtes,Gerasimos Spanakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为基于度量的损失加权（Metric-based Loss Weighting）的训练方法，用于提升多模态机器翻译中模型对图像的视觉敏感性，使翻译更好利用伴随图像消除歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态机器翻译虽可融合图像等非文本模态，但模型可能忽略视觉信息，因此如何增强模型对视觉信号的利用仍是重要问题。

Method: 使用 Point-wise Cross-mutual Information（PCXMI）指标，通过比较有无视觉上下文时模型的输出概率来识别受益于图像的 token，并对这些 token 增加损失权重；同时提出基于一致性的 PCXMI 指标，并将两种指标结合使用。在三个翻译方向上微调三个预训练多模态大语言模型。

Result: 在 CoMMuTE 对比数据集上，基于度量的损失加权优于其他测试方法，相比标准微调准确率提升最多超过 7 个百分点，同时保持较强的通用翻译性能。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效提升图像引导机器翻译的视觉 grounding 能力，并在专门评测和通用翻译表现之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Multimodal Machine Translation aims to incorporate additional signal from non-textual modalities to improve translations by resolving ambiguities. While models, through multimodal fusion, are able to accept images related to the source text, they can ignore this information. Therefore, increasing their visual sensitivity remains an active research area. In this work, we introduce a training method, Metric-based Loss Weighting, that improves visual grounding of translations by increasing the loss function for tokens that benefit from the accompanying image. We identify these tokens using the Point-wise Cross-mutual Information (PCXMI) metric, which compares the model's output probabilities with and without visual context. We introduce a Congruency-based PCXMI metric and experimentally show that both metrics working in combination yield the best results. We evaluate our method by fine-tuning three pretrained Multimodal Large Language Models on the task of Image-guided Machine Translation for three language directions. Metric-based Loss Weighting outperforms other tested methods on the CoMMuTE contrastive dataset, improving accuracy by up to more than 7 percentage points compared to standard fine-tuning, while maintaining strong general translation performance.

</details>


### [224] [RupeeBias: Auditing Demographic Bias in Indian Economic Guidance from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31245)
*Pavithra P M Nair,Bhavik Talaviya,Shourya Bhushan,Rahul Pankajakshan,Seema Guruvadoo,Avinash Agarwal,Gilad Gressel,Krishnashree Achuthan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出RupeeBias基准，用于审计LLM在印度经济场景中生成的经济建议的人口偏见，覆盖四个用例和六个印度特定人口轴，发现LLM输出存在系统性差异，平均差异达20.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人使用LLM获取经济建议，LLM可能重现社会偏见，进而影响用户对自身价值的认知和决策。在印度，种姓和城乡等人口类别显著影响经济结果，但现有LLM偏见基准主要基于西方人口类别，忽略了印度背景下的关键经济差异轴。

Method: 引入RupeeBias基准，包含39,150个提示，覆盖薪资估计、加薪估计、还价建议和服务定价建议四个用例。采用单属性反事实设计，固定用户资质、经验或服务描述，每次只改变一个人口标识符。覆盖87个印度特定人口标识符，涵盖种姓、宗教、地区身份、性别、残疾、城乡位置六个轴，提示以英语和Hinglish构建。评估了九个LLM。

Result: 在所有六个轴上发现系统性人口差异。对于仅人口标识符不同的相同提示，LLM生成的经济输出平均差异20.2%。

Conclusion: 公开发布RupeeBias，以支持未来关于印度特定人口和经济背景下LLM生成经济建议中人口偏见的研究。

Abstract: Individuals turn to large language models (LLMs) for guidance across a wide range of economic tasks, from comparing loan options and planning savings to deciding what raise to ask for or how much to charge for their services. LLMs are known to reproduce social biases, and biased economic guidance may influence what users believe they are worth, what they ask for, and what they ultimately accept. This risk is especially salient in India, where economic outcomes are shaped by demographic categories such as caste and urban-rural location. Existing LLM bias benchmarks, however, are largely designed around Western demographic categories and therefore miss key axes of economic disparity in the Indian context. We introduce RupeeBias, a benchmark for auditing demographic bias in LLM-generated economic guidance across Indian economic settings. RupeeBias consists of 39,150 prompts spanning four use cases: salary estimation, salary increment estimation, counter-offer recommendation, and service pricing recommendation. The benchmark follows a single-attribute counterfactual design, holding the description of the user's qualifications, experience, or service offering fixed while varying one demographic identifier at a time. RupeeBias covers 87 India-specific demographic identifiers across six axes: caste, religion, regional identity, gender, disability, and urban-rural location, with all prompts constructed in both English and Hinglish. We evaluate nine LLMs on RupeeBias and find systematic demographic disparities across all six axes. For otherwise identical prompts that differ only in demographic identifier, LLM-generated economic outputs differ by 20.2% on average. We publicly release RupeeBias to support future research on demographic bias in LLM-generated economic guidance across India-specific demographic and economic contexts.

</details>


### [225] [PIA: A Personal Intelligence Agent Turning Health Conversations into Records and Records into Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31255)
*Jeonghun Yoon,Dongchan Kim,Hongyeon Yu,Young-Bum Kim,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出PIA，一个与消费级健康代理并行的个人智能代理，通过将对话转化为类型化临床记录并合成为用户理解，解决通用代理记忆摘要化在健康场景中不可用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用代理记忆通过提取片段、嵌入和检索摘要来总结对话，但健康场景需要精确剂量、明确时间解析和长期趋势分析；摘要会导致信息损失，文本相似度也无法回答三个月血糖趋势等问题。

Method: PIA接收代理的自然语言请求，自主决定是否及如何读写，将对话转为类型化临床记录，并将记录合成为用户理解。其记忆框架含四个控制：提取、记忆、检索、理解；每个都是领域无关机制，并配有可插拔健康模块：模式、医学别名词典、知识图谱和时间规则。

Result: 同一查询在记忆注入从一维回忆、二维健康快照到三维带因果轨迹时得到不同答案；运营经验表明自报健康数据为非随机缺失，问题措辞影响合成理解质量，且近三分之一候选因果链接是结构噪声，仅靠规则即可移除。

Conclusion: 健康代理不能依赖对话摘要式记忆，PIA通过类型化记录、可插拔医学模块和因果轨迹支持更可靠的个性化健康理解；时间规则和结构化控制对去除噪声、提升回答质量至关重要。

Abstract: General-purpose agent memory summarizes conversations: it extracts salient snippets, embeds them, and retrieves the top-k into the prompt. A health agent cannot run on summaries: a dose becomes a sentence, "since last week" is resolved at the model's discretion, and a three-month glucose trend cannot be answered by text similarity. We present PIA, a personal intelligence agent deployed alongside a consumer health agent. PIA receives the agent's natural-language requests, decides for itself whether and how to write or read, and turns conversations into typed clinical records and records into a synthesized understanding of the user. Its memory harness consists of four controls -- extraction, memory, retrieval, and understanding -- each a domain-agnostic mechanism with a pluggable health module: schema, medical alias dictionary, knowledge graph, and temporal rules. We show how the same query receives a different answer as the memory injected into the response context deepens from one-dimensional recall, to a two-dimensional health snapshot, to a three-dimensional trajectory with causality, and report lessons from operation: self-reported health data are missing not at random, question phrasing governs the quality of synthesized understanding, and nearly a third of candidate causal links are structural noise that rules alone remove.

</details>


### [226] [Where a Model Sends Its Own Repeated Token](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31181)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用退化输入“同一token重复两次”探测黑箱语言模型，将 argmax p(. | t, t) 视为词表上的映射，并区分不动点与非常数目的地；研究发现非常数目的地能有效区分模型家族，且结果稳健并预注册。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱模型识别通常依赖自然语言提示评分；已有工作用退化输入寻找失败模式，而非识别模型身份。作者追问：当模型不落入不动点时，“流向”哪里，以及这一非不动点映射是否可作为模型指纹。

Method: 对每个token t，单次前向计算 argmax p(. | t, t)，构建全词表映射；将源token配对以消除基数混杂；在12个模型、19模型池、7个tokenizer组、多语料上评估家族归因；设置频率匹配与独立边缘两个零假设；预注册所有估计量与kill条件；测试8-bit/4-bit量化及语料去重等稳健性。

Result: 不动点部分被报告为失败估计量：自然距离83%由基数解释，家族归因0.5833。非不动点目的地此前被记为0；源token配对后r从0.9128降至-0.0932，家族归因0.8333，随机0.1389；零假设仅0.1429和0.0798。家族比tokenizer更能预测一致性（0.2031 vs 0.1205）；循环架构平衡准确率1.0，排除主导目的后0.90。8-bit量化影响小于去重语料（0.9004 vs 0.6353），4-bit破坏映射（0.0098；部署粒度0.1812），精度下限0.201至0.9778。

Conclusion: 非不动点映射在消除基数混杂后是有效的黑箱模型家族信号，尤其对循环架构；研究同时完整报告失败的估计量，强调预注册与稳健性边界。

Abstract: Black-box model identification works by scoring a model's response to natural-language prompts. One line of work feeds models a degenerate input -- their own token, repeated -- to find a failure mode rather than an identity. We take that input and ask where the model goes when it does not. For each token t, read argmax p(. | t, t) in one forward pass; the result is a map on the whole vocabulary, with two halves. The first -- which tokens are fixed points -- is partially anticipated, and we report it as a failed estimand: the natural distance on it is 83% cardinality, separates a corpus manipulation by two bits in 3471 against a precision floor of zero, and attributes families at 0.5833. The second half, where the map sends tokens that are not fixed points, is unrecorded; the one paper holding those tokens logged them as a zero. Pairing on the source token removes the cardinality confound by construction (r from 0.9128 to -0.0932) and attributes families at 0.8333 -- twelve models scored against a pool of nineteen -- with chance 0.1389, across seven tokenizer groups and several corpora. Two nulls clear it: frequency-matched destinations agree at 0.1429, independent marginals at 0.0798. Family predicts agreement better than tokenizer (0.2031 against 0.1205), and recurrent architectures cluster at balanced accuracy 1.0 against a 0.7895 majority rate, or 0.90 once each model's dominant destination is excluded -- the figure we stand behind. We measure the robustness envelope: 8-bit weight rounding moves the map less than deduplicating the training corpus does (0.9004 against 0.6353, on one support), 4-bit destroys it (0.0098; 0.1812 at deployment granularity, so not a coarseness artefact), and the precision floor varies by model from 0.201 to 0.9778. All estimands and kill conditions were registered before the data, and the failed one is reported as fully as the surviving one.

</details>


### [227] [MoSAR: Mixture of Semantic Attention Regimes for Learning Adaptive and Approximable Attention Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31261)
*Michele Paolicelli,Alessandro Petruzzelli,Alessandro Franceso Maria Martina,Cataldo Musto,Giovanni Semeraro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 MoSAR，将稀疏/局部注意力近似视为可从数据中学习的几何问题，通过 query/key 路由器混合短程、中程和全局注意力机制，实现自适应且可控衰减的注意力几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集自注意力二次复杂度是长上下文语言建模的主要瓶颈；现有高效方法通常预先设定稀疏或局部模式，但自然语言依赖关系是输入相关的，难以事先规定，因此模型应学习位置相关性在何处衰减、何处需要更广交互。

Method: MoSAR 在位置编码之后使用输入条件化的 query 和 key 路由器，在短、中、全局三种注意力范围上选择混合，诱导出连续的距离依赖注意力场，而非固定稀疏模式；该几何在训练中学习，并可通过 top-1 路由离散化。

Result: 在匹配 500M 参数模型的受控预训练中，MoSAR 学到更低到达范围的注意力几何且不降低语言建模质量，在训练上下文长度上困惑度优于密集 RoPE；在长度外推下，其困惑度优于所有评估变体，包括 ALiBi 等强基线；学习到的几何在确定性 top-1 离散化下保持稳定。

Conclusion: MoSAR 表明注意力近似可通过学习自适应、可控衰减的交互几何来改进长上下文建模，并且该几何在推理时适合低成本近似。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of dense self-attention remains a central bottleneck for long-context language modeling. Many efficient alternatives address this cost by deciding in advance where attention should be sparse or local. We argue that attention approximation should instead be approached as a geometric problem, with the relevant interaction geometry learned from data: natural-language dependencies are input-dependent and difficult to prescribe in advance, so the model should learn where positional relevance can decay and where broader interactions must be preserved. We introduce Mixture of Semantic Attention Regimes (MoSAR), which learns such an adaptive, controlled-decay geometry over query--key interactions. Input-conditioned query and key routers, applied after positional encoding, select mixtures over short, medium, and global regimes, inducing a continuous distance-dependent attention field rather than a fixed sparsity pattern. This geometry is learned during training and can subsequently be discretized through top-1 routing. In controlled pre-training experiments with matched 500M-parameter models, MoSAR learns a substantially lower-reach attention geometry without degrading language-modeling quality, improving perplexity over dense RoPE at the training context length. Under length extrapolation, MoSAR achieves the best perplexity among all evaluated variants, including strong baselines such as ALiBi. Moreover, the learned geometry remains stable under deterministic top-1 discretization, suggesting that it is not only adaptive, but also amenable to low-cost approximation at inference time.

</details>


### [228] [Identifying Scientists on X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31264)
*Philipp Meier,Katarina Boland,Laura Kallmeyer,Stefan Dietze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于X/Twitter用户简介和推文自动识别科学家与非科学家账户的方法，并在两个数据集上取得最高0.96的F1分数，同时发布了带标注的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络科学相关话语日益重要，传统知识秩序受到侵蚀，因此需要自动识别科学家等不同用户群体。

Method: 使用用户简介和推文，结合语言特征与随机森林，并采用对比微调的DeBERTa模型进行集成分类。

Result: 在两个数据集上，随机森林最高F1为0.88，对比微调DeBERTa集成模型最高F1为0.96。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效区分科学家与非科学家账户，并提供了两个含用户标签、推文和简介的数据集。

Abstract: With the growing importance of science-related discourse on the Web and the erosion of the classical knowledge order, it is important to identify different user groups, such as scientists, automatically. This work proposes an approach for identifying scientists and non- scientists on X/Twitter based on their user biographies and tweets. We show that we are able to classify accounts as scientists and non- scientists on two different datasets, reaching an F1 score of up to 0.88 using Random Forests with linguistic features and up to 0.96 using a contrastively fine-tuned DeBERTa model in an ensemble setup. Furthermore, we provide two datasets with X users labeled as scientists or non scientists and their respective tweets and user biographies.

</details>


### [229] [Stale-Document Poisoning: When Outdated Retrieval Overrides Correct Model Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31342)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Lukas Galke Poech,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文识别了检索增强生成中的“过期文档投毒”问题：当检索到的证据已过时，即使模型不检索时能答对，也可能被误导。作者构建了跨医学、法律、软件和平台政策的317项知识反转基准，并系统评估了多种模型与干预方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG常被用来提供外部证据以解决知识过时问题，但检索只在证据仍然有效时才有帮助。现有研究忽视了一个时间对齐失败：过时的检索证据可能反而使模型出错，因此需要研究模型能否判断检索证据是否仍然适用。

Method: 构建包含317项经验证知识反转的基准，覆盖医学、法律、软件和平台政策，并基于有日期的官方来源。评估12个模型；通过固定历史证据、仅改变评估日期来隔离时间适用性；使用显式有效性停止说明和因果干预；测试固定且具时效感知的混合重排序器。

Result: 过期检索使Llama 30%、Qwen 37%的回答翻转，即使没有要求信任文档；显式“遵循文档”指令将比例提高到66%和75%。四个开源模型、四个领域中投毒率为17%-91%，而匹配的最新证据被遵循率为97%-100%。仅靠日期带来有限适应，但明确告知旧证据何时停止适用时，较大模型几乎都能切换到正确答案；因果干预确认有效性信息直接影响最终决策。固定重排序器在日期准确时减少4.6-10.0个百分点的投毒。

Conclusion: 可靠的RAG需要选择性信任：模型不仅要理解检索证据的内容，还必须判断该证据是否仍然适用。时间适用性判断可调用通用比较推理机制，并依赖可靠的时间元数据。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is often used to address outdated knowledge by providing external evidence. But retrieval helps only when that evidence is still valid. We identify a temporal alignment failure, stale-document poisoning, in which outdated evidence makes a model wrong despite answering correctly without retrieval. We construct a benchmark of 317 verified knowledge reversals across medicine, law, software, and platform policy, grounded in dated official sources. Across 12 models, recent medical reversals are harder than long-established ones. More importantly, outdated retrieval flips 30% of Llama and 37% of Qwen answers even without instructions to trust the document; explicit follow instructions raise these rates to 66% and 75%. Across four open models and four domains, poisoning ranges from 17-91%, while matched up-to-date evidence is followed in 97-100% of trials. To isolate temporal applicability, we keep the historical evidence unchanged across 50 reversals and vary only the evaluation date. A clear pattern emerges: dates alone produce only modest adaptation, but when models are explicitly told when the old evidence stops applying, the larger models switch to the appropriate answer almost perfectly. Causal interventions confirm that this validity information directly shapes the final decision. The same internal components also support broader comparison tasks, suggesting that temporal applicability can recruit a general reasoning mechanism used for other comparisons. Finally, a fixed recency-aware hybrid re-ranker reduces poisoning by 4.6-10.0 points when dates are accurate, with gains that depend on reliable temporal metadata. Reliable RAG therefore requires selective trust: models must determine not only what retrieved evidence says, but whether it still applies.

</details>


### [230] [Highlight-Then-Summarize: Learning to Compress Evidence for Long-Context Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31382)
*Zhaoyuan Xia,Qinghongbing Xie,Yung Xiang Hue,Jianguang Jiang,Gaofeng Lu,Zhenyu Jiao,Xing Yuan,Dai Dai,Tong Mo,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Highlight-Then-Summarize（H2S）压缩-再推理范式：先定位与问题相关的源证据，再整合为问题条件下的紧凑摘要，最后生成答案，从而提升长上下文推理并实现更紧凑输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文理解中，任务相关证据往往稀疏且分散在大量无关、冗余内容中，直接推理效率低、效果受限。

Method: 提出H2S范式；构建H2S-Dataset（6,647个样例、11个基准族、平均上下文长度43.9K tokens）；提出H2S-RL，在最终答案正确性之外，对证据选择和摘要构建提供过程级奖励；在H2S-Bench七任务长上下文套件上评估，统一采用128K输入、4K输出预算。

Result: H2S-14B平均得分32.60，比Qwen3.8-27B高10.17分，在受评开源模型中总体表现最佳；取得最高Evidence-Summary Quality分数；仅用4K输出预算即保留其16K预算性能的97.1%。

Conclusion: 显式地选择并整合证据能够改善长上下文推理，同时支持更紧凑的生成。

Abstract: Long-context understanding requires large language models (LLMs) to reason over lengthy documents, conversations, and code, yet task-relevant evidence is often sparse and scattered amid substantial irrelevant and redundant content. We propose Highlight-Then-Summarize (H2S), a compress-then-reason paradigm that first identifies source-grounded, question-relevant evidence and then integrates it into a compact, question-conditioned summary before producing the final answer. To train this behavior, we construct H2S-Dataset, comprising 6,647 examples from 11 benchmark families with an average context length of 43.9K tokens, and introduce H2S-RL, which provides process-level rewards for evidence selection and summary construction in addition to final-answer correctness. We evaluate on H2S-Bench, a seven-task long-context suite. Under a shared 128K input and 4K output budget, H2S-14B achieves an average score of 32.60, outperforming Qwen3.8-27B by 10.17 points and obtaining the strongest overall result among the evaluated open-source models. H2S-14B also achieves the highest Evidence-Summary Quality score and retains 97.1% of its 16K-budget performance with only a 4K output budget. These results show that explicitly selecting and integrating evidence improves long-context reasoning while enabling more compact generation.

</details>


### [231] [Sorry Robot, Happy Human: Vision-Language Models Read Only One of Two Legible Typographic Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31403)
*Mert İncidelen,Yamen Kashkash,Asya Berker,Murat Aydoğan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究构建了 DecoyBench 数据集，评估近期闭源视觉语言模型在包含多层文字图像中的 OCR 表现，发现模型存在与空间频率层相关的稳定行为局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型虽在 OCR 任务上表现成功，但易受排版攻击，且对包含多层文字的图像结构较为脆弱。

Method: 使用 Decoy Font 方法创建 DecoyBench，包含 300 张图像，每张图像由清晰轮廓文字叠加在柔和阴影文字上构成。评估来自三个模型家族的六个近期闭源模型，在 naive 与 guided 两种提示条件、512×512 与 64×64 两种分辨率下测试，并开展人类验证研究。

Result: 人类被试能高准确率读取两层文字。高分辨率下，多数模型变体及两种提示方法对轮廓文字接近人类准确率，但几乎无法完整提取阴影文字。低分辨率下，模型与人类均无法读取轮廓文字，而阴影文字可被高准确率提取。

Conclusion: 所评估的视觉语言模型在处理包含多个空间频率层的排版结构时，表现出了一致的行为局限。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs), despite their success in optical character recognition (OCR) tasks, are vulnerable to typographic attacks and have a fragile structure for images with multiple text layers. In this study, the DecoyBench dataset was created using the Decoy Font method. The dataset consists of 300 images, each containing text with sharp contour lines superimposed on another text with soft shading. Six recent closed-source models from three different model families were evaluated using this dataset under two different prompting conditions (naive and guided) and at two different resolutions ($512\times512$ and $64\times64$). A validation study showed that human participants could read both text layers with high accuracy. In contrast, the models, with most variants and both prompting methods, read the contour text with near-human accuracy at high resolution, but almost never fully extracted the shading text. At low resolution, the contour text could not be read by either the models or humans, while the shading text could be extracted with high accuracy. The findings indicate that the evaluated VLMs exhibit a consistent behavioral limitation when processing typographic structures containing multiple spatial frequency layers.

</details>


### [232] [ViSTA: A Simple Bridge Extends Visual Alignment to Clinical Time-Series Understanding in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31448)
*Junyi Gao,Yu Shi,Pingzhao Hu,Ewen M Harrison*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 ViSTA，一种紧凑适配器，将不规则数值测量融入预训练视觉-语言模型的图表表示中，以提升临床结构化时间序列预测和时序问答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型可基于患者测量值估计风险，大语言模型擅长医学文本理解与问答，但语言能力并不能保证从结构化、高维临床时间序列中准确预测。研究希望将风险估计与对患者病情变化的灵活提问连接起来。

Method: ViSTA 是一种小型适配器，在预训练视觉-语言模型的图表表示中引入不规则数值测量；它学习对视觉 token 的修正，同时保持所有预训练参数不变。作者在 MIMIC-IV 上评估急性肾损伤和死亡预测，并测试时序问答。

Result: 在 2-9 亿/十亿参数模型上，ViSTA 在急性肾损伤和死亡预测的四项指标上均取得最高平均分。仅 0.516 百万可训练参数时，2 亿参数模型在急性肾损伤上的 AUROC 为 0.7376，接近 GPT-5.6 Sol 使用文本输入和高推理努力时的 0.7380。时序问答训练在 4 亿参数下达到 69.27% 准确率，可训练参数比使用图表或数值文本的低秩适配少 90% 以上，准确率差距为 2.82-4.88 个百分点。

Conclusion: ViSTA 可将预训练语言模型扩展到数值预测和时序问题回答任务。

Abstract: Clinical prediction models estimate risk from patient measurements, while large language models support medical text understanding and question answering. Yet their language capabilities do not ensure accurate prediction from structured, high-dimensional clinical time series. Improving this ability would connect risk estimation with flexible questions about a patient's evolving condition. We introduce ViSTA, a compact adapter that incorporates irregular numerical measurements into a pretrained vision-language model's chart representations. It learns corrections to visual tokens while leaving all pretrained parameters unchanged. On MIMIC-IV, ViSTA has the highest mean scores among the compared adaptations on all four metrics for acute kidney injury and mortality prediction across models with 2-9 billion parameters. With 0.516 million trainable parameters, the 2-billion-parameter model reaches an area under the ROC curve of 0.7376 for acute kidney injury, compared with GPT-5.6 Sol's 0.7380 with text input and high reasoning effort. Training for temporal question answering yields 69.27% accuracy at 4 billion parameters with over 90% fewer trainable parameters than low-rank adaptation using charts or numerical text, at a 2.82-4.88 percentage-point accuracy gap. ViSTA extends pretrained language models to numerical prediction and temporal questions.

</details>


### [233] [Evaluating Cultural Awareness of LLMs for Haitian Creole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31506)
*Christelle Clervilsson,Yanzhu Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文首次系统评估大语言模型在海地克里奥尔语中的文化意识，从特异性、偏见、多样性和变异性四个维度与高资源法语对比，发现海地克里奥尔语文化意识明显不足且受法语干扰，故事生成中还存在刻板叙事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在高资源与低资源语言间性能差距大，且常无法理解低资源社区的文化规范与价值观；海地克里奥尔语使用者众多却在数字资源中严重不足，因此需要评估其文化意识。

Method: 构建由母语者策划的文化显著提示基准，在文本填空设置下，从特异性、偏见、多样性和变异性四个互补维度评估LLM的海地克里奥尔语文化意识，并进行故事生成分析，同时与法语对比。

Result: 结果显示海地克里奥尔语与法语的文化意识存在明显差距：海地语表现跨领域更不均衡，且更受法语语言干扰；故事生成反复将海地人物描绘为经历苦难并展现韧性，说明即使正面刻画也可能编码刻板叙事。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了首个针对海地克里奥尔语LLM文化意识的系统评估，揭示低资源语言文化意识不足和刻板表征问题，并公开代码、基准与评估框架以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit substantial performance disparities between high- and low-resource languages. Beyond lower task performance, they often fail to capture the cultural norms and values of underrepresented communities. In this work, we present the first systematic evaluation of cultural awareness in LLMs for Haitian Creole, a language spoken by millions but severely underrepresented in digital resources. We assess cultural awareness along four complementary dimensions---specificity, bias, diversity, and variation---using a benchmark of culturally salient prompts curated by native speakers in a text infilling setting. Our results reveal a clear gap between cultural awareness in Haitian Creole and higher-resource French, with Haitian performance being more uneven across domains and more affected by French linguistic interference. Story generation further reveals recurring portrayals of Haitian characters through hardship and resilience, showing that even positive characterizations can encode stereotypical narratives. Our code, benchmark, and evaluation framework are publicly available.

</details>


### [234] [Muslim: A Deployed Arabic Voice AI Platform for Grounded Islamic Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31511)
*Yahya Mohamed Elnawasany*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Muslim —— 一个面向真实用户、提供有出处且可溯源的伊斯兰知识的生产级阿拉伯语语音 AI 平台。系统包含实时语音管线（NeMo 阿拉伯语 ASR、兼容 OpenAI 的 LLM 接口、自托管 TTS）与跨六个 MCP 服务器的确定性多源检索层，并额外贡献了原型通常缺失的三项内容：已发布的阿拉伯语伊斯兰微调模型工件、账户与计量层、以及三层可观测性栈，同时给出实测延迟与准确率数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究原型往往缺乏真正可运营产品所需的关键组件，例如可发布的模型、防滥用机制和生产级可观测性。作者希望将伊斯兰知识语音问答从演示系统推进为面向真实用户、可运维且抗滥用的产品。

Method: 构建实时语音管线（NeMo 阿拉伯语 ASR + 兼容 OpenAI 的 LLM 端点 + 自托管 TTS），并设计跨六个 MCP 服务器的确定性多源检索层；发布微调模型：工具路由 LLM Muslim-6B-PRO（5.94B 参数）与 MSA TTS 模型 Fasih-TTS-V1；引入账户与计量层（免费按账户轮次配额、基于容量的拒绝、延后至关键环节的邮箱验证）；搭建三层可观测性栈（存活检测、错误上报、产品分析），针对 GPU 受限的 agent 主机静默而 Web 层仍正常服务这一典型故障模式。

Result: Fasih-TTS-V1 在社区投票的阿拉伯语 TTS Arena（MSA）中总体排名 17 个系统中的第 5，在 11 个开放权重系统中排名第 2；背诵验证在 124 个样例上达到 98.4% 准确率；端到端语音延迟为 0.9-1.7 秒。

Conclusion: 论文报告了在真实生产环境中运行伊斯兰知识语音产品的实测延迟与准确率，并讨论了相应的工程权衡与局限。

Abstract: We present Muslim, a production Arabic voice AI platform serving grounded, sourced Islamic knowledge to real users. Beyond a real-time voice pipeline (NeMo Arabic ASR, an OpenAI-compatible LLM endpoint, self-hosted TTS) and a deterministic multi-source retrieval layer routed across six Model Context Protocol servers, we report three things a research prototype typically lacks. First, a released family of fine-tuned Arabic Islamic model artifacts: an efficient tool-routing LLM (Muslim-6B-PRO, 5.94B parameters) and a Modern Standard Arabic TTS model (Fasih-TTS-V1) that ranks 5th of 17 overall and 2nd of 11 open-weight systems on the community-voted Arabic TTS Arena for MSA. Second, an account and metering layer - a free per-account turn allowance, capacity-aware refusal, and email verification deferred to the point it actually matters - that turns an open demo into an operable, abuse-resistant product. Third, a three-layer observability stack (liveness, error reporting, product analytics) built specifically around the system's characteristic failure mode: a GPU-bound agent host going silent while the web tier keeps serving normally. We report real, measured latency and accuracy figures (98.4% recitation-validation accuracy on 124 cases; end-to-end voice latency of 0.9-1.7s) and discuss the concrete engineering trade-offs and limitations of running an Islamic-knowledge voice product in production.

</details>


### [235] [MexHat: A Dataset for Hate Speech Detection in Mexican Spanish Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31553)
*Itzel Tlelo-Coyotecatl,Hugo Jair Escalante*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 MexHat，一个面向墨西哥西班牙语语境的仇恨言论检测视频数据集，约含1千个视频片段，并设置粗粒度三分类和细粒度仇恨子类两类标注任务，同时提供数据统计与基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线安全中的内容监控使仇恨言论检测成为关键任务，而该任务依赖语境线索。现有自动检测方法虽进展显著，但非英语资源稀缺，尤其难以适应多模态内容中隐晦、依赖语境和文化相关的特征。

Method: 构建 MexHat 视频数据集，捕捉墨西哥西班牙语的语言与文化线索；收集约1千个视频片段；设计两类标注任务：三分类（无负面内容、冒犯内容、仇恨言论）和细粒度三仇恨言论子类别；给出数据集统计和基线实验。

Result: 数据集包含约1千个视频片段并完成两类标注；数据统计与基线结果表明该任务存在内在挑战。

Conclusion: MexHat 为墨西哥西班牙语仇恨言论检测提供了视频资源，支持多模态与文化相关研究，并显示该任务仍然困难。

Abstract: Ensuring online safety through content monitoring had raised Hate Speech Detection as a crucial task to be addressed. By essence the task demands the capture of contextual cues, which are essential for a precise understanding of the content's intent. Although automated detection approaches for the task have advanced significantly, the scarcity of non-English resources persists, limiting the ability of models to adapt to the subtle, context-dependent, and culturally related nature of multimodal content. In this paper, we introduce MexHat, a video dataset designed to capture the linguistic and cultural cues for the hate-speech detection task in a Mexican Spanish context. Our dataset comprises around 1k video clips annotated across two tasks: a three-way class evaluation (no negative content, offensive content and hate-speech content), and a fine-grained class evaluation including three hate-speech sub-categories. The dataset statistics and the baseline results highlight the inherent challenges associated with the task. Disclaimer: This paper contains sensitive content that may be disturbing to some readers.

</details>


### [236] [Strategically Diverse Sampling for Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31571)
*Alexander Gurung,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,Mirella Lapata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究自训练数据构建中的策略多样性，提出按策略而非仅按正确性筛选样本，能显著提升模型在困难任务上的表现，并优于IID训练与大规模教师蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多LLM训练与推理方法依赖重复采样，但只有当回答有实质差异时才有收益。现有自训练通常用IID采样并主要按正确性过滤，导致模型偏好的策略被过度代表。

Method: 作者提出策略多样性作为自训练数据构建原则，并使用两种采样方法：GROOT，构建分层方法树并采样不同路径；以及Verbalized Sampling（VS），改造后生成无结构的方法集合。在竞赛编程与Next-Chapter Prediction领域进行实验。

Result: 使用策略多样性数据训练的模型在困难任务上优于IID训练模型，并为RL和测试时扩展提供强初始化。最显著的是，用Qwen3-4B生成策略多样但错误的轨迹进行自训练，效果超过从235B教师模型的IID蒸馏。

Conclusion: 研究结果挑战了关于自训练数据有效性的主流假设，表明方法多样性可能比正确性或教师规模更重要。

Abstract: Many LLM training and inference methods, including RL and test-time scaling, depend on repeated sampling, but benefit only when the responses meaningfully differ. Self-training faces the same challenge: training data is typically constructed by sampling IID responses and filtering primarily for correctness, thereby overrepresenting strategies a model already favours. We investigate strategic diversity, or substantive variation among approaches to a problem, as an alternative principle for constructing self-training data. We generate strategically diverse data with two sampling methods: GROOT, a new method which constructs a hierarchical tree of approaches and samples distinct paths, and Verbalized Sampling (VS), adapted to produce an unstructured set of approaches. Across competitive programming and Next-Chapter Prediction domains, models trained on strategically sampled data outperform IID-trained counterparts on difficult tasks and provide strong initializations for RL and test-time scaling. Most strikingly, self-training on strategically diverse but incorrect traces from Qwen3-4B outperforms IID distillation from a 235B teacher. These results challenge prevailing assumptions about what makes useful self-training data and show that diversity of approaches can matter more than correctness or teacher scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Actively Resolving Contextual Uncertainty for Underspecified Tasks in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30428)
*Zachary Ravichandran,Jonathan Diller,Fernando Cladera,Varun Murali,George J. Pappas,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出CLUE框架，用于在自然语言任务描述不充分且环境陌生时，通过闭环交互主动消解上下文不确定性，并在真实机器人上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件策略通常假设目标明确且先验地图已提供任务相关信息；但在陌生环境和模糊任务中，机器人需要同时推断任务成功标准、相关信息及其位置，导致高上下文不确定性。

Method: CLUE利用LLM衍生策略提出任务相关概念与潜在计划，并在线构建语言嵌入地图将假设落地为动作；随后通过闭环环境交互依次评估假设，并根据新信息持续优化计划。

Result: 在Boston Dynamics Spot上于3个室内外环境和15个任务中测试，CLUE成功率距oracle策略仅差7个百分点，比无闭环反馈的LLM规划器高4倍；仅构建并查询语言地图的方法成功率约为CLUE的1/3，且消耗超过10倍VLM token。

Conclusion: 闭环上下文不确定性消解对于处理不充分语言任务和陌生环境至关重要；仅依赖语言地图查询不足以解决复杂上下文规划问题。

Abstract: Foundation models provide robots with the ability to interpret natural language and reason about environmental context, yet most language-conditioned policies assume that goals are well-specified and that task-relevant information is provided upfront via a prior map. Operating in unfamiliar environments with underspecified tasks entails high contextual uncertainty: the robot must jointly infer what constitutes task success, what constitutes relevant information, and where (or whether) that information exists. We address these limitations via CLUE (Closed-Loop contextual Uncertainty rEsolution), a framework for actively resolving contextual uncertainty given underspecified tasks in natural language. CLUE uses an LLM-derived policy to hypothesize task-relevant concepts and potential plans. It then uses a language-embedded map, which is constructed online, to ground these hypotheses into actions. The policy sequentially evaluates hypotheses via closed-loop environment interaction and refines its plans as it gathers new information. We deploy CLUE on a Boston Dynamics Spot across three real indoor and outdoor environments spanning 15 tasks that require object disambiguation, functional inference, and occlusion reasoning. CLUE achieves a success rate within 7 percentage points of an oracle policy and outperforms an LLM-enabled planner without closed-loop feedback by a 4x margin. Supporting experiments demonstrate that simply building and then querying a language-enriched map is insufficient to resolve complex contextual planning tasks; these approaches achieve roughly one third the success rate of CLUE while requiring over 10x more VLM tokens. We provide additional information at https://zacravichandran.github.io/CLUE.

</details>


### [238] [TinyCVIO: A Constellation-Aided Visual-Inertial Odometry System for Nanodrones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30358)
*Derin Ozturk,Kaan Akan,Irwin Wang,Christopher Batten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TinyCVIO 是一种面向纳米无人机的视觉惯性里程计系统，在双核微控制器（520 kB SRAM）上实现实时状态估计，通过 LED 星座、流式视觉前端和刚性板测量模型降低误差与计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米无人机在严重受限的传感和计算资源下，仍需要准确、实时的状态估计。

Method: 协同设计微型传感、视觉处理和估计流程；使用轻量 LED 星座提供已知几何信息，无需预先测量位置或偏航角，并假设位于同一水平面；流式视觉前端以 29.2 FPS 跟踪毫米级相机中的 LED 观测；采用刚性板测量模型保留 LED 间约束，并用流式 QR 限定估计工作空间以保持固定滤波器状态大小。

Result: 在 19 个手持硬件在环数据集上，刚性板模型相比平面点模型将平均绝对轨迹误差降低 27%。完整系统在 Crazyflie 上完成 9 次飞行、覆盖三种速度，平均绝对轨迹误差为 3.5-3.7 cm，10 m 段相对位姿误差为 0.50-0.60%，平均估计延迟为 15.7-16.3 ms。

Conclusion: TinyCVIO 能够在商品化微控制器上实现机载、实时且较准确的视觉惯性里程计，适用于资源严重受限的纳米无人机。

Abstract: Nanodrones require accurate, real-time state estimation under severe sensing and computational constraints. We present TinyCVIO, a visual-inertial odometry system that co-designs miniature sensing, visual processing, and estimation for a commodity dual-core microcontroller with 520 kB SRAM. Lightweight LED constellations provide known geometry without surveyed positions or yaw angles, assuming placement on a common level plane. A streaming visual frontend tracks LED observations from a millimeter-scale camera at 29.2 FPS, while a rigid-board measurement model retains inter-LED constraints and streaming QR bounds estimation workspace for a fixed filter-state size. Across 19 hand-held hardware-in-the-loop datasets, the rigid-board model reduces mean absolute trajectory error by 27% relative to planar points. The complete system runs onboard a Crazyflie across nine flights at three speeds, achieving 3.5-3.7 cm mean absolute trajectory error and 0.50-0.60% relative pose error over 10 m segments, with mean estimate latency of 15.7-16.3 ms.

</details>


### [239] [POIL: Point-based One-Shot Imitation Learning with Stable Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30404)
*Sang Min Kim,Jinwoo Seo,Hyeongjun Heo,Junho Lee,Yonghyeon Lee,Young Min Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POIL是一个基于点的单次模仿学习框架，结合稳定动力系统，通过物体功能部件上的3D点集共享表示，同时实现轨迹迁移和闭环执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次模仿可避免收集大量演示，但成功的单次操作不仅需要将演示轨迹迁移到新物体，还需在场景条件、抓取配置变化及外部扰动下鲁棒执行。

Method: 采用物体功能部件上的3D点集作为共享表示：利用点对应实现单次轨迹迁移，并用多模态大语言模型锚定功能部件，跨视角、位姿和物体类别迁移轨迹；执行时通过多视角在线跟踪同一组点，并利用Point-set BCSDM将逐点速度投影到由跟踪点计算的单一刚体扭转上，实现闭环控制。该方法将稳定动力学模型从SE(3)位姿扩展到点集，无需已知3D模型或位姿估计器；在目标处控制器变为经典SO(3)势能上的梯度流，其终端阶段在刚体假设下继承近全局收敛性。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，POIL能够将单次演示迁移到不同物体类别、抓取位姿和目标几何形状，并能在执行过程中从外部扰动中恢复。

Conclusion: POIL通过基于点的共享表示与稳定动力系统，有效解决了单次模仿学习中的轨迹迁移和鲁棒闭环执行问题。

Abstract: We present POIL, a point-based one-shot imitation learning framework with stable dynamical systems. While one-shot imitation avoids collecting extensive demonstrations, successful one-shot manipulation requires not only transferring a demonstrated trajectory to a novel object but also executing it robustly under changing scene conditions, grasp configurations, and external disturbances. POIL addresses both problems through a shared representation: a set of 3D points on the object's functional part, used jointly for trajectory transfer and closed-loop execution. The one-shot transfer from the demonstrated trajectory is enabled with point correspondences. POIL grounds the shared functional part with a multi-modal large language model, and transfers the trajectory across viewpoint, pose, and object category changes. During execution, multi-view tracking observes the same points online, and Point-set BCSDM drives them in closed loop by projecting per-point velocities onto a single rigid-body twist computed from the tracked points alone. This extends stable dynamical models from an SE(3) pose to a point set without requiring a known 3D model or pose estimator. We show that at the goal the controller becomes a gradient flow on the classical SO(3) potential, so its terminal phase inherits the almost-global convergence of that potential under a rigid-object assumption. Across simulation and real-robot experiments, POIL transfers a single demonstration across object category, grasp pose, and goal geometry, while recovering from external disturbances during execution. Project page: https://sangminkim-99.github.io/poil

</details>


### [240] [VkVIO: Cross-platform GPU Acceleration for Visual-Inertial Odometry with Vulkan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30459)
*Ole Hoffmann,Mateo de Mayo,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VkVIO，据称是首个跨平台GPU加速的视觉惯性里程计（VIO）方法，基于与厂商无关的Vulkan API实现，并在多种设备上达到先进精度和实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人、XR等领域的感知依赖良好的状态估计，VIO/VI-SLAM是低成本高精度的方案。GPU加速可降低延迟，但现有工作多局限于CUDA，限制了硬件厂商和部署选择。

Method: 利用面向3D图形严格性能需求设计的Vulkan API，实现跨平台GPU加速VIO；提供实时运行所需的因果估计；在工作站、笔记本和低成本单板计算机等多种设备上部署。

Result: 在多种设备上实现最先进精度；在同一硬件上性能超过基于CUDA的系统；可在从工作站到极低成本单板计算机的多样平台上运行。

Conclusion: VkVIO为机器人和XR中的低延迟、低功耗、低成本VIO提供了新的可能性，并展示了跨平台GPU加速状态估计的可行性。

Abstract: Perception in robotics and XR fundamentally relies on good state estimation. Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) and Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (VI-SLAM) are proven ways of achieving this goal in a cost-effective and accurate manner. Efficiency in these systems allows for smaller, cooler, and lighter devices. GPU acceleration is a natural approach for reducing latency, thanks to their wide availability in platforms like embedded computers, mobile phones, and XR headsets. However, previous works in the literature have limited themselves to the use of CUDA for this task, significantly reducing deployment options to a single vendor. We instead leverage the vendor-agnostic Vulkan API, originally designed for the strict performance requirements of 3D graphics applications. In this work, we present VkVIO, the first, to the best of our knowledge, cross-platform GPU-accelerated VIO method. We provide state-of-the-art accuracy with causal estimates required for real-time operation. We deploy VkVIO on a diverse range of devices spanning a workstation, a laptop, and an extremely inexpensive single-board computer, while outperforming CUDA-based systems on the same hardware. VkVIO enables possibilities for low-latency, low-power, and low-cost VIO in robotics and XR.

</details>


### [241] [WALT: Learning World-Model-Aligned Latent Trajectories for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30436)
*Mingkai Jia,Jiaxin Guo,Zhijian Shu,Jiawei Xu,Mingxiao Li,Jintao Cheng,Ping Tan,Wei Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WALT，通过从冻结的驾驶世界模型向轨迹潜在空间转移语义知识，提升轨迹规划性能并降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型预测准确，但视觉世界状态与原始几何轨迹之间存在不匹配，限制了规划器利用世界模型编码的动作相关语义。

Method: WALT采用双分支轨迹自编码器将原始路径点映射为紧凑潜在表示，并从冻结的预训练驾驶世界模型转移语义知识到轨迹空间，同时系统研究了基于JEPA和REPA的潜在学习与特征对齐。

Result: 在NAVSIM基准上，WALT将NAVSIMv1的PDMS从89.4提升至89.8，将NAVSIMv2的EPDMS从87.3提升至87.9，并减少轨迹规划器FLOPs 30.5%。

Conclusion: 在保留世界表示的同时提取动作相关信息，为基于世界模型的轨迹规划提供了有效接口。

Abstract: Driving world models learn rich predictive representations of the surrounding environment from visual observations, yet accurate visual prediction does not necessarily translate into effective trajectory planning. We argue that a key bottleneck lies in the mismatch between visual world states and raw geometric trajectories, which may limit the planner's ability to exploit action-relevant semantics encoded by the world model. To address this issue, we propose World-Model Alignment for Latent Trajectories (WALT), which learns a compact generative trajectory latent space by transferring information from a frozen pretrained driving world model without modifying the world model itself. Rather than directly generating raw waypoints, WALT maps them into compact representations through a dual-branch trajectory autoencoder and transfers semantic knowledge from the frozen visual world model into this trajectory space, encouraging the learned action representation to capture scene-level cues relevant to future motion and planning. Beyond our proposed formulation, we systematically study latent learning based on Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) and feature alignment following Representation Alignment (REPA) to investigate how trajectory-only representation learning affects downstream planning. We evaluate WALT on the NAVSIM benchmarks. Relative to the raw-waypoint baseline, WALT improves PDMS from 89.4 to 89.8 on NAVSIMv1 and EPDMS from 87.3 to 87.9 on NAVSIMv2 while reducing trajectory planner FLOPs by 30.5%. These results suggest that preserving world representations while extracting action-relevant information provides an effective interface for world-model-based trajectory planning.

</details>


### [242] [Realizability Is Not Enough: Encoding, Liveness, and Auditing of Synthesized Robot Supervisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30460)
*David C. Conner,Joshua Luzier,William J. Doyle,Emma R. Faith,Aubrie B. Kooiker,Andrew J. Farney,Sebastian Fox,Evangelina Grimes,Ian G. Conner,Kyle Bloom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向ROS 2 FlexBE监督器的开源流水线，将能力型GR(1)规范生成、假设分析、策略审计、行为保持的状态约简及可执行状态机生成结合起来，并在四组案例（含两套四旋翼硬件）中比较了编码方式与活跃性公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应式合成虽能生成具有形式化保证的机器人高层监督器，但仅有GR(1)规范可实现性并不足以部署：还需编码易失败能力、选择符合重试意图的活跃性假设、审计策略，并翻译为机器人软件。

Method: 提出面向ROS 2 FlexBE监督器的开源流水线：生成基于能力的GR(1)规范，合成前分析假设，审计策略，使用行为保持证明约简状态，并输出可执行状态机；在四个案例（六次比较，包括两套四旋翼硬件）中比较枚举编码与one-hot编码、两种活跃性表述；审计器针对四类结构缺陷（协议违反、死锁、有界失败违反、目标不可达陷阱）。

Result: 在测试后端下，枚举编码通常合成更快，但命题数更少并不能可靠预测控制器更小或符号成本更低；System-Goal without pending memory是唯一在两种编码和报告网格下均确认生成可执行控制器的活跃性方案，Fair-Outcome可能允许可实现循环但未按设计者意图完成。审计器对四类结构缺陷可靠且完备，但不是通用活跃性验证器；状态约简保持能力层行为。

Conclusion: 建议在此后端与模型下采用枚举编码与System-Goal，并审计每个实现策略，因为命题数量和可实现性并不能衡量可部署性；该流水线缩小了形式化可实现性与通过协议及结构进展检查的控制器之间的差距。

Abstract: High-level robotic supervisors coordinate capabilities whose reported outcomes determine the robot's next action. Reactive synthesis can generate such supervisors with formal guarantees, but deployment requires more than proving a Generalized Reactivity (1) (GR(1)) specification realizable. Designers must encode failure-prone capabilities, choose liveness assumptions that match retry intent, audit strategies, and translate them into robot software. We present an open-source pipeline for Robot Operating System (ROS) 2 Flexible Behavior Engine (FlexBE) supervisors that generates capability-based GR(1) specifications, analyzes assumptions before synthesis, audits strategies, reduces states with a behavior-preservation proof, and emits executable state machines.
  Across four case studies (six comparisons), including hardware on two quadcopter platforms, we compare enumerated and one-hot encodings and two liveness formulations. Under the tested backend, enumerated encoding usually synthesizes faster, although fewer propositions do not reliably predict smaller controllers or lower symbolic cost. System-Goal without pending memory is the only liveness treatment confirmed to yield executable controllers under both encodings across the reported grid; Fair-Outcome can permit realizable cycles without designer-intended completion. For this backend and model, we recommend enumerated encoding with System-Goal and auditing every realized strategy, since proposition count and realizability do not measure deployability.
  The auditor is sound and complete for four structural defect classes (protocol violations, deadlocks, bounded-failure violations, goal-unreachable traps) but is not a general liveness verifier, and the reduction preserves capability-level behavior. Together, these stages narrow the gap between formal realizability and controllers that pass protocol and structural-progress checks.

</details>


### [243] [DGT-Map: Directional Global Traversability Mapping Utilizing Multi-Task Learning for Heterogeneous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30461)
*Jaskrit Singh,Kashif K. Noori,Jing Xiao,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DGT-MAP 是一个自监督框架，利用 RGB-D 观测和运动信号学习全局、方向相关且车辆条件化的越野可通行性代价地图，并集成到 Hybrid A* 导航栈中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局地图通常为每个位置分配单一各向同性代价，忽略了可通行性的方向依赖性和车辆差异性；已有学习估计器也多为每辆车独立训练，导致车辆间无法共享通用地形表示。

Method: 使用共享多任务骨干网络学习跨车辆的通用地形特征，并用车辆特定预测头保留平台相关响应；通过 RGB-D 观测和运动信号进行自监督训练，推理时生成按航向索引的代价地图，供方向感知规划器使用。

Result: 在仿真中集成到 Hybrid A* 导航栈，并在具有挑战性的地形上评估下游任务成功率，包括下坡可通行但上坡不可通行的斜坡，以及仅部分车辆可通过的山脊障碍。相比几何、二值和学习型方向无关基线，DGT-MAP 取得最高或并列最高的导航成功率。

Conclusion: DGT-MAP 能同时建模方向相关和车辆特定的可通行性，并通过共享地形表示支持多车辆学习与方向感知规划，在复杂越野导航任务中优于现有基线。

Abstract: Off-road traversability is direction-dependent and vehicle specific, yet most global maps assign a single isotropic cost to each location. Existing learned estimators are also commonly trained independently for each vehicle; this preserves vehicle-specific behavior but prevents vehicles from sharing common terrain representations. DGT-MAP addresses both limitations through a self-supervised framework that learns global, directional, and vehicle-conditioned traversability costmaps from RGB-D observations and locomotion signals. A shared multi-task backbone learns common terrain features across training vehicles while vehicle-specific prediction heads preserve platform-dependent responses. At inference, DGT-MAP produces a heading-indexed costmap that can be used by a direction-aware planner. We evaluate DGT-MAP in simulation by integrating it into a Hybrid A* navigation stack and measuring downstream task success on challenging terrains, including slopes that are traversable downhill but not uphill and a ridge obstacle that is traversable by some vehicles, but not by others. Across evaluated tasks, DGT-MAP achieves the highest or tied-highest navigation success rate when compared against geometric, binary, and learned direction-agnostic baselines.

</details>


### [244] [Policy-Calibrated DAgger: Offline Calibrated Noise Injection for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30462)
*Jenny Wang,George Kantor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Policy-Calibrated DAgger，利用生成式扩散策略自身预测动作分布的离散程度，在离线条件下估计策略的噪声水平，并通过部分去噪引导策略沿专家轨迹执行，从而更有效地收集纠正数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略会随时间累积误差并偏离训练分布。现有缓解协变量偏移的方法要么将机器人置于不安全状态，要么需要人工选择合适的噪声分布来采集专家演示。

Method: 在专家轨迹的观测点上测量扩散策略预测动作的分散程度，并测量其相对于记录轨迹的闭环误差；针对多模态动作空间中的误差测量问题，在闭环控制中通过部分去噪引导策略靠近轨迹，再利用扩散模型性质将误差反归一化，以模拟未引导时的误差。

Result: 在机器人于杂乱狭窄环境中到达发动机拉杆的任务上，以及在 3D 照片级真实感模拟器和 2D 平面 reacher 环境中，该方法优于未加噪声的数据集聚合策略，并达到事后最佳噪声水平的性能，且无需扫描噪声水平。

Conclusion: 利用生成式策略的预测动作分布可离线估计策略噪声并指导 DAgger 数据收集，从而避免不安全探索和噪声水平调参，提升模仿学习策略的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Policies trained with imitation learning can accumulate errors over time, causing the robot to drift outside the training distribution. Existing methods mitigate this covariate shift by collecting additional data where the policy fails or is likely to fail. The first places the robot in unsafe conditions and the second requires choosing an appropriate noise distribution to collect new expert demonstrations under that noise. We propose Policy-Calibrated DAgger, a method that makes use of the properties of recent generative policies to estimate the policy's noise offline by using its own predicted action distribution. We measure a diffusion policy's spread of predicted actions at observations along the expert trajectory and measure its closed-loop error relative to a recorded trajectory. To address issues with measuring error in a multimodal action space, we guide the policy towards the trajectory during closed-loop control through partial denoising, and use properties of a diffusion model to unnormalize the measured error as if we did not guide it. We experiment in a scenario where a robot is tasked to reach an engine lever in a cluttered and narrow environment and show results in a 3D photorealistic simulator and a 2D planar reacher environment. We show that our method surpasses policies trained with dataset aggregation without noising and matches the performance of the best noise level in hindsight, without requiring a sweep over noise levels.

</details>


### [245] [Learning-Based Pressure Predictive Control of a Vertebraic Soft Robotic Tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30479)
*Wenjian Yang,Nan Huang,Yukang Nie,Fang Chen,Wanchao Chi,Jiansheng Dai,Sicong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LSTM的压力预测控制（PPC）方法，用于脊椎式软体机器人尾部的运动控制及其与四足机器人的协调运动，可同时实现非静态和准静态控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人具有人机交互安全、柔顺性强等优点，但其连续体结构和非线性材料特性导致运动学建模复杂，尤其在非静态运动中建模困难。

Method: 提出LSTM-based pressure predictive control（PPC），由逆运动学（IK）模型、正运动学（FK）模型和压力补偿（P-comp）模型组成，用于软体尾部运动控制，并通过预测数据集训练实现下一时刻动作预测。

Result: 与仅使用IK模型相比，PPC仿真轨迹平均RMSE降低69.8%；在软体尾部与四足机器人协调运动中，PPC减少60.9%计算时间，提升尾部对四足躯干运动速度的实时响应能力。

Conclusion: PPC为软体尾部提供了一种简单有效的非静态和准静态运动建模与控制方法，并使软体尾部四足机器人具备与环境交互的功能。

Abstract: Soft robots have attracted much attention for their safe human-robot interaction and flexibility, but the typical continuum structure and nonlinear material behavior make the kinematics modelling complex, especially in non-static motions. In this work, we proposed an LSTM-based pressure predictive control (PPC) for the motion control of a vertebraic soft robotic tail and the coordination with a quadruped robot. The PPC consists of an inverse kinematics (IK) model, a forward kinematics (FK) model and a pressure compensation (P-comp) model, and achieves non-static and quasi-static motion control of the tail. Compared with the IK-only model, the average RMSE of the PPC's simulation trajectories reduces by 69.8%, when executing target trajectories. In the coordinated motions of the soft tail quadruped, using a prediction data set to train the PPC enables next-moment action prediction and reduces computation time by 60.9%, which enhances the real-time response of the tail to match the quadruped torso's moving rate. The PPC provides a simple and effective method to model the soft tail for both non-static and quasi-static motion control, and grants the soft tail quadruped with the functionality of interacting with the environment.

</details>


### [246] [Memory-Aware Multi-Sensor Perception for Efficient and Safe Navigation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30495)
*Jingshuo Li,Yifan Xue,Yifei Li,Shubhodeep Shiv Aditya,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种记忆感知的多传感器导航框架，融合LiDAR与RGB感知、在线距离场表示学习和阶段自适应MCBF-QP控制器，在未知环境中实现避障与高效到达目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于感知的方法通常依赖先验地图或短时观测，难以充分利用先前观测到的环境结构，从而限制自主导航效率与避障能力。

Method: 提出记忆感知多传感器导航框架：集成LiDAR与RGB感知、在线距离场表示学习，以及阶段自适应调制控制障碍函数二次规划（MCBF-QP）。该框架持续表示静态基础设施并跟踪动态障碍物，使控制器能利用已观测几何进行绕障，并自适应调整安全约束与引导。

Result: 在复杂室内外环境实验表明，该方法在狭窄通道和动态障碍物场景中保持避障的同时，提升了导航效率和到达目标性能。

Conclusion: 记忆感知与多传感器融合结合阶段自适应MCBF-QP，可有效利用历史观测几何，在未知复杂环境中实现更高效、安全的自主导航。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in previously unseen environments requires effective perception, persistent environmental representation, and collision avoidance while maintaining progress toward a goal. Existing perception-based methods often rely on prior maps or short-horizon observations, limiting their ability to exploit previously observed structure. We propose a memory-aware multi-sensor navigation framework that integrates LiDAR and RGB perception, online distance-field representation learning, and a stage-adaptive Modulated Control Barrier Function Quadratic Program (MCBF-QP). The framework persistently represents static infrastructure while tracking dynamic obstacles, enabling the MCBF-QP controller to exploit previously observed geometry for obstacle circumvention and adapt its safety constraints and guidance to local conditions. Experiments in complex indoor and outdoor environments demonstrate improved navigation efficiency and goal-reaching performance while maintaining collision avoidance in narrow passages and around dynamic obstacles.

</details>


### [247] [Tactile Sensing Array for Multi-Phalanx Sensing in Humanoid Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30506)
*Neel Adwani,Muhaiminul Islam Akash,Rituja Bhattacharya,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Velostat和导电胶带的低成本人形手指触觉阵列，覆盖指尖、近节和中节，并验证其迟滞、恢复时间、接触几何区分和三指节抓握检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形手抓取和操作物体需要整根手指的触觉反馈，而不仅是指尖；仅靠视觉和本体感觉不可靠，尤其当手指自身遮挡相机视线时。

Method: 用Velostat和导电胶带制作触觉阵列：指尖有7个接触点，包括环绕前、左、右面的2x3矩阵和指尖端点；近节和中节各有一条前向接触线。通过重复加载和按压-释放测试测量迟滞与释放后恢复时间。还测试了一种带间隙和凸起的顺应性3D打印接触结构，并与齐平接触基线对比。

Result: 带间隙和凸起的结构将恢复时间较齐平基线缩短74%。传感器能对指尖不同接触几何（平面、边缘、角）产生不同激活模式，并在抓握时记录到三个指节的接触。

Conclusion: 该低成本全指触觉阵列可提供多指节接触信息并区分接触几何；制造改进仍有限，未来可用软件补偿更直接地解决剩余迟滞问题。

Abstract: Humanoid hands require tactile feedback across the whole finger, not just the fingertip, to grasp and manipulate objects properly. Vision and proprioception alone cannot reliably provide this information, particularly when the hand's own fingers occlude the camera's view of the grasp. We present a low-cost tactile array for a humanoid finger, made from Velostat and conductive tape. The fingertip carries seven contact points including a 2x3 matrix wrapped across its front, left, and right faces, and a separate contact point at the tip. The proximal and middle phalanges each carry a single front-facing contact line. We measure the sensor's hysteresis and recovery time after release, through repeated loading and press-release tests. We also test a compliant, 3D-printed contact structure with a gap and a bump, inspired by similar designs in prior work, and show it cuts recovery time by 74% compared to a flush-contact baseline. We then show the sensor can produce distinct activation patterns for different contact geometries (flat, edge, corner) at the fingertip, and that it registers contact across all three phalanges during a grip. Finally, we discuss the limits of our fabrication changes and point to software-based compensation as a promising way to more directly fix the remaining hysteresis in the future.

</details>


### [248] [Aerial Manipulation in the Wild with Onboard Perception, Policy Learning, and Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30521)
*Yuanzhu Zhan,Yufei Jiang,Zemu Zhang,Junyi Geng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种户外空中操作框架，集成了模仿学习、机载LiDAR惯性状态估计和全身模型预测控制，并在物理空中操作平台上验证了户外操作任务的成功执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外空中操作面临可靠状态估计、稳定空中运动以及外部干扰下精确操作的挑战；现有方法多局限于受控室内环境，需要消除对外部运动捕捉基础设施的依赖。

Method: 使用扩散策略从操作演示中学习，根据机载观测生成期望的末端执行器运动；采用全身模型预测控制联合协调空中平台和机械臂；使用机载LiDAR惯性里程计进行状态估计。

Result: 在物理空中操作平台上验证了完整框架，成功执行了户外操作任务；结果表明，演示驱动的操作策略可以与机载状态估计和基于模型的全身控制有效集成。

Conclusion: 该框架使得空中操作能够超越受控室内环境，在户外环境中实现有效操作。

Abstract: Aerial manipulation in outdoor environments remains challenging due to the simultaneous requirements of reliable state estimation, stable aerial motion, and precise manipulation under external disturbances. In this work, we present a real-world outdoor aerial manipulation framework that integrates imitation learning, onboard LiDAR-inertial state estimation, and whole-body model predictive control. A Diffusion Policy is trained from manipulation demonstrations to generate desired end-effector motions from onboard observations. These learned commands are executed by a whole-body MPC that jointly coordinates the aerial platform and manipulator to realize the desired end-effector trajectory. To eliminate reliance on external motion-capture infrastructure, the platform employs onboard LiDAR-inertial odometry for state estimation during outdoor operation. We validate the complete framework on a physical aerial manipulator and demonstrate successful execution of outdoor manipulation tasks.
