The experimental results show that demonstration-driven manipulation policies can be effectively integrated with onboard state estimation and model-based whole-body control to enable aerial manipulation beyond controlled indoor environments.

</details>


### [249] [Containing Behavioral Cascades from Manipulated Claims in LLM-Powered Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30523)
*Waleed Bin Khalid,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种主动验证框架，用于在LLM驱动的多机器人系统遭受语义操纵并接受虚假世界状态后，遏制全队行为级联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的多机器人系统易受语义操纵：一旦接受虚假世界状态声明，可能触发全队级联反应，导致不必要的重规划、路径成本增加、拥堵或任务看似不可行。

Method: 在假设操纵已成功的前提下，设计专门验证模块生成结构化Verify-Adapt-Hold计划：选择部分机器人检查关键区域，少量机器人必要时临时适应，其余机器人保持可信计划。在多机器人运输环境中注入不同影响程度和团队规模的虚假障碍声明，评估级联遏制、总成本、完工时间和覆盖率。

Result: 结果表明，将受损后的验证视为团队级规划问题，而非二值信任决策，能有效限制语义操纵带来的级联物理后果。

Conclusion: 主动验证与团队级规划可提升LLM多机器人系统对语义操纵的鲁棒性，减少下游连锁影响。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered multi-robot systems are vulnerable to semantic manipulation: an accepted false world-state claim can trigger a fleet-wide behavioral cascade, causing unnecessary replanning, increased path costs, congestion, or apparent mission infeasibility. Conditioning on a successful manipulation, we propose an active verification framework that contains its downstream effects before they propagate across the fleet. A dedicated verification module generates a structured Verify-Adapt-Hold plan: selected robots inspect consequential regions, a limited subset provisionally adapts when necessary, and the remaining robots retain their trusted plans. We evaluate the framework in a multi-robot transportation environment using injected false obstacle claims across different impacts and team sizes. Evaluation measures cascade containment, Sum-of-Costs, makespan, and coverage ratio. Results show that treating post-compromise verification as a team-level planning problem, rather than a binary trust decision, effectively limits the cascading physical consequences of semantic manipulation.

</details>


### [250] [A Long-Legged, Direct-Drive Monopedal Robot Achieves Exceptional Jump Height](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30530)
*Gihyeok Na,Justin K. Yim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种仅用单条长腿、直接驱动且无弹性储能的高跳机器人，可实现7.6米高、190毫秒支撑时间的快速跳跃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在突破电驱动、无弹簧储能跳跃系统在跳跃高度和速度上的限制，并探索无需弹性储能的轻量高跳机器人方案。

Method: 采用新型织物包裹传动实现可变机械优势，使小电机在行程中接近峰值功率输出，同时支撑长而轻的腿防止屈曲；腿部顶端安装平衡模块，用小型螺旋桨控制地面和空中姿态，长腿提供大力臂。

Result: 机器人重281克，实现7.6米高、190毫秒支撑时间的跳跃，是已报道的电驱动、无弹簧系统中跳跃最高的；并在户外验证了垂直跳跃、地面和飞行姿态控制以及倾斜跳跃。

Conclusion: 该机器人证明了无需弹性储能、仅靠直接驱动和可变传动也能实现高速高跳，并可通过螺旋桨平衡模块实现地面与空中姿态控制。

Abstract: We demonstrate a jumping robot that reaches high (7.6 m) and fast (190 ms stance time) jumps from a single long leg driven by a direct-drive transmission, without elastic energy storage. At 281 g, it achieves the highest jump yet reported for an electrically actuated, spring-free system. The leg uses a new fabric-wrap transmission that provides a variable mechanical advantage, keeping a small electric motor near its peak power output through the stroke while bracing the long, lightweight leg against buckling. A balancing module at the top of the leg uses small propellers to control the leg's orientation on the ground and in the air, where the long leg provides a large moment arm for the control torques. The robot is validated outdoors with vertical jumps, attitude control on the ground and in flight, and tilted jumps.

</details>


### [251] [ST-pRRTC: Parallel Space-Time RRT-C with Adaptive Goal-Time Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30533)
*Duo Zhang,Jintong Li,Junshan Huang,Jingjin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 ST-pRRTC，一种面向障碍物轨迹已知且到达时间未指定的 GPU 并行时空 RRT-Connect 运动规划器。它通过共享前向树和自适应后向目标时间树森林，在扩大时间覆盖的同时提升规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在到达时间未指定的动态环境中，搜索多个到达时间可扩大时间覆盖范围，但也会在更多后向树之间分摊有限规划预算，导致效率下降。

Method: ST-pRRTC 构建共享前向树和自适应后向目标时间树森林；采用区间根公式连续采样目标到达时间，并在有界时间域内保证概率完备性和渐近到达时间最优性。实际使用的根回收策略虽无同类保证，但可自适应固定数量的后向树，替换较晚根并保留有用搜索进展。

Result: 在三个动态基准实验中，对于所有对比方法都能求解的问题，两种变体均比 ST-RRT* 和 SI-RRT 获得更低的平均首次求解时间和更早的平均最终到达时间。进一步实验表明，在较宽到达时间范围内根回收具有优势。真实机器人实验展示了根回收 ST-pRRTC 为 UR5e 在移动 Crazyflie 四旋翼间规划运动。

Conclusion: ST-pRRTC 能有效处理障碍物轨迹已知且到达时间未指定的动态运动规划问题；根回收策略在广泛到达时间范围内具有实际优势，并已在真实机器人场景中验证。

Abstract: We propose ST-pRRTC, a GPU-parallel space- time RRT-Connect motion planner for problems with known obstacle trajectories and unspecified arrival time. Searching over many arrival times broadens temporal coverage but divides a finite planning budget among more backward trees. To address the challenge, ST-pRRTC builds a shared forward tree and an adaptive forest of backward goal-time trees. Its interval root formulation samples goal arrival times continuously and guarantees probabilistic completeness and asymptotic arrival- time optimality under the stated assumptions in a bounded time domain. The practical root recycling policy has no such guar- antees. It adapts a fixed number of backward trees, replacing later roots while retaining useful search progress. Experiments on three dynamic benchmarks show that both variants achieve lower mean first-solution times and earlier mean final arrivals than ST-RRT* and SI-RRT on problems solved by all compared methods. Further experiments demonstrate the benefit of recy- cling over broad arrival-time ranges. Real-robot demonstrations show root-recycling ST-pRRTC planning motions for a UR5e among moving Crazyflie quadrotors.

</details>


### [252] [GraspTwin: Zero-Shot Task-Oriented Grasp Optimization via a Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30543)
*Daniel J. Evans,Yinlong Dai,Simon Stepputtis,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GraspTwin 的 real-to-sim-to-real 框架：基于单张 RGB-D 构建数字孪生，用基础模型生成任务相关的抓取先验，再通过贝叶斯优化与域随机物理仿真优化，实现任务导向且物理可行的真实机器人抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人进入家庭后需要抓取多样物体，且抓取往往要服务于后续任务，例如倒咖啡时需抓杯柄。现有方法要么偏向任务无关的鲁棒无碰撞抓取，要么依赖基础模型给出任务合适但缺乏细粒度物理可行的抓取位置。

Method: 基于单张 RGB-D 观测构建环境数字孪生；查询大型基础模型，根据物体可供性和任务描述提出候选抓取；将这些候选视为语义先验和优化种子；使用贝叶斯优化与 Thompson 采样生成批量邻近抓取位姿；在域随机化物理仿真中并行 rollout 评估；最后将优化后的抓取部署到真实机器人执行。

Result: 真实世界零样本迁移只需几分钟，任务导向抓取成功率相较其他最先进流程最高提升 33%。代码已开源：https://github.com/VT-Collab/GraspTwin/。

Conclusion: 将基础模型的抓取提议作为语义先验，并结合局部无梯度优化与物理仿真评估，可以桥接任务语义与物理可行性，实现高效的真实机器人任务导向抓取。

Abstract: As robots transition from structured factory settings into homes, they are required to interact with an ever-increasing variety of objects. Many tasks require grasping, and often it is not sufficient to just pick up the target object. Consider a task like "pouring coffee" --- to facilitate the subsequent pouring, the robot should grasp the mug by its handle. Existing learning-based approaches for grasping either find robust and collision-free grasps that are largely agnostic to the task (e.g., picking up the mug by its rim), or leverage foundation models to propose task-appropriate grasp locations that lack fine-grained physical grounding (e.g., reaching for and missing the handle). In this work, we bridge these approaches with a real-to-sim-to-real framework. Based on a single RGB-D observation, we construct a digital twin of the environment, query a large foundation model to propose grasps that align with the object's affordances and task description, and then optimize the proposals to ensure robustness and plausibility before executing the result on the real robot. Our key insight is that the grasp proposals of the foundation model should be regarded as semantic priors that serve as seeds for local, gradient-free optimization. We leverage Bayesian optimization with Thompson sampling to draw batches of nearby poses, which are subsequently evaluated in parallel under domain-randomized physics rollouts. The resulting grasp is both task-oriented and physically feasible for execution by the robot arm. Our full zero-shot real-world transfer only takes a few minutes and improves task-oriented grasping success by up to 33% as compared to other state-of-the-art pipelines. Our code is available here: https://github.com/VT-Collab/GraspTwin/

</details>


### [253] [Privacy-Preserving Prompted Policy Search for Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30554)
*Ali Irshayyid,Feng Lin,Chong Li,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 PP-ProPS，一种隐私保护的提示式策略搜索框架，使 LLM 能在不暴露原始策略参数和奖励历史的情况下指导强化学习策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 作为上下文策略优化器在强化学习中展现出潜力，但现有方法需将原始策略参数和奖励历史发送到云端 LLM API，导致专有控制策略泄露给第三方服务商。

Method: PP-ProPS 在客户端对策略参数和奖励值进行秘密变换后再发送给 LLM，使服务商只能看到编码后的参数和缩放后的奖励；同时不要求将真实最优回合回报告知 LLM，并提供各奖励分量而非仅总回报，还采用有界历史避免提示无限增长。

Result: 在 MuJoCo 运动、经典控制、高速驾驶和机械臂操作等连续与离散控制任务上评估，PP-ProPS 在 10 个任务中的 7 个优于 Vanilla ProPS，并在 6 个任务中的 5 个超过 PPO、SAC 和 TRPO 等传统 RL 方法。

Conclusion: PP-ProPS 能在保护策略和环境参数隐私的同时实现有效的 LLM 引导策略优化，并在多个控制任务上提升搜索性能，支持高维策略和开放权重 LLM 的使用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated promising capabilities as in-context policy optimizers for Reinforcement Learning (RL), enabling policy search driven by both numerical reward signals and natural language reasoning. However, deploying such methods in practice requires transmitting raw policy parameters and rewards history to cloud-based LLM APIs, exposing proprietary control strategies to third-party service providers. To address this issue, this paper introduces Privacy-Preserving Prompted Policy Search (PP-ProPS), a framework that enables LLM-guided policy optimization while keeping policy and environmental parameters confidential. PP-ProPS encodes policy parameters and reward values using secret client-side transformations before they are included in each API request, ensuring that the LLM provider observes only encoded policy parameters and scaled reward information. Furthermore, unlike Vanilla ProPS, the proposed framework does not require the true optimal episodic return to be known or disclosed to the LLM. Beyond protecting the optimization data, PP-ProPS improves the search process in two ways. First, it provides the LLM with individual reward components instead of only a single total return, offering more informative feedback about each candidate policy. Second, it uses a bounded history that prevents the prompt from growing indefinitely, improving search with high-dimensional policies and supporting the use of open-weight LLMs. The proposed PP-ProPS is evaluated on both continuous and discrete control problems spanning Multi-Joint dynamics with Contact (MuJoCo) locomotion, classic control, highway driving, and robotic arm manipulation. Compared to Vanilla ProPS, the proposed PP-ProPS outperforms ProPS in seven of the ten evaluated tasks, and surpasses conventional RL methods including PPO, SAC, and TRPO, in five of the six tasks.

</details>


### [254] [Learning Vision-Based Agile Gap Traversal: Differentiable Simulation with a Warm-Started Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30696)
*Nuthasith Gerdpratoom,Tianchen Sun,Yichao Gao,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段强化学习框架，利用可微仿真中的准解析策略梯度（QPG）和critic暖启动，高效训练以自我为中心视觉运动的四旋翼穿越窄缝策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主四旋翼穿越窄缝具有挑战性，尤其当控制指令直接来自高维视觉观测时；现有端到端方法依赖行为克隆或完整rollout的BPTT，存在策略性能受限或训练成本高的问题。

Method: 两阶段强化学习框架：第一阶段使用包括缝隙几何在内的特权观测训练专家actor和critic，并通过QPG避免对视觉渲染进行反向传播，且无需沿优化参考轨迹重置智能体；第二阶段使用两个自我中心相机的二值缝隙掩码和低维观测训练视觉策略，并用第一阶段特权critic进行暖启动。

Result: 相比冷启动critic或完整rollout BPTT，显著提升训练效率和穿越成功率；系统参数变化时无需重新训练专家actor，跨无人机平台泛化优于基于动作监督的SOTA视觉穿越方法；视觉策略可泛化到未见缝隙形状；真实世界实验验证了使用在线渲染二值掩码的稳健穿越。

Conclusion: 该框架高效、可泛化且具有通用性，可扩展到其他视觉运动机器人学习任务。

Abstract: Traversing narrow gaps is challenging for autonomous quadrotors, especially when control commands come directly from high-dimensional visual observations. Existing end-to-end methods often rely on behavior cloning or full-rollout backpropagation through time (BPTT) via differentiable simulation, which can limit policy performance or incur high training costs. We propose a two-stage reinforcement learning framework for more efficient ego-centric visuomotor gap-traversal policy training, leveraging quasi-analytical policy gradients (QPG) via differentiable simulation and critic warm-starting. The framework utilizes QPG to avoid backpropagation through visual rendering, reducing computation and memory costs while improving sample efficiency. In the first stage, an expert actor and critic are trained using privileged observations, including gap geometry. Unlike prior gap-traversal approaches, our training utilizing QPG does not require resetting the agent along optimized reference trajectories. In the second stage, a visual policy is trained using binary gap masks from two ego-centric cameras and low-dimensional observations, while its privileged critic is warm-started from the first stage. This substantially improves training efficiency and traversal success compared with cold-starting the critic or using full-rollout BPTT. Our framework does not require retraining the expert actor when system parameters change, enabling more efficient generalization across drone platforms than state-of-the-art visual gap-traversal methods based on action supervision. The learned visual policy also generalizes to gaps with unseen shapes. Extensive real-world experiments further demonstrate robust gap traversal using binary masks rendered online. Beyond gap traversal, the proposed framework is generic and can be extended to other visuomotor robot learning tasks.

</details>


### [255] [Auditing Latent-Space Monitors for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30557)
*Nikhil Kamalkumar Advani,Vishwajeet Shivaji Hogale,Saurav Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究审计了利用模型内部表示预测运行时失败的方法，在LaneSegNet在线矢量化地图生成和VAD端到端规划两个自动驾驶任务中，发现帧级错误可预测，但仅用可观测输入输出即可达到同等甚至更好的预测性能，内部潜特征未带来统计显著的增量价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有失败监控常假设模型内部表示包含超出输入输出的额外失败信息，但缺少对该假设的严格审计，尤其是在自动驾驶在线地图生成和端到端规划任务中。

Method: 在LaneSegNet和VAD两个自动驾驶任务上训练监督式潜变量探针，分别预测高Chamfer误差和mean-ADE失败；同时构建仅使用预测输出、自车状态、驾驶指令和预测轨迹等非潜变量的基线监控器，并比较加入潜特征后的增量表现。

Result: LaneSegNet潜探针AUROC为0.780，输出-only监控器达0.825；VAD规划潜探针AUROC为0.868，而使用自车状态、指令和预测轨迹的基线达0.924。加入潜特征后均无统计显著提升，且该现象在多种规划失败端点上持续存在。

Conclusion: 从内部表示预测失败，并不能证明该表示提供了超越可观测输入输出的有用信息；作者提出评估潜访问增量价值的协议，并发布逐帧失败端点标签。

Abstract: Runtime failure monitors can use a model's internal representations to anticipate failures. We audit this monitoring strategy across two autonomous-driving tasks: online vectorized map generation with LaneSegNet and end-to-end planning with VAD. We find that frame-level errors are predictable at inference in both tasks. For LaneSegNet, a supervised latent probe reaches Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve (AUROC) 0.780 for high Chamfer error; to our knowledge, this is the first post-hoc frame-level failure monitor for online vectorized map generation. For VAD, a supervised planning-latent probe reaches AUROC 0.868 for mean-ADE failure.
  Our audit shows that internal access is not necessary for strong failure prediction. A monitor using only LaneSegNet's prediction outputs reaches AUROC 0.825, while for VAD, ego state, driving command, and the planner's predicted trajectory reach 0.924 on the same mean-ADE endpoint. Adding latent features to either baseline yields no statistically resolved improvement. This observation persists across a broad suite of planning failure endpoints, including endpoints whose labels depend on geometry unavailable to the non-latent baseline. Thus, predicting failure from an internal representation does not establish that the representation provides useful information beyond observable inputs and outputs. We propose an evaluation protocol for testing the incremental value of latent access and release our per-frame failure endpoint labels.

</details>


### [256] [Moving Horizon Estimation for Quadrotors: An $\mathcal{L}_1$ Adaptive Optimizer Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30777)
*Thinh Nguyen,Minkyung Kim,Sandeep Banik,Jinrae Kim,Naira Hovakimyan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种线性平滑移动 horizon 估计（MHE）的稠密二次规划（QP）形式，并使用连续时间牛顿法结合 L1 自适应优化器（L1-AO）的时变求解器进行求解。仿真表明，该方法在四旋翼平台上比基线时不变求解器更高效，并在挑战条件下比扩展卡尔曼滤波和标准 MHE 精度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MHE 相比基于卡尔曼滤波的方法精度更高，但计算量更大。传统方法将时变 MHE 视为一系列独立时不变问题并逐步迭代求解，既可能不准确，计算负担也重。时变求解器可利用问题的时间演化以更少迭代跟踪最优解，但实际中因传感器噪声和先验知识缺乏常导致预测不准确，因此需要补偿。

Method: 将线性平滑 MHE 建模为稠密二次规划（QP）；设计由连续时间牛顿法增强的时变求解器，并加入 L1 自适应优化器（L1-AO）来补偿预测误差。

Result: 在四旋翼平台上的仿真结果显示：L1-AO 增强方法比基线时不变求解器更高效地求解 MHE 优化问题，并在挑战条件下相比扩展卡尔曼滤波和标准 MHE 获得更高的估计精度。

Conclusion: 通过时变求解器结合 L1-AO 补偿，可同时提升 MHE 的性能与效率，尤其在噪声和先验不足导致预测不准时效果显著。

Abstract: Moving Horizon Estimation (MHE) is a state estimation method based on finite-horizon optimization that can offer higher accuracy at the cost of increased computation compared to Kalman filter-based approaches. We present a linear smoothing MHE formulation as a dense Quadratic Program (QP), and a solver consisting of a continuous-time Newton's method augmented with the $\mathcal{L}_1$ Adaptive Optimizer ($\mathcal{L}_1$-AO). While MHE is inherently time-varying, conventional approaches treat it as a sequence of independent, time-invariant problems and employ iterative solvers at each time step, which can be both inaccurate and computationally burdensome. In contrast, time-varying solvers track the optimal solution with fewer iterations by exploiting the temporal evolution of the problem, thereby reducing the computational load. In this research, we enhance both the performance and efficiency of MHE through a time-varying solver with an $\mathcal{L}_1$-AO augmentation that compensates for the prediction inaccuracy, which is common in practice due to noisy sensors and the lack of prior knowledge of the system. Simulation results on a quadrotor platform show that the $\mathcal{L}_1$-AO-augmented approach solves the MHE optimization problem more efficiently than the baseline time-invariant solver and achieves higher estimation accuracy under challenging conditions, compared with both the Extended Kalman Filter and the standard MHE.

</details>


### [257] [SoGuDiff: Socially Guided Diffusion for Steerable, Norm-Grounded Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30560)
*Christian Schaible,Haoran Ji,Yash Vardhan Pant,Stephen L. Smith*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扩散模型的机器人导航框架，可在部署时调节社交行为风格，并通过可行性投影层保证运动学可行与避碰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导航不仅需要避碰，还需遵循随场景、文化和部署需求变化的隐含社交规范；传统方法通常只学习单一固定行为，无法在运行时调整。

Method: 使用扩散式导航框架，允许指定并组合连续风格轴（如通过距离、让行方向、对群体的礼让程度）；通过可行性投影层将学习到的社交行为与运动学可行性和碰撞避免分离。

Result: 单轴扫描展示了行为权衡曲线，严格优于所评估的固定行为基线配置；风格差异也在真实世界演示中得到复现。

Conclusion: 该框架支持部署时可调的社交导航行为，使机器人能在更广的行为空间中适应不同环境、文化和部署要求。

Abstract: Beyond collision avoidance, socially competent robot navigation requires adherence to implicit social conventions that vary across contexts, cultures, and deployment requirements. Many conventional navigation policies learn a single normative behavior, either through reinforcement learning against a fixed reward function or imitation of human demonstrations, exposing no interface for adjusting that conduct at runtime. We present a diffusion-based navigation framework whose social behavior can be tuned at deployment: a desired style is specified, such as how closely the robot passes, which side it yields to, or how much it defers to groups, and the planner adapts accordingly. Continuous style axes can be followed independently or composed, spanning a behavioral space rather than discrete, primitive-based specifications. A feasibility projection layer separates learned social behavior from kinematic feasibility and collision avoidance. A single-axis sweep illustrates a tradeoff curve that strictly dominates the evaluated fixed-behavior baseline configurations, and stylistic differences are replicated in real-world demonstrations.

</details>


### [258] [HuGo: LLMs as Whole-Body Policy Code Designers for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30594)
*Seoyeon Choi,Shizhao Ye,Nicholas Bui,Aayushi Shrivastava,Kanghyun Ryu,Dhruva Tirumala,Markus Wulfmeier,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HuGo，一种利用大语言模型生成可执行高层策略代码的层级式人形机器人移动操作框架，底层全身策略冻结，无需逐任务奖励设计或示教即可适应新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要完成大量移动与操作耦合的任务，但现有方法通常依赖奖励工程或示教并逐任务训练，扩展到新任务成本高、难以规模化。

Method: HuGo 采用分层结构：底层使用冻结的全身策略，高层由 LLM 根据任务描述、观测与命令规范生成闭环可执行策略代码；随后利用 rollout 中的数值轨迹和选定视频帧进行反馈，迭代 refinement 并定向更新代码。

Result: 在五个仿真任务、两种底层策略上，HuGo 显著优于高层强化学习基线，并接近基于示教的基线性能；无需任务特定奖励设计或示教收集。还实现了仿真策略到硬件的零样本迁移，并证明对真实 rollout 使用相同 refinement 循环可进一步提升迁移性能，无需专家示教或策略重训练。

Conclusion: HuGo 表明，通过 LLM 代码生成与 rollout 反馈迭代，可以以层级化方式高效获得人形机器人移动操作策略，降低新任务扩展成本，并具备仿真到现实迁移潜力。

Abstract: For humanoids to be useful in everyday environments, they must perform a wide range of tasks that couple locomotion and manipulation. Existing approaches commonly acquire a loco-manipulation policy through reward engineering or demonstrations followed by task-specific training, making it costly to scale to new tasks. In this work, we propose a hierarchical approach to humanoid loco-manipulation that eliminates these per-task requirements. HuGo, Humanoid policy code Generation, uses a Large Language Model (LLM) to generate executable, closed-loop high-level policy code from a task description on top of a frozen low-level whole-body policy. Given the task, observation, and command specifications, the LLM constructs the task logic in code. HuGo then refines the policy from its rollouts using numerical trajectories and selected video frames to produce feedback and targeted code updates. Across five simulation tasks, using two different low-level policies, HuGo substantially outperforms a high-level reinforcement learning baseline and approaches the performance of a demonstration-based baseline. We achieve this level of performance without task-specific reward design or demonstration collection. We further demonstrate zero-shot transfer of simulation-generated policies to hardware and show that applying the same refinement loop to real-world rollouts can further improve transfer performance without expert demonstrations or policy retraining. Project website is https://iconlab.negarmehr.com/HuGo/

</details>


### [259] [SeA-RVINS: Semantic-Aware Tightly Coupled RTK-Visual-Inertial System with Correlation-Preserving Robust Estimation for Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30814)
*Wang Hu,Bo Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 SeA-RVINS，一种用于城市环境的固定滞后因子图 RTK 视觉惯性系统，通过语义感知双目前端、鲁棒 GNSS 动态协方差缩放和混合模糊度延续策略，提升绝对位姿估计的鲁棒性与可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境中，GNSS 多路径和视觉错误关联会造成外点与错误时间关联，并在紧耦合估计器中持续污染位姿估计，因此需要同时利用全局 GNSS 与局部视觉惯性约束并增强鲁棒性。

Method: 采用语义感知的学习型双目前端，在持久地标进入因子图前剔除不可靠轨迹；对双差 GNSS 观测使用动态协方差缩放，提供批处理、标量及潜枢轴等鲁棒形式，并保留共享枢轴相关结构；提出混合模糊度延续策略，短弧内共享一个模糊度状态并验证连续性，弧间用随机游走因子软连接。

Result: 在 TEX-CUP 约 20 km 路线（约 50% 深度城市驾驶）上，潜枢轴配置实现 100% 可用性，最大水平误差 1.6 m；分别有 96.16% 和 99.90% 的历元水平误差低于 1.0 m 和 1.5 m。

Conclusion: SeA-RVINS 能在复杂城市环境中实现鲁棒、实时的 RTK 视觉惯性绝对位姿估计，并已开源发布。

Abstract: Reliable absolute pose estimation in urban environments is undermined by outlier measurements and incorrect temporal associations that can persist in tightly coupled estimators. Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) observations provide globally referenced measurements but are prone to multipath effects. Visual-inertial sensing supplies local motion constraints, but false visual associations can corrupt the estimator. We present SeA-RVINS, a fixed-lag factor-graph Real-Time Kinematic (RTK) visual-inertial system for robust urban pose estimation. A semantic-aware learned stereo frontend rejects unreliable tracks before persistent landmarks enter the graph. For double-differenced GNSS measurements, SeA-RVINS applies Dynamic Covariance Scaling through configurable batch, scalar, and latent-pivot robust formulations while retaining the shared-pivot correlation structure. We propose a hybrid ambiguity-continuation strategy that shares one ambiguity state over short arcs with verified continuity and softly links successive arcs through random-walk factors. On an approximately 20-km route from the public TEX-CUP dataset, including about 50\% deep-urban driving, the latent-pivot configuration achieves 100\% availability and a 1.6-m maximum horizontal error, with 96.16\% and 99.90\% of epochs below 1.0 and 1.5 m, respectively. The implementation is released as open-source software

</details>


### [260] [MOCHA: Multi-Objective Co-Design using Hypernetwork Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30570)
*Varun Madabushi,Neil Janwani,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MOCHA，一种基于强化学习的方法，用单一网络在机器人设计空间中计算一族Pareto最优策略，并利用超网络学习设计依赖的策略参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人设计与控制通常需要联合优化，且涉及多个目标和不同形态设计；传统方法往往需要为每个设计或目标单独训练策略，效率低且难以覆盖权衡关系。

Method: MOCHA采用超网络架构，学习多目标设计超网络（MDH），根据目标与参数化机器人设计生成专用策略网络参数；并通过在学习到的策略网络上进行进化搜索，生成Design Pareto集，同时提出计算通用机器人设计的方法。

Result: 在两种机器人形态上验证，每种包含六个设计维度和2-3个目标；MDH能表示复杂的、依赖设计的策略族，并为不同目标优先级生成最优设计-策略组合，以及在整个目标集上累计性能最佳的通用设计。

Conclusion: MOCHA能够用单一超网络高效实现多目标机器人设计与策略联合优化，生成Pareto最优设计-策略组合和通用机器人设计。

Abstract: In this work, we present MOCHA, the first, to our knowledge, reinforcement learning based approach to computing a family of Pareto-optimal policies across the design space of a robot using a single network. Specifically, MOCHA leverages the hypernetwork architecture to learn a network that produces specialized network parameters optimized for a given objective and parameterized robot design; we term this a multi-objective design hypernetwork (MDH). We demonstrate the capabilities of MDHs to represent a complex family of design-dependent strategies on two distinct robot morphologies, each with six design dimensions and across 2-3 objectives. Moreover, we propose an approach for efficiently producing a Design Pareto set using evolutionary search of the learned policy network, generating the optimal design-policy combination for each objective prioritization. Lastly, we provide an efficient method for computing generalist robot designs which achieve the best cumulative performance across the entire set of objectives.

</details>


### [261] [Precision at Speed: Sample-Efficient Online Model-Based Reinforcement Learning for Hydraulic Excavator Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31025)
*Claudio Canales,Fang Nan,Marco Hutter,Javier Ruiz-del-Solar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在线基于模型的强化学习框架，通过从零学习概率动力学集成模型并结合采样型模型预测控制，在液压挖掘机上实现高精度、高速路径跟踪，且无需演示或仿真预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂驱动动力学机器人的精确高速控制仍具挑战，直接在硬件上学习又受真实交互成本限制，因此需要高样本效率的在线学习方法。

Method: 使用在线基于模型的强化学习，从零训练概率动力学集成模型，用于采样型模型预测控制；设计精度门控轮廓目标，将进度奖励与路径精度挂钩，优先保证精度而非速度。

Result: 在数据驱动挖掘机仿真器中，样本效率高于所评估的基于模型强化学习基线；在11.5吨Menzi Muck M445液压挖掘机上直接学习，20分钟交互后跟踪精度媲美此前用100-150分钟数据训练的控制器，40分钟后在高运行速度下保持亚厘米级平均路径误差。

Conclusion: 该框架能够以极少真实交互时间直接在复杂硬件上学习精确高速控制，显著降低对演示数据和仿真预训练的依赖。

Abstract: Precise, high-speed control remains challenging for robots with complex actuation dynamics. Learning directly on hardware is further constrained by the cost of real-world interaction. We present an online model-based reinforcement learning framework that learns a probabilistic dynamics ensemble model from scratch for sampling-based model predictive control. A precision-gated contouring objective conditions the progress reward on path accuracy, prioritizing precision over speed. In a data-driven excavator simulator, the framework achieves higher sample efficiency than the evaluated model-based reinforcement learning baselines. We validate the framework by learning directly on an 11.5-ton Menzi Muck M445 hydraulic excavator, without demonstrations or simulation pretraining. After 20 minutes of interaction, the controller reaches tracking accuracy comparable to prior learned controllers trained on 100-150 minutes of data. After 40 minutes, it sustains sub-centimeter mean path error at high operating speeds.

</details>


### [262] [Learning-Accelerated Narrow-Phase Collision Detection via Check Ordering for Sampling-Based Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30599)
*Hao Jiang,Yinghan Wang,Jianping He,Xiaoming Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于学习的方法，通过优化窄阶段检查顺序来加速基于阶段的碰撞检测，从而提升运动规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞检测对路径安全至关重要但计算负担大。现有阶段式方法中，宽阶段快速剔除无碰撞对，窄阶段对剩余对做详细检查，但窄阶段通常按宽阶段默认顺序执行，缺乏显式优化，尤其在拥挤环境中窄阶段耗时显著。

Method: 首先形式化窄阶段期望时间成本，推导最小化该期望的最优检查顺序准则；由于所需先验难以预先获得，设计基于超网络的模型预测碰撞概率，并用其近似最优检查顺序，指导窄阶段精确网格检查执行，不替换底层几何碰撞检测器。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法有效加速基于阶段的碰撞检测，并在拥挤环境中提升基于采样的运动规划的效率和成功率。

Conclusion: 通过优化窄阶段检查顺序，可在不替换几何碰撞检测器的前提下减少检测时间，改善运动规划性能。

Abstract: Collision detection is critical for ensuring the safety of planned paths. However, it imposes a non-negligible computational burden on motion planners, motivating extensive studies on collision-detection acceleration. In commonly used phase-based collision-detection methods, the broad phase employs hierarchical structures to rapidly discard object pairs that are clearly collision-free, while the subsequent narrow phase performs detailed collision checks on the remaining object pairs whose collision status cannot be determined by the broad phase. Although these methods effectively reduce the number of detailed checks through broad-phase pruning, the narrow phase is usually executed in the default order returned by the broad phase, with little explicit optimization of the check order. This leaves room for further acceleration, especially in cluttered environments where many object pairs may remain after the broad phase and the narrow phase can account for a significant portion of the total detection time. In this work, we propose a learning-based method to accelerate phase-based collision detection by optimizing the check order in the narrow phase. We first formulate the expected time cost of the narrow phase and derive an optimal check-ordering criterion that minimizes this expectation. Since the priors required by this criterion are difficult to obtain in advance, we design a hypernetwork-based model to predict collision probabilities, which are then used to approximate the optimal check order. The resulting order guides the execution of exact mesh checks in the narrow phase, thereby reducing detection time without replacing the underlying geometric collision checker. Simulation results show that our method effectively accelerates phase-based collision detection and improves the efficiency and success rate of sampling-based motion planning, especially in cluttered environments.

</details>


### [263] [A Second Torque Port for Series Elastic Actuators: Parallel-Integrated Design and Time-Scale Torque Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30873)
*Donghao Jia,Xiubo Xia,Junhang Liu,Zeyu Zhu,Xiaoyu Geng,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并联集成串联弹性执行器 Pi-SEA：在负载侧增加直接驱动微电机并与固定刚度弹簧并联，形成第二扭矩端口；通过时间尺度分配扭矩，无需传感器读取输出扭矩。相比传统 SEA，小幅值跟踪带宽和环境残余力各改善约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 SEA 只有一个扭矩端口，受齿轮电机反射惯量与弹簧影响，饱和后输出幅值随命令频率按 ω^-2 衰减；低于传动静摩擦的指令无法传递，因此需要扩展负载侧扭矩端口以改善小幅值、宽频带和低残余力。

Method: 在负载侧并联无框直接驱动微电机与固定刚度弹簧；由弹簧形变和微电流无传感器估计输出扭矩；按时间尺度而非滤波器设计分配指令扭矩；微扭矩环作为快子系统使闭环奇异摄动，将通道分离转化为可检验边界；引入带泄漏的中值积分器使稳态负载回归弹簧；由幅值上限反推闭式整定规则，匹配弹簧、齿轮电机和微电机。

Result: Pi-SEA 相对传统 SEA，在小幅值下拓宽了可跟踪频带，并将关节施加给环境的残余力降低约一个数量级。

Conclusion: Pi-SEA 通过负载侧第二扭矩端口和时间尺度分层控制，克服单端口 SEA 的幅值衰减与静摩擦死区，并提供可按应用幅频需求进行元件匹配的整定方法，显著提升小信号跟踪与环境交互性能。

Abstract: A series elastic actuator has a single torque port and pays for it twice: the geared motor must swing its own reflected inertia through the spring, so the amplitude it delivers collapses as $ω^{-2}$ in the command frequency $ω$ once it saturates, while commands below the transmission's breakaway friction never arrive at all. This letter opens a second torque port on the load side, placing a frameless direct-drive micro motor in parallel with a fixed-stiffness spring -- a parallel-integrated SEA, or Pi-SEA, whose delivered torque is read from spring deflection and micro current without a sensor -- and dividing the commanded torque between the two channels by time scale rather than by filter design. The micro torque loop is the fast subsystem, which makes the closed loop singularly perturbed and turns the separation the channels need into a bound to check rather than a crossover to tune; a leaky mid-ranging integrator returns the steady load to the spring; and the amplitude ceiling, read backwards, becomes a closed-form sizing rule that matches spring, geared motor and micro motor to the amplitudes and frequencies an application asks for. Against SEAs, the Pi-SEA widens the tracked band at small amplitudes and lowers the residual the joint imposes on its environment, each by an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [264] [ExoLaN: Physics-Consistent Context-Aware Dynamics Learning for Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31434)
*Lucas Schulze,Maximilian Schwarz,Jona Hoppe,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ExoLaN，一种面向人-外骨骼交互的上下文感知 DeLaN，可学习完整耦合系统动力学并适应交互上下文变化，实现无需任务标签的力矩估计与正向预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务无关的辅助外骨骼控制比依赖预定义任务或运动模式的方法更灵活；人类关节力矩估计可支持任务无关辅助，但现有 DeLaN 等方法在多用户场景中无法无需重训练地适应特定用户，也未考虑行走中的间歇接触。

Method: ExoLaN 将时间上下文与来自力敏鞋垫的部分接触力测量相结合，推断潜在动力学嵌入并估计广义接触力矩；同时学习完整耦合系统动力学，并作为统一模型支持正向预测，训练中采用多步预测损失。

Result: 在 7 名未见用户和 21 项未见任务上，ExoLaN 比黑盒基线降低力矩估计 MSE 7%；正向预测中，相比单步损失，多步预测损失使加速度 MSE 降低 59%，长时域位置和速度误差分别降低 60% 和 93%；学习到的潜在上下文无需显式任务标签即可捕获任务信息。

Conclusion: ExoLaN 能提升任务无关力矩估计与正向预测性能，其潜在上下文可作为任务感知辅助控制的有前景信号。

Abstract: Task-agnostic assistive exoskeleton control based on human intention offers greater flexibility than conventional approaches that rely on predefined tasks or motion patterns. Human joint torque estimation enables task-agnostic assistance by characterizing user actions. Physics-consistent methods such as Deep Lagrangian Networks (DeLaN) have been applied to estimate the human torques in multi-user settings, but existing approaches cannot adapt to a specific user without retraining, and do not account for intermittent contacts during locomotion. We propose ExoLaN, a Context-Aware DeLaN for human-exoskeleton interaction that learns the full coupled system dynamics while adapting to changes in interaction context. ExoLaN combines temporal context with partial contact-force measurements from force-sensitive insoles to infer latent dynamics embeddings and estimate generalized contact torques. On seven unseen users performing 21 unseen tasks, ExoLaN reduces torque estimation MSE by 7% compared to a black-box baseline. Beyond inverse dynamics, ExoLaN serves as a unified model that also enables accurate forward prediction: training with a multi-step prediction loss reduces acceleration MSE by 59% and long-horizon position and velocity errors by 60% and 93%, respectively, compared with a single-step loss. Moreover, the learned latent context captures task information without explicit task labels, making it a promising signal for task-aware assistive control.

</details>


### [265] [Audit Before You Commit: Locating Belief Failures in Active Identification for One-Shot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30608)
*Mohamed Abouagour,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出在机器人执行不可逆动作前进行少量探测时，应分别审计两个条件：信念是否仍覆盖决定动作坐标上的真实值，以及失败模型是否跟踪真实失败。作者在探针-提交流水线上离线审计，并发现观察模型误差是导致失败的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法没有区分“信念是否覆盖动作相关真值”和“失败模型是否反映实际失败”这两个条件，导致机器人可能在证据不足或模型错误时过早提交不可逆动作。

Method: 在已部署的探针-提交流水线上分别审计两条件：粒子信念、场景失败分数和一次提交。包括模拟插入实验、共形校准、手部扫描检查敲击边界误差、跨七个任务族和三个引擎测试，以及物理机械臂触觉插入刚性孔实验和带注入模型误差的回放审计。

Result: 更多敲击使信念更尖锐，但真值在16.9%回合中离开支撑集，失败分数乐观偏差0.31。共形校准恢复覆盖率但不改善决策，自信错误实例仍通过置信门。手部扫描发现敲击边界2.1 mm误差，修正后失败率从0.354降至0.112，并无需重新拟合迁移到第二个引擎；第三个引擎则提示执行模型不匹配。固定执行器下少量探测可降低漏检或失败，物理机械臂实验复现了增益与两个条件。

Conclusion: 提交前审计应分开检查信念覆盖和失败模型保真度。观察或执行模型的微小误差可能是失败主因，修正后可显著降低失败并迁移。该审计方法在多种任务和真实触觉插入中有效。

Abstract: A robot that probes a few times before one irreversible action, such as tapping a surface before inserting a peg, must decide when the evidence is enough to commit. We argue that this decision rests on two conditions that existing methods do not separate: the belief must still cover the truth in the coordinate that decides the action, and the failure model that scores actions must track realized failure. We audit both conditions separately, offline and with ground truth, on a deployed probe-then-commit pipeline: a particle belief, a scenario failure score, and one commit. On simulated insertion, more taps sharpen the belief while the truth leaves its support on 16.9% of episodes and the failure score turns optimistic by 0.31. Conformal calibration restores coverage but not the decision: confidently wrong instances still pass a confidence gate. The audit's signatures instead point at the observation model, where a hand scan finds a 2.1 mm error in the tap boundary. Correcting that one number cuts failure from 0.354 to 0.112 on untouched instances and transfers unrefitted to a second engine, while in a third engine the same audit suggests an execution-model mismatch instead. Across seven task families in three engines, a few probes at a fixed executor reduce miss or failure. On a physical arm inserting a tool into a rigid pocket by touch, the gain and the audit's two conditions reproduce, and replaying the recorded taps under an injected model error shows the audit's signature on real data. Additional materials are available at https://sites.google.com/view/auditbeforeyoucommit.

</details>


### [266] [Frequency-Modulated Piezoelectric Haptic Display](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30626)
*Boyuan Liang,Lingfeng Sun,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于压电振动执行器的调频（FM）触觉显示方案，并开发了共享源调频（SSFM）结构，通过振动频率而非振幅编码触觉强度，以降低高密度、大面积可穿戴触觉显示中独立驱动系统的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉显示多通过单个触觉像素的形变振幅来编码强度，但独立控制大量像素振幅会导致驱动系统复杂且庞大，难以扩展到高密度、大面积可穿戴显示。

Method: 研究FM设计原理，用振动频率编码触觉强度；提出SSFM结构，使多个触觉像素由公共功率放大器供电而振动频谱可单独控制；构建概念验证压电触觉显示，并通过志愿者实验评估空间和时间触觉模式的渲染效果。

Result: 志愿者能够可靠区分在研究所涉工作范围内通过FM原理编码的空间和时间模式。

Conclusion: 结果证明了基于FM的分布式触觉渲染可行性，并为未来高密度、大面积可穿戴触觉显示更紧凑的驱动架构提供了潜在路径。

Abstract: We present a frequency-modulated (FM) haptic display based on piezoelectric vibrating actuators. Existing haptic displays commonly encode haptic intensity through the deformation amplitude of individual haptic pixels. Although amplitude-modulated (AM) approaches have enabled compact haptic pixels, independently controlling the deformation amplitude of a large number of pixels can require increasingly complex and bulky driving systems, posing challenges for scaling toward high-density, large-area wearable displays. To address this scaling challenge, we investigate an FM design principle in which haptic intensity is encoded through vibration frequency. We further develop a \textit{Shared-Source Frequency Modulation} (SSFM) structure in which multiple haptic pixels are powered by a common power amplifier while their vibration spectrum are controlled individually, reducing the need for independent high-power amplification at each pixel. A proof-of-concept piezoelectric haptic display was built and evaluated on rendering spatial and temporal haptic patterns through volunteer tests. The results show that participants reliably distinguished spatial and temporal patterns encoded using FM principles within the investigated operating range. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of FM-based distributed haptic rendering and suggest a potential pathway toward more compact driving architectures for future high-density, large-area wearable haptic displays.

</details>


### [267] [MR. POP: Multi-Robot Parallel Optimizing Planner for Almost-Surely Asymptotically Optimal Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30644)
*Chih H. Huang,Roy Xing,Brian Plancher,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 MR. POP，一种基于 GPU 的几乎必然渐近最优（a.s.a.o.）多机器人运动规划器，结合 dRRT 与 AO-x 元算法，利用大规模 GPU 并行实现可扩展的多机器人路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局最优路径规划仍是多机器人运动规划的核心难题；现有基于 CPU 并行的 a.s.a.o. 规划器虽有概率收敛保证和较好性能，但仍难以扩展到多机器人场景。

Method: MR. POP 使用 GPU 上的 SIMT 大规模并行，同时运行数百个 roadmap 构建和树搜索迭代，并并行执行最近邻搜索与碰撞检测；其设计基于 dRRT 和 AO-x 元算法。

Result: MR. POP 成为唯一在最高 35-DOF 多机器人系统中达到 100% 求解率、且比最先进 a.s.a.o. 规划器更快的规划器；它还能将下游运动优化器的成功率从 4% 提升到 72%，因为它生成了高质量、多样化的种子，有助于避免局部极小值。

Conclusion: MR. POP 通过 GPU 并行显著提升了多机器人 a.s.a.o. 运动规划的可扩展性和求解性能，并能产生优质多样化种子以改善后续优化效果。

Abstract: Finding globally optimal paths remains a fundamental challenge in multi-robot motion planning. Despite acceleration of almost-surely asymptotically optimal (a.s.a.o.) planners via CPU-based parallelism, achieving both probabilistic convergence guarantees and strong computational performance, these algorithms still struggle to scale to multi-robot settings. As such, we introduce MR. POP, a GPU-based a.s.a.o. multi-robot planner based on dRRT and the AO-x meta-algorithm. MR. POP uses large-scale GPU-based SIMT-parallelism to simultaneously run hundreds of roadmap construction and tree search iterations with underlying parallel nearest neighbor search and collision checking operations. We show that this enables MR. POP to become the only planner achieving a 100% solve rate while being faster than state-of-the-art a.s.a.o. planners in multi-robot systems up to 35-DOF. MR. POP also raises the success rate of downstream motion optimizers (e.g., from 4% to 72%), by creating high-quality, diverse seeds that help avoid local minima.

</details>


### [268] [Can a Robot Read Braille? - Learning to Adapt Contact via Imitation Learning for Tactile Braille Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30676)
*Xi Chen,Yunlong Shan,Sihan Chen,Jun Hu,Zhongxuan Li,Shiyao Zhang,Sichao Liu,Zhong Zhao,Kosta Jovanovic,Peng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向机器人触觉盲文阅读的自适应接触框架，先评估并物理校正接触质量，再进行识别与重建；在多块盲文板上取得较高触觉质量与重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人盲文阅读研究多关注接触后的模式识别，较少解决如何主动建立可读的物理接触；接触不理想会限制触觉观测质量，从而影响可靠阅读。

Method: 提出自适应接触框架；使用多智能头策略学习，基于专家引导的接触调整示范，联合学习接触可接受性与位姿校正；部署时机器人迭代评估并重新建立接触，保留可靠触觉观测，进行姿态感知融合和盲文重建。

Result: 在20块用于学习与评估的物理盲文板上，方法在10块在线评估板上达到94.0%的触觉质量和88.6%的触觉重建率。

Conclusion: 主动建立可读接触，而非仅在不完美触觉观测下进行识别，对实现可靠的机器人盲文阅读至关重要。

Abstract: For people who are blind, touch provides an essen-tial channel for accessing written information through Braille. Bringing a similar capability to robots requires them not only to recognize tactile patterns, but also to actively establish physical contact that makes those patterns readable. Yet existing robotic Braille readers largely focus on recognition after contact, leaving contact establishment itself insufficiently addressed. We present an adaptive-contact framework for robotic tactile Braille reading that assesses contact quality and physically corrects unsuitable contact before recognition and reconstruc-tion. Multi-Head Policy Learning uses expert-guided contact-adjustment demonstrations to jointly learn contact acceptability and pose corrections. During deployment, the robot iteratively evaluates and re-establishes contact, retaining reliable tactile observations for pose-aware fusion and Braille reconstruction. Across 20 physical Braille plates used for learning and eval-uation, the proposed approach achieves 94.0% tactile quality and 88.6% tactile reconstruction on the ten online-evaluation plates. These results demonstrate the importance of actively establishing readable contact, rather than relying solely on recognition under imperfect tactile observations, for reliable robotic Braille reading.

</details>


### [269] [Multi-Objective Human-in-the-Loop Bayesian Optimization of a Lower-Limb Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30695)
*Neil Janwani,Matthew T. Lerner,Aaron J. Young,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多目标人在回路贝叶斯优化（MO-HILBO），用于推断个性化的帕累托最优控制器，并在下肢外骨骼上以代谢成本和舒适度反馈为双目标进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HILO方法通常只考虑单一目标或对多目标采用固定权重，无法表达个体在不同环境、情绪或能量状态下对目标偏好的动态变化。

Method: 基于显式多目标贝叶斯优化提出MO-HILBO，并与已有单目标/固定权重多目标HILO方法对比；在下肢外骨骼上优化两个目标：代谢成本（效率）和序数人体反馈（舒适度）。

Result: MO-HILBO能够发现帕累托最优控制器；帕累托前沿上点对的排序与验证试验结果一致；并开源了mohilo Python包，用于可穿戴设备上的HILO和MO-HILBO。

Conclusion: MO-HILBO可有效推断个性化帕累托最优控制器，优于现有无法表示个体多目标偏好的HILO方法，适用于可穿戴辅助设备控制优化。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop optimization (HILO) is a common approach for optimizing the control of assistive devices to account for the wearer's unique biomechanics and subjective preferences. However, despite research suggesting that a person may have a different prioritization of objectives depending on time-varying factors such as the environment, their mood, or energy levels, existing HILO approaches only consider a single objective or enforce a fixed weighting on a set of objectives. Neither approach is capable of representing an individual's preferences over objectives. In this work, we propose Multi-Objective Human-in-the-loop Bayesian Optimization (MO-HILBO), which builds on explicit multi-objective Bayesian optimization to efficiently infer a personalized set of Pareto-optimal controllers. We compare our approach with an existing multi-objective HILO method and experimentally demonstrate MO-HILBO on a lower-limb exoskeleton across two objectives: metabolic cost (efficiency) and ordinal human feedback (comfort). We find that MO-HILBO (1) discovers Pareto-optimal controllers, and (2) that the pairwise ordering of points on the Pareto front itself is consistent with validation trials. Lastly, we open-source mohilo, a Python package for running both HILO and MO-HILBO on wearable devices: https://dynamicmobility.github.io/mohilo/.

</details>


### [270] [RoboMonitor: Label-Efficient Runtime Monitoring of Robot Task Execution via Predictive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30715)
*Abhiroop Ajith,Gokul Narayanan,Kyle Coelho,Tingji Zhao,Yash Shahapurkar,Brian Zhu,Melih Erdogan,Ted Krubasik,Constantinos Chamzas,Eugen Solowjow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboMonitor，一种标签高效的视觉-语言机器人执行监控器，先在策略学习数据上预训练，再用少量标注进行时序监督微调，部署时仅需任务指令和相机观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的机器人策略只输出动作，无法判断执行是否按预期进行；执行监控需要识别当前阶段、检测失败和判断任务完成，但相关标注在机器人策略学习数据集中十分稀缺。

Method: 在25小时、12个操作任务、2种机器人本体的多相机轨迹上预训练，使用动作条件未来特征预测、逆动力学和掩码当前预测；将视觉与上下文编码器迁移到因果监控器，并采用 Temporal SFT，结合观测窗口内监督与重叠窗口内及跨窗口一致性目标。

Result: 在四任务监控基准上，仅用52个标注回合达到93.1%平均阶段准确率和85.9%宏召回，优于相同监控监督的Qwen3-VL和Robometer，阶段准确率也超过用100回合训练的二者；Temporal SFT将Qwen3-VL平均虚假阶段切换从15.23%降至4.95%；闭环部署完成40次仿真Toolbox Sorting中的39次和40次真实Reel Packing中的35次，未见误恢复触发。

Conclusion: 标签高效预训练结合时序监督微调能有效实现机器人执行监控，并在仿真与真实闭环任务中展现出可靠性能。

Abstract: Learned robot policies produce actions, but their outputs alone do not establish whether execution is progressing as intended. Robot execution monitoring requires identifying the current execution phase, detecting failures, and recognizing task completion from observations available during execution. Training such monitors requires annotations that are scarce in datasets collected for robot-policy learning. We present RoboMonitor, a label-efficient vision--language execution monitor that learns from these datasets before introducing monitoring supervision. We pre-train on 25 hours of multi-camera trajectories spanning 12 manipulation tasks and two robot embodiments, using action-conditioned future-feature prediction, inverse dynamics, and masked-present prediction. We then transfer the learned visual and context encoders to a causal monitor and apply temporal supervised fine-tuning (Temporal SFT), which combines supervision throughout each observation window with consistency objectives within and across overlapping windows. At deployment, RoboMonitor requires only the task instruction and camera observations.
  On a four-task monitoring benchmark, RoboMonitor trained with 52 labeled episodes achieves 93.1% mean phase accuracy and 85.9% macro recall over two fine-tuning seeds, exceeding Qwen3-VL and Robometer trained with the same monitoring supervision. Its phase accuracy also exceeds that of both Qwen3-VL and Robometer trained with 100 episodes. A Qwen3-VL ablation shows that Temporal SFT reduces mean spurious phase switching from 15.23% to 4.95%. In closed-loop deployment, the integrated system completes 39 of 40 simulated Toolbox Sorting trials and 35 of 40 real-world Reel Packing trials, with no false recovery triggers observed.

</details>


### [271] [From Visual Search to Movement Control: A Priority Field for Artificial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30704)
*Han Zhang,Zhong Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将基于优先级的计算扩展到人工代理的运动控制，提出了一种基于整合优先级图的视觉搜索模型，并进一步为人工代理配备优先级场，在避障任务中展现出更高效的训练和更好的泛化能力，且加入记忆机制后能产生类似人类的历史驱动效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类空间注意通常被认为由整合感知显著性、当前目标和过去经验的优先级图引导，作者希望将这种基于优先级的计算扩展到人工代理的运动控制中。

Method: 首先提出一种轻量级的基于整合优先级图的视觉搜索模型，并用人类眼跳数据训练；然后扩展该模型，为人工代理配备优先级场，在需要到达目标同时避开移动障碍的reach-avoid任务中评估其性能，并与替代架构进行比较，还加入了简单的记忆机制。

Result: 视觉搜索模型复现了关键行为模式，包括眼动抑制和历史驱动的选择；优先级场代理训练更高效，在未见过的复杂场景中表现更好，即使仅从简单演示学习；加入简单记忆机制后，在预测即将到来的目标可能位置时产生了类似人类的历史驱动效应。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明，基于优先级的计算可能为人工代理的运动控制提供有前景的基础。

Abstract: Human spatial attention is widely conceptualized as being guided by a priority map that integrates perceptual salience, current goals, and past experiences. Here, we extend priority-based computation to movement control in artificial agents. We first introduce a lightweight model of visual search based on an integrated priority map. Trained on human saccades, it reproduced key behavioral patterns, including oculomotor suppression and history-driven selection. Extending the search model, we equipped an artificial agent with a priority field and evaluated its performance in a reach-avoid task that required reaching a goal destination while avoiding moving obstacles. Compared with alternative architectures, priority-field agents trained more efficiently and performed better in unseen, complex scenarios, even from simple demonstrations. Adding a simple memory mechanism also produced human-like, history-driven effects in anticipating the likely location of the upcoming goal. These findings suggest that priority-based computation may provide a promising foundation for movement control in artificial agents.

</details>


### [272] [Praxis: Distilling Physical Interaction Priors from Egocentric Videos for Generalizable Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30735)
*Shuliang He,Ruiyan Xu,Bo Yue,Hengming Zhang,Huayi Zhou,Shuai Wang,Wei-Shi Zheng,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Praxis全身移动人形操作框架，利用单次第一人称视频演示中的物理交互先验，结合闭环姿态校准与在线感知，协调导航、校准和灵巧操作三阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动人形操作需要同时实现可达工作空间和精确手-物交互，但对象位姿和接触条件变化使从有限任务数据中学习这些行为仍然困难。

Method: 结合单次第一人称视频演示的物理交互先验、闭环姿态校准和在线视觉/触觉感知；三阶段为视觉语言引导导航、闭环姿态校准对齐臂手工作空间、上下肢同步灵巧操作；在线视觉反馈在新位姿和场景下重定位交互几何，触觉反馈适应实际接触；每个技能仅需一次人类演示，无需任务专用操作策略重训练。

Result: 在五个长时程操作任务中展示了空间、视觉和跨物体泛化能力，并在三个阶段均能应对外部物理扰动后恢复。

Conclusion: Praxis表明，单次演示先验、闭环校准与在线感知结合，可提升移动人形全身操作的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Mobile humanoid manipulation requires both reaching a usable workspace and preserving precise hand-object interactions as object poses and contact conditions change. Learning these behaviors from limited task-specific data remains challenging. To bridge this gap, we introduce Praxis, a whole-body manipulation framework that combines physical interaction priors from one-shot egocentric video demonstrations with closed-loop posture calibration and online perception. The framework coordinates three stages: vision-language-guided navigation toward target objects, closed-loop posture calibration to align the arm-hand workspace, and dexterous manipulation with synchronized upper- and lower-body control. Online visual feedback re-grounds demonstrated interaction geometry under new object poses and scene configurations, while tactile feedback adapts hand motions to actual contact conditions. Each manipulation skill is specified by one human demonstration, without task-specific manipulation-policy retraining. Experiments across five long-horizon manipulation tasks demonstrate spatial, visual, and cross-object generalization, as well as recovery from external physical disturbances across all three stages.

</details>


### [273] [Anatomy-Aware Dexterity-Driven Design Optimization of Surgical Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30745)
*Tony Qin,Peter Connor,Khoa Dang,Carter Hatch,Caleb Rucker,Robert J. Webster,Ron Alterovitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种兼顾灵活性与解剖环境的设计优化方法，用于连续体机器人，并引入RVDSA指标，通过高效运动规划与模拟退火优化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人执行复杂医疗操作时，需要根据具体解剖环境选择几何设计参数，以保证机器人具有高灵活性。

Method: 提出可达体积灵活立体角（RVDSA）作为优化目标，衡量末端执行器从起始构型经无碰撞路径，从不同方向到达目标体积内各点的能力；设计计算高效的运动规划器来计算该目标函数，并使用渐近最优的模拟退火优化器求取优化设计。

Result: 将该方法应用于双机械臂灵活鞘管机器人，用于在结肠解剖结构中执行癌性息肉手术，平均RVDSA比仅优化3D体素覆盖率高出78%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，同时考虑灵活性与解剖结构的设计优化方法能显著提升连续体机器人的操作能力，优于仅考虑覆盖范围的优化方式。

Abstract: Performing complex medical procedures with continuum robots requires careful selection of their geometric design parameters. The robot should have high dexterity in the specific anatomical environment of its procedure. This work presents a design optimization method that considers both dexterity and anatomy. We introduce the Reachable Volumetric Dexterous Solid Angle (RVDSA) metric as our objective, which measures the ability of a robot's end effector to reach the points in a goal volume from different directions via collision-free paths from a start configuration. We present a computationally efficient motion planner to compute this objective function for a given robotic design, and we use an asymptotically optimal simulated annealing optimizer to compute an optimized design. We applied our new method to optimize the design of a bimanual dexterous sheaths robot for performing procedures on cancerous polyps in colon anatomies, achieving a 78% higher RVDSA on average than optimizing for 3D voxel coverage alone.

</details>


### [274] [Design and Characterization of a Variable-Length Continuum Mechanism with Force Locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30759)
*Katelyn King,Veronica Fish,Allison M. Okamura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于杆驱动连续螺旋结构拮抗伸缩的新型连续体机构，通过力锁定实现柔性/刚性切换，从而提升工作空间与刚度，并实验验证其弯曲和刚度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性连续体机构虽适合灵巧导航，但刚度低，在高力操作场景中效果受限，因此需要一种兼具柔顺性和刚性/可调刚度的新机构。

Method: 设计杆驱动的连续螺旋结构，利用拮抗式伸长与收缩实现柔性和刚性行为；螺旋设计结合可变长度能力与力锁定；通过实验量化弯曲范围、定位误差、轴向/弯曲刚度及驱动杆张力对刚度的影响。

Result: 实现180°弯曲运动范围，平均末端定位误差小于10%；力锁定直接提高轴向刚度，并各向异性地间接提高弯曲刚度，轴向分量大的载荷路径上弯曲刚度最大；力锁定状态下增加驱动杆张力可比例提高弯曲刚度，无量纲增益为0.56。

Conclusion: 该连续体机构通过力锁定和张力调节实现刚度可调，兼顾柔顺运动与高力操作能力，为高力场景下的灵巧导航与操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The utility of flexible continuum mechanisms for dexterous navigation is often impaired by their low stiffness, making them ineffective at manipulation in high-force scenarios. To address this challenge, we propose a novel continuum mechanism that achieves both flexible and rigid behavior by antagonistic extension and contraction of a rod-driven continuum helical structure. The helical design combines variable-length capacity with force locking for workspace and stiffness enhancement. In this article, we present the detailed design of the proposed mechanism and characterize its performance through experiments that quantify bending and stiffness. The results demonstrate 180 degree bending range of motion with an average distal positioning error of <10%. Further tests demonstrate that force locking directly improves axial stiffness and thus indirectly increases bending stiffness anisotropically, with maximum bending stiffness along load paths with a large axial component. Tensioning the driving rods provides additional stiffness tunability in the force-locked state, where increasing rod tension proportionally increases bending stiffness with a dimensionless gain of 0.56.

</details>


### [275] [NavGen: Visual Generative Models as a Scalable Data Engine for Embodied 3D Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30770)
*Xijie Huang,Yongyang Wan,Chengbin Dong,Zimo Ding,Mo Zhu,Yijin Wang,Zhiyang Liu,Fei Gao,Yuze Wu,Xin Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 NavGen，一种基于高保真视觉生成模型的文本到视频数据生成流程，用于生成室内外视觉语言导航（VLN）数据，并验证其可扩展性与真实世界迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身3D导航数据存在根本权衡：仿真数据可大规模生成，但视觉上存在虚实差距；真实飞行数据真实但采集成本高。作者希望利用高保真视觉生成模型作为可扩展的数据引擎。

Method: 提出 NavGen 文本到视频数据生成流程，生成多样化室内外 VLN 情节；提出风格多样化方法扩展难以采集的长尾数据；构建约 40 万导航情节数据集；与现有 UAV 导航数据集进行多指标对比；并在世界动作模型范式中部署训练模型到真实飞行实验。

Result: 生成数据集约 400K 导航情节；基于该数据训练的模型通常随规模提升而改善，并优于基于现有数据集训练的模型；真实世界飞行实验中，最终模型在不同导航任务和环境下达到 75% 成功率。

Conclusion: 高保真视觉生成模型可作为具身3D导航的可扩展数据引擎，生成的大规模数据有助于模型扩展并支持真实世界迁移。

Abstract: General-purpose robot models increasingly rely on large and diverse datasets. For embodied 3D navigation, however, existing data sources face a fundamental trade-off: simulated data can be generated at scale but often suffer from the visual sim-to-real gap, whereas real-world flight data provide realistic observations but are costly to collect. This paper studies another direction: the use of high-fidelity visual generative models as scalable data engines for embodied 3D navigation. We introduce NavGen, a text-to-video data generation pipeline that produces diverse vision-language navigation (VLN) episodes across indoor and outdoor scenes. We also propose a style-diversification method that scales up long-tail data that are difficult and costly to collect. The resulting dataset contains approximately 400K navigation episodes. We evaluate our dataset against existing UAV navigation datasets across multiple metrics, and find that the model trained on our data generally improves with scale, outperforming those trained on existing datasets. To validate real-world transferability, we deploy the trained model in world-action-model paradigm to real-world flying experiments. The final model achieves a 75\% success rate across different navigation tasks and environments.

</details>


### [276] [Evaluation Is All You Need for Multi-Modal Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30818)
*Zeyu He,Shiqi Liu,Ke Chen,Yun Yan,Jinzi Wu,Dianqiao Lei,Sirui Wang,ShuRui Peng,Tao Chen,Zhuo Huang,Yu Wu,Yadong Shao,Zhichao Li,Ke Sun,Yang Guan,Keqiang Li,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 iDriveVLA 多模态规划框架，缓解多模态规划中的生成-评估不对称问题，提升候选轨迹空间与可靠、场景自适应的轨迹评估，在 NAVSIM v1 上达到 94.95 PDMS 的新 SOTA，并超过人类专家参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态规划在模糊和长尾场景中具有前景，但现有方法多聚焦于提升轨迹多模态性、轨迹表示或重塑候选分布，存在明显的生成-评估不对称：规划器虽有强 oracle 性能，却常无法可靠选出最佳候选，导致规划潜力未被充分释放。

Method: iDriveVLA 引入统一轨迹评估器，包括用于质量与风险评估的 Safety-aware Scorer，以及用于场景自适应准则加权的 VLM-guided Modulator；同时改进候选轨迹空间，并采用 oracle-aligned 渐进训练策略，包括候选模仿预训练、候选空间精炼和语义排序对齐。

Result: 在公开 NAVSIM v1 榜单上，iDriveVLA 取得 94.95 PDMS，达到新的 state-of-the-art，并超过人类专家参考。

Conclusion: 通过改进候选轨迹空间并实现更可靠、上下文感知的轨迹评估，iDriveVLA 有效缓解了多模态规划中的生成-评估不对称，实现了超越人类专家参考的规划性能。

Abstract: Multi-modal planning is promising for autonomous driving by representing multiple plausible behaviors in ambiguous and long-tail scenarios. Existing methods mainly focus on improving trajectory multi-modality, enhancing trajectory representations, or reshaping the candidate distribution. Nevertheless, we identify a pronounced generation-evaluation asymmetry in multi-modal planning: despite strong oracle performance, existing planners often fail to reliably select the best available candidate, leaving substantial planning potential unrealized. To address this challenge, we propose iDriveVLA, a multi-modal planning framework that improves the candidate trajectory space while enabling more reliable and context-aware trajectory evaluation. Specifically, iDriveVLA introduces a unified trajectory evaluator comprising a Safety-aware Scorer for quality and risk estimation, together with a VLM-guided Modulator for scene-adaptive criterion weighting. We further develop an oracle-aligned progressive training strategy consisting of candidate imitation pretraining, candidate space refinement, and semantic ranking alignment. On the public NAVSIM v1 leaderboard, iDriveVLA achieves a new state-of-the-art performance of 94.95 PDMS, surpassing the human-expert reference.

</details>


### [277] [HIRE: History-Conditioned Interaction Reasoning and High-Rate Execution for Visually Aliased Precision Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30828)
*Rongji Li,Wenhao He,Cewu Lu,Xingyu Chen,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HIRE跨速率框架，结合历史条件交互状态推理器与高频交互流形执行器，解决精密接触操作中历史依赖的部分可观测问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉相似观测可能对应不同潜在交互状态并需不同动作，且小误差会改变结果；仅靠当前视觉无法消歧，现有力觉/记忆增强与高频反应策略将长时程推理与精密执行分离，导致视觉混叠下精密操作不可靠。

Method: HIRE包含历史条件交互状态推理器（ISR）和高频交互流形执行器（IME）。ISR用时序力觉编码器与Force Perceiver编码有序wrench历史作为持续物理证据，生成状态一致动作；IME将接触关键运动分解为内在进度与横向校正以实现精密执行；跨速率循环使物理痕迹反馈后续推理。

Result: 在表面、插入和旋转交互的真实机器人实验中，HIRE在所有评估任务阶段达到至少90%完成率，并提升交互状态消歧、执行精度和泛化能力。

Conclusion: HIRE为历史依赖部分可观测下的精密操作提供了统一的推理-执行视角，其中物理交互既实现任务意图，又揭示潜在状态证据以支持未来决策。

Abstract: Precision manipulation with contact-critical interactions is often history-dependent: visually similar observations can correspond to different latent interaction states and therefore require different actions, while small execution errors can alter task outcomes. Policies relying on the current visual observation alone cannot resolve such ambiguity; force-aware and memory-augmented methods enrich physical or temporal context, while reactive high-rate policies improve local contact response, yet long-horizon temporal reasoning and precision execution remain largely decoupled in existing methods, limiting reliable progression in visually aliased precision manipulation. To bridge this gap, we introduce History-Conditioned Interaction Reasoning and Execution (HIRE), a cross-rate framework comprising a history-conditioned Interaction-State Reasoner (ISR) and a high-rate Interaction-Manifold Executor (IME). ISR encodes ordered wrench history with a temporal wrench encoder and Force Perceiver as persistent physical evidence for state-consistent action generation, while IME structures contact-critical motion into intrinsic progress and transverse correction for precise execution; their cross-rate loop allows the resulting physical traces to inform subsequent reasoning. In real-robot experiments across surface, insertion, and rotational interactions, HIRE achieves at least 90% completion across all evaluated task stages while improving interaction-state disambiguation, execution precision, and generalization. More broadly, HIRE provides a unified reasoning--execution perspective on precision manipulation under history-dependent partial observability, where physical interaction both realizes task intent and reveals latent-state evidence for future decisions. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [278] [Fast Plans, Faithful Actions: Closing the Planning-Execution Gap in Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30833)
*Chuanliang Xie,Boyu Ma,Gen Li,Yizhou Liu,Houwang Chen,Xinyu Zhou,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究一种改自π0.5的waypoint分层VLA系统，发现其规划延迟高且规划未被有效利用；通过waypoint对齐的块自回归解码（Block-AR）和归一化目标调制（NGM）同时解决这两个问题，大幅降低VLM前向次数与规划延迟并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层VLA（高层VLM规划器+底层动作专家）只有在规划器足够快以满足实时控制、且规划真正参与动作生成时才有实用价值。但基线Token-AR需57次昂贵的VLM前向，且实验发现擦除waypoint端点对任务成功率几乎无影响，暴露出规划-执行器错位：规划粒度过细、执行器对规划利用不足。

Method: 提出waypoint对齐的块自回归解码（Block-AR）以降低规划延迟；提出归一化目标调制（NGM），这是一种受相位门控和反捷径训练约束的逐层目标路径，使waypoint能影响动作生成，同时保留其他控制信号。

Result: 在LIBERO上VLM最大前向次数从57降至8（含1次prefix prefill），在Rokae双臂机器人上规划延迟降低8.7倍。结合NGM与反捷径训练后，Block-AR在LIBERO-Long成功率从91.0%提升至96.2%，四个套件平均成功率从95.85%提升至98.45%；该机器人上的三个双臂任务成功率在各方法间保持相当。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了分层规划器中规划粒度过细与执行器规划利用不足的错位问题，并证明Block-AR与NGM可分别有效缓解规划延迟与规划利用不足，从而提升分层VLA系统的实时性与任务表现。

Abstract: Hierarchical vision-language-action (VLA) systems consist of a high-level vision-language planner and a low-level action expert that generates continuous actions. This hierarchical design has practical value only if the planner can generate plans fast enough to meet real-time control requirements, and the resulting plans actually contribute to the generation of action. We study one such system, a waypoint hierarchy pipeline adapted from $π_{0.5}$, and find that neither requirement is satisfied. This baseline relies on token-level autoregressive decoding (Token-AR) to generate a waypoint plan, requiring 57 very expensive vision-language model (VLM) forward passes. However, we find that erasing the waypoint endpoints has little effect on task success. Two findings reveal the misalignment of planner-executor: the planner generates outputs at an excessively fine granularity, and the executor underuses plans as a control condition. We address the latency issue with waypoint-aligned block-autoregressive decoding (Block-AR), and plan underuse issue with normalized goal modulation (NGM), a layer-wise goal path constrained by phase gating and anti-shortcut training so that the waypoint influences action generation maintaining other signals. Our method reduces the maximum number of VLM forward passes from 57 to 8 on LIBERO, including one prefix prefill, and achieves an $8.7\times$ reduction in planning latency on a Rokae dual-arm robot. With normalized goal modulation and anti-shortcut training, Block-AR's success rate on LIBERO-Long increases from 91.0% to 96.2%, while its average success rate across the four suites increases from 95.85% to 98.45%. On three bimanual tasks with this robot, success rates remain comparable across methods.

</details>


### [279] [PHASE: Compliance-Enabled Tactile Phase Retrieval for Few-Shot Insertion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30889)
*Jeremy Siburian,Cristian C. Beltran-Hernandez,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Masashi Hamaya,Mai Nishimura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PHASE框架利用顺应性手腕产生的触觉信号进行相位分割与检索，提升少样本接触密集型插孔任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限演示中学习接触密集型装配（如轴孔插入）仍然困难，现有检索增强模仿学习在该场景下的应用尚未被充分探索，且插入任务的多阶段特性要求按阶段检索经验。

Method: 提出PHASE框架，包括多模态接触感知表示学习、基于触觉信号的变长相位分割，以及面向策略学习的相位一致检索。

Result: 在真实世界轴孔插入任务中，针对五种销几何形状，PHASE相比最强非相位感知基线整体成功率提高13个百分点，在未见初始位置下性能提升30个百分点。

Conclusion: 将检索与交互定义的接触相位对齐，能显著提升少样本插入学习的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Contact-rich assembly tasks such as peg-in-hole insertion remain difficult to learn from limited demonstrations. While retrieval-augmented imitation learning, which augments target demonstrations with relevant prior data, offers a promising direction, its applicability to contact-rich manipulation remains largely unexplored. Contact-rich insertion unfolds over multiple phases from search to insert, and retrieving phase-specific experience from prior data in principled ways remains an open question. Our key insight is that a compliant wrist enables the robot to sustain contact throughout execution, producing rich tactile and force signals that naturally reveal the phase structure of insertion and inform what should be retrieved. Based on this insight, we present PHASE (PHase-Aware Segmentation and REtrieval), a framework for compliance-enabled tactile phase retrieval that integrates multimodal contact-aware representation learning, variable-length phase segmentation from tactile signals, and phase-consistent retrieval for policy learning. We evaluate PHASE on real-world peg-in-hole insertion across five peg geometries, comparing against retrieval strategies drawn from state-of-the-art methods under a shared policy architecture. PHASE improves the overall success rate by 13 percentage points over the strongest non-phase-aware baseline, and improves performance under unseen initial positions by 30 percentage points. These results demonstrate that aligning retrieval with interaction-defined contact phases substantially improves robustness in few-shot insertion learning.

</details>


### [280] [VLaRL: Augmenting Vision-Language-Action Models with Simulation-Trained Latent-Conditioned Residual RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30868)
*Namiko Saito,Kinam Kim,Heecheol Kim,Katsushi Ikeuchi,Yasuyuki Matsushita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出VLaRL，一种面向冻结VLA模型的潜在条件残差强化学习方法。它利用VLA内部视觉-语言潜在表示作为残差控制条件和仿真到现实迁移接口，使残差策略可在仿真中训练并直接部署到真实机器人，无需真实世界RL或在线自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型能根据指令执行广泛操作，但在接触丰富任务中物理执行精度不足。残差RL可修正错误且保持VLA冻结，但真实机器人RL成本高且涉及安全风险。核心挑战是仿真与真实之间的视觉差距，导致仿真训练的残差策略难以迁移。

Method: 提出VLA Latent-Conditioned RL（VLaRL）。它使用VLA内部的视觉-语言潜在表示来条件化残差控制，并将其作为仿真到现实的迁移接口；同时学习一个轻量级映射器，将仿真得到的潜在表示转换为接近真实潜在分布。该方法无需像素级视觉对应，也无需真实世界RL或在线自适应。

Result: 在四个接触丰富操作任务和两个VLA骨干模型上，VLaRL在所有任务-骨干组合中都提升了真实世界成功率。控制变量消融实验表明，潜在条件化和潜在对齐对于迁移仿真训练的残差控制都很重要。

Conclusion: VLaRL表明，利用VLA潜在表示进行条件化与对齐，可以有效实现冻结VLA的残差RL仿真训练到真实部署，避免昂贵且危险的真实机器人RL，并提升接触丰富操作的真实世界性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide broad, instruction-conditioned manipulation behaviors, but their physical execution can remain imprecise during contact-rich interaction. Residual reinforcement learning (RL) can correct such errors while keeping the VLA frozen, but real-robot RL is costly and safety-critical. We propose VLA Latent-Conditioned RL (VLaRL), which enables residual RL for frozen VLAs to be trained in simulation and deployed on real robots without real-world RL or online adaptation. The key challenge is transferring the learned residual policy despite the visual gap between simulation and reality. Rather than requiring pixel-level visual correspondence, VLaRL uses the VLA's internal vision-language latent representation to condition residual control and as the sim-to-real transfer interface, and learns a lightweight mapper that transforms simulation-derived latents toward the real latent distribution. Across four contact-rich manipulation tasks and two VLA backbones, VLaRL improves real-world success in all task-backbone combinations, while controlled ablations demonstrate the importance of both latent conditioning and latent alignment for transferring simulation-trained residual control.

</details>


### [281] [Impedance Cloning: Learning Equilibrium Point Parameters for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30842)
*Hayato Takahashi,Ryoga Oishi,Yuki Kasuga,Toshiaki Tsuji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Impedance Cloning，通过模仿生物力学先验（刚度和平衡点）而非轨迹，从双边遥操作演示中用粒子滤波提取阻抗参数，无需力/扭矩传感器，从而在接触丰富操作中被动吸收接触不确定性，并在 CRANE-X7 上验证了泛化性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作要求机器人应对外表面几何偏离训练条件的情况。基于轨迹的模仿学习只复现可观测输出，在几何偏移下容易失效。因此需要模仿生成运动的阻抗意图，而非结果轨迹，以泛化到不同表面几何。

Method: 提出 Impedance Cloning，模仿刚度与平衡点。通过粒子滤波从双边遥操作演示中提取这些参数，无需力/扭矩传感器。在两台 CRANE-X7 机械臂上评估擦拭、pick-and-place 和抓取任务，并与轨迹基线、固定阻抗及变阻抗控制基线对比，同时用 ILBiT 和 Mamba backbone 验证通用性。

Result: 擦拭任务中，关节空间轨迹基线在 -6 cm 以下失去接触，本方法在 -6 cm 以上保持 4-5 N 接触力并逐渐下降；笛卡尔空间下，0 到 +8 cm 的力-高度斜率为 0.13 +/- 0.03 N/cm，优于固定阻抗基线的 0.34-0.83 N/cm。10 种杯子 100 次 pick-and-place 中成功 84 次，优于固定阻抗 74/100，接近变阻抗 82/100，但仅需 1 次演示而非 10 次。抓取任务中降低力矩跟踪误差，并适用于 ILBiT 和 Mamba。

Conclusion: 模仿刚度和平衡点等生物力学先验可编码操作意图而非结果，从而在表面几何变化下更好泛化。该方法无需力/扭矩传感器，能提升接触丰富操作的鲁棒性，并具有跨架构通用性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires robots to regulate force against surfaces whose geometry deviates unpredictably from training conditions. Trajectory-based imitation learning, which reproduces observable outputs, breaks down under such shifts. We propose Impedance Cloning, which instead imitates the biomechanical priors that generate motion -- the stiffness and equilibrium point -- and thereby passively absorbs contact uncertainty. Because these parameters encode intent rather than outcome, they generalize across surface geometries where trajectory reproduction does not. We extract them from bilateral teleoperation demonstrations via a particle filter without force/torque sensors and evaluate the framework on two CRANE-X7 manipulators. In a wiping task with joint-space actions, the trajectory-based baseline loses contact below -6 cm, whereas the proposed method maintains a consistent 4-5 N contact force above -6 cm, with a gradual decrease below; with Cartesian-space actions, its force-height slope over 0 to +8 cm is 0.13 +/- 0.03 N/cm, versus 0.34-0.83 N/cm for fixed-impedance baselines. In a pick-and-place task with 10 diverse cups (100 trials), the proposed method succeeds in 84 trials, outperforming the fixed-impedance baseline (74/100) and performing comparably to a variable impedance control baseline (82/100) with one demonstration instead of ten. In a grasping task, the representation reduces torque tracking error with both ILBiT and Mamba backbones, confirming its generality across architectures.

</details>


### [282] [Bundled Contact Gradients: Stabilizing Differentiable Simulation for Deployable Dynamic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30951)
*Dyuman Aditya,Jin Cheng,Clemens Schwarke,Quan Nguyen,Gaurav Sukhatme,Stelian Coros,Gabriele Fadini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Bundled Contact Gradients (BCG)，一种面向可微分机器人仿真的接触局部随机平滑方法，用于在刚性接触下稳定一阶策略优化，并成功实现人形机器人动态动作的零样本真机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微分仿真虽能提供解析梯度并提升策略优化效率，但刚体接触通常需要软化模型才能获得平滑梯度，这会牺牲物理保真度，限制策略的仿真到现实迁移。对于动态人形运动，接触动力学准确性至关重要；提高接触刚度虽能提升保真度，却会导致梯度对状态扰动更敏感、方差更大，进而使一阶策略学习不稳定。

Method: 提出 BCG 框架：当检测到刚性接触时，在刚性接触构型附近生成一组局部随机扰动 rollout，并聚合其梯度信号，以降低梯度方差，同时保留较刚性的接触动力学。

Result: 该方法成功训练了动态运动策略，并将其零样本迁移到真实 Unitree G1 人形机器人平台上。

Conclusion: BCG 通过在刚性接触下进行接触局部随机平滑，缓解了可微分仿真中物理保真度与梯度稳定性之间的权衡，使动态人形运动策略能够从仿真有效迁移到现实世界。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation provides analytic gradients of robot dynamics, enabling fast and sample-efficient first-order policy optimization. However, obtaining smooth and informative gradients through rigid-body contact typically requires softened contact models, often at the expense of physical fidelity and thereby limiting learned policies largely to simulation. This trade-off becomes particularly consequential for dynamic humanoid motions, where accurate contact dynamics are critical for transferring policies to the real world. Increasing contact stiffness in rigid-body simulation improves the fidelity of interactions, but also makes the dynamics increasingly sensitive to small state perturbations, producing high-variance gradients that can destabilize first-order policy learning. To address this, we propose \emph{Bundled Contact Gradients (BCG)}, a contact-local randomized smoothing framework for differentiable policy learning. When stiff contact is detected, our method evaluates a local bundle of randomized perturbation rollouts around the stiff contact configuration and aggregates their gradient signal thereby reducing gradient variance. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method by successfully training and transferring dynamic motions zero-shot onto a real-world Unitree G1 humanoid platform. Videos and supplementary information can be found at https://bundledcontactgradients.github.io/

</details>


### [283] [Causeway: Restoring Task Accessibility for Instruction Switching in VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30913)
*Qingzi Wang,Kaixi Feng,Guangyao Shi,Xiyang Wu,Ang Li,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在指令切换时的失败问题，提出免训练推理时干预方法 Causeway，通过在冻结解码过程中进行状态导向写入，使策略能从先前任务造成的“任务孤岛”状态恢复到目标任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略通常能从标准初始状态执行任务，但当另一任务改变了机器人物理状态后，新指令可能失败。作者将这种从标准初始状态可靠完成、却无法从先前任务产生的状态进入的目标任务状态称为“任务孤岛”，希望解决指令切换中的可达性问题。

Method: 提出 Causeway，一种免训练推理时干预。给定当前状态和目标任务的重新进入姿态，Causeway 在冻结的解码计算中反向传播，并在动作流表示中施加状态导向写入。VLA 自行解码返回运动，无需更新参数、新增动作头或外部动作生成。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Goal 上，覆盖 71 个跨物体配对、3 种切换时机和 3 种 VLA 架构，Causeway 将裸切换成功率从 3-26% 提升到 47-65%，并将到达交接邻域的比例提高 42-72 个百分点。LIBERO-Object 和真实 xArm 平台实验表明，该恢复能力可扩展到主设置之外，并同时适用于仿真和真实机器人。

Conclusion: Causeway 能有效缓解 VLA 策略在指令切换后因状态改变而无法恢复目标任务的问题，且无需训练即可跨模型、跨任务和跨仿真/真实平台提升恢复成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies can execute many tasks from standard initial states, yet a new instruction may fail after another task has altered the robot's physical state. We study instruction switching, where a new task is issued during or after the execution of a different one. We observe that a target task that is reliably completed from its standard initial states can become inaccessible from states produced by a preceding task. We call such states task islands. We propose Causeway, a training-free inference-time intervention. Given the current state and a re-entry pose for the target task, Causeway back-propagates through the frozen decoding computation and applies a state-directed write within the action-stream representation. The VLA decodes the return motion itself, without parameter updates, a new action head, or external action generation. Across 71 cross-object pairs, three switch timings, and three VLA architectures on LIBERO-Goal, Causeway raises bare-switch success from 3-26% to 47-65% and increases the rate of reaching the handoff neighborhood by 42-72 percentage points across models. Additional experiments on LIBERO-Object and a real xArm platform show that the recovery extends beyond the main LIBERO-Goal setting, both in simulation and on a robot.

</details>


### [284] [VisTacAlign: Co-Training Dexterous Policies on Tactile Human and Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30959)
*Julien Poffet,Matthew Strong,Ankush Dhawan,Baiyu Shi,Shalika Neelaveni,Yujia Yuan,Zhenan Bao,Monroe Kennedy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VisTacAlign 框架，通过视觉与触觉对齐，将人类演示与机器人演示联合训练 3D 视觉-触觉灵巧操作策略，并在三项真实任务上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示是灵巧操作的低成本数据来源，但人类与机器人在策略所使用的每个模态上都存在差异（人-机鸿沟），直接联合训练效果受限，因此需要在视觉与触觉层面统一两种数据。

Method: 将手套追踪的人手运动重定向到 17 自由度触觉机器人手，并进行一次性指尖校正；从立体视图中擦除人手，替换为用机器人录像像素着色的机器人手网格，再对合成图像重跑实时立体基础模型，使人点云与机器人点云具有相同的立体误差与可见性；将电容式触觉手套与机器人指尖传感器在其信号空间中对齐，得到统一且可解释的逐指力表示；使用扩散 Transformer 消费点云、本体感觉与逐指触觉 token。

Result: 在乐高组装、不同尺寸草莓采摘、启动并举起电钻三项需要精确力的真实任务中，在已有机器人数据上加入对齐后的人类演示，性能优于仅用机器人数据的策略；消融实验表明触觉输入与视觉对齐均为必要。

Conclusion: 通过统一视觉与触觉模态的人-机对齐，人类演示可有效增强机器人灵巧操作策略的学习，且视觉对齐与触觉感知对性能提升不可或缺。

Abstract: Human demonstrations are a cheap source of data for dexterous manipulation, but co-training a robot policy on them requires closing the human--robot gap in every modality the policy consumes. We present VisTacAlign, a framework for co-training 3D-visual-tactile dexterous policies on human and robot demonstrations. Glove-tracked human hand motion is retargeted to a 17-DoF tactile robot hand with a one-time fingertip correction. The human hand is then erased from both stereo views and replaced by a posed robot-hand mesh painted with pixels from robot recordings, and a real-time stereo foundation model is re-run on the composite, so the human point clouds carry the same stereo errors and visibility as the robot ones. Finally, a capacitive tactile glove is aligned to the robot's fingertip sensors in its signal space, giving one interpretable per-finger force representation. A diffusion transformer consumes point-cloud, proprioceptive, and per-finger tactile tokens. On three real-world tasks requiring precise force -- Lego assembly, plucking strawberries of varying size, and activating and lifting a power drill -- adding aligned human demonstrations to existing robot data improves over robot-only policies, and ablations show that both tactile input and visual alignment are necessary. Project page: https://vis-tac-align.github.io

</details>


### [285] [FRAM: Trajectory-Guided Visual Feature Selection for Compact Language-Conditioned Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30965)
*Hiroshi Ito,Hyogo Hiruma,Yoshiki Kanai,Takahiro Yoshida,Akira Kanazawa,Hiroki Yamada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出小型机器人策略FRAM，通过将预测的未来末端轨迹与当前视觉输入关联，实现高性能与强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型在机器人操作中表现强，但通常参数规模很大，因此希望用更小的策略同时获得高成功率与鲁棒性。

Method: FRAM利用预测未来末端轨迹的图像坐标作为空间指针，从当前图像中读取与运动相关的局部视觉特征，将动作生成信息组织为参考位置（Where）、视觉状态（What）和未来运动（Future）。轨迹标签由演示和相机几何自动生成，无需人工标注。模型参数量138.7M，包含冻结的语言编码器。

Result: 在四个标准LIBERO套件上平均成功率达92.2%，接近3.3B参数π0的94.2%；无需额外训练在LIBERO-Plus上平均达67.3%。消融验证未来轨迹和局部视觉特征均提升性能与鲁棒性。在真实双臂UR5e上，仅用腕部相机完成叠杯子，并能选择和切换左右臂。

Conclusion: 基于未来运动选择视觉信息，是小型机器人策略同时获得高表现和鲁棒性的有效方式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models achieve strong performance in robot manipulation, but often require large numbers of parameters. In this work, we propose the Future Representation Action Model (FRAM), a small policy that explicitly links the future end-effector trajectory to the current visual input.
