Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向移动储能系统（MESS）的三阶段调度框架，用于提升电网韧性，并引入图神经网络方法提高预配置与动态重定位阶段的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端天气等高影响、低概率扰动日益频繁，提升电网韧性成为关键需求。MESS具有高运行灵活性和快速部署能力，可快速支援关键负荷、支持孤岛运行并适应电网变化，因此有望补充或替代传统韧性提升手段。

Method: 提出三阶段调度框架：第一阶段正常运行阶段，优化MESS在母线处的充放电调度以实现经济套利；第二阶段主动预配置阶段，在收到预警信号后转向韧性目标，考虑线路故障概率分布，确定运输网络中的最优待命点以最小化到达关键负荷的期望时间；第三阶段动态重定位阶段，在灾后恢复阶段根据电网更新（如后续线路故障）重新部署MESS。同时提出基于图神经网络的求解方法，以提升预配置和重定位阶段中MESS最优布点的计算效率。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提框架能够降低期望未供能量（EENS），并加快恢复速度。

Conclusion: 三阶段MESS调度框架结合图神经网络求解方法，可有效提升电网韧性，减少关键负荷停电损失并改善灾后恢复性能。

Abstract: As high-impact, low-probability disturbances such as extreme weather events increase in frequency, enhancing power grid resilience has become a critical priority. Mobile Energy Storage Systems (MESSs) have strong potential for helping with this purpose due to their high operational flexibility and fast deployability. They can be rapidly relocated to supply critical loads, support islanded operation, and adapt to changing conditions of the grid, making them a promising addition or alternative to conventional resilience-providing methods. This paper proposes a three-stage framework for scheduling MESS units. In the first stage (Normal Operation), charging/discharging scheduling of MESSs at buses is optimized for economic arbitrage. Upon receiving an early warning signal, the second stage (Proactive Positioning) shifts the focus to resilience. Here, the model determines the optimal staging points within the transportation network to minimize the expected time of arrival at critical loads, considering the probability distribution of line outages. The third stage (Dynamic Relocation) addresses the post-event restoration phase, in which MESSs are relocated in response to grid updates, such as subsequent line failures. Furthermore, a graph neural network-based solution approach is proposed to further enhance computational efficiency in determining the optimal placing of MESS units during the pre-allocation and relocation stages. The simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework reduces expected energy not served and improves the restoration speed.

</details>


### [322] [Certificate-Carrying Distributed Model Predictive Control on Product Manifolds with $\mathrm{SO}(3)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30655)
*Shengjun Zhang,Tingyi Liu,Lei Xu,Tao Yang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该文研究同步分布式模型预测控制中，邻居预测在采样间隔内变化时的约束认证问题，提出基于移位预测和更新预算的硬信任域与可行性保障机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步DMPC中，各智能体并行求解时邻居未来预测可能变化，导致约束满足和递归可行性难以保证，且传统固定或在线裕度可能过于保守。

Method: 在并行局部求解前，各智能体通信移位预测和公布的更新预算；利用硬轨迹信任域使预算可执行，并基于移位数据包计算边级可行性上限以保持回退可行；对距离和相对姿态约束使用显式Lipschitz常数和两层预算，分别处理邻居同步更新和可检查的移位储备。

Result: 证明了在标称执行和终端假设下硬成对约束满足与递归可行性，给出执行误差导致的额外残差，并推导局部实用价值下降界；航天器编队示例包含硬终端约束、成对约束、SO上的测地相对姿态约束和可复现终端集检查。

Conclusion: 与固定裕度、仅轨迹裕度和窗口在线裕度相比，所提预算在保持正移位可行性裕度的同时降低了保守性。

Abstract: This paper studies constraint certification in synchronous distributed model predictive control (DMPC) when neighboring predictions change between sampling instants. Before the parallel local solves, each agent communicates a shifted prediction and an announced update budget. A hard trajectory trust region makes that budget enforceable, while an edge-wise feasibility cap computed from the shifted packets keeps the fallback feasible without using any current optimizer output. Distance and relative-attitude constraints are tightened with explicit Lipschitz constants and two budget layers: one accounts for the simultaneous neighbor update and the other retains a checkable shift reserve. We prove hard pairwise constraint satisfaction and recursive feasibility under stated nominal-execution and terminal assumptions, give the additional residual caused by execution error, and derive a local practical value-decrease bound. A spacecraft formation example uses hard terminal and pairwise constraints, a geodesic relative- attitude constraint on $\SO$, and reproducible terminal-set checks. Comparisons with fixed, trajectory-only, and windowed online margins show that the proposed budget reduces conservatism while preserving a positive shifted-feasibility margin.

</details>


### [323] [Decision-Gated Surrogate-Assisted Stochastic Optimization with Independent High-Fidelity Certification for Photovoltaic Hosting-Capacity Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30847)
*Ali Abubakar,Samuel Essamuah Assabil,Akhtar Hussain,Van-Hai Bui*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向决策、代理模型辅助的随机多目标优化框架，用于在高光伏渗透规划中权衡渗透率与总电气损耗，并满足可靠性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光伏渗透规划需要在辐照度和负荷不确定下反复进行非线性光伏与潮流评估，同时优化渗透率和总电气损耗两个竞争目标并满足可靠性约束，计算成本高。

Method: 采用校准光伏模型与配电网仿真器作为高保真参考，构建数据驱动和物理信息两种代理模型加速优化；引入面向优化筛选的决策评估、决策门控富集策略，以及跨馈线迁移验证。

Result: 两种代理模型留出误差接近，但优化筛选后的运行有效性分别为44.4%和93.4%；物理信息代理将60场景设计评估从286.0 ms降至0.46 ms，约620倍加速。富集策略使预测误差降低39.1%。500场景独立认证验证80个候选，识别46个非支配设计；所选折中方案期望渗透率70.74%，期望总电气损耗297.9 kW。跨馈线迁移在85和69节点系统消除基线电压违规，但在33节点系统产生过压并使馈线网络损耗增加62.7%。

Conclusion: 认证后的渗透率具有拓扑特异性，需进行决策级审计、独立认证和馈线特定验证；代理模型选择应基于决策性能而非测试精度。

Abstract: High photovoltaic (PV) penetration planning requires repeated nonlinear PV and power-flow evaluations under uncertain irradiance and demand, particularly when two competing objectives, penetration and total electrical loss, are optimized simultaneously subject to reliability constraints. This paper develops a decision-focused, surrogate-assisted stochastic multiobjective framework in which a calibrated PV model coupled with a distribution-network simulator provides the high-fidelity reference, while matched data-only and physics-informed surrogates accelerate optimization and are evaluated empirically for decision reliability across the loss-penetration tradeoff. Although the two surrogates exhibit nearly identical held-out errors, optimizer-realistic screening yields markedly different operational validity rates of 44.4% and 93.4%, respectively, motivating selection of the physics-informed model on decision performance rather than test accuracy. Surrogate inference cuts the cost of a 60-scenario design evaluation from 286.0 to 0.46 ms, an approximately 620 time speedup. A decision-gated enrichment strategy further targets Pareto-critical, constraint boundary, and surrogate-disagreement regions, reducing prediction error by 39.1%. Independent 500 scenario certification verifies all 80 screened candidates and identifies 46 non-dominated designs; the selected compromise attains 70.74% expected penetration with 297.9 kW expected total electrical loss. Cross-feeder transfer eliminates baseline voltage violations on the 85- and 69-bus systems, but produces overvoltage and a 62.7% increase in feeder-network loss on the 33-bus system, demonstrating that certified penetration is topology-specific and demands decision-level auditing, independent certification, and feeder-specific validation.

</details>


### [324] [AC Power Flow Contingency Analysis Using a Single Deep Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30859)
*Md Obaidur Rahman,Junjie Qin,Vassilis Kekatos*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该文提出一种仅使用基态AC潮流数据训练的单一机器学习模型，通过不动点迭代估计任意单线路故障后的运行状态，并用半定规划验证深度神经网络的收敛条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AC潮流的预想事故分析对电网安全评估很重要，但计算负担随运行场景和故障配置数量增加而增大；现有机器学习方法通常需要针对具体故障的训练数据，导致离线训练成本随故障数增长。

Method: 将故障后状态预测建模为不动点迭代；当机器学习模型为深度神经网络时，推导保证收敛的充分条件，并建立半定规划形式来认证给定DNN是否满足这些条件；在IEEE 118节点系统上开展数值测试。

Result: 数值测试表明，所提半定规划形式较紧，认证条件在所有测试故障下均成立，且该方法仅需少数几次迭代即可得到准确的故障后状态估计。

Conclusion: 该框架能够复用仅在基态AC潮流数据上训练的单一ML模型，对任意单线路故障进行故障后状态估计，从而降低离线训练负担，并具备可认证的收敛性。

Abstract: Contingency analysis using the AC power flow (AC-PF) model is a critical tool for accurate grid security assessment, but its computational burden increases with the number of operating scenarios and outage configurations to evaluate. Recent ML-based approaches typically require outage-specific training data, leading to offline training costs that scale with the number of contingencies. This work proposes a framework that reuses a single ML model trained solely on basecase AC-PF data to estimate post-contingency operating states under arbitrary single-line outages. The proposed approach formulates post-contingency state prediction as a fixed-point iteration. If the ML model is a deep neural network (DNN), we derive sufficient conditions that guarantee convergence and develop semidefinite programming (SDP) formulations to certify these conditions for a given DNN. Numerical tests on the IEEE 118-bus system demonstrate that the proposed SDP formulations are tight, that the certified conditions hold for all tested contingencies, and that the resulting method produces accurate post-contingency state estimates within only a few iterations.

</details>


### [325] [Resource-Aware Rolling-Horizon Controller for Renewable-Based Virtual Power Plants Providing Automatic Frequency Restoration Reserves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31017)
*Marco Vinicio Avendaño-Caiza,Juan Diego Rios-Peñaloza,Javier Roldán-Pérez,José Luis Rodríguez-Amenedo,Milan Prodanovic*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于滚动时域优化的虚拟电厂中央控制器，旨在最大化收益的同时提供频率恢复服务，并通过日前优化与每分钟滚动优化实现功率和备用参考的实时分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟电厂可提高可再生能源渗透率并提供辅助服务，但现有研究多集中于调度优化层面，实时运行问题尚未充分解决。

Method: 采用滚动时域优化的中央控制器。日前优化定义VPP功率设定点及上、下备用；每分钟执行的滚动优化将功率与备用参考分配给各VPP单元，并考虑资源可用性、运行约束和电池退化。仿真基于两区域互联电力系统，在MATLAB/Simulink中建立详细动态模型，优化问题用YALMIP建模并由Gurobi求解。

Result: 在不同可再生能源出力和需求曲线下进行仿真，结果表明VPP能够通过优化资源分配支持电网频率恢复，即使在预测不确定性存在时也有效。

Conclusion: 所提控制器具有实际系统适用性，能够弥补VPP调度优化与实时运行之间的差距，在保障收益的同时提供频率支撑服务。

Abstract: Virtual power plants (VPPs) are an effective solution for increasing the penetration of renewable energy sources (RES) in power systems. Moreover, VPPs can provide ancillary services if generators and loads are closely connected and properly coordinated. This aspect has been addressed in several studies, yet they mostly focus on the dispatch optimisation level, leaving real-time operation aspects unresolved. To close this gap, a central controller for VPPs based on a rolling-horizon optimisation is proposed in this work. Its main objective is to maximise revenues while delivering frequency restoration services. A day-ahead optimisation firstly defines the power setpoint and the upward and downward reserves of the VPP. Then, the rolling-horizon optimiser, executed every minute, distributes the power and reserve references between the VPP units to achieve their close tracking while taking into account resource availability (wind speed, irradiance, etc.), operational constraints and battery degradation. The effectiveness of the controller is tested using a two-area interconnected power system under different RES generation and demand profiles. The simulations are performed in MATLAB/Simulink and include detailed dynamic models of the VPP elements, demonstrating the applicability of the algorithm to real systems. The day-ahead and the rolling-horizon optimisation problems are modelled using YALMIP and solved using Gurobi. The obtained results demonstrate the VPP supporting grid frequency restoration by optimally allocating its resources, even under the presence of forecast uncertainty.

</details>


### [326] [Optimal Power Sharing for Hybrid Energy Storage Systems Based on Karush-Kuhn-Tucker Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31100)
*Juan Diego Rios-Peñaloza,Milan Prodanovic,Javier Roldán-Pérez*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于混合储能系统（HESS）的功率分配控制器，在考虑各储能技术功率相关效率曲线的前提下最小化损耗，并通过KKT条件解析验证实现实时可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源电站需提供辅助服务，但光伏等波动性要求额外运行灵活性；HESS可借助互补储能技术分担功率需求，然而现有研究多假设恒定效率，且优化方法计算量大、难以实时求解。

Method: 建立考虑功率相关效率曲线的HESS损耗最小化控制器，基于Karush-Kuhn-Tucker（KKT）条件的解析验证进行功率分配；以10 MW光伏电站配锂离子电池与液流电池各2.5 MW/5 MWh为测试案例，在MATLAB/Simulink中仿真，并在OPAL-RT上实时实现验证。

Result: 数值仿真验证了所提控制器的主要贡献，OPAL-RT实时实现表明该算法可用于实时应用。

Conclusion: 所提方法计算高效且适合实际部署，能够在考虑详细效率特性的同时实现HESS功率合理分配并降低损耗。

Abstract: Renewable energy power plants are increasingly expected to provide ancillary services to the grid. Yet, the variability of sources such as solar photovoltaics requires additional operational flexibility to deliver these services. In this context, hybrid energy storage systems (HESSs) offer an appropriate solution, because different technologies with complementary characteristics can be applied and leveraged to share the power demand and improve the overall performance. Yet, a proper power sharing requires a detailed model of the storage elements. This aspect has barely been studied in the literature, where most models consider constant efficiencies. Moreover, most formulations are based on optimisation problems that are computationally too demanding to be solved in real time. In this paper, a controller is proposed to minimise the losses of a HESS considering detailed power-dependent efficiency curves of each storage technology. The power sharing method is based on the analytical verification of the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions, which makes it computationally efficient and suitable for real-world deployment. The proposed controller is applied to a test case consisting of a 10 MW PV power plant with a HESS based on a lithium-ion battery and a redox-flow battery, each with 2.5 MW power and 5 MWh capacity. The main contributions are verified via numerical simulations performed in MATLAB/Simulink, whereas a real-time implementation deployed in OPAL-RT demonstrates the viability of the algorithm for real-time applications.

</details>


### [327] [A Modified Rao-Blackwellised Particle Filter Based on Updated Process Noise Covariance for Chemical Reaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31158)
*Suryasnata Dash,Abhishek Dey*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对随机化学反应网络（CRN）中多个未知参数与状态的联合估计问题，以简化基因表达系统为例，提出一种基于改进Rao-Blackwellised粒子滤波（RBPF）的估计框架，其中参数由粒子滤波估计，状态由扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）估计，并利用化学Langevin方程（CLE）递归更新过程噪声协方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确刻画化学反应网络对合成生物学和天然生物系统的工程化改造至关重要；在CRN中估计多个未知参数和状态时，需要先进行可辨识性分析，再采用合适的估计技术。

Method: 以简化阶数的基因表达系统为案例，对多个未知参数进行参数敏感性分析；采用改进的Rao-Blackwellised粒子滤波（RBPF）联合估计参数与状态。具体地，参数使用粒子滤波估计，状态使用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）估计，过程噪声协方差基于化学Langevin方程（CLE）递归更新。

Result: 将所提滤波器与固定噪声协方差选择的方案进行比较，评估参数估计精度、状态估计误差和新息序列白化程度。结果表明，采用更新噪声协方差的RBPF能在三项指标之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 该方法适合用于随机CRN的联合状态与参数估计，证明其在随机化学反应网络建模与估计中具有适用性。

Abstract: It is necessary to characterize chemical reaction networks (CRNs) accurately to facilitate engineering of both synthetic and naturally occurring biological systems. Estimation of multiple unknown parameters and states in CRNs requires first, an identifiability analysis, and then adoption of a suitable estimation technique. In this paper, we took an example of a reduced order gene expression system, performed parameter sensitivity analysis for multiple unknown parameters, and used a modified Rao-Blackwellised particle filter (RBPF) to estimate parameters and states. The proposed framework estimates parameters using a particle filter and states using an extended Kalman filter (EKF) with process noise covariance updated recursively based on chemical Langevin equation (CLE). We compared the accuracy of parameter estimation, error in state estimation, and whiteness of the innovation sequence for the proposed filter with fixed choices of noise covariance. We found that the RBPF with updated noise covariance finds a balance between these three criteria, demonstrating its suitability for joint state and parameter estimation for stochastic CRNs.

</details>


### [328] [Data-Driven Switched State Estimation with Sparse Sensor Scheduling for Nonlinear Networked Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31172)
*Gianfranco Gagliardi,Franco Angelo Torchiaro,Vincenzo Gallelli,Ayman El Qemmah,Alessandro Casavola*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动框架，用于未知非线性网络系统的联合观测器设计与稀疏传感器调度。通过在线分段局部线性化得到切换线性表示，并用4SID递归辨识；引入正交Procrustes对齐保证状态坐标一致性；设计双率预测校正观测器，其增益由带L2,1正则和谱范数约束的凸优化求解。在Aimsun Next的18链路交通网络中验证，可准确重构宏观交通状态并显著减少所需活跃传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知非线性网络系统的实时状态估计通常面临模型未知、传感器资源有限以及局部线性模型切换时坐标不一致等问题，因此需要联合设计观测器与稀疏传感器调度方案，在保证估计精度和稳定性的同时自动选择信息量大的测量通道。

Method: 将非线性动态在线近似为局部线性化离散时间系统序列，形成切换线性表示，并通过子空间状态空间系统辨识（4SID）递归识别；引入正交Procrustes对齐以促进连续工况切换间的坐标一致性，减轻任意状态空间坐标变化造成的人工不连续。基于辨识出的局部实现设计双率预测校正观测器，观测器增益通过凸优化求解，同时考虑估计精度、传感器稀疏性和稳定性；其中L2,1范数正则项促进增益矩阵列稀疏，自动选择信息测量通道，谱范数约束保证估计误差动态的Schur稳定性。

Result: 在Aimsun Next仿真的交通网络上进行验证。18链路网络结果表明，该观测器能够准确重构宏观交通状态，同时显著减少实时估计所需的活跃传感器数量。

Conclusion: 所提数据驱动框架能够有效实现未知非线性网络系统的联合观测器设计与稀疏传感器调度，在交通网络案例中兼顾状态估计精度与传感器稀疏性，表明其具有实时状态估计应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a data-driven framework for joint observer design and sparse sensor scheduling for unknown nonlinear networked systems. The nonlinear dynamics are approximated online as a piecewise sequence of locally linearized discrete-time systems, resulting in a switched linear representation recursively identified through Subspace State-Space System Identification (4SID). To ensure consistency across regime transitions, an Orthogonal Procrustes alignment is introduced to promote coordinate consistency across consecutive regime transitions and mitigate artificial discontinuities caused by arbitrary state-space coordinate changes. Based on the identified local realizations, a dual-rate predictor-corrector observer is designed. The observer gain is computed through a convex optimization problem that jointly addresses estimation accuracy, sensor sparsity, and stability requirements. In particular, an L_{2,1}-norm regularization term promotes column sparsity in the gain matrix, enabling the automatic selection of informative measurement channels, while a spectral-norm constraint guarantees Schur stability of the estimation error dynamics. The proposed framework is first validated on a traffic network simulated in Aimsun Next. Results obtained on an 18-link network show that the observer accurately reconstructs macroscopic traffic states while significantly reducing the number of active sensors required for real-time estimation.

</details>


### [329] [Sensitivity-driven Personalization of a Glucoregulatory Model for Digital Twin Therapeutics in Type 1 Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31259)
*Clara Escorihuela-Altaba,Vihangkumar V. Naik,Eleonora Manzoni,Jose Garcia-Tirado*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该研究通过扩展Sobol敏感性分析到时序，分析Hovorka血糖调节模型在192名1型糖尿病患者中的参数影响，并基于全局排序筛选参数进行个体化辨识，使60分钟血糖预测误差降低约60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病数字孪生需要准确辨识个体血糖动态模型的参数，但传统敏感性分析常基于有限条件，可能遗漏特定扰动或个体中重要的参数。

Method: 使用Type 1 Diabetes and Exercise Initiative中192名接受自动胰岛素输注治疗参与者的数据，分析Hovorka模型；将Sobol敏感性分析扩展至时间序列，在全天、单独进餐、胰岛素推注和餐后响应四种条件下排序参数影响，并合并为全局排序，用于选择个体化辨识参数子集。

Result: 与使用人群参数相比，仅辨识敏感性分析筛选出的参数子集可将60分钟血糖预测的均方根误差降低60%，降至约31 mg/dL。

Conclusion: 全局排序能够捕捉跨个体和动态条件下的参数影响；缩小需辨识的参数范围可降低计算成本，并有望加速个体化糖尿病数字孪生的发展。

Abstract: Digital twins are increasingly used in diabetes research, but reproducing individual glucose dynamics requires accurate identification of glucoregulatory model parameters. Traditional sensitivity analysis can identify influential parameters, yet a ranking based on limited conditions may miss parameters that matter during specific disturbances or for particular individuals. We therefore examine both the magnitude and timing of parameter influence across dynamic input-output conditions and assess whether a common ranking holds across participants.
  We analyze the Hovorka glucoregulatory model using data from 192 participants receiving automated insulin delivery therapy in the Type 1 Diabetes and Exercise Initiative dataset. We extend Sobol sensitivity analysis to time series and rank parameter influence under four conditions: full-day profiles, isolated meal disturbances, insulin bolus injections, and postprandial responses. We combine the condition-specific results into a global ranking and use it to select parameters for participant-specific identification. Compared with population parameters, identification restricted to the sensitivity-derived subset reduces the root mean square error of 60-minute glucose predictions by 60%, to approximately 31 mg/dL.
  These findings suggest that a global ranking can capture parameter influence across individuals and dynamic conditions. By narrowing the parameters requiring identification, this approach reduces computational cost and could accelerate the development of personalized diabetes digital twins.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [330] [Noise Stability and Rotational Equivariance of Image-Induced Hamiltonian Spectra for Robust Unsupervised Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30280)
*Soumic Sarkar*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文为基于薛定谔型哈密顿量的无监督图像分割建立理论稳健性保证，推导噪声扰动下特征值、特征向量及分割掩码的偏差界，并分析平面刚性运动下的等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类量子启发谱分割方法在自然图像和体数据上经验表现良好，但其对传感器噪声和几何变换的鲁棒性此前缺乏理论保证。

Method: 采用离散图-哈密顿量建模，推导有限样本扰动界，将一维半经典信号分析中的噪声误差分析推广到二维图设置；将特征向量级界传播到二值分割掩码；并进行等变性分析，刻画谱在平面刚体运动群下精确变换的条件及各向异性势项引入的等变性差距。

Result: 得到特征值与基态特征向量漂移同谱隙、噪声方差之间的显式关系，首次给出噪声下分割稳定性的定量保证；量化了各向异性项导致的等变性差距；在自然图像和噪声扰动基准上验证，理论界与观测的谱及掩码偏差吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作为薛定谔型谱分割提供了理论支撑，并为实践者在已知噪声条件下选择算子参数提供了原则性准则。

Abstract: Unsupervised segmentation methods that embed an image as the potential of a Schrödinger-type Hamiltonian and read out object structure from its low-lying eigenstates have demonstrated strong empirical performance across natural and volumetric imagery, yet the robustness of this spectral representation to sensor noise and geometric transformation has never been established theoretically. This paper closes that gap. A discrete graph-Hamiltonian formulation is adopted, consistent with existing quantum-inspired segmentation frameworks, and an explicit finite-sample perturbation bound is derived that links the drift of its eigenvalues and ground-state eigenvector under additive noise of known variance to the operator's spectral gap, extending a one-dimensional noise-error analysis originally developed for semiclassical signal analysis to the two-dimensional graph setting used in image segmentation. The eigenvector-level bound is further propagated to the induced binary segmentation mask, giving, for the first time, a quantitative guarantee on segmentation stability under noise rather than an empirical observation of it. A complementary equivariance analysis characterizes the conditions under which the operator's spectrum transforms exactly under the planar rigid-motion group, and quantifies the equivariance gap introduced by anisotropic potential terms commonly used in practice. Theoretical predictions are validated numerically on natural-image and noise-perturbed benchmarks, showing close agreement between the derived bounds and observed spectral and segmentation-mask deviation, and offering practitioners a principled criterion for selecting operator parameters under known noise conditions.

</details>


### [331] [SatoyamaCT: A Multi-Axis Night-IR Camera-Trap Benchmark for Monitoring Crop-Damaging Wildlife in Japanese Agroforestry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30278)
*Keito Inoshita,Kohei Hisayama,Haruto Sugeno,Kota Nojiri*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了SatoyamaCT数据集，包含来自日本里山地区夜间红外相机陷阱的12,642个专家验证裁剪图像，用于联合评估区域域偏移、新物种检测和不确定性分诊。研究发现区域差距持续存在且开放集检测与分类联合退化，而选择性预测可显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日本里山地区农林交错，鹿、野猪和猕猴造成的作物和森林损害是严重经济问题。相机陷阱可实现可扩展监测，但现有基准多在分布内评估识别，很少优先考虑夜间红外图像，且未在单一数据集上联合解决区域域偏移、新物种检测和基于不确定性的分诊。

Method: 引入SatoyamaCT数据集，包含三个里山地区夜间红外主导相机陷阱的12,642个专家验证裁剪。使用结合BioCLIP嵌入和置信度排序确认的迭代标注协议以减少专家工作量，所有标签由领域生态学家验证，物种级标注者间一致性Cohen's kappa为0.900。采用多轴协议联合评估区域、相机位置和光照的域泛化、开放集新物种检测和选择性预测，均基于捕获事件防泄漏控制。

Result: 难度分为不同失败模式：和歌山地区存在数据固有的16至27个百分点区域差距，在四种骨干网络和包括CORAL、DANN、GroupDRO的域泛化方法中持续存在；开放集检测总体AUROC为0.93至0.96，但在和歌山与分类同时退化。选择性预测在50%覆盖率下将和歌山准确率从0.593提升至0.815。

Conclusion: 该研究支持面向农林业实际害虫监测的不确定性感知分诊工作流。

Abstract: Crop and forest damage from sika deer, wild boar, and Japanese macaque is a serious economic problem in Japanese satoyama, where farmland and forest intermingle. Camera traps enable scalable monitoring, yet existing benchmarks evaluate recognition in-distribution, rarely prioritize night infrared imagery, and do not jointly address regional domain shift, novel-species detection, and uncertainty-based triage on a single dataset. We introduce the Satoyama Camera Trap Dataset (SatoyamaCT), 12,642 expert-verified crops from night-IR-dominant camera traps across three satoyama regions. An iterative annotation protocol combining BioCLIP embeddings with confidence-ordered confirmation reduced expert effort while all labels were verified by domain ecologists; species-level inter-annotator agreement reached Cohen's kappa = 0.900. A multi-axis protocol jointly evaluates domain generalization across region, camera placement, and illumination; open-set novel-species detection; and selective prediction, all under capture-event-based leakage control. Difficulty separates into distinct failure modes: a data-inherent regional gap of 16 to 27 percentage points persists in Wakayama across four backbones and domain-generalization methods including CORAL, DANN, and GroupDRO, and open-set detection reaches AUROC 0.93 to 0.96 overall yet degrades jointly with classification in Wakayama. Selective prediction recovers Wakayama accuracy from 0.593 to 0.815 at 50% coverage, supporting an uncertainty-aware triage workflow for practical pest monitoring in agroforestry.

</details>


### [332] [PGDM-MRSRGAN: Physics-Guided Degradation Model with an SRGAN Framework for Magnetic Resonance Image Super-Resolution: Applications in Low-Field MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30431)
*Yashwant Kurmi,Charlotte R. Sappo,Sai Abitha Srinivas,Selin D. Kandemir,Malvika Viswanathan,Zhongliang Zu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出物理引导退化模型(PGDM)和MRSRGAN超分辨率框架，用于MRI图像超分辨率重建，提升信噪比和空间分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI常受低信噪比和有限空间分辨率影响，损害临床精度，需改进超分辨率重建。

Method: 两阶段：1) PGDM模拟低场MRI退化（模糊、B0不均匀、化学位移、下采样、噪声）生成配对LR/HR训练数据；2) MRSRGAN重建网络，采用混合损失（改进光谱角制图MSAM和基于切片轮廓核反卷积的锐化真值图像）恢复高SNR高分辨率图像。

Result: 实验表明方法在空间分辨率、SNR、PSNR、SSIM、MSAM误差、NIQE指标上更优，减少伪影，在真实MRI数据集上鲁棒。

Conclusion: MRSRGAN结合物理引导退化模型显著提升MRI图像质量、空间分辨率和诊断准确性，在真实数据上有效，有望用于精确诊断成像。

Abstract: Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) often suffers from low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and limited spatial resolution, which compromise clinical precision. This study aims to address these challenges by developing a physics-guided degradation model (PGDM) and a novel deep learning framework, Magnetic Resonance Super-Resolution GAN Imaging (MRSRGAN), for improved super-resolution reconstruction of MRI images. The proposed approach consists of two stages: (1) a physics-guided degradation model that simulates low-field MRI conditions by incorporating artifacts such as blur, B0 inhomogeneity, chemical shift effects, down-sampling, and noise to generate paired low-resolution (LR) and high-resolution (HR) training datasets; and (2) an MRSRGAN-based reconstruction network utilizing a hybrid loss function, including modified spectral angle mapper (MSAM) and sharpened ground truth images based on de-convolution of slice profile-based kernels to restore high-SNR and high-resolution images. Experimental validation demonstrates that the proposed method achieves superior spatial resolution, enhanced SNR, improved peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), higher structural similarity index (SSIM), reduced MSAM error, and better non-reference image quality evaluation (NIQE) metrics. Furthermore, it effectively reduces artifacts and demonstrates robustness on real MRI datasets. The MRSRGAN framework, guided by the physics-informed degradation model, provides a significant improvement in MRI image quality, enhancing spatial resolution and diagnostic accuracy. Its demonstrated robustness and effectiveness on real MRI datasets highlight its potential to resolution enhancement for precise diagnostic imaging.

</details>


### [333] [Image Reconstruction from Phase with Untrained Neural Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30659)
*Ene Meco,Ahmet Enis Cetin*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于投影的两阶段框架，将傅里叶相位、空间支撑约束与图像特定神经先验结合，用于在缺少频谱幅度测量时重建图像；在77张显微图像上评估，最佳变体经500次最终精修后达到31.41 dB pooled PSNR、35.75 dB mean PSNR和0.9531 mean SSIM，较基线提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 傅里叶相位编码重要的空间图像结构，但在没有测得频谱幅度的情况下恢复图像需要额外约束，且绝对强度存在模糊性。

Method: 提出基于投影的两阶段框架，结合傅里叶相位和空间支撑约束与图像特定神经先验。第一阶段交替执行约束施加与正则化神经先验更新，第二阶段仅进行相位/支撑精修并保证收敛。在相同的77张显微图像上评估两种神经先验实现，并与仅约束基线比较。

Result: 经过500次最终精修后，最佳变体达到31.41 dB pooled PSNR、35.75 dB mean PSNR和0.9531 mean SSIM，较基线将pooled PSNR提高1.51 dB，并将pooled MSE降低29.3%。结果说明在评估迭代预算下，神经引导与显式约束精修结合有益，同时表明仅较低的相位残差并不保证更高的重建精度。

Conclusion: 在给定的迭代预算下，将神经先验引导与显式约束精修相结合能够提升无频谱幅度条件下的图像重建性能，但相位残差本身不是重建精度的充分指标。

Abstract: Fourier phase encodes important spatial image structure, but recovering an image without measured spectral magnitude requires additional constraints and leaves absolute intensity ambiguous. We propose a projection-based two-stage framework that combines Fourier-phase and spatial-support constraints with an image-specific neural prior. The first stage alternates constraint enforcement with regularized neural-prior updates, while the second performs phase/support refinement alone with guaranteed convergence. We evaluate two neural-prior implementations on the same 77 microscopy images and compare them with a constraint-only baseline. After 500 final refinement passes, the best-performing variant achieves 31.41 dB pooled PSNR, 35.75 dB mean PSNR, and 0.9531 mean SSIM, improving pooled PSNR by 1.51~dB and reducing pooled MSE by 29.3% relative to the baseline. The results demonstrate the benefit of combining neural guidance with explicit constraint refinement at the evaluated iteration budget, while showing that lower phase residual alone does not guarantee greater reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


### [334] [Deep Pseudo-Proximal Map: A Self-Supervised Data-Fitting Agent for Iterative Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30727)
*Haley Duba-Sullivan,Emma J. Reid,Charles A. Bouman,Gregery T. Buzzard*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 论文提出伪近端映射（PPM），将数据拟合近端映射重新表述为合成概率模型的最小均方误差估计，并实现为仅用采样高斯噪声自监督训练的深度PPM；在去模糊、超分辨和X射线CT中作为迭代重建的数据拟合模块，精度接近参考重建，且XCT中比内层共轭梯度快18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代逆成像算法常需交替求解子问题，其中数据拟合近端映射往往没有解析解，必须在每次外层迭代中通过内层循环近似求解，导致计算成本显著增加。

Method: 提出伪近端映射（PPM），把数据拟合近端映射建模为合成概率模型的MMSE估计；实现深度PPM，一种仅使用采样高斯噪声训练的自监督神经网络，无需真值图像；只要前向模型A及其转置A^T可计算即可训练，线性A时可证明等价于近端映射。

Result: 在高斯去模糊、4倍超分辨（有解析近端映射）和X射线CT（无解析解）上验证；作为迭代重建中的数据拟合模块，三种算子均将参考重建复现到1% NRMSE以内；XCT中用一个网络评估替代内层共轭梯度循环，速度提升18倍。

Conclusion: 深度PPM能有效近似数据拟合近端映射，无需真值训练数据，适用于多种成像逆问题，并可在无解析解场景中显著加速迭代重建。

Abstract: Iterative algorithms for inverse imaging problems split reconstruction into alternating subproblems, one of which requires evaluating the data-fitting proximal map. In many practical cases, this proximal map has no analytic solution and so must be approximated by an inner loop solver at every iteration, compounding the cost of the outer reconstruction loop. To address this, we propose the pseudo-proximal map (PPM), a reformulation of the data-fitting proximal map as the minimum mean square error estimate of a synthetic probabilistic model. We implement the deep PPM as a self-supervised neural network trained only on sampled Gaussian noise, requiring no ground-truth training images. The deep PPM can be trained for any operator for which the forward model $A$ and its transpose $A^T$ can be evaluated, with provable equivalence to the proximal map when $A$ is linear. We validate the deep PPM on Gaussian deblurring and 4x super-resolution, where the proximal map has an analytic solution, and on X-ray computed tomography (XCT), where no analytic solution exists. Used as the data-fitting agent within an iterative reconstruction method, the deep PPM reproduces the reference reconstruction to within 1% NRMSE for all three operators, and for XCT, it replaces the inner conjugate-gradient loop with a single network evaluation that is 18x faster.

</details>


### [335] [Quantum Diffusion Models for Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31070)
*Francesco Aldo Venturelli,Stefano Martina,Marco Parigi,Filippo Caruso,Alba Cervera-Lierta,Miguel A. González Ballester*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的混合量子扩散模型，用于医学图像分析；其前向扩散过程采用在真实量子设备上执行的离散时间量子行走，反向去噪过程则由经典学习模型完成，并在灰度、RGB图像及中等规模3D体数据上验证了生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习可借助叠加、纠缠和干涉等量子力学原理改进机器学习方法，但现有量子图像分析工作受限于量子设备规模，难以处理真实世界的大尺寸医学数据。

Method: 提出混合量子扩散模型：使用离散时间量子行走算法并在真实量子设备上执行，以建模扩散模型的前向动力学；反向步骤使用经典学习模型进行去噪。同时构建基于离散状态空间扩散模型的经典对照方法进行基准比较。

Result: 在灰度图像、RGB图像以及中等规模3D体数据上进行了实验，并使用图像生成领域三种先进指标比较生成能力；结果表明该方法具有竞争力和应用前景，且能够处理真实世界较大规模的医学数据。

Conclusion: 该混合量子扩散模型为量子机器学习在医学图像分析中的应用提供了一种可扩展方案，在现有量子设备限制下仍能取得有希望的结果，并可与经典离散状态空间扩散模型相竞争。

Abstract: Quantum Machine Learning is a novel field of research aimed at devising machine learning approaches exploiting principles of quantum mechanics, such as superposition, entanglement and interference. In this context, we present a scalable hybrid Quantum Diffusion Model, and evaluate its use for medical image analysis. Specifically, our method is based on a Discrete-Time Quantum Walk algorithm, executed on a real quantum device, to model the forward dynamics of the diffusion model. For the backward step of the diffusion model, we devise and evaluate a classical learning model, which is used to reversely denoise the data. In contrast with other existing attempts at applying quantum machine learning for image analysis tasks, severely limited by the size of existing quantum devices, our method allows to process real-world large size medical data. In particular, we present results on grayscale and RGB images, as well as 3D volumes of moderate sizes. We benchmark our results by reproducing an alternative classical counterpart model, based on diffusion models on discrete state spaces. By doing so, we compare the generation capabilities of both models in terms of three distinct state-of-the-art metrics in the field of image generation, showing the competitive, promising results of our approach.

</details>


### [336] [Enhancing Photogrammetric Digital Surface Models with Pretrained Diffusion Models and Multimodal Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31199)
*Antoine Lorentz,Stéphane May,Valentine Bellet,Dawa Derksen,Bastien Nespoulous*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文利用条件扩散模型，结合摄影测量DSM与Pléiades影像来精化DSM，提升高程精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星立体摄影测量可低成本生成大规模DSM，但结果常含噪声、异常值和空洞；机载LiDAR精度高但成本昂贵。

Method: 提出改进的Stable Diffusion 3架构，剪枝文本流并采用patch-wise归一化策略；以摄影测量DSM和Pléiades影像为条件，在LiDAR数据上稳定训练，实现从自然图像到高程图的迁移。

Result: 在法国城市实验中，多模态条件提升高程精度：密集城区RMSE从6.00降至3.45米，在留出城市波尔多从4.16降至2.77米。

Conclusion: 扩散模型能有效精化垂直配准的摄影测量DSM，多模态条件可显著提高精度并泛化到未见城市。

Abstract: Large-scale Digital Surface Models (DSMs) can be produced cost-effectively from satellite images via stereo-photogrammetry. However, the resulting 3D maps are often contaminated by noise, outliers, and voids. On the other hand, aerial LiDAR provides high-accuracy elevation measurements at a substantially higher cost. In this work, we study diffusion models conditioned both on photogrammetric DSMs and Pléiades imagery to refine vertically co-registered DSMs. We introduce a modified Stable Diffusion 3 architecture with a pruned text stream and a patch-wise normalization strategy, enabling stable training on LiDAR data and transfer from natural images to elevation maps. Experiments in French cities demonstrate that multimodal conditioning improves elevation accuracy, reducing Dense Urban RMSE from 6.00 to 3.45 m in the in-context cities and from 4.16 to 2.77 m in the held-out city of Bordeaux.

</details>


### [337] [Towards Whole-Study Screening for Congenital Heart Disease in Fetal Ultrasound Using Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31376)
*Mohamed Azzam,Ruobing Liu,Esther C. Ugwueke,Ziyang Xu,Shibiao Wan,Alex Foy,Abraham Zabih,Jason Christensen,Neil Hamill,Ling Li,Jieqiong Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种面向整个胎儿超声检查的两阶段框架，无需预先筛选关键诊断帧，即可实现先天性心脏病（CHD）病例级筛查，并在内部和外部队列上取得高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHD是最常见的出生缺陷，但产前超声漏诊率仍较高。现有AI方法通常假设临床医生或视图分类器已从检查中分离出关键诊断帧，这限制了其直接用于全检查筛查。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：先在无标签胎儿超声上通过自监督掩码自编码器预训练学习可迁移帧表示；再用疾病鲁棒模块识别心脏帧，并用基于Transformer的多实例学习（MIL）模型仅利用检查级标签进行病例级诊断。模型还返回最高分帧供医生复核，并通过分层头区分危重与非危重CHD。外部验证时采用无标签CORAL自适应。

Result: 在内部多来源开发队列FUSE上，所提心脏门控MIL模型达到AUC 0.985、特异性0.990，优于NATMED集成（AUC 0.861、特异性0.600）和FetalCLIP基础模型（AUC 0.867、特异性0.710）。在独立外部队列上，所有模型初始性能接近随机，但CORAL自适应将所提模型AUC从0.513提升至0.944，而全检查和视图依赖基线未能恢复。

Conclusion: 全检查MIL结合疾病鲁棒心脏帧识别是实现产前CHD筛查的准确且可部署路径。

Abstract: Congenital heart disease (CHD) is the most common birth defect, yet a large fraction of cases remain undetected on prenatal ultrasound, in part because current artificial-intelligence methods assume that the key diagnostic frames have already been isolated from a study, by a clinician or by a view classifier. We remove that assumption and address CHD screening directly at the level of the whole ultrasound study. We propose a two-stage framework that first learns transferable frame representations by self-supervised masked-autoencoder pre-training on unlabeled fetal ultrasound, then identifies cardiac frames with a disease-robust module and aggregates them with a transformer-based multiple instance learning (MIL) model that produces a case-level diagnosis from study-level labels alone. The model further returns its highest-scoring frames for clinician review, and a hierarchical head separates critical from non-critical CHD. On the internal test set of our multi-source development cohort (FUSE), the proposed cardiac-gated MIL model reaches an area under the curve (AUC) of 0.985 with a specificity of 0.990, outperforming the reproduced NATMED ensemble (AUC 0.861, specificity 0.600) and the FetalCLIP foundation model (AUC 0.867, specificity 0.710). On an independent external cohort, all models initially perform near chance, but label-free CORAL adaptation raises the proposed model from an AUC of 0.513 to 0.944, whereas whole-study and view-dependent baselines do not recover. These results indicate that whole-study MIL with disease-robust cardiac-frame identification is an accurate and deployable route to prenatal CHD screening.

</details>
